### [1] [BrainWorld: A Structural-Prior-Conditioned Generative Model for Whole-Brain 4D fMRI Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17742)
*Junfeng Xia,Wenhao Ye,Junxiang Zhang,Xuanye Pan,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainWorld是一个基于结构先验条件生成的全脑4D fMRI动力学模型，利用sMRI引导去噪过程，在22个数据集上稳定生成长达400帧的fMRI轨迹，并通过生成样本增强提升下游任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有fMRI基础模型主要聚焦于表征学习和下游预测任务，缺乏条件性预测生成能力，无法有效建模全脑4D fMRI动态。

Method: 提出BrainWorld生成模型，以sMRI作为受试者级别的解剖上下文条件，将结构信息整合进去噪过程而非作为平行模态，从而引导未来fMRI的生成。

Result: 在22个覆盖不同队列和脑状态的数据集上，BrainWorld能稳定生成长达400帧的4D fMRI轨迹；通过生成样本增强提升了下游任务性能；学到的跨模态表征优于基线方法。

Conclusion: BrainWorld建立了一个条件感知的生成框架，可用于长时序脑动力学建模和多模态表征学习。

Original Abstract: Whole-brain 4D fMRI generation is valuable for modeling functional brain dynamics, yet existing fMRI foundation models mainly target representation learning and downstream prediction rather than conditional predictive generation. We introduce BrainWorld, a structural-prior-conditioned generative model for whole-brain 4D fMRI dynamics. BrainWorld uses sMRI as subject-level anatomical context to guide future fMRI generation, integrating structural information into the denoising process rather than treating it as a parallel modality. Evaluated on 22 datasets spanning diverse cohorts and brain states, BrainWorld generates stable 4D fMRI trajectories up to 400 frames, improves downstream performance through generated-example augmentation, and learns transferable multimodal representations that outperform baselines. Together, these results establish BrainWorld as a condition-aware generative framework for long-horizon brain dynamics modeling and multimodal representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 全脑4D功能磁共振成像生成对于建模大脑功能动力学具有重要价值，然而现有的fMRI基础模型主要针对表征学习和下游预测任务，而非条件性预测生成。本文提出BrainWorld，一个基于结构先验条件生成的全脑4D fMRI动力学模型。BrainWorld利用结构磁共振成像作为受试者水平的解剖上下文来引导未来fMRI的生成，将结构信息整合进去噪过程而非将其作为平行模态处理。在覆盖不同队列和脑状态的22个数据集上的评估表明，BrainWorld能够稳定生成长达400帧的4D fMRI轨迹，通过生成样本增强提升下游任务性能，并学习到可迁移的多模态表征，其性能优于基线方法。综上，这些结果确立了BrainWorld作为一个条件感知的生成框架，用于长时序大脑动力学建模和多模态表征学习。

</details>


### [2] [Not Truly Multilingual: Script Consistency as a Missing Dimension in VLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17188)
*Prabhjot Singh,Bhushan Pawar,Madhu Reddiboina,Rajvee Sheth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前多语言视觉语言模型（VLM）假设语言与书写系统一一对应，忽视了多文字语言用户。本文提出旁遮普语多模态视觉推理基准（PuMVR），包含1000个严格并行的图像-文本实例，涵盖旁遮普语的三种活跃文字（Gurmukhi、Shahmukhi、Roman）。评估10个SOTA VLM后发现存在显著且系统的文字鸿沟：模型在一种文字上可解决视觉任务，在另一种文字上却失败，准确率差值高达16%。视觉输入均匀提升绝对性能但无法弥合文字鸿沟，跨文字上下文迁移高度脆弱。提出文字一致性率（SCR）作为多文字评估的强制性指标，该指标在基准上低至24.8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM多语言评测假设语言与书写系统一一对应，忽视了使用多种文字的数亿用户（如旁遮普语三种文字），导致模型在实际多文字场景中出现系统性失败。

Method: 构建PuMVR基准，包含1000个严格并行的图像-文本对（三种文字）。评估10个SOTA VLM，采用McNemar检验对文字对进行显著性分析，提出SCR作为跨文字一致性度量指标。

Result: 模型在三种文字间准确率差值高达16%；视觉输入均匀提升性能但无法缩小文字鸿沟；跨文字上下文迁移高度脆弱；SCR低至24.8%；McNemar检验证实所有文字对间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前"多语言"VLM并非真正多文字模型，文字一致性应作为公平AI访问的强制性评估指标。

Original Abstract: Current multilingual evaluations for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) assume a one-to-one mapping between language and orthography, overlooking billions of users of multi-script languages. We introduce PuMVR (Punjabi Multimodal Visual Reasoning), a benchmark of 1,000 strictly parallel image-text instances across Punjabi's three active scripts: Gurmukhi, Shahmukhi, and Roman. Evaluating 10 state-of-the-art VLMs, we expose a substantial and systematic Script Gap. Models frequently solve visual tasks in one script while failing identical tasks in another, with accuracy deltas reaching 16%. Crucially, visual input boosts absolute performance uniformly yet does not close the orthographic gap. Furthermore, cross-script in-context transfer is highly brittle, exposing script-locked knowledge representation. Supported by McNemar tests across all script pairs, our findings demonstrate that current "multilingual" VLMs are not truly multi-script. We propose the Script Consistency Rate (SCR), which falls as low as 24.8% on our benchmark, as a mandatory metric for script-agnostic evaluation to ensure equitable AI access. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/prabhjotschugh/Not-Truly-Multilingual-PuMVR.

中文摘要翻译: 当前视觉语言模型（VLM）的多语言评估假设语言与书写系统存在一一对应关系，忽视了使用多文字语言的数十亿用户。我们提出了PuMVR（旁遮普语多模态视觉推理）基准，包含1,000个严格并行的图像-文本实例，涵盖旁遮普语的三种活跃文字：Gurmukhi、Shahmukhi和Roman。通过对10个最先进的VLM进行评估，我们揭示了一个显著且系统的文字鸿沟。模型经常能在一种文字上解决视觉任务，却在另一种文字的相同任务上失败，准确率差值高达16%。关键的是，视觉输入能均匀地提升绝对性能，但无法弥合文字上的差距。此外，跨文字的上下文内迁移高度脆弱，暴露出锁定于特定文字的知识表征。在McNemar检验对所有文字对的支持下，我们的研究结果表明，当前"多语言"VLM并非真正的多文字模型。我们提出了文字一致性率（SCR）作为文字无关评估的强制性指标，该指标在我们的基准上低至24.8%，以确保公平的AI访问。数据和代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/prabhjotschugh/Not-Truly-Multilingual-PuMVR。

</details>


### [3] [Quantum Enchanced Multi-Scale CNN with Bi-directional Mamba for Crop Field Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17222)
*Mohammad Salman Khan,Ehsan Atoofian,Saad B. Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于BiSpectral Mamba的高光谱图像农作物分类框架，结合多尺度卷积、光谱注意力、双向状态空间建模和量子启发学习，在UAVHSI-Crop数据集上达到84.83%的总体分类精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高光谱图像农作物分析面临光谱维度高、空间复杂、类别不平衡和标注样本有限等挑战，现有方法难以充分建模光谱-空间特征的长程依赖关系。

Method: 提出BiSpectral Mamba框架：(1) 多尺度CNN骨干网络提取层次化空间-光谱特征；(2) 光谱注意力机制增强信息波段、抑制冗余噪声；(3) BiSpectral Mamba模块通过双向状态空间建模将特征图建模为序列令牌以捕获长程依赖；(4) 引入类别加权优化和特征融合策略缓解类别不平衡。

Result: 在UAVHSI-Crop数据集上总体分类精度达到84.83%，验证了卷积、注意力与状态空间建模的集成有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了鲁棒的农作物分类，并在作物病害检测、产量预测、土壤湿度估计等农业遥感应用中展现出潜力，同时凸显了结构化状态空间和量子启发架构在高光谱图像分析中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) crop analysis is essential for precision agriculture because it captures rich spectral and spatial information for accurate crop monitoring and assessment. However, HSI classification remains challenging due to high spectral dimensionality, spatial complexity, class imbalance, and limited labeled samples. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a BiSpectral Mamba-based framework that combines multi-scale convolutional feature extraction, spectral attention, bidirectional state-space modeling, and quantum-inspired learning. A multi-scale CNN backbone first extracts hierarchical spatial-spectral representations through feature fusion across multiple resolutions. A spectral attention mechanism then emphasizes informative bands while suppressing redundant and noisy channels. The refined features are processed by a BiSpectral Mamba module that captures long-range dependencies in both forward and backward directions by modeling hyperspectral feature maps as sequential tokens. In addition, class-weighted optimization and feature fusion strategies are incorporated to improve training stability and mitigate class imbalance. Experimental evaluation on the UAVHSI-Crop dataset demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving an overall accuracy of 84.83%. The results show that integrating convolutional, attention-based, and state-space modeling components enables robust spatial-spectral feature learning for crop classification. The proposed framework also shows potential for broader agricultural and remote sensing applications, including crop disease detection, yield prediction, and soil moisture estimation, while highlighting the effectiveness of structured state-space and quantum-inspired architectures for hyperspectral image analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱图像（HSI）作物分析对于精准农业至关重要，因为它能够捕获丰富的光谱和空间信息，用于准确的作物监测与评估。然而，由于光谱维度高、空间复杂度大、类别不平衡以及标注样本有限，HSI分类仍然具有挑战性。为应对这些挑战，本文提出了一种基于BiSpectral Mamba的框架，该框架结合了多尺度卷积特征提取、光谱注意力、双向状态空间建模和量子启发学习。多尺度CNN骨干网络首先通过跨多分辨率的特征融合提取层次化的空间-光谱表示；随后光谱注意力机制增强信息波段并抑制冗余噪声通道；精炼后的特征由BiSpectral Mamba模块处理，该模块通过将高光谱特征图建模为序列令牌，在正向和反向两个方向上捕获长程依赖关系。此外，还引入了类别加权优化和特征融合策略，以提高训练稳定性并缓解类别不平衡。在UAVHSI-Crop数据集上的实验评估表明了所提框架的有效性，总体分类精度达到84.83%。结果表明，集成卷积、注意力和状态空间建模组件能够实现稳健的空间-光谱特征学习，用于作物分类。所提框架还展现出在更广泛的农业和遥感应用中的潜力，包括作物病害检测、产量预测和土壤湿度估计，同时突显了结构化状态空间和量子启发架构在高光谱图像分析中的有效性。

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Benchmarks: Continuous Edge Inference for Fine-Grained Roadside Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17241)
*Aditya Mishra,Haroon Lone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Edge-TSR提出了一种面向部署的持续边缘推理系统，通过轻量级轨迹感知的时间稳定化机制，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实现了持续的路边感知。研究发现，从静态图像评估转向真实流式部署时，性能会持续下降20-30%，而Edge-TSR通过时间推理稳定化可恢复高达10.16%的分类准确率，并在单嵌入式设备上实现16.18 FPS的持续运行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基准评估无法捕捉资源受限边缘硬件上持续AI推理的部署效应（如时间不稳定、热节流、性能变异性），导致评估结果严重高估实际部署性能。需要一种部署感知的评估方法和推理稳定化机制来弥合这一差距。

Method: Edge-TSR集成了检测、跟踪、细粒度分类以及轻量级轨迹感知的时间稳定化机制。该机制利用轨迹信息对连续帧的推理结果进行时序平滑，在不引入显著计算开销的前提下提升流式推理的一致性。

Result: 1）从静态图像评估转向真实流式部署时，三个基线模型一致出现20-30%的性能退化；2）Edge-TSR的时间稳定化机制可恢复高达10.16%的分类准确率；3）在55分钟26公里的车辆部署中，在单嵌入式设备（无云卸载）上实现16.18 FPS的持续运行并保持在安全热阈值内。

Conclusion: 部署感知的评估和时间推理稳定化是面向真实世界传感部署的持续运行边缘AI系统的必要组成部分。论文发布了带注释的流式视频评估数据集和完整系统实现以支持可复现的部署中心评估。

Original Abstract: Continuous AI inference on resource-constrained edge hardware introduces deployment effects that are largely invisible to conventional benchmark evaluation, including temporal instability in streaming video, thermal throttling under sustained load, and workload-dependent performance variability. We present Edge-TSR, a deployment-oriented continuous edge inference system for sustained roadside perception on the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. Edge-TSR integrates detection, tracking, fine-grained classification, and a lightweight track-aware temporal stabilization mechanism that improves streaming inference consistency with negligible computational overhead. Our central finding is that benchmark-centric evaluation systematically overstates deployed edge inference performance. Across three state-of-the-art baselines, we observe consistent 20-30% relative degradation when transitioning from static-image evaluation to real-world streaming deployment. Edge-TSR addresses this gap through temporal inference stabilization, recovering up to 10.16% classification accuracy over per-frame inference baselines while maintaining sustained real-time performance under continuous operation. We evaluate the complete system under diverse real-world deployment conditions, jointly characterizing inference quality, latency, throughput, and thermal behavior during long-duration operation. A 55-minute vehicular deployment over a 26 km route demonstrates sustained operation at 16.18 FPS within safe thermal limits on a single embedded device without cloud offload. Our findings show that deployment-aware evaluation and temporal inference stabilization are necessary components of continuously operating edge AI systems intended for real-world sensing deployments. We release a sample annotated streaming video evaluation dataset and full system implementation to support reproducible deployment-centric evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 在资源受限的边缘硬件上进行连续AI推理会引入传统基准评估通常不可见的部署效应，包括流式视频的时间不稳定性、持续负载下的热节流以及工作负载相关的性能变异性。我们提出Edge-TSR，一个面向部署的、在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实现持续路边感知的连续边缘推理系统。Edge-TSR集成了检测、跟踪、细粒度分类以及一个轻量级的轨迹感知时间稳定化机制，该机制以可忽略的计算开销提升了流式推理的一致性。我们的核心发现是：以基准为中心的评估系统性地高估了已部署的边缘推理性能。在三个最先进的基线上，从静态图像评估转向真实流式部署时，我们观察到一致的20-30%的相对退化。Edge-TSR通过时间推理稳定化弥补了这一差距，相比逐帧推理基线可恢复高达10.16%的分类准确率，同时保持在持续运行下的实时性能。我们在多样化的真实部署条件下评估了整个系统，联合表征了长时间运行期间的推理质量、延迟、吞吐量和热行为。在一次55分钟、26公里路线的车辆部署中，该系统在单嵌入式设备上以16.18 FPS持续运行在安全热阈值内，无需云卸载。我们的研究结果表明，部署感知的评估和时间推理稳定化是面向真实世界传感部署的持续运行边缘AI系统的必要组成部分。我们发布了一个带注释的流式视频评估数据集样本以及完整的系统实现，以支持可复现的部署中心评估。

</details>


### [5] [Landsat-Sentinel-2 Algal Bloom Mapping Using Vision Transformers: Model Description, Implementation, and Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17242)
*Thainara Lima,Vitor Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次成功实现基于视觉Transformer（ViT）的沿海藻华遥感监测，利用Landsat-8/9和Sentinel-2 A/B/C的30米中分辨率影像，通过深度学习分类方法克服了传统生物光学方法在光谱覆盖和产品一致性上的局限。Swin Transformer在云和耀斑干扰条件下优于传统光谱指数方法，有效减少误报，证明了深度学习在动态沿海环境中进行中分辨率、一致性藻华监测的可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 沿海藻华监测需要高频次、高空间分辨率且全球一致的观测数据。虽然Landsat-8/9和Sentinel-2系列卫星提供了超过十年的中分辨率多光谱影像（每2-3天覆盖近全球），但其在 aquatic 环境中的应用仍面临光谱覆盖有限、缺乏统一反射率产品等挑战。传统生物光学方法难以克服这些限制，因此需要探索数据驱动的深度学习方法。

Method: 研究构建了全球分布的藻华斑块数据集，覆盖全球易发生藻华的沿海热点区域。比较了四种基于Transformer的架构（视觉Transformer变体）与标准卷积神经网络基线模型在精细尺度藻华检测中的表现，并在不同光学水体类型、大气和地表条件下进行评估。其中Swin Transformer被重点应用于时间序列分析中，与传统的基于光谱指数的藻华检测方法进行对比。

Result: 所有深度学习模型在检测漂浮藻华区域方面均表现出强大能力，遗漏误差和误报误差在8-65%之间。在云和耀斑干扰的时间序列分析中，Swin Transformer显著优于传统光谱指数方法——传统方法产生大量误报，而Swin Transformer有效避开了云和耀斑影响的像素。与MODIS产品的对比进一步凸显了更高空间分辨率在检测碎片化、不规则藻华方面的优势。

Conclusion: 深度学习可作为中分辨率影像下沿海动态环境中漂浮藻华一致性监测的可靠工具，特别是基于Transformer的架构（如Swin Transformer）在复杂光照和大气条件下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Coastal algal bloom monitoring requires frequent, spatially detailed, and globally consistent observations, provided by Landsat-8/9 and Sentinel-2 A/B/C. Together, these missions offer over a decade of medium-resolution multispectral imagery with near-global coverage every 2-3 days, enabling the detection of fragmented bloom structures not resolvable by coarse ocean-color sensors. However, their use in aquatic environments remains challenging due to limited spectral coverage and a lack of harmonized reflectance products. As an alternative to traditional bio-optical methods, deep learning-based image classification offers a data-driven approach that can overcome many of these limitations. This study presents the first successful implementation of vision transformer-based coastal algal bloom mapping using 30-m Landsat-Sentinel-2 images. A globally distributed bloom patch dataset was generated across bloom-prone coastal hotspots worldwide. Four transformer-based architectures were compared against a standard convolutional baseline for fine-scale bloom detection, and assessed under different optical water types and atmospheric and surface conditions. All deep learning models showed strong capabilities in detecting floating bloom areas, with omission and commission errors of 8-65%. Under cloud and glint stress in a time series, the Swin Transformer outperformed traditional spectral-index approaches, which produced widespread false positives, effectively avoiding cloud- and glint-affected pixels. Comparisons with MODIS-derived products further highlighted the benefits of higher spatial resolution in detecting fragmented and irregularly affected blooms. Our findings support deep learning as a reliable tool for medium-resolution, consistent monitoring of floating algal blooms in dynamic coastal environments.

中文摘要翻译: 沿海藻华监测需要高频次、空间精细且全球一致的观测，Landsat-8/9和Sentinel-2 A/B/C卫星系列恰好提供了这样的数据。这些任务共同提供了超过十年的中分辨率多光谱影像，每2-3天覆盖近全球范围，能够检测粗分辨率海洋水色传感器无法分辨的碎片化藻华结构。然而，由于光谱覆盖有限且缺乏统一的反射率产品，这些数据在水体环境中的应用仍然面临挑战。作为传统生物光学方法的替代方案，基于深度学习的图像分类提供了一种数据驱动的方法，可以克服许多上述限制。本研究首次成功实现了基于视觉Transformer的沿海藻华制图，使用了30米分辨率的Landsat-Sentinel-2影像。研究中构建了一个全球分布的藻华斑块数据集，覆盖了全球易发生藻华的沿海热点区域。四种基于Transformer的架构与标准卷积基线模型在精细尺度藻华检测上进行了比较，并在不同光学水体类型、大气和地表条件下进行了评估。所有深度学习模型在检测漂浮藻华区域方面均表现出强大能力，遗漏误差和误报误差在8-65%之间。在云和耀斑干扰的时间序列分析中，Swin Transformer优于传统光谱指数方法——后者产生了广泛的误报，而Swin Transformer有效避开了云和耀斑影响的像素。与MODIS派生产品的比较进一步凸显了更高空间分辨率在检测碎片化和不规则藻华方面的优势。我们的研究结果表明，深度学习可作为中分辨率下动态沿海环境中漂浮藻华一致性监测的可靠工具。

</details>


### [6] [GeoDisaster: Benchmarking Orchestrated Agents for Operational Disaster Geo-Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17246)
*Maram Hasan,Aman Verma,Savitra Roy,Hariseetharam Gunduboina,Daksh Jain,Muhammad Haris Khan,Subhasis Chaudhuri,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoDisaster——一个面向卫星遥感灾害推理的基准数据集（包含2,921个实例、43种问题类型、五个任务族），以及一个基于多智能体编排的框架，通过角色合约期望对齐（RCEA）方法提升工具使用、证据锚定、状态一致性和决策生成能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型（RS-VLMs）在视觉理解和指令跟随方面取得进展，但缺乏面向操作化地理智能的工具化空间推理和结构化、有证据支撑的决策能力，亟需一个可验证的灾害推理基准和相应的推理框架。

Method: 构建GeoDisaster基准（整合光学/SAR影像、栅格掩膜、矢量几何、道路网络、暴露层等多模态证据）；提出多智能体编排框架，包含18个面向灾害的工具，角色专业化智能体通过显式执行合约协调；采用角色合约期望对齐（RCEA）方法，即失败感知的有监督微调结合基于合约的强化学习（在密集的步骤级信号上）。

Result: 实验表明GeoDisaster对现有RS-VLMs和智能体系统构成挑战，RCEA方法显著提升了工具使用、证据锚定、状态一致性和决策生成能力。

Conclusion: GeoDisaster基准和RCEA框架推动了遥感领域从简单视觉理解走向操作化地理智能决策，为工具化空间推理提供了可验证的评估标准和有效的训练范式。

Original Abstract: Remote-sensing vision-language models (RS-VLMs) have advanced Earth-observation analysis toward visual interpretation and instruction-following, yet fall short of operational geo-intelligence, which demands tool-grounded spatial reasoning and structured, evidence-backed decisions. We introduce GeoDisaster, an operational geospatial disaster reasoning benchmark with 2,921 verified instances across 43 question types and five task families: deforestation monitoring, multi-hazard analysis, building-damage assessment, flood-safe routing, and Sentinel-1 SAR flood monitoring. Instances integrate heterogeneous EO/GIS evidence-optical and SAR imagery, raster masks, vector geometries, road networks, and exposure layers-spanning hazard detection, damage assessment, exposure estimation, and diagnostic report generation. Ground-truth answers are grounded in executable geospatial workflows and deterministic consistency checks, removing the need for language-model annotation. We further propose an orchestrated multi-agent framework with 18 disaster-oriented tools, where role-specialized agents coordinate through explicit execution contracts, aligned via Role-Contract Expectation Alignment (RCEA): failure-aware supervised fine-tuning combined with contract-grounded reinforcement learning over dense step-level signals. Experiments show that GeoDisaster challenges existing RS-VLMs and agentic systems, while RCEA improves tool use, evidence grounding, state consistency, and decision generation.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感视觉语言模型（RS-VLMs）在地球观测分析中取得了向视觉理解和指令跟随的进展，但在操作化地理智能方面仍存在不足，后者要求工具化的空间推理以及结构化、有证据支撑的决策。我们提出了GeoDisaster——一个面向操作化地理空间灾害推理的基准，包含2,921个经过验证的实例，覆盖43种问题类型和五个任务族：森林砍伐监测、多灾害分析、建筑损毁评估、洪水安全路径规划以及Sentinel-1 SAR洪水监测。实例整合了异质的EO/GIS证据——光学与SAR影像、栅格掩膜、矢量几何、道路网络和暴露层——涵盖灾害检测、损毁评估、暴露估计和诊断报告生成。真值答案基于可执行的地理空间工作流和确定性一致性检查，无需语言模型标注。我们进一步提出了一个编排后的多智能体框架，包含18个面向灾害的工具，角色专业化智能体通过显式执行合约进行协调，并通过角色合约期望对齐（RCEA）方法进行对齐：即失败感知的有监督微调结合基于合约的强化学习，在密集的步骤级信号上进行训练。实验表明，GeoDisaster对现有RS-VLMs和智能体系统构成挑战，而RCEA方法改进了工具使用、证据锚定、状态一致性和决策生成能力。

</details>


### [7] [Pulling The REINS: Training-Free Safety Alignment of Video Diffusion Models via Representation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17257)
*Rohit Kundu,Arindam Dutta,Sarosij Bose,Athula Balachandran,Amit K. Roy-Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REINS是一种无需训练的推理时安全引导方法，通过在视频扩散模型的隐藏状态中沿单一安全方向进行干预，在不更新权重、不枚举概念的前提下将有害生成重定向为语义相关的安全内容，在9个模型上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有防御方法要么需要昂贵的安全微调且会降低通用能力，要么使用容易被对抗性提示绕过的外部过滤器，因此需要一种不降低生成质量且难以被绕过的安全对齐方法。

Method: 通过监督PCA在二值安全标签上发现隐藏状态中线性编码的安全相关方向，在推理时对中间Transformer层的隐藏状态添加该方向向量，将生成轨迹从有害内容重定向到语义相关的安全替代内容。

Result: 在9个视频扩散模型（1.3B-5B参数）、文本到视频和图像到视频生成任务上验证了有效性；发现安全信息随Transformer深度单调累积，但引导效果在中间层（约50%深度）达到峰值，揭示了信息可用性与下游传播能力之间的基本权衡。

Conclusion: REINS提供了一种训练免费、无需权重更新、计算开销可忽略的安全对齐方法，在视频生成领域进行了最广泛的安全评估，为扩散模型的安全部署提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Open-weight video diffusion models can generate photorealistic unsafe content, from violence to misinformation, yet existing defenses either require expensive safety fine-tuning that degrades general capability, or apply external filters that are trivially bypassed by adversarial prompts. We present REINS (REpresentation-space INference-time Safety steering), a training-free method that aligns video diffusion models at inference time by steering their internal representations toward safe generation. Our key finding is that safety-relevant structure is linearly encoded in the hidden-state activations of video diffusion transformers, and a single direction, discovered via Supervised PCA on binary safety labels, suffices to separate safe from unsafe generation trajectories. At inference, adding this direction to hidden states at an intermediate transformer layer redirects generation from harmful content to semantically related safe alternatives, with no weight updates, no concept enumeration, and negligible computational overhead. Through mechanistic analysis, we reveal that while safety information accumulates monotonically with transformer depth, steering effectiveness peaks at intermediate layers (~50% depth), exposing a fundamental tradeoff between information availability and downstream propagation capacity. We evaluate REINS across 9 video diffusion models, multiple parameter scales (1.3B-5B), and both text-to-video and image-to-video generation, to our knowledge, the broadest safety evaluation suite in the video generation literature.

中文摘要翻译: 开放权重的视频扩散模型能够生成从暴力到虚假信息的逼真不安全内容，然而现有的防御方法要么需要昂贵的安全微调且会降低通用能力，要么应用容易被对抗性提示绕过的外部过滤器。我们提出REINS（表示空间推理时安全引导），一种无需训练的方法，通过在推理时引导其内部表示朝向安全生成来对齐视频扩散模型。我们的关键发现是，安全相关结构线性地编码在视频扩散Transformer的隐藏状态激活中，通过监督PCA在二值安全标签上发现的一个单一方向，就足以区分安全与不安全的生成轨迹。在推理时，将该方向添加到中间Transformer层的隐藏状态，即可将生成从有害内容重定向到语义相关的安全替代内容，无需权重更新、无需概念枚举，且计算开销可忽略。通过机制分析，我们发现虽然安全信息随Transformer深度单调累积，但引导效果在中间层（约50%深度）达到峰值，揭示了信息可用性与下游传播能力之间的基本权衡。我们在9个视频扩散模型、多个参数规模（1.3B-5B）以及文本到视频和图像到视频生成任务上评估REINS，据我们所知，这是视频生成文献中最广泛的安全评估套件。

</details>


### [8] [Training LLMs with Reinforcement Learning over Digital Twin Representations for Reasoning-Intensive Surgical VideoQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17279)
*Yiqing Shen,Han Zhang,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，训练大语言模型将感知与推理解耦，通过在手术基础模型构建的数字孪生表示上操作，并结合分层表示与不确定性校准奖励，在结肠镜手术视频问答任务上取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将视频压缩为离散token表示，将视觉感知与推理耦合在一起，破坏了连续的时空关系，限制了多步推理能力。因此需要一种解耦感知与推理的新方法。

Method: 提出强化学习框架训练LLM，在手术基础模型构建的数字孪生表示上解耦感知与推理；引入帧级、时间窗口级和手术流程级的分层表示及概率不确定性估计；设计结合格式验证、临床合理性评估和不确定性感知校准的新型奖励函数。

Result: 在REAL-Colon-Reason（含2000个问答对，三个复杂度层级）以及REAL-Colon-VQA和EndoVis18-VQA两个现有基准上均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦感知与推理、引入分层表示和不确定性感知奖励，有效提升了手术视频问答的多步推理能力，验证了该框架的通用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Surgical video question answering requires multi-step reasoning across semantic, spatial, and temporal dimensions. Existing methods architecturally compress videos into discrete token representations and couple visual perception with reasoning. This approach fragments continuous spatial-temporal relationships and has been shown to restrict multi-step reasoning capabilities. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that trains large language models (LLMs) to decouple perception from reasoning by operating over digital twin representations constructed from surgical foundation models. Additionally, we introduce hierarchical representations across frame, temporal window, and procedure levels with probabilistic uncertainty estimates. Finally, we propose a novel reward that combines format validation with accuracy assessment through clinical plausibility evaluation and uncertainty-aware calibration for training. To demonstrate the capabilities of this approach, we introduce REAL-Colon-Reason, a colonoscopic benchmark with 2000 question-answer pairs across three complexity levels. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on REAL-Colon-Reason and two existing surgical VideoQA benchmarks REAL-Colon-VQA and EndoVis18-VQA.

中文摘要翻译: 手术视频问答需要在语义、空间和时间维度上进行多步推理。现有方法在架构上将视频压缩为离散的令牌表示，并将视觉感知与推理耦合在一起。这种方法割裂了连续的时空关系，已被证明会限制多步推理能力。我们引入了一个强化学习框架，训练大语言模型在手术基础模型构建的数字孪生表示上进行操作，从而将感知与推理解耦。此外，我们引入了跨帧级、时间窗口级和手术流程级的分层表示，并带有概率不确定性估计。最后，我们提出了一种新颖的奖励函数，将格式验证与通过临床合理性评估和不确定性感知校准的准确性评估相结合。为了展示该方法的能力，我们引入了REAL-Colon-Reason——一个包含2000个问答对、涵盖三个复杂度层级的结肠镜基准数据集。我们在REAL-Colon-Reason以及两个现有手术视频问答基准REAL-Colon-VQA和EndoVis18-VQA上均达到了最优性能。

</details>


### [9] [Pareto LoRA: Mitigating Modality Imbalance in Unified Multimodal Models via Pareto-Optimal Gradient Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17296)
*Xiwen Wei,Mark Nutter,Madhusudhanan Srinivasan,Radu Marculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对统一多模态模型在LoRA微调中出现的模态不平衡问题，提出Pareto LoRA策略，通过帕累托最优梯度整合平衡文本与图像目标，显著提升图像生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 统一多模态模型在指令微调时存在显著模态不平衡：语言梯度主导优化，导致图像生成质量下降，尤其是在LoRA等参数高效微调场景下。

Method: 将多模态指令微调重构为双目标优化问题，提出Pareto LoRA——一种帕累托最优梯度整合策略，通过调制梯度方向和强度来平衡文本与图像目标。

Result: 在CoMM基准和Emu2模型上，Pareto LoRA相比原始LoRA在感知图像质量上提升高达44.9%，同时保持可比的文本性能。

Conclusion: Pareto LoRA能有效缓解多模态微调中的模态不平衡问题，实现更均衡的多模态生成。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for integrating multimodal understanding and generation within a single autoregressive transformer. However, during multimodal instruction tuning, these models often exhibit pronounced modality imbalance: language gradients dominate optimization, thus leading to lower image generation quality, especially under parameter-efficient fine-tuning such as LoRA. In this work, we systematically analyze modality imbalance in LoRA-based fine-tuning of UMMs for interleaved text-image generation. We show that vision modality performance degrades substantially more than text modality performance when compared to unimodal counterparts, and that modality-specific gradients can differ by orders of magnitude across various tasks and layers. Motivated by this observation, we reformulate the multimodal instruction tuning as a bi-objective optimization problem and propose Pareto LoRA, a Pareto-optimal gradient integration strategy that balances the text and image objectives by modulating the gradient direction and strength. Experiments on the CoMM benchmark with Emu2 demonstrate that Pareto LoRA consistently improves multimodal generation balance, achieving up to 44.9% gains in perceptual image quality over vanilla LoRA while maintaining comparable text performance.

中文摘要翻译: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）近期作为一种有前景的范式出现，将多模态理解和生成整合在单一自回归Transformer中。然而，在多模态指令微调过程中，这些模型常表现出显著的模态不平衡：语言梯度主导优化，导致图像生成质量下降，尤其是在参数高效微调（如LoRA）下。本文系统分析了UMMs在LoRA微调中用于交错文本-图像生成时的模态不平衡问题。我们发现，与单模态对应版本相比，视觉模态性能下降幅度远大于文本模态，且不同任务和层之间模态特定梯度可能相差数个数量级。受此观察启发，我们将多模态指令微调重构为双目标优化问题，并提出了Pareto LoRA——一种帕累托最优梯度整合策略，通过调制梯度方向和强度来平衡文本和图像目标。在CoMM基准和Emu2模型上的实验表明，Pareto LoRA持续改善多模态生成平衡，相比原始LoRA在感知图像质量上实现高达44.9%的提升，同时保持可比的文本性能。

</details>


### [10] [MeiBRD: Meta-Learning Intraoperative Biomechanical Residual Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17379)
*Casey Meisenzahl,Jon Heiselman,Michael Holtz,Yubo Ye,Michael Miga,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合配准框架，利用稀疏术中对应点通过图神经扩散函数学习残差变形场来校正生物力学先验，从而提升肝脏配准精度和泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物力学模型存在简化假设导致的预测偏差，而数据驱动方法面临数据效率低、泛化差和物理不可行的问题，亟需一种兼具物理先验与数据适应能力的混合方案。

Method: 将稀疏术中测量点视为“上下文”样本，构造残差变形函数的输入-输出对；用图神经扩散函数（含几何感知注意力）在三维肝脏网格上建模该残差，并通过前馈元学习器从上下文样本中学习该残差函数。

Result: 在可变形肝脏体模数据集上，该方法在配准精度和泛化能力上优于刚性配准、纯生物力学模型及纯数据驱动基线，尤其在几何形状和形变分布外场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: 通过学习残差来校正线性生物力学预测，结合物理先验与稀疏数据的元学习策略，能有效解决术中肝脏配准中变形大、测量稀疏的难题。

Original Abstract: Accurate intraoperative liver registration is challenging due to substantial soft-tissue deformation yet sparse intraoperative measurements. Biomechanical models regularize this ill-posedness with prior knowledge but exhibit persistent prediction bias due to simplifying assumptions, while data-driven learning solutions struggle with data efficiency, generalization, and physical plausibility. We propose a hybrid registration framework that adapts a biomechanical prior using sparse intraoperative correspondences. Rather than learning a full deformation field, we learn a residual deformation function that corrects linear biomechanical predictions, modeled as a graph neural diffusion function with geometry-aware attention over the 3D liver mesh. To enable long-range information transfer of sparse observations, we take a novel perspective of sparse intraoperative measurements as \textit{context} samples where input-output pairs of the residual deformation function are fully observed, casting the problem into learning-to-learn this residual function from intraoperative context samples with feedforward meta-learners. Experiments on a deformable liver phantom dataset demonstrate improved registration accuracy and generalization compared to rigid, biomechanical, and data-driven baselines, particularly for out-of-distribution geometries and deformations.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的术中肝脏配准因存在显著的软组织变形而仅有稀疏的术中测量，极具挑战性。生物力学模型利用先验知识正则化这一病态问题，但由于简化假设而存在持续的预测偏差；数据驱动学习方法则在数据效率、泛化能力和物理合理性方面存在困难。我们提出一种混合配准框架，利用稀疏术中对应点来适配生物力学先验。该框架不学习完整的变形场，而是学习一个残差变形函数来校正线性生物力学预测，该残差函数建模为一种图神经扩散函数，并在三维肝脏网格上引入几何感知注意力。为了实现稀疏观测的长程信息传递，我们采用一种新颖视角，将稀疏术中测量视为“上下文”样本，其中残差变形函数的输入-输出对完全可观，从而将问题转化为利用前馈元学习器从术中上下文样本中学习该残差函数的“学习如何学习”过程。在可变形肝脏体模数据集上的实验表明，与刚性配准、纯生物力学模型及纯数据驱动基线相比，该方法在配准精度和泛化能力上均有提升，尤其在几何形状和形变分布外场景下表现尤为突出。

</details>


### [11] [Reasoning Text-to-Video Retrieval for Operating Room Clips via Action-Driven Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17298)
*Yiqing Shen,Hao Ding,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OR3方法，通过动作驱动数字孪生（ActDT）和想象检索，实现手术室视频中隐式查询的文本-视频检索，在MM-OR基准上大幅超越已有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 手术室中安全关键事件可能不遵循常见结构，现有基于全局嵌入的文本-视频检索方法无法处理需要推理的隐式查询（如“剪切前一步”），因此需要一种能够对复杂时间动作关系进行推理的检索方法。

Method: 提出OR3方法：(1) 将视频片段转化为动作驱动数字孪生（ActDT），即按非重叠时间区间分组的主语-动作-宾语三元组；(2) 基于想象检索，用大语言模型从查询生成假设的ActDT；(3) 通过单个编码器进行模态内匹配，并利用ActDT定制的难负样本训练；(4) 基于证据的细化，根据与top候选的差异修正假设ActDT以捕捉手术流程特定模式。

Result: 在MM-OR数据集上构建包含276个隐式查询（覆盖4类推理）和386个膝关节镜手术视频片段的基准，OR3达到57.6% R@1和77.3% R@5，显著优于最强基线。

Conclusion: OR3通过时间动作推理实现了对视觉相似的手术室视频片段进行细粒度区分，有效支持了手术室安全相关的隐式查询文本-视频检索任务。

Original Abstract: Text-to-video retrieval in operating rooms (OR) is an enabling technology for OR safety, as it allows stakeholders to retrieve and inspect recordings of specific events. However, because the most safety-critical events may not follow the common structure, to unlock its full potential text-to-video retrieval must be able to handle implicit queries that require reasoning to identify the right video (e.g., the step right before clipping). However, existing methods rely on global embeddings that cannot reason over such queries. We propose OR3, a text-to-video retrieval method that converts clips into action-driven digital twins (ActDTs), grouping concurrent subject-action-object triplets under non-overlapping temporal intervals. Moreover, rather than cross-modal matching through paired encoders, OR3 performs imagination-based retrieval where an LLM generates hypothetical ActDTs from queries. This enables intra-modal matching via a single encoder trained with ActDT-tailored hard negatives. Finally, evidence-grounded refinement revises imagined ActDTs based on discrepancies with top candidates to capture procedure-specific patterns. We construct a benchmark from MM-OR with 276 implicit queries across four reasoning categories over 386 clips from robotic knee procedures. OR3 achieves 57.6 R@1 and 77.3 R@5, outperforming the strongest baseline. These results demonstrate that OR3 enables fine-grained discrimination between visually similar OR video clips through temporal action reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 手术室中的文本到视频检索是一项支持手术室安全的关键技术，因为它允许相关人员检索和查看特定事件的录像。然而，由于最安全关键的事件可能不遵循常见结构，为充分发挥文本到视频检索的潜力，系统必须能够处理需要推理才能确定正确视频的隐式查询（例如，裁剪前的步骤）。然而，现有方法依赖于无法对这些查询进行推理的全局嵌入。我们提出OR3，一种文本到视频检索方法，该方法将视频片段转化为动作驱动数字孪生（ActDT），在非重叠的时间区间内对并行的主语-动作-宾语三元组进行分组。此外，OR3并非通过配对编码器进行跨模态匹配，而是执行基于想象的检索，即由大语言模型从查询生成假设的ActDT。这使得通过单个编码器（使用ActDT定制的难负样本训练）实现模态内匹配成为可能。最后，基于证据的细化根据与top候选项的差异修正假设的ActDT，以捕捉流程特定的模式。我们从MM-OR数据集构建了一个基准，包含来自膝关节镜手术流程的386个视频片段上的276个隐式查询（覆盖四个推理类别）。OR3达到了57.6%的R@1和77.3%的R@5，优于最强基线。这些结果表明，OR3通过时间动作推理实现了对视觉相似的手术室视频片段进行细粒度区分。

</details>


### [12] [SierpinskiCam: Camera-Controlled Video Retaking with Sierpinski Triangle Pattern Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17310)
*Suttisak Wizadwongsa,Hyelin Nam,Supasorn Suwajanakorn,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SierpinskiCam通过引入谢尔宾斯基圆顶纹理线索和负RoPE索引的参考视频条件机制，增强了几何引导的视图合成能力，在单目视频重拍任务中显著提升了相机可控性、几何一致性和视频质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于几何引导的方法在目标相机轨迹偏离源轨迹时，新暴露区域会变得稀疏或缺失，导致引导质量下降。作者旨在解决大视角变化下几何引导不足的问题。

Method: 1) 提出Sierpinski Dome纹理线索，该纹理包含丰富的可追踪特征，即使在大视角变化下也能提供稳定的几何引导；2) 引入参考视频条件机制，将源视频token附加到目标token序列中，并通过负RoPE索引将两个流分离，实现外观对齐而无需修改架构或逐视频适配。

Result: 大量实验表明，SierpinskiCam在多种具有挑战性的重拍场景中，在相机可控性、几何一致性和视频质量方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: SierpinskiCam通过增强几何引导和创新的条件机制，有效解决了单目视频重拍中大视角变化下的新视图合成问题，提升了生成质量和可控性。

Original Abstract: Generating novel renderings of a scene along user-defined camera trajectories from a single monocular video, dubbed video retaking, is a compelling but difficult problem in content creation and visual effects. Existing geometry-guided approaches reconstruct a 4D representation from the source video and render it along the target trajectory to condition video diffusion models. However, this guidance degrades as the target camera departs from the source trajectory, leaving newly revealed regions sparse or entirely missing. We propose SierpinskiCam, which addresses this limitation by augmenting geometry-based guidance with Sierpinski dome texture cues that contains rich trackable features even under large viewpoint changes. We further introduce a reference video conditioning mechanism that appends source-video tokens to the target-token sequence and separates the two streams with negative RoPE indices, enabling appearance grounding without architectural modification or per-video adaptation. Extensive experiments show that SierpinskiCam achieves significant gains in camera controllability, geometric consistency, and video quality across diverse and challenging retaking scenarios. Project page: https://hyelinnam.github.io/SierpinskiCam/.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频中沿着用户定义的相机轨迹生成新颖的场景渲染（称为视频重拍），是内容创作和视觉特效领域中一个引人入胜但极具挑战性的问题。现有的几何引导方法从源视频重建4D表示，并沿目标轨迹进行渲染，以此作为视频扩散模型的条件。然而，当目标相机偏离源轨迹时，这种引导的质量会下降，导致新暴露区域变得稀疏甚至完全缺失。我们提出SierpinskiCam，通过引入谢尔宾斯基圆顶纹理线索来增强基于几何的引导，该纹理包含丰富的可追踪特征，即使在大视角变化下也能稳定工作。我们进一步引入了一种参考视频条件机制，将源视频token附加到目标token序列中，并通过负RoPE索引将两个流分离，从而在无需架构修改或逐视频适配的情况下实现外观对齐。大量实验表明，SierpinskiCam在多种多样且具有挑战性的重拍场景中，在相机可控性、几何一致性和视频质量方面均取得了显著提升。项目页面：https://hyelinnam.github.io/SierpinskiCam/。

</details>


### [13] [FATE: Pillar Encoding and Frequency-Aware Training for Event-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17334)
*Md Tawheedul Islam Bhuian,Kyoung-Don Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FATE是一个统一框架，通过柱状编码（Pillar Encoding）避免事件相机数据内部时间子分箱，利用连续时间正交多项式基近似事件演化，并结合频率感知训练（FAT）弥合低频监督与高频推理的错配，实现高达200Hz的鲁棒目标检测，开销极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件相机方法将累积窗口划分为固定时间子箱，这种内部离散化丢弃了细粒度时间结构，并将推理限制在训练监督所施加的低时间频率下，无法充分利用事件数据的异步稀疏优势。

Method: 提出FATE框架，核心包括：1) 柱状编码（PE）：在离散宏累积窗口内避免时间子分箱，将事件组织为空间柱体，通过投影到连续时间正交多项式基上来近似其窗口内演化，得到L2最优的密集伪图像表示；2) 频率感知训练（FAT）：一种软均值教师课程学习，生成时间密集伪标签，桥接低频监督与高频推理之间的错配。

Result: FATE在不同架构范式上均优于强基线，在高达200Hz的时间分辨率下实现鲁棒目标检测，且参数量和推理延迟增量极小。

Conclusion: FATE通过避免内部时间离散化并引入频率感知训练，有效保留了事件数据的精细时间动态，实现了从低频监督到高频推理的高效泛化，是一种通用且高效的事件相机处理框架。

Original Abstract: Event cameras are bio-inspired sensors that asynchronously capture logarithmic intensity changes, offering inherent advantages in high-speed and high-dynamic-range scenarios. However, the sparse and asynchronous nature of event streams poses a fundamental challenge for modern deep learning architectures. To enable compatibility with standard models, most existing approaches partition the accumulation window into fixed temporal sub-bins. While effective for spatial processing, this internal discretization discards fine-grained temporal structure and constrains inference to the low temporal frequencies imposed by training supervision. To address this limitation, we propose FATE, a unified framework built upon a novel Pillar Encoding (PE). While operating over discrete macro-accumulation windows dictated by the target frequency, PE avoids internal temporal sub-binning. It organizes events into spatial pillars and approximates their intra-window evolution via projection onto a continuous-time orthogonal polynomial basis. This formulation yields an L2-optimal representation that retains rich temporal dynamics in a dense pseudo-image, mitigating information loss under sparse event conditions. To fully leverage this representation, we introduce Frequency-Aware Training (FAT), a soft mean-teacher curriculum that generates temporally dense pseudo-labels, effectively bridging the mismatch between low-frequency supervision and high-frequency inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FATE generalizes across architectural paradigms and consistently outperforms strong baselines. It enables robust object detection at high temporal resolutions up to 200 Hz, while incurring minimal overhead in parameter count and inference latency

中文摘要翻译: 事件相机是一种受生物启发的传感器，它异步捕捉对数强度变化，在高速和高动态范围场景中具有固有优势。然而，事件流的稀疏性和异步性给现代深度学习架构带来了根本性挑战。为了与标准模型兼容，大多数现有方法将累积窗口划分为固定的时间子箱。虽然这种方法对于空间处理有效，但内部离散化丢弃了细粒度的时间结构，并将推理限制在训练监督所施加的低时间频率下。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了FATE，一个基于新型柱状编码（Pillar Encoding, PE）的统一框架。PE在目标频率决定的离散宏累积窗口上操作，同时避免内部时间子分箱。它把事件组织成空间柱体，并通过投影到连续时间正交多项式基上来近似其在窗口内的演化。该公式产生了一个L2最优表示，在密集伪图像中保留了丰富的时间动态，减轻了稀疏事件条件下的信息损失。为充分利用这一表示，我们引入了频率感知训练（Frequency-Aware Training, FAT），这是一种软均值教师课程学习机制，用于生成时间密集的伪标签，有效弥合低频监督与高频推理之间的错配。大量实验表明，FATE能够泛化到不同架构范式，并一致优于强基线。它能够在高达200Hz的时间分辨率下实现鲁棒目标检测，同时参数量和推理延迟开销极小。

</details>


### [14] [Geometry-Consistent Endoscopic Representations for Image-Guided Navigation via Structured Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17340)
*Hongchao Shu,Roger D. Soberanis-Mukul,Hao Ding,Morgan Ringel,Mali Shen,Saif Iftekar Sayed,Hedyeh Rafii-Tari,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，通过合成数据管道和层级感知的几何-语义适配（替代标准LoRA），学习几何一致且域鲁棒的单目内窥镜图像表示，在姿态估计、深度估计等下游任务中取得更优性能，并展现出良好的合成到真实迁移能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目内窥镜导航面临深度线索有限、组织纹理弱、非刚性形变和跨域外观变化等挑战，现有视觉基础模型学到的表示缺乏几何一致性，导致特征对应不稳定，限制了在下游导航任务中的可靠性。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，包含：(1) 合成数据管线，提供精确的几何监督；(2) Hierarchy-Aware Geometry-Semantic Adaptation (HAGSA)，一种替代标准LoRA的结构化方法，在Transformer层级中选择性插入低秩适配器，并配合逐层训练目标，鼓励中间特征学习几何对应、深层特征学习语义一致性。

Result: 在公开和专有数据集上，该方法在姿态估计和单目深度估计等下游导航任务中取得了更优的性能；在临床支气管镜上表现出良好的合成到真实迁移能力；为鼻窦内窥镜和结肠镜的有限监督适配提供了有效的初始化；展现出随模型大小和训练数据规模的良好扩展性。

Conclusion: 层级感知的、几何引导的适配方法是内窥镜表示学习的一种实用有效方案，能够同时提升几何一致性和语义质量，并具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Accurate vision-based navigation in monocular endoscopy is difficult due to limited depth cues, weak tissue texture, non-rigid deformation, and substantial appearance variation across domains, all of which complicate pose estimation, depth prediction, and image-to-anatomy alignment. Although recent vision foundation models have shown promise, their learned representations often remain insufficiently geometry-consistent, hindering stable feature correspondence and limiting their reliability for downstream navigation tasks. We propose a unified framework for learning geometry-consistent and domain-robust image representations for monocular endoscopy. The framework combines a synthetic data pipeline that provides accurate geometric supervision with Hierarchy-Aware Geometry-Semantic Adaptation, a structured alternative to standard LoRA that inserts low-rank adapters selectively across the transformer hierarchy and couples them with layer-wise training objectives to encourage geometric correspondence in intermediate features and semantic consistency in deeper features. Experiments on public and proprietary datasets show improved geometric and semantic representation quality, leading to better performance on downstream navigation tasks including pose estimation and monocular depth estimation. The learned representations show favorable synthetic-to-real transfer on clinical bronchoscopy and provide a useful initialization for adaptation to sinus endoscopy and colonoscopy under limited supervision. The framework also shows favorable scaling with model size and training data. These results support hierarchy-aware, geometry-guided adaptation as a practical approach for endoscopic representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 由于深度线索有限、组织纹理微弱、非刚性形变以及跨域外观的巨大差异，基于精确视觉导航的单目内窥镜成像极具挑战性，这些问题使得姿态估计、深度预测和图像与解剖结构对齐变得复杂。尽管最近的视觉基础模型展现出潜力，但它们学到的表示往往缺乏足够的几何一致性，阻碍了稳定的特征对应，限制了其在下游导航任务中的可靠性。本文提出了一个统一框架，用于学习几何一致且域鲁棒的单目内窥镜图像表示。该框架结合了一个提供精确几何监督的合成数据管线，以及层级感知的几何-语义适配（Hierarchy-Aware Geometry-Semantic Adaptation）——这是一种标准LoRA的结构化替代方案，它在Transformer层级中选择性地插入低秩适配器，并配合逐层训练目标，以鼓励中间特征学习几何对应、深层特征学习语义一致性。在公开和专有数据集上的实验表明，该方法提升了几何和语义表示质量，从而在姿态估计和单目深度估计等下游导航任务上取得了更优性能。所学习的表示在临床支气管镜上展现出良好的合成到真实迁移能力，并为在有限监督下适配鼻窦内窥镜和结肠镜提供了有效的初始化。该框架还展现出随模型大小和训练数据规模的良好的扩展性。这些结果支持层级感知、几何引导的适配作为内窥镜表示学习的一种实用方法。

</details>


### [15] [Learning a Maximum Entropy Model for Visual Textures using Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17342)
*Xinyuan Zhao,Eero P. Simoncelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于最大熵原理无监督学习纹理统计量的方法，利用扩散模型训练和采样，仅用512个统计量即可达到甚至超越当前最优模型（~177k统计量）的纹理生成质量，并支持平滑插值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有纹理模型的统计量要么是手工设计的，要么基于为其他任务（如物体识别）预训练的神经网络，缺乏一种原则性的、无监督的统计量学习方法。

Method: 基于最大熵概率模型，利用生成扩散模型的训练和采样方法，无监督地学习一组紧致的统计量（512个）来约束纹理生成。对比了传统匹配统计量的采样方法。

Result: 1) 仅512个统计量即可生成质量媲美或超越当前最优模型（~177k统计量）的纹理图像；2) 通过生成对某一模型不可区分但对另一模型差异最大的图像，揭示了两模型的相对优劣；3) 该模型在表示空间中沿直线轨迹可生成平滑插值纹理样本。

Conclusion: 提出的无监督统计量学习方法在纹理生成上达到了最优水平，同时模型更紧凑，并具备平滑插值这一前所未有的能力。

Original Abstract: Visual textures -- spatially homogeneous image regions containing repeated elements (e.g. a field of grass, the bark of a tree) -- are ubiquitous in visual scenes and provide important cues for recognizing and analyzing materials and objects. A number of existing texture models extract essential statistics from a single texture image, and can then generate high-quality samples that are visually similar to the original by matching these statistics. However, their statistics are either hand-designed or based on a network pretrained for another purpose (e.g., object recognition). Here, we develop the first principled method for unsupervised learning of a set of statistics that are used to constrain a maximum entropy probability model. We leverage methods developed for generative diffusion models to derive training and sampling procedures, and compare these to the traditional method of sampling via matching the statistics. Despite the compactness of our trained model (512 statistics), it generates texture images whose quality is as good as or better than the current state-of-the-art model (~177k statistics). A more direct comparison of the two models, obtained by synthesizing images that are indistinguishable for one model but maximally different for the other, reveals their relative strengths and weaknesses. Finally, we show that unlike previous statistical texture models, a straight trajectory in the representation space of our model generates homogeneous texture samples that interpolate smoothly between the features of the two end points.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉纹理——包含重复元素的空间均匀图像区域（例如草地、树皮）——在视觉场景中无处不在，为识别和分析材料与物体提供了重要线索。现有许多纹理模型从单张纹理图像中提取关键统计量，并通过匹配这些统计量生成与原始图像视觉相似的高质量样本。然而，这些统计量要么是手工设计的，要么基于为其他目的（如物体识别）预训练的神经网络。本文提出了首个基于最大熵概率模型无监督学习一组统计量的原则性方法。我们利用生成扩散模型的方法推导训练和采样流程，并将其与传统的匹配统计量采样方法进行比较。尽管我们的训练模型非常紧凑（仅512个统计量），它生成的纹理图像质量与当前最优模型（约177k个统计量）相当甚至更优。通过对两个模型进行更直接的比较——生成对一个模型不可区分但对另一个模型差异最大的图像——揭示了它们各自的相对优势和不足。最后，我们展示了与以往统计纹理模型不同，我们的模型在表示空间中沿直线轨迹可生成在两个端点特征之间平滑插值的均匀纹理样本。

</details>


### [16] [Bayesian Magnetic Resonance Joint Image Reconstruction and Uncertainty Quantification using Sparsity Prior Models and Markov Chain Monte Carlo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17343)
*Ahmed Karam Eldaly,Matteo Figini,Daniel C. Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的压缩感知MRI不确定性量化方法，通过MCMC采样实现图像重建与不确定性估计，在单线圈和多线圈数据集上均优于优化方法，且不确定性图与误差图高度相关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统压缩感知MRI重建方法仅提供点估计，缺乏对重建结果不确定性的量化能力，而现有的深度学习方法虽然在重建质量上表现优异，但同样难以提供可靠的不确定性估计。

Method: 将压缩感知MRI重建建模为贝叶斯线性逆问题，对未知参数施加稀疏先验（总变分先验和小波变换先验）；采用基于分裂增强吉布斯采样器的MCMC方法从后验分布中采样；利用近端MCMC方法高效处理不可微的条件分布。

Result: 在单线圈和多线圈数据集上，使用不同k空间欠采样模式和采样率进行验证，每种方法的重建效果均优于对应的优化方法；不确定性图与真实误差图之间的相关性优于现有深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯框架不仅能实现高质量的压缩感知MRI图像重建，还能有效量化重建结果的不确定性，不确定性图可作为重建可靠性的重要参考指标。

Original Abstract: We propose a novel framework for uncertainty quantification using compressed sensing magnetic resonance image reconstruction. The problem is formulated within a Bayesian framework as a linear inverse problem, with prior distributions assigned to the unknown model parameters. Specifically, the image to be reconstructed is assumed to be sparse in a given basis. We develop a general framework applicable to any basis and as examples, we test the sparsity of the image in its (1) spatial gradients using a total variation prior model, and in its (2) wavelet transform. A Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) method, based on a split-and-augmented Gibbs sampler, is then employed to sample from the posterior distribution of the unknown parameters. The non-differentiable conditional distributions are efficiently sampled using a proximal MCMC method. The proposed algorithms are validated on both single-coil and multi-coil datasets using various k-space sub-sampling patterns and ratios. The results demonstrate the superior performance of each proposed approach in reconstructing images compared to its counterpart optimisation-based method. Moreover, our framework effectively quantifies uncertainty, showing a notable correlation between estimated uncertainty maps and error maps computed using ground truth and reconstructed images, compared with existing deep learning-based methods.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种基于压缩感知磁共振图像重建的不确定性量化新框架。该问题在贝叶斯框架下被建模为一个线性逆问题，并为未知模型参数赋予先验分布。具体而言，待重建图像在给定基下被假设为稀疏的。我们开发了一个适用于任意基的通用框架，并以图像在(1)空间梯度上的总变分先验模型和(2)小波变换中的稀疏性作为示例进行测试。随后，采用基于分裂增强吉布斯采样器的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法，从未知参数的后验分布中进行采样。对于不可微的条件分布，使用近端MCMC方法进行高效采样。所提出的算法在单线圈和多线圈数据集上，使用不同的k空间欠采样模式和采样率进行了验证。结果表明，与对应的基于优化的方法相比，每种方法在图像重建方面均表现出优越性能。此外，与现有的深度学习方法相比，我们的框架能够有效量化不确定性，估计的不确定性图与基于真实值和重建图像计算的误差图之间表现出显著的相关性。

</details>


### [17] [Complex Layout Classification in the Wild: A Low-Resource Approach with Layout-Preserving Augmentations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17355)
*Sharva Gogawale,Iddo Hakim,Gal Grudka,Mohammad Suliman,Omer Ventura,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低资源语料库中布局分类困难的问题，该论文构建了一个包含八种布局类型的数据集，并提出一种基于CNN的分类器，通过领域感知的强数据增强（各向异性高斯掩码和反射诱导标签变换）来提升低标注稀缺条件下的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多数字化语料库面临低资源问题——标注稀缺、页面扫描噪声大且分辨率低、布局结构复杂，这些都影响了自动转录的质量。低资源语言的鲁棒分类模型开发受限于大规模标注数据的缺乏以及页面布局频繁的语义复杂性。

Method: 1) 构建了一个复杂布局数据集，根据分隔区域人工分为八种布局类型；2) 提出基于CNN的分类器，采用强领域感知增强策略：各向异性高斯掩码抑制文本细节而保留分隔结构，迫使模型学习全局几何排列；3) 反射诱导标签变换在不改变不对称类别标签一致性的前提下丰富训练分布。

Result: 实验结果表明，布局特定的数据增强方法能够在严重标注稀缺的条件下显著提升页面级布局分类的性能。

Conclusion: 布局特定的数据增强策略能够有效提升低资源场景下页面布局分类的准确性，为低资源语料库的自动处理提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Many digitized corpora suffer from low resources because annotations may be scarce, page scans are noisy and of poor resolution, or layouts are structurally complex in ways that negatively affect the quality of automatic transcription. Developing robust classification models for low-resource languages is inhibited by the lack of large-scale annotated data and by the frequent semantic complexity of page layouts. To this end, we have curated a complex-layout dataset, manually classified into eight distinct layout types based on their separator regions. To overcome data scarcity, we propose a novel training strategy in the form of a CNN-based classifier that employs strong, domain-aware augmentations to improve generalization. We utilize narrow anisotropic Gaussian masking to suppress incidental textual details while preserving essential separations, compelling the model to learn global geometric arrangements. Additionally, we implement reflection-induced label transformations to enrich the training distribution while maintaining label consistency across asymmetric categories. The results demonstrate that layout-specific augmentations can substantially improve page-level layout classification under severe annotation scarcity.

中文摘要翻译: 许多数字化语料库因标注稀缺、页面扫描噪声大且分辨率低、或布局结构复杂而对自动转录质量产生负面影响，从而面临低资源问题。为低资源语言开发鲁棒的分类模型受到缺乏大规模标注数据以及页面布局频繁的语义复杂性的制约。为此，我们整理了一个复杂布局数据集，根据其分隔区域人工标注为八种不同的布局类型。为了克服数据稀缺，我们提出了一种新颖的训练策略，即基于CNN的分类器，采用强领域感知增强来提升泛化能力。我们利用窄各向异性高斯掩码抑制偶然的文本细节，同时保留必要的分隔结构，迫使模型学习全局几何排列。此外，我们实现了反射诱导的标签变换，以丰富训练分布，同时保持非对称类别间的标签一致性。结果表明，布局特定的增强方法可以在严重标注稀缺条件下显著提升页面级布局分类的性能。

</details>


### [18] [DriveJudge: Rethinking Autonomous Driving Evaluation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17362)
*Xinglong Sun,Kevin Xie,Jenny Schmalfuss,Despoina Paschalidou,Xiuming Zhang,Sanja Fidler,Kashyap Chitta,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveJudge，一种结合规则评估与VLM推理的驾驶评估智能体，通过选择性调用物理规则函数实现可解释且上下文感知的驾驶质量评估。引入大规模数据集（33,577个样本）和两个人类对齐基准任务，在驾驶质量分类和轨迹偏好选择上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶评估方法存在两难：基于规则的指标（如EPDMS）可解释但缺乏上下文感知能力；基于VLM的评估虽具备上下文感知，但受限于模糊的VLM输出和较弱的物理基础。亟需一种兼具可解释性和上下文感知能力的驾驶评估方法。

Method: DriveJudge采用"VLM推理+规则执行"的混合架构：首先通过VLM解释环境上下文，然后选择性调用具有物理基础的确定性规则函数，将规则评估与VLM推理结合。此外，构建了包含33,577个挑战性驾驶样本的大规模数据集，并设计了驾驶质量分类和轨迹偏好选择两个人类对齐的基准任务。

Result: 驾驶质量分类任务：DriveJudge比EPDMS提升21.23 AUC；轨迹偏好选择任务：比最新VLM方法DriveCritic提升6.5%。

Conclusion: DriveJudge通过结合规则评估与VLM推理，在可解释性和精确性上均达到新标准，为上下文感知且可解释的驾驶评估提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Autonomous driving has shifted towards end-to-end policy learning, where reliable, interpretable policy evaluation is a fundamental challenge as driving quality is highly context-dependent. Commonly used rule-based driving metrics like EPDMS are interpretable but lack context-awareness, while recent VLMbased evaluations are context-aware but limited by ambiguous VLM outputs and weak physical grounding. To evaluate driving in a manner that is both interpretable and context-aware, we introduce DriveJudge. DriveJudge is a driving evaluation agent that combines rule-grounded evaluation with Vision-Language Model (VLM) reasoning and selectively invokes physically-grounded deterministic rule functions after interpreting the environmental context. To train and evaluate DriveJudge, we curate a large-scale dataset of 33,577 challenging driving samples with human annotations on whether the driving behavior is reasonable in the given scenario. With this dataset, we address the underexplored problem of driving metric evaluation, and introduce two human-aligned benchmark tasks: Driving Quality Classification and Trajectory Preference Selection. DriveJudge outperforms EPDMS for driving quality classification by 21.23 AUC, and the recent VLM-based DriveCritic for trajectory preference selection by 6.5%, setting a new standard for interpretable and precise driving evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶已转向端到端策略学习，其中可靠且可解释的策略评估是一项基本挑战，因为驾驶质量高度依赖于上下文。常用的基于规则的驾驶指标（如EPDMS）具有可解释性但缺乏上下文感知能力，而近期基于VLM的评估虽具备上下文感知能力，但受限于模糊的VLM输出和较弱的物理基础。为了实现既具可解释性又具上下文感知能力的驾驶评估，我们引入了DriveJudge。DriveJudge是一种驾驶评估智能体，它将规则驱动的评估与视觉语言模型（VLM）推理相结合，并在解释环境上下文后选择性调用具有物理基础的确定性规则函数。为了训练和评估DriveJudge，我们整理了一个大规模数据集，包含33,577个具有挑战性的驾驶样本以及人类标注——指示给定场景中的驾驶行为是否合理。利用该数据集，我们解决了驾驶指标评估中尚未充分探索的问题，并引入了两个与人类对齐的基准任务：驾驶质量分类和轨迹偏好选择。在驾驶质量分类任务中，DriveJudge比EPDMS提升了21.23 AUC；在轨迹偏好选择任务中，比最新的基于VLM的DriveCritic方法提升了6.5%，为可解释且精确的驾驶评估设立了新标准。

</details>


### [19] [Improving and Evaluating Hand-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17384)
*Ahmad Darkhalil,Dima Damen,David Fouhey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOI-DETR框架，通过将手-物体和物体-物体交互引入Co-DETR架构，在多个手-物交互理解基准上取得了显著性能提升（Hands23和FineBio上mAP提升超20个百分点），并提供了综合评估套件和预训练模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解手及其交互的物体（包括直接交互和通过工具交互）是动作感知、3D重建和机器人等任务的关键步骤，现有方法在手-物交互理解方面仍有较大提升空间。

Method: 提出HOI-DETR框架，将手-物体交互和物体-物体交互引入Co-DETR架构，构建了一个包含4个不同数据集的综合HOI评估套件（含HD-EPIC视频基准和改进的Hands23标注），并训练了预训练检查点。

Result: 在Hands23、HOIST、FineBio和HD-EPIC四个数据集上显著超越现有最先进方法，在Hands23和FineBio上实现超过20个百分点的mAP提升。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实了每个模型组件的贡献，HOI-DETR框架有效推动了手-物交互理解领域的发展。

Original Abstract: Understanding hands and the objects they interact with, both directly and through tools, is a key step for tasks ranging from action perception to 3D reconstruction and robotics. Our paper provides several contributions to the Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) understanding literature: (1) HOI-DETR, a new framework that introduces hand-object and object-object interactions to the Co-DETR architecture to produce a state-of-the-art method; (2) a comprehensive HOI evaluation suite of 4 diverse datasets, including a video benchmark derived from the HD-EPIC dataset and fresh annotations that improve the Hands23 benchmark and (3) a trained checkpoint that significantly improves the state of the art across Hands23, HOIST, FineBio, and HD-EPIC, including mAP gains of over 20 percentage points on Hands23 and FineBio. Our ablations confirm the contributions of each model component.

中文摘要翻译: 理解手及其交互的物体（包括直接交互和通过工具交互）是动作感知、3D重建和机器人等任务的关键步骤。本文为手-物交互（HOI）理解文献做出了若干贡献：（1）HOI-DETR，一种新框架，将手-物体和物体-物体交互引入Co-DETR架构，从而产生最先进的方法；（2）一个包含4个不同数据集的综合HOI评估套件，包括源自HD-EPIC数据集并带有新标注的视频基准，改进了Hands23基准；（3）一个预训练检查点，在Hands23、HOIST、FineBio和HD-EPIC上显著提升了最先进水平，包括在Hands23和FineBio上超过20个百分点的mAP增益。我们的消融实验证实了每个模型组件的贡献。

</details>


### [20] [TerraTransfer: Learning End-to-End Driving Policies Without Expert Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17386)
*Zikang Xiong,Weixin Li,Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Saarth Bonde,Grantland Hall,Chen Tang,Yihan Hu,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将驾驶决策（学习驾驶）与视觉感知（学习看见）解耦的端到端自动驾驶方法：在矢量化模拟器中通过自我对弈预训练策略，然后利用动作KL散度和批量关系低秩结构损失将其潜在空间与预训练的视觉骨干对齐，从而无需昂贵的专家演示即可实现高效训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶的标准训练流程在所有阶段都成本高昂：收集和标注数百万驾驶帧非常昂贵，基于图像的闭环强化学习受限于每一步的照片级渲染开销和大规模视觉骨干网络的前向传播成本。现有方法依赖模仿学习的专家演示数据，成本高且难以获取。

Method: 1) 在矢量化模拟器中通过自我对弈预训练一个驾驶策略，利用模拟器每秒数百万步的快速滚转和自然丰富的碰撞、近失和恢复状态分布；2) 将预训练策略的潜在空间与预训练的视觉骨干网络对齐，通过动作KL散度和批量关系低秩结构损失进行对齐；3) 对齐的目标来自自我对弈策略，而非日志轨迹，因此仅需图像-场景状态配对数据集，无需专家演示。

Result: 在基于照片级3D高斯泼溅的闭环场景中，所得到的端到端策略达到或超过了先前端到端方法的性能。

Conclusion: 利用模拟器和真实世界之间的不对称性，通过解耦学习驾驶和学习看见，可以在不使用昂贵专家演示的情况下，实现与现有方法相当甚至更优的端到端自动驾驶性能。

Original Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved state-of-the-art performance on benchmarks and real-world deployments. Its standard training recipe, however, is expensive across all stages: collecting and labeling millions of driving frames is costly, and closed-loop RL on images is bottlenecked by the per-step cost of photorealistic rendering plus a forward pass through a large vision backbone. Self-play in vectorized simulators changes the economics: millions of rollout steps per second, and a state distribution naturally rich in collisions, near-misses, and recoveries that no driving log contains. Our approach exploits this asymmetry by decoupling learning to drive from learning to see. We pretrain a single policy by self-play, then align its latent space with a pretrained vision backbone, through the action KL divergence and a batch-relational low-rank structural loss. The action target comes from the self-play policy, so alignment never supervises against a logged trajectory: a paired dataset of (image, scene-state) frames suffices, with no need for the curated expert demonstrations that imitation pretraining is built on. On photorealistic 3D Gaussian splatting closed-loop scenarios, the resulting end-to-end policy matches or exceeds prior end-to-end methods.

中文摘要翻译: 端到端自动驾驶在基准测试和实际部署中已取得最先进的性能。然而，其标准训练流程在所有阶段都成本高昂：收集和标注数百万驾驶帧代价不菲，而基于图像的闭环强化学习受限于照片级渲染和大型视觉骨干网络前向传播的每步成本。在矢量化模拟器中进行自我对弈改变了这种经济性——每秒可进行数百万步的滚转，且状态分布天然包含驾驶日志中不存在的碰撞、近失和恢复场景。我们的方法通过解耦学习驾驶与学习看见来利用这种不对称性。我们通过自我对弈预训练一个单一策略，然后通过动作KL散度和批量关系低秩结构损失将其潜在空间与预训练的视觉骨干网络对齐。动作目标来自自我对弈策略，因此对齐过程从不基于日志轨迹进行监督：仅需一个(图像,场景状态)帧的配对数据集即可，无需模仿学习所依赖的精心策划的专家演示。在基于照片级3D高斯泼溅的闭环场景中，所得到的端到端策略达到或超过了先前的端到端方法。

</details>


### [21] [Visuals Lie, Consistency Speaks: Disentangling Spatial Attention from Reliability in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17389)
*Logan Mann,Yi Xia,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Saadullah Ismail,Shikhar Shiromani,Emily Huang,Ruizhe Li,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统跨模型研究，挑战了“注意力-置信度假设”（即视觉注意力集中程度与回答可靠性正相关）。作者提出VLM可靠性探针（VRP），引入结构注意力指标，发现视觉编码器的空间注意力与最终生成准确性几乎无关（R≈0.001），可靠性信号实际源自生成动态和内部状态分布，而非视觉定位图。自一致性（自我一致性）是正确性的最强预测因子（R=0.429）。因果干预实验还揭示了不同架构在可靠性上的根本差异：LLaVA依赖脆弱的后期瓶颈，而PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL具有全局分布的抗毁性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态基础模型越来越多被用作推理智能体，因此其可靠性（即模型何时可能产生幻觉）变得至关重要。常见直觉（注意力-置信度假说）认为，模型在视觉感知上的注意力集中程度能反映其回答的可靠性。本文旨在挑战这一假设，系统探究当前VLM中真正的可靠性信号来源。

Method: 提出VLM可靠性探针（VRP），进行跨模型家族的可靠性信号系统研究。引入结构注意力指标：簇计数（C_k）和空间熵（H_s）来量化视觉编码器的注意力集中程度，并追踪其跨层演化（ΔH_s）。对比分析了注意力结构、生成动态（自一致性）、以及因果干预下的内部状态分布与准确性的关系。

Result: 1) 发现“符号解耦”现象：模型早期锁定视觉特征后后期注意力扩散，早期感知与最终生成脱节。2) 空间注意力与准确性近乎零相关（R≈0.001），即“聚类失败”。3) 自一致性（推理路径一致性）是正确性的主导预测因子（R=0.429）。4) 因果干预揭示架构差异：LLaVA预测依赖脆弱的后期瓶颈层，而PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL可靠性全局分布，即使破坏50%以上最关键层仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 对于当前的视觉语言模型，可靠性信号与视觉定位图解耦，最佳推断方式来自生成时刻的动态和隐藏状态探针，而非视觉注意力结构。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Foundation Models are increasingly used as reasoning agents, making reliability, knowing when a model may hallucinate, critical. A common intuition, which we call the Attention-Confidence Assumption, holds that reliability follows from "structural" visual perception: tight attention on relevant regions should signal a trustworthy answer, while scattered attention signals confusion. We challenge this through the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP), a systematic cross-family study of reliability signals in contemporary Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We introduce structural-attention metrics, cluster counts (C_k) and spatial entropy (H_s), to quantify the visual encoder's gaze, and track its evolution (Delta H_s) across layers. This reveals a "Symbolic Detachment": models often "Early Lock" visual features only to diffuse attention later, severing early perception from final generation. Contrary to the grounding hypothesis, we find a "Cluster Failure": spatial attention has near-zero correlation (R approx 0.001) with accuracy. Instead, reliability is a phenomenon of generation dynamics and internal-state distributions. Self-Consistency, the agreement rate across sampled reasoning paths, is the dominant predictor of truth (R = 0.429). Scaling causal interventions exposes a sharp architectural divergence: LLaVA locks its prediction in a fragile late-stage bottleneck, whereas PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute reliability globally, staying resilient even when ~50% or more of their most predictive layer is destroyed. For current VLMs, reliability signals are detached from visual grounding maps and are best inferred from generation-time dynamics and hidden-state probes.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态基础模型日益被用作推理智能体，这使得可靠性——即判断模型何时可能产生幻觉——变得至关重要。一种常见的直觉（我们称之为"注意力-置信度假说"）认为，可靠性源于"结构化的"视觉感知：对相关区域的紧密注意力应标志着可信的回答，而分散的注意力则标志着混乱。我们通过VLM可靠性探针（VRP）——一项针对当代视觉语言模型（VLM）中可靠性信号的系统性跨家族研究——来挑战这一观点。我们引入了结构注意力指标——簇计数（C_k）和空间熵（H_s）——来量化视觉编码器的"注视"模式，并追踪其跨层的演化（ΔH_s）。这揭示了一种"符号解耦"现象：模型常常"早期锁定"视觉特征，仅在后期才扩散注意力，从而将早期感知与最终生成割裂开来。与接地假说相反，我们发现存在"聚类失败"：空间注意力与准确性之间近乎零相关（R≈0.001）。相反，可靠性是生成动态和内部状态分布的一种现象。自一致性（即采样推理路径间的一致性程度）是正确性的主导预测因子（R=0.429）。通过缩放因果干预，我们揭示出尖锐的架构性差异：LLaVA将其预测锁定在一个脆弱的后期瓶颈中，而PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL则全局分布可靠性，即使其最具预测性的层中约50%或更多被破坏，仍能保持鲁棒性。对于当前的VLM而言，可靠性信号与视觉定位图解耦，最佳推断方式来自生成时刻的动态和隐藏状态探针。

</details>


### [22] [Bridging Spatial And Frequency Views For Disaster Assessment: Benefits And Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17403)
*Shikha V. Chandel,Yadav Raj Ghimire,Timothy Agboada,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了空间域、频域及双域深度学习方法在基于卫星影像的建筑损伤多分类任务中的表现。基于EfficientNet-B0骨干网络和xView2 (xBD)数据集，实验表明双域模型优于单域模型，但所有方法在检测轻微损伤类别时仍面临类别不平衡和视觉模糊性的挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习建筑损伤评估方法大多依赖空间域特征，而频域表示能够捕获互补的结构线索（如碎片模式和倒塌纹理），但尚缺乏对空间域、频域及双域方法的系统公平比较。

Method: 所有模型基于EfficientNet-B0骨干网络，在相同训练设置下进行训练，仅输入表示和融合策略不同。对比了空间域、频域以及双域（空间+频域）三种深度学习方法的建筑损伤多分类性能，使用准确率、宏F1分数、每类指标和混淆矩阵进行评估。

Result: 双域空间配置取得最高测试准确率（0.4688）和最低损失；纯空间模型取得最佳宏F1分数（0.4254），类别性能更均衡；纯频域模型表现最差且存在过拟合。所有模型均难以检测轻微损伤等级（Minor类）。

Conclusion: 双域方法相比单域方法有可衡量的提升，但混合表示仍存在局限性，未来工作需要关注数据平衡、高级融合策略和正则化方法。

Original Abstract: Rapid assessment of building damage from satellite imagery is essential for effective disaster response and recovery. While most deep learning methods rely on spatial-domain features, frequency-domain representations can capture complementary structural cues such as debris patterns and collapse-induced textures. This study presents a controlled comparison of spatial-domain, frequency-domain, and dual-domain deep learning approaches for multi-class building damage classification using post-disaster imagery from the xView2 (xBD) dataset. To ensure fairness, all models are built on an EfficientNet-B0 backbone and trained under identical settings, differing only in their input representations and fusion strategies. Performance is evaluated using accuracy, macro F1-score, per-class metrics, and confusion matrices. Results show that dual-domain models provide measurable improvements over single-domain approaches. The dual spatial configuration achieves the highest test accuracy (0.4688) and lowest loss, while the spatial-only model attains the best macro F1-score (0.4254), indicating more balanced class performance. In contrast, frequency-only models perform worst and exhibit overfitting, suggesting limited generalization. Despite these gains, all models struggle to detect subtle damage levels, particularly the Minor class, due to class imbalance and fine-grained visual ambiguity. While dual-domain approaches improve detection of severe damage, challenges remain. These findings highlight the benefits and limitations of hybrid representations and motivate future work on data balancing, advanced fusion, and regularization.

中文摘要翻译: 基于卫星影像的建筑损伤快速评估对于有效的灾害响应和恢复至关重要。虽然大多数深度学习方法依赖于空间域特征，但频域表示可以捕获互补的结构线索，如碎片模式和倒塌引起的纹理。本研究提出了空间域、频域和双域深度学习方法在基于xView2 (xBD)数据集的灾后图像上进行多类建筑损伤分类的受控比较。为确保公平性，所有模型均基于EfficientNet-B0骨干网络构建，并在相同设置下训练，仅输入表示和融合策略不同。使用准确率、宏F1分数、每类指标和混淆矩阵评估性能。结果表明，双域模型相比单域方法提供了可衡量的改进。双空间配置取得了最高测试准确率（0.4688）和最低损失，而纯空间模型获得了最佳宏F1分数（0.4254），表明类别性能更均衡。相比之下，纯频域模型表现最差并表现出过拟合，表明泛化能力有限。尽管有这些提升，所有模型在检测细微损伤等级时仍存在困难，特别是Minor类别，原因在于类别不平衡和细粒度视觉模糊性。虽然双域方法改善了对严重损伤的检测，但挑战依然存在。这些发现凸显了混合表示的利弊，并为未来在数据平衡、高级融合和正则化方面的研究提供了动力。

</details>


### [23] [Graph Neural Networks for Semi-Supervised Image Classification with Multi-Feature Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17406)
*Marina Chagas Bulach Gapski,Vinicius Atsushi Sato Kawai,Gustavo Rosseto Leticio,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette,Mohand Said Allili*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多种特征提取器和图表示，并利用排序聚合与流形学习的图神经网络方法，在半监督图像分类中显著提升了分类准确率，特别是在标注数据稀缺的场景下。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有特征提取器（如CNN、VIT）种类繁多，不同提取器提供互补的特征信息，但如何有效整合这些异构特征和图表示以提升半监督分类性能，尤其是在标注数据稀缺的情况下，仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于GNN（特别是GCN）的新框架，集成来自不同特征提取器的多组特征和图表示，并应用排序聚合技术融合特征，同时结合流形学习进行图处理。

Result: 实验表明，策略性地组合特征和图表示，并应用流形学习处理图，在大多数实验条件下显著提升了分类准确率；排序聚合技术进一步增强了分类性能。

Conclusion: 通过整合多样化的特征和图表示，结合排序聚合与流形学习，能够有效提升GNN在标注数据稀缺场景下的半监督图像分类性能。

Original Abstract: Feature extraction involves the identification and extraction of salient characteristics or patterns, including edges, textures, shapes, and color attributes. Contemporary feature extractors predominantly leverage deep learning architectures, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (VITs). The availability of diverse feature extractors in the literature provides a wide range of feature representations. Features extracted from an image depend on the specific application, the chosen extractor, and its configuration. Therefore, integrating complementary information by combining distinct extractors offers a promising way to enhance performance. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), particularly Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), have emerged as powerful and widely adopted approaches for semi-supervised image classification, as they effectively leverage both labeled and unlabeled data while exploiting the underlying graph structures that capture relationships among samples. This study proposes a novel approach for GNNs in scenarios where labeled data is scarce, by integrating diverse sets of feature and graph representations derived from various extractors in classification scenarios. Experimental investigations were conducted, encompassing combinations of distinct feature and graph extractors, as well as rank aggregation strategies. The primary contributions of this work are underscored by the experimental findings, which demonstrate that the strategic combination of feature and graph representations, coupled with the application of manifold learning for graph processing, leads to significant improvements in classification accuracy across the majority of experimental conditions. Furthermore, the utilization of rank aggregation techniques to integrate features from different extractors was shown to enhance classification accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 特征提取涉及识别和提取显著特征或模式，包括边缘、纹理、形状和颜色属性。现代特征提取器主要依赖深度学习架构，如卷积神经网络（CNN）和视觉Transformer（ViT）。文献中多样化的特征提取器提供了丰富的特征表示。从图像中提取的特征取决于具体应用、所选提取器及其配置。因此，通过组合不同提取器来整合互补信息，为提升性能提供了一条有前景的途径。图神经网络（GNN），特别是图卷积网络（GCN），已成为半监督图像分类中强大且广泛采用的方法，因为它们能够有效利用有标签和无标签数据，同时利用捕获样本间关系的底层图结构。本研究针对标注数据稀缺的场景，提出了一种新的GNN方法，通过集成来自不同提取器的多样化特征集和图表示进行分类。实验研究涵盖了不同特征和图提取器的组合以及排序聚合策略。本文的主要贡献由实验结果所证实，这些结果表明，特征和图表示的策略性组合，结合流形学习进行图处理，在大多数实验条件下显著提升了分类准确率。此外，利用排序聚合技术整合来自不同提取器的特征，被证明能够进一步提高分类准确率。

</details>


### [24] [Attention Alignment Between Humans and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17410)
*Isaac R. Christian,Udith Haputhanthrige,Hanna Hornfeld,Declan Campbell,Samuel Nastase,Taylor Webb,Michael Graziano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了六种视觉-语言模型的空间注意力图与人类注视热力图，发现解码器架构（LSTM vs Transformer）对注视预测对齐度的主导作用远超编码器（CNN vs ViT），但高对齐的LSTM模型注意力图空间弥散且任务区分度低，而低对齐的Transformer模型注意力最锐利且任务区分最强，表明自上而下与自下而上组件在预测行为与合成神经数据时存在权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究视觉-语言模型中自上而下（任务目标驱动）和自下而上（感官刺激驱动）的注意力机制各自如何影响模型与人类视觉注视行为的一致性，并厘清不同架构组件在其中所扮演的角色。

Method: 选取六种视觉-语言模型（CNN vs ViT编码器 × LSTM vs Transformer解码器的2×2因子设计，外加Molmo 7B-D和Qwen3.5 9B），在200张图像上执行两项任务（通用描述和社会性描述），将模型的空间注意力图与人类注视热力图进行比较，并进行半球忽视仿真和TRIBE合成神经响应预测分析。

Result: ①解码器架构主导对齐度：LSTM解码器对齐度比Transformer高40-50个百分点（80-87% vs 40-59%噪声天花板）；②编码器贡献次要优势（5-20个百分点），CNN-LSTM整体最优（85-87%）；③LSTM解码器注意力图空间弥散、任务区分度低，ViT-Transformer注意力最锐利、任务区分最强；④半球忽视仿真中LSTM解码器受注意力消融影响更大；⑤注意力图与合成神经响应解离：CNN-Transformer虽注视对齐度低，但更好预测合成脑活动。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型的自上而下和自下而上组件在预测行为数据和神经数据时存在权衡，解码器架构对注视行为预测起主导作用，但高行为对齐并不等价于神经编码的准确性。

Original Abstract: Visual perception depends on top-down goals and bottom-up sensory mechanisms. Vision-language models implement both, allowing us to treat each component as a separable hypothesis about what drives where we look. We compared spatial attention maps from six vision-language models against human fixation heatmaps recorded on 200 images during two tasks (general description and social captioning). The six models spanned a 2$\times$2 factorial of CNN vs.\ ViT encoders crossed with LSTM vs.\ Transformer decoders, plus Molmo 7B-D and Qwen3.5 9B. We found that both decoder and encoder architecture shaped alignment, but decoder choice dominated. LSTM vs.\ Transformer decoders increased alignment by 40--50 percentage points (80--87\% vs.\ 40--59\% of the human noise ceiling). In contrast, CNN vs.\ ViT encoders contributed a secondary 5--20 point advantage depending on decoder family, with CNN-LSTM the most aligned model overall (85--87\%). Despite their alignment advantage, LSTM-decoder attention maps were spatially diffuse and minimally task-differentiated; ViT-Transformer, the weakest in alignment, showed the sharpest spatial concentration and strongest task differentiation. A hemispatial-neglect simulation confirmed that ablating attention impacted LSTM decoders more than Transformer decoders. In an exploratory extension using TRIBE-simulated synthetic neural responses, fixation alignment and neural relevance dissociate: CNN-Transformer attention maps better predicted synthetic brain activity despite lower fixation alignment, with attention maps best predicting early visual cortex. Together, top-down and bottom-up components trade off what they predict in behavioral and synthetic neural data.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉感知依赖于自上而下的目标和自下而上的感官机制。视觉-语言模型同时实现了这两者，使我们能够将每个组件视为关于什么驱动我们目光的可分离假设。我们比较了六个视觉-语言模型的空间注意力图与人类在两项任务（通用描述和社会性描述）中在200张图像上记录的注视热力图。这六个模型采用2×2因子设计，包括CNN与ViT编码器分别与LSTM与Transformer解码器交叉组合，外加Molmo 7B-D和Qwen3.5 9B。我们发现解码器和编码器架构均影响对齐度，但解码器选择起主导作用。LSTM与Transformer解码器相比，对齐度提高了40-50个百分点（达到人类噪声天花板的80-87% vs 40-59%）。相比之下，CNN与ViT编码器根据解码器家族不同贡献了5-20个百分点的次要优势，其中CNN-LSTM整体对齐度最高（85-87%）。尽管对齐度有优势，LSTM解码器的注意力图空间上弥散且任务区分度极小；ViT-Transformer作为对齐度最弱的模型，却展现出最锐利的空间集中性和最强的任务区分度。半球忽视仿真实验证实，注意力消融对LSTM解码器的影响大于Transformer解码器。在使用TRIBE模拟合成神经响应的探索性扩展中，注视对齐度和神经相关性发生了解离：CNN-Transformer的注意力图虽然注视对齐度较低，但能更好地预测合成脑活动，其中注意力图对早期视觉皮层的预测效果最佳。总之，自上而下和自下而上的组件在预测行为数据和合成神经数据方面存在权衡。

</details>


### [25] [Enhancing Pathological VLMs with Cross-scale Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17412)
*Chi Phan,Tianyi Zhang,Qiaochu Xue,Yufeng Wu,Dan Hu,Zeyu Liu,Sudong Wang,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个跨尺度训练与评估范式，将病理图像分析建模为多倍率推理任务。针对多图像VQA中的文本捷径问题，设计了泄漏感知数据筛选流程，构建了Scale-VQA基准数据集，并通过强化学习训练ScaleReasoner-R1模型，在跨尺度和单尺度病理基准上均达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有病理视觉-语言数据集虽包含多尺度图像，但缺乏显式的跨尺度推理目标，导致模型无法捕获关键的跨尺度表征并学习基于证据的推理。

Method: (1) 提出首个跨尺度训练与评估范式，将病理诊断形式化为多倍率推理任务；(2) 设计泄漏感知数据筛选流程，结合对抗性文本筛选和约束引导的问题设计，消除文本捷径；(3) 构建Scale-VQA基准数据集（4,685道多选题，2,537张病理图像）；(4) 使用强化学习训练ScaleReasoner-R1模型优化跨尺度VQA性能。

Result: ScaleReasoner-R1在跨尺度推理基准上达到最优，并泛化至现有单尺度基准达到最优性能。实验表明，即使有限的跨尺度监督也能显著提升病理图像理解能力。

Conclusion: 跨尺度监督学习能有效提升视觉-语言模型在病理图像分析中的表现，所提出的方法为病理多倍率推理提供了可行范式。

Original Abstract: Pathological images are inherently multi-scale, requiring pathologists to integrate evidence from global tissue architecture at low magnification to cellular morphology at higher magnification for accurate diagnosis. While existing pathological datasets for vision-language model (VLM) include various scales, they often lack an explicit cross-scale reasoning objective. This limitation prevents VLMs from capturing essential cross-scale representations and learning evidence-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first cross-scale training and evaluation paradigm that formulates pathology interpretation as multi-magnification reasoning. However, creating such a task reveals a critical challenge: multi-image visual question answering (VQA) is prone to text-only shortcuts, which allow models to guess answers using magnification-dependent artifacts rather than visual evidence. To address this, we propose a leakage-aware curation pipeline that combines adversarial text-only screening with constraint-guided question design. Using this pipeline, we construct Scale-VQA, a high-quality benchmark with 4,685 multiple-choice questions grounded in 2,537 pathology images across multiple magnification levels. Finally, we present ScaleReasoner-R1, a model trained via reinforcement learning to optimize performance on the cross-scale VQA task. ScaleReasoner-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance on our cross-scale reasoning benchmark and generalizes to SOTA performance on established single-scale benchmarks. Findings suggest that even the limited cross-scale supervision can significantly improve pathological understanding. The code and demos will be open-sourced.

中文摘要翻译: 病理图像本质上是多尺度的，病理学家需要整合从低倍镜下全局组织结构到高倍镜下细胞形态的证据才能做出准确诊断。虽然现有的视觉-语言模型病理数据集包含多种尺度，但它们通常缺乏显式的跨尺度推理目标。这一局限性阻碍了视觉-语言模型捕获关键的跨尺度表征并学习基于证据的推理。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了首个跨尺度训练与评估范式，将病理诊断形式化为多倍率推理任务。然而，创建此类任务暴露出一个关键挑战：多图像视觉问答容易陷入仅基于文本的捷径——模型可借助与倍率相关的伪影而非视觉证据来猜测答案。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种泄漏感知数据筛选流程，将对抗性文本筛选与约束引导的问题设计相结合。利用该流程，我们构建了Scale-VQA，一个高质量基准数据集，包含4,685道基于多倍率下2,537张病理图像的单选题。最后，我们提出了ScaleReasoner-R1，一个通过强化学习训练的模型，用于优化跨尺度VQA任务的性能。ScaleReasoner-R1在我们的跨尺度推理基准上取得了最优性能，并在已有的单尺度基准上泛化至最优水平。研究结果表明，即使是有限的跨尺度监督也能显著提升病理图像理解能力。代码和演示将公开。

</details>


### [26] [Impact of Hand Impairment and Occlusions on Hand Pose Estimation Accuracy in Augmented Reality Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17427)
*Damian M. Manzone,Mathew Szymanowski,Olga Taran,Shuo Cai,Melissa Marquez-Chin,Tammy Zeng,Hardeep Singh,Cesar Marquez-Chin,José Zariffa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了HoloLens 2头显与多种姿态估计算法在颈椎损伤患者和健康对照者手部姿态估计中的准确性，发现这些方法对受损手部同样有效，且HoloLens 2可用于手部康复应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 混合现实手部康复应用依赖精确的手部姿态估计，但现有研究缺乏对实际手部损伤或物体交互遮挡影响姿态估计精度的探讨，且尚未将AR头显预测与主流姿态估计算法进行系统比较。

Method: 以颈椎损伤患者（13人）和健康对照（15人）为被试，在交互透明与不透明物体场景下，评估HoloLens 2头显及WiLoR、HaMeR、WildHands、MediaPipe四种姿态估计算法的精度，通过多相机三角测量获取3D关节位置的真值。

Result: 1) 患者组与对照组的手部姿态估计精度无显著差异；2) 透明物体比不透明物体精度略高（0.1 mm）；3) WiLoR和HaMeR的预测精度略优于HoloLens 2（约2 mm）。

Conclusion: HoloLens 2在手部康复应用中具有可行性，且生成的公开数据集可用于改进针对手部受损人群的姿态估计方法。

Original Abstract: Mixed reality applications can be designed for hand rehabilitation. Augmented reality (AR) head mounted displays (HMDs) specifically allow for ecologically valid tasks because individuals can see their real environment and interact with real objects while receiving additional cues on the HMD. While these applications rely on accurate hand pose estimation, there is a gap in investigating the influence of hand impairment or occlusion from real-object interactions on pose estimation accuracy. Further, comparisons between AR HMD predictions and state-of-the-art pose estimation methods have not been established. The current study assessed pose estimation accuracy of the HoloLens 2 HMD and state-of-the-art pose estimation algorithms (WiLoR, HaMeR, WildHands, and MediaPipe) while individuals with cervical spinal cord injury (cSCI; n = 13, Neurological Level of Injury: C3-C6; American Spinal Injury Association Impairment Scale: A-D) and 15 uninjured controls interacted with clear and opaque objects. Ground truth estimates of 3D joint positions were generated via triangulation from a multi-camera setup. Pose estimation accuracy did not differ between the cSCI and uninjured control groups suggesting that 3D joint predictions from the HoloLens 2 and pose estimation algorithms can generalize to populations with hand impairment. Further, clear objects provided a small accuracy advantage over opaque objects (0.1 mm) and predictions from both WiLoR and HaMeR were slightly more accurate than the HoloLens 2 (2 mm). Overall, these results suggest that the HoloLens 2 may be viable for hand rehabilitation applications and the dataset generated can be used to refine pose estimation methods for hand-impaired populations.

中文摘要翻译: 混合现实应用程序可设计用于手部康复。增强现实头戴式显示器特别适用于生态效度高的任务，因为个体可以在看到真实环境并与真实物体交互的同时，接收头显上的额外提示。虽然这些应用依赖于精确的手部姿态估计，但现有研究缺乏对实际手部损伤或真实物体交互遮挡影响姿态估计精度的探讨。此外，AR头显预测与最先进姿态估计方法之间的比较尚未建立。本研究评估了HoloLens 2头显和最先进的姿态估计算法（WiLoR、HaMeR、WildHands和MediaPipe）在颈椎损伤患者（cSCI；n = 13，神经损伤水平：C3-C6；美国脊柱损伤协会损伤量表：A-D）和15名健康对照组与透明及不透明物体交互时的姿态估计精度。通过多相机设置三角测量生成3D关节位置的真值。结果表明，cSCI组与健康对照组之间的姿态估计精度无显著差异，表明HoloLens 2和姿态估计算法的3D关节预测可推广到手部受损人群。此外，透明物体比不透明物体提供了微小的精度优势（0.1 mm），且WiLoR和HaMeR的预测精度略优于HoloLens 2（约2 mm）。总体而言，这些结果表明HoloLens 2在手部康复应用中具有可行性，且生成的公开数据集可用于改进针对手部受损人群的姿态估计方法。

</details>


### [27] [CIAN: Multi-Stage Framework for Event-Enriched Image Captioning via Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17430)
*Trinh Thi Thu Hien,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIAN多阶段框架，通过检索文章增强图像描述，补充事件上下文信息（时间、地点、参与者），在OpenEvents-V1上取得高检索性能（mAP 0.979）并提升描述质量（CIDEr从0.030提升至0.094）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像描述模型局限于像素级视觉内容，无法描述事件的时间、地点、参与者等更广泛的上下文信息，缺乏对外部叙事知识的利用。

Method: 提出Contextual Image-Article Narrator (CIAN)多阶段框架：1) 使用SigLIP检索相关文章；2) 用LoRA微调Qwen模型进行叙事生成；3) 应用基于N-Gram的精炼策略提升流畅性和连贯性。

Result: 在OpenEvents-V1基准上，检索mAP达到0.979，图像描述质量CIDEr从0.030提升至0.094。

Conclusion: 检索增强推理结合语言精炼策略能够有效生成具有上下文感知能力、类人化的图像描述。

Original Abstract: Event-enriched image captioning describes not only visible content but also the broader context of events, including timing, location, and participants, capabilities missing in most pixel-bound models. We propose the Contextual Image-Article Narrator (CIAN), a multi-stage framework that enriches captions with external narratives. CIAN retrieves relevant articles using SigLIP, summarizes them to guide a Narrative Generation stage with a LoRA-fine-tuned Qwen model, and applies N-Gram-based Refinement for fluency and coherence. On the OpenEvents-V1 benchmark, CIAN achieves high retrieval performance (mAP 0.979) and improves caption quality, increasing CIDEr from 0.030 to 0.094. These results highlight the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented reasoning combined with linguistic refinement for generating context-aware, human-like captions.

中文摘要翻译: 事件增强的图像描述不仅描述可见内容，还描述事件的更广泛上下文，包括时间、地点和参与者——这是大多数像素级模型所不具备的能力。我们提出了上下文图像-文章叙述器（CIAN），一个多阶段框架，利用外部叙事来丰富图像描述。CIAN使用SigLIP检索相关文章，对其进行摘要以引导基于LoRA微调Qwen模型的叙事生成阶段，并应用基于N-Gram的精炼策略以提高流畅性和连贯性。在OpenEvents-V1基准上，CIAN实现了高检索性能（mAP 0.979），并提升了描述质量，将CIDEr从0.030提升至0.094。这些结果突显了检索增强推理结合语言精炼策略在生成上下文感知、类人化描述方面的有效性。

</details>


### [28] [Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Silhouette-Guided Animal Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17431)
*Quoc-Duy Tran,Anh-Tuan Vo,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉检索增强生成（Visual-RAG）框架，从自然剪影中生成动物艺术图像，通过检索结构相似的动物形状作为参考，引导扩散模型生成，实现了计算形态错觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人类具有从随机形状（如云朵、石头、树叶）中感知有意义形态的空想性错觉能力，而现有生成式AI缺乏这种将模糊形状创造性解读为具体物体的想象力，本文旨在用计算方式复现这一过程。

Method: 构建包含28,586个高质量动物剪影的语料库，从自然剪影中检索结构相似的动物形状作为参考示例，利用ControlNet和IP-Adapter引导基于扩散模型的图像生成；使用RANSAC进行形状上下文对齐，并对形状进行标准化处理。

Result: 消融实验表明，基于RANSAC的形状上下文对齐提供了最精准的匹配，去除形状标准化后内点率降至仅13.4%；用户研究（12人）显示Visual-RAG能提供合理的视觉解读，但在感知冲击力方面仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 本工作为计算空想性错觉奠定了基础，展示了机器如何在想象力发现的早期阶段发挥作用。

Original Abstract: Generative AI has advanced the ability to render photorealistic or artistic images, yet it remains limited in a key aspect of human creativity: interpreting ambiguous shapes. This phenomenon, rooted in pareidolia, allows humans to perceive meaningful forms in random patterns such as clouds, stones, or leaves. To computationally replicate this imaginative process, we introduce Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Visual-RAG), a framework that generates animal art directly from natural silhouettes. Our method retrieves structurally similar animal shapes from a curated corpus of 28,586 high-quality silhouettes and uses them as reference exemplars to guide diffusion-based generation with ControlNet and IP-Adapter. Ablation studies confirm that shape Context with RANSAC provides the most accurate alignment, while removing shape standardization reduces the inlier ratio to just 13.4\%, underscoring the importance of structural fidelity in Visual-RAG. A user study with 12 participants evaluated the outputs in terms of aesthetics, silhouette fidelity, and overall impression. Results reveal that while Visual-RAG provides plausible interpretations, challenges remain in achieving high perceptual impact. This work lays the foundation for computational pareidolia, showing how machines can contribute to the early stages of imaginative discovery.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI已大幅提升了渲染照片级真实或艺术化图像的能力，但在人类创造力的一个关键方面仍存在局限：解读模糊形状。这一根植于空想性错觉（pareidolia）的现象，使人类能够从云朵、石头或树叶等随机图案中感知有意义的形态。为了以计算方式复现这一想象过程，我们提出了视觉检索增强生成（Visual-RAG）框架，该框架能够直接从自然剪影中生成动物艺术图像。我们的方法从一个包含28,586个高质量剪影的精选语料库中检索结构相似的动物形状，并将其作为参考示例，利用ControlNet和IP-Adapter引导基于扩散模型的图像生成。消融研究证实，结合RANSAC的形状上下文（Shape Context）提供了最精确的对齐，而去除形状标准化后内点率下降至仅13.4%，这凸显了结构保真度在Visual-RAG中的重要性。一项包含12名参与者的用户研究从美学效果、剪影保真度和整体印象三个维度评估了生成结果。结果表明，虽然Visual-RAG能够提供合理的视觉解读，但在实现高感知冲击力方面仍面临挑战。本工作为计算空想性错觉奠定了基础，展示了机器如何在想象力发现的早期阶段做出贡献。

</details>


### [29] [LADBench: A Benchmark for Logical Fault Detection in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17433)
*Sahasra Kondapalli,Lara Radovanovic,Aadi Palnitkar,Mingyang Mao,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了LAD-Bench基准测试，包含1000多张带有逻辑异常的合成图像，用于评估大型视觉语言模型（VLM）在自主逻辑推理方面的能力。通过渐进式提示协议（Tiered Prompting Protocol）测试模型，发现即使最优模型准确率也仅为70.11%，表明隐含逻辑故障检测仍是未解决难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的异常检测基准侧重于视觉错误或直接提示，而非开放世界部署所需的物理和社会常识推理。大型视觉语言模型在自主逻辑推理方面的能力尚未得到充分探索，缺乏评估模型识别隐含逻辑异常能力的标准化框架。

Method: 构建了LAD-Bench基准，包含1000多张涵盖住宅、城市、协作和自然四个领域的合成图像，每张图像包含逻辑异常。提出渐进式提示协议（Tiered Prompting Protocol），通过逐步暴露信息来衡量模型定位和推理逻辑故障所需的外部辅助程度。

Result: 评估主流基础模型显示，最佳模型整体准确率仅达70.11%；即使在更深层级获得明确提示后，模型仍经常无法识别异常。这表明隐含逻辑故障检测仍是未解决难题，揭示了模型在序列多模态推理中的显著弱点。

Conclusion: LAD-Bench通过揭示模型在序列多模态推理中的局限性，为提升自主视觉系统的安全性、可靠性和认知对齐提供了严谨的评估框架。

Original Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (VLMs) excel at visual question answering and semantic grounding, but their capacity for autonomous logical reasoning remains underexplored. Existing anomaly benchmarks emphasize visual errors or direct prompting rather than the physical and social common sense needed for open-world deployment. To address this, we introduce LAD-bench, a benchmark of more than 1,000 curated synthetic images with logical anomalies across four domains: Residential, Urban, Collaborative, and Nature. We further propose a Tiered Prompting Protocol based on progressive disclosure, which measures how much explicit assistance a model needs to localize and reason about a logical fault. Evaluating leading foundation models reveals substantial weaknesses: even the best achieves only 70.11% overall accuracy, showing that implicit logical fault detection remains unsolved.
