We present adversarial Voronoi camouflage that optimizes only seed-point locations under fixed, printable palettes using a soft assignment, producing structured, splinter camouflage-like patterns without additional regularization. Evaluated on person detection with COCO-style AP@[.5:.95], naive placement (Inria -> COCO) performs comparably bad, while garment-level application via segmentation mask (3DPeople) results in a significant AP drop. The attack transfers to out-of-domain backgrounds and across detector families (YOLOv9/10/11/12), indicating robustness in black-box settings. Repainting with different palettes largely nullifies the effect, and single-color tweaks show limited tolerance (<=0.17), highlighting a structure-palette coupling. The parameter-efficient, palette-constrained design improves visual plausibility while degrading real-time detector performance. Physical validation and color calibration are left for future work.
  Code: https://github.com/JensBayer/Voronoi
  This paper was originally presented at the International Conference on Military Communication and Information Systems (ICMCIS), organized by the Information Systems Technology (IST) Scientific and Technical Committee, IST-224-RSY - the ICMCIS, held in Bath, United Kingdom, 12-13 May 2026.

中文摘要翻译: 逐像素对抗补丁计算负担重且通常视觉上可察觉，限制了其在安全关键系统中的实用性。本文提出对抗性Voronoi伪装，该方法在固定可打印调色板下仅优化种子点位置，通过软分配产生结构化的碎花伪装图案，无需额外正则化。在基于COCO风格AP@[.5:.95]的行人检测评估中，朴素放置（Inria→COCO）表现同样较差，而通过分割掩码（3DPeople）进行服装级应用导致AP显著下降。该攻击可迁移至域外背景及跨检测器家族（YOLOv9/10/11/12），表明其在黑盒设置下的鲁棒性。使用不同调色板重新绘制基本可以抵消攻击效果，单色微调容忍度有限（≤0.17），突显了结构与调色板的耦合性。这种参数高效、调色板约束的设计在提高视觉合理性的同时降低了实时检测器的性能。物理验证和颜色校准留作未来工作。

</details>


### [58] [Heterogeneous SAR-optical fusion for near-real-time land use and land cover mapping under cloud contamination: A novel framework and global benchmark dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17713)
*Jiangong Xu,Weibao Xue,Xiaoyu Yu,Jun Pan,Xinlian Lianga,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CloudLULC-Net，一种端到端的异源SAR-光学融合框架，通过光学可靠性调制、异源信息自适应聚合和统一语义映射Transformer，直接从云污染的Sentinel-2影像和时序相邻的Sentinel-1 SAR数据预测LULC图，在CloudLULC-Set基准数据集上达到86.60% OA、83.29% F1-score和73.51% mIoU，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光学遥感影像常受云雾污染，限制了其在近实时土地覆被制图中的可靠性；现有SAR-光学融合方法通常假设光学观测可靠，未能有效处理云污染引入的语义不确定性。

Method: 提出CloudLULC-Net框架，包含三大核心模块：(1) 光学可靠性调制——抑制不可靠的光学响应；(2) 异源信息自适应聚合——建模光学与SAR表示间的高阶空间-通道交互；(3) 统一语义映射Transformer——将融合特征组织到面向LULC的隐空间中。此外引入语义锚引导优化策略以提升中间语义表示的一致性。同时构建包含40,223个SAR-光学-标签三元组的大规模基准数据集CloudLULC-Set。

Result: CloudLULC-Net在CloudLULC-Set上达到OA 86.60%、F1-score 83.29%、mIoU 73.51%，优于代表性异源重建优先方法和端到端SAR-光学映射方法。与现有全球LULC产品的对比及不同云覆盖水平下的分析进一步验证了其鲁棒性和实用价值。

Conclusion: CloudLULC-Net有效解决了云污染条件下的SAR-光学融合LULC制图问题，在云多发区域的目标日期土地覆被制图任务中展现出强鲁棒性和实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Optical remote sensing imagery is frequently degraded by cloud and cloud-shadow contamination, which limits its reliability for near-real-time land use and land cover (LULC) mapping. Although synthetic aperture radar (SAR) can provide cloud-penetrating structural information, existing SAR-optical fusion methods often assume reliable optical observations and insufficiently address the semantic uncertainty introduced by cloud contamination. To address this issue, we propose CloudLULC-Net, an end-to-end heterogeneous SAR-optical fusion framework that directly predicts LULC maps from cloud-contaminated Sentinel-2 imagery and temporally adjacent Sentinel-1 SAR observations. The proposed network incorporates optical reliability modulation to suppress unreliable optical responses, heterogeneous information adaptive aggregation to model high-order spatial-channel interactions between optical and SAR representations, and a unified semantic mapping transformer to organize fused features in a LULC-oriented latent space. A semantic anchor-guided optimization strategy is further introduced to improve the consistency of intermediate semantic representations. To support this task, we construct CloudLULC-Set, a large-scale benchmark dataset containing 40,223 curated SAR-optical-label triplets with pixel-level LULC annotations across diverse geographic regions and cloud conditions. Experimental results show that CloudLULC-Net achieves an OA of 86.60%, an F1-score of 83.29%, and an mIoU of 73.51%, outperforming representative heterogeneous reconstruction-first and end-to-end SAR-optical mapping methods. Comparisons with existing global LULC products and analyses under different cloud-cover levels further demonstrate the robustness and practical value of CloudLULC-Net for target-date LULC mapping in cloud-prone regions.The project is publicly available at: https://github.com/RSIIPAC/CloudLULC

中文摘要翻译: 光学遥感影像经常受到云和云阴影污染的干扰，这限制了其在近实时土地利用与土地覆盖（LULC）制图中的可靠性。尽管合成孔径雷达（SAR）能够提供穿透云层的结构信息，但现有的SAR-光学融合方法通常假设光学观测可靠，未能充分处理云污染带来的语义不确定性。为解决这一问题，我们提出CloudLULC-Net——一种端到端的异源SAR-光学融合框架，能够直接从受云污染的Sentinel-2影像和时序相邻的Sentinel-1 SAR观测数据中预测LULC图。该网络集成了光学可靠性调制机制以抑制不可靠的光学响应、异源信息自适应聚合模块以建模光学与SAR表示之间的高阶空间-通道交互，以及统一语义映射Transformer将融合特征组织到面向LULC的隐空间中。此外，还引入了一种语义锚引导优化策略，以提高中间语义表示的一致性。为支撑该任务，我们构建了CloudLULC-Set——一个包含40,223个经精心整理的SAR-光学-标签三元组的大规模基准数据集，涵盖多样化的地理区域和云况条件，并配有像素级LULC标注。实验结果表明，CloudLULC-Net达到了86.60%的总体精度（OA）、83.29%的F1分数和73.51%的平均交并比（mIoU），优于具有代表性的异源重建优先方法和端到端SAR-光学映射方法。与现有全球LULC产品的对比以及在不同云覆盖水平下的分析进一步证明了CloudLULC-Net在云多发区域进行目标日期LULC制图的鲁棒性和实用价值。该项目已公开可访问：https://github.com/RSIIPAC/CloudLULC

</details>


### [59] [GSPan: A Continuous Gaussian Primitive Representation for Arbitrary-Scale Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17722)
*Fangyi Li,Xiaoyuan Yang,Yixiao Li,Zongyang Sui,Kangqing Shen,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSPan框架，将二维高斯泼溅（2D Gaussian Splatting）引入全色锐化任务，通过连续可学习的二维高斯基元表示波段残差细节，替代传统的固定网格像素预测，实现了任意尺度下的图像融合，无需重新训练。同时提出尺度解耦非对称推理（SDAI）策略，在降低分辨率下估计基元并以目标分辨率渲染，显著加速大场景处理。在多个卫星数据集上取得了最先进的融合性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将全色锐化视为固定网格预测，限制了模型的尺度自适应能力，即无法在不重新训练的情况下适应不同分辨率的输入或输出需求。

Method: 提出GSPan框架，核心包括：(1) 将波段残差细节表示为连续可学习的二维高斯基元；(2) 设计双流层次交互（DSHI）架构和空间-频谱交互注意（SSIA）模块，从PAN和MS观测中估计高斯基元；(3) 将预测的基元渲染为残差细节场，注入上采样后的MS图像；(4) 提出尺度解耦非对称推理（SDAI）策略，在低分辨率下估计基元，在目标分辨率下渲染融合图像。

Result: 在QuickBird、GaoFen-2、WorldView-3和WorldView-3-4K数据集上，GSPan取得了最先进的融合性能；SDAI策略显著加速推理，实现了计算效率与融合质量之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 连续高斯残差表示是固定网格预测的一种灵活且尺度解耦的替代方案，展示了在全色锐化任务中实现尺度自适应的潜力。

Original Abstract: Pansharpening aims to generate high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) images by fusing low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) and panchromatic (PAN) observations. Most existing deep learning methods treat pansharpening as fixed-grid prediction, which limits scale adaptation. To address this, we propose GSPan, a framework that introduces 2D Gaussian Splatting (GS) into pansharpening. Instead of directly predicting pixels, GSPan represents band-wise residual details as continuous and learnable 2D Gaussian primitives. We design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Interaction (DSHI) architecture with a Spatial-Spectral Interactive Attention (SSIA) module to estimate these primitives from complementary PAN and MS observations. The predicted primitives are rendered as a residual detail field and injected into the upsampled MS image. This continuous representation allows GSPan to render fused images on arbitrary target sampling grids without scale-specific retraining. It further enables a Scale-Decoupled Asymmetric Inference (SDAI) strategy, which estimates primitives at a reduced resolution and renders the fused image at the target resolution for efficient large-scene pansharpening. Experiments on QuickBird, GaoFen-2, WorldView-3, and WorldView-3-4K datasets show that GSPan delivers state-of-the-art fusion performance. Moreover, SDAI markedly accelerates inference, achieving a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency and fusion quality. Our results demonstrate the potential of continuous Gaussian residual representations as a flexible and scale-decoupled alternative to fixed-grid prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 全色锐化旨在通过融合低分辨率多光谱（LRMS）图像与全色（PAN）图像来生成高分辨率多光谱（HRMS）图像。现有的大多数深度学习方法将全色锐化视为固定网格预测，这限制了模型的尺度自适应能力。为解决这一问题，我们提出GSPan框架，将二维高斯泼溅（2D Gaussian Splatting, GS）引入全色锐化任务。不同于直接预测像素值，GSPan将波段残差细节表示为连续且可学习的二维高斯基元。我们设计了一个双流层次交互（Dual-Stream Hierarchical Interaction, DSHI）架构，并配备空间-频谱交互注意力（Spatial-Spectral Interactive Attention, SSIA）模块，以从互补的PAN和MS观测中估计这些高斯基元。预测得到的基元被渲染为残差细节场，并注入到上采样后的MS图像中。这种连续表示使得GSPan能够在任意目标采样网格上渲染融合图像，而无需针对特定尺度重新训练。此外，该表示还支持一种尺度解耦非对称推理（Scale-Decoupled Asymmetric Inference, SDAI）策略，即在降低的分辨率下估计基元，并在目标分辨率下渲染融合图像，从而实现高效的大场景全色锐化。在QuickBird、GaoFen-2、WorldView-3和WorldView-3-4K数据集上的实验表明，GSPan取得了最先进的融合性能。同时，SDAI显著加速了推理过程，实现了计算效率与融合质量之间的良好权衡。我们的研究结果证明了连续高斯残差表示作为固定网格预测的一种灵活且尺度解耦的替代方案的潜力。

</details>


### [60] [ActWorld: From Explorable to Interactive World Model via Action-Aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17730)
*Zhexiao Xiong,Yizhi Song,Hao Kang,Qing Yan,Liming Jiang,Jenson Yang,Zhoujie Fu,Stathi Fotiadis,Angtian Wang,Zichuan Liu,Bo Liu,Yiding Yang,Xin Lu,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActWorld 是一个支持用户实时物体交互的交互式世界模型。它通过构建10万段人-物交互视频数据集，以及引入层级式动作感知记忆机制，解决了现有模型仅支持导航、无法与场景中物体交互的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型的动作词汇局限于导航（如行走、转向、环顾），缺乏与场景中物体的交互能力（如拾取盘子、开门等），导致世界虽然视觉上可探索但不可真正操作。作者指出这一"导航-交互鸿沟"源于两个瓶颈：数据瓶颈（缺乏带有精确密集标注的人-物交互数据）和记忆瓶颈（现有世界模型中基于近期偏好的历史压缩会丢弃因果性地决定后续物体状态的事件转换帧，导致动作遗忘）。

Method: (1) 数据层面：构建了10万段交互视频数据集，每段通过思维链推理标注了每块的文字描述。(2) 模型层面：引入层级式动作感知记忆设计，按交互重要性对历史压缩进行路由，并配备持久记忆库，在长序列生成过程中维护事件更新和物体身份标记。(3) 整体框架：在块自回归框架内扩展了原有的导航中心生成器，支持生成过程中的物体交互。

Result: 实验表明，ActWorld 在单一模型中同时支持灵活的导航和丰富的物体交互，与仅支持导航的基线相比，显著提高了交互保真度，同时不牺牲视角控制能力。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模交互视频数据集并设计层级式动作感知记忆机制，ActWorld 成功弥合了导航与交互之间的鸿沟，使交互式世界模型从"可探索"迈向"可操作"。

Original Abstract: Interactive world models aim to simulate environment dynamics under real-time user actions. However, their action vocabulary is largely confined to navigation: most actions correspond to motion (e.g., walk, turn, look around), while interaction with objects in the scene (e.g., pick up plates, open doors, or trigger physical responses) is either absent, restricted to game domains, or relegated to prompt-to-full-video scenarios. The resulting worlds are visually explorable but not truly actionable. In this work, we present ActWorld, an interactive world model that extends prior navigation-centric generators to support mid-rollout object interaction within a chunk-autoregressive framework. We argue that the navigation-interaction gap stems from two bottlenecks. First, a data bottleneck: the lack of human-object interaction data with accurate, dense labels. Second, a memory bottleneck: recency-biased history compression in existing world models discards the event-transition frames that causally determine subsequent object states, leading to an action-forgetting pathology. On the data side, we construct a 100K interaction video dataset, each annotated with per-chunk captions via chain-of-thought reasoning. On the model side, we introduce a hierarchical action-aware memory design that routes history compression by interaction importance, complemented by a persistent memory bank that maintains event-update and object-identity tokens across long rollouts. Experiments show that ActWorld supports both flexible navigation and rich object interaction within a single model, substantially improving interaction fidelity over navigation-only baselines without sacrificing viewpoint control. Project page is available at https://interactwm.github.io/ActWorld.

中文摘要翻译: 交互式世界模型旨在模拟用户实时动作下的环境动态。然而，它们的动作词汇很大程度上局限于导航：大多数动作对应运动（如行走、转向、环顾），而与场景中物体的交互（如拾取盘子、开门或触发物理响应）要么缺失，要么局限于游戏领域，要么被降级为提示到全视频场景。由此产生的世界在视觉上可探索，但并非真正可操作。在这项工作中，我们提出了 ActWorld，这是一个交互式世界模型，它将先前的以导航为中心的生成器扩展到在块自回归框架内支持生成过程中的物体交互。我们认为导航-交互鸿沟源于两个瓶颈。首先是数据瓶颈：缺乏带有精确密集标注的人-物交互数据。其次是记忆瓶颈：现有世界模型中基于近期偏好的历史压缩会丢弃因果性地决定后续物体状态的事件转换帧，导致动作遗忘的病理现象。在数据方面，我们构建了包含10万段交互视频的数据集，每段视频通过思维链推理标注了逐块描述。在模型方面，我们引入了一种层级式动作感知记忆设计，按照交互重要性对历史压缩进行路由，并辅以一个持久记忆库，在长序列生成过程中维护事件更新和物体身份标记。实验表明，ActWorld 在单一模型中同时支持灵活的导航和丰富的物体交互，与仅导航的基线相比，显著提高了交互保真度，同时不牺牲视角控制能力。项目页面可在 https://interactwm.github.io/ActWorld 获取。

</details>


### [61] [LiveStarPro: Proactive Streaming Video Understanding with Hierarchical Memory for Long-Horizon Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17798)
*Zhenyu Yang,Kairui Zhang,Bing Wang,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveStarPro 是一种面向长时视频流的实时直播助手，通过流式验证解码（SVeD）、流式因果注意力掩码（SCAM）和树状分层记忆（TSHM）三个组件，解决了在线视频-大语言模型难以同时处理连续视频流、自主决定响应时机和保留长程上下文记忆的问题，在语义正确性上提升28.9%，时序误差降低18.2%，推理速度提升1.58倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前在线视频大语言模型无法同时处理连续视频流、自主决定响应时机和保留长程上下文记忆，导致实时响应能力不足和长时间交互中的严重遗忘问题。

Method: 提出三个互补组件：(1) 流式验证解码（SVeD），通过单次困惑度验证识别合适的响应时机，无需依赖显式静默令牌；(2) 流式因果注意力掩码（SCAM），一种训练策略，在变长流上实现增量式视频-语言对齐；(3) 树状分层记忆（TSHM），一种递归记忆架构，将历史信息组织为事件链，支持从无限视频流中高效检索。

Result: 语义正确性提升28.9%，时序误差降低18.2%，流式键值缓存带来1.58倍推理加速。同时构建了OmniStarPro基准，涵盖15个真实世界场景和小时级长视频流。

Conclusion: LiveStarPro在长时视频流的实时理解和主动响应上显著优于现有方法，验证了三个组件相结合的有效性；代码和模型已开源。

Original Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress of Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs), current online architectures still struggle to simultaneously process continuous video streams, decide autonomously when to respond, and preserve long-horizon contextual memory. These obstacles undermine real-time responsiveness and cause severe forgetting throughout prolonged interactions. In this work, we introduce LiveStarPro, a live streaming assistant that is designed for proactive video understanding over long-horizon streams. The design of LiveStarPro rests on three complementary components. The first component is Streaming Verification Decoding (SVeD), an inference framework that identifies the appropriate response timing through single-pass perplexity verification, thereby eliminating the dependency on explicit silence tokens. The second component is Streaming Causal Attention Masks (SCAM), a training strategy that enforces incremental video-language alignment over variable-length streams. The third component is Tree-Structured Hierarchical Memory (TSHM), a recursive memory architecture that organizes evicted historical information into event chains and consequently enables efficient retrieval from effectively unbounded video streams. To facilitate a comprehensive evaluation under realistic online conditions, we further present OmniStarPro, a large-scale benchmark that spans 15 diverse real-world scenarios and that extends to hour-scale streams for the assessment of long-term recall. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiveStarPro consistently surpasses existing methods, attaining a 28.9% improvement in semantic correctness and an 18.2% reduction in timing error, while its streaming key-value cache further yields a 1.58x inference speedup over the same model without caching. The model and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/sotayang/LiveStarPro.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视频大语言模型取得了显著进展，当前在线架构仍难以同时处理连续视频流、自主决定响应时机以及保留长程上下文记忆。这些障碍损害了实时响应能力，并导致长时间交互中的严重遗忘。本文提出了 LiveStarPro，一个面向长时视频流的直播助手，其设计基于三个互补组件。第一个组件是流式验证解码（SVeD），一种通过单次困惑度验证来识别合适响应时机的推理框架，从而消除对显式静默令牌的依赖。第二个组件是流式因果注意力掩码（SCAM），一种在变长流上强制实现增量式视频-语言对齐的训练策略。第三个组件是树状分层记忆（TSHM），一种递归记忆架构，将历史信息组织为事件链，从而能够从无限长的视频流中进行高效检索。为促进在真实在线条件下的全面评估，我们进一步提出了 OmniStarPro，一个覆盖 15 种多样化真实场景并延伸至小时级流的大规模基准，用于评估长期记忆。大量实验表明，LiveStarPro 持续优于现有方法，语义正确性提升 28.9%，时序误差降低 18.2%，而其流式键值缓存相比无缓存模型进一步实现了 1.58 倍推理加速。模型和代码已开源：https://github.com/sotayang/LiveStarPro。

</details>


### [62] [MaineCoon: Pursuing A Real-Time Audio-Visual Social World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17800)
*Lichen Bai,Tianhao Zhang,Shitong Shao,Dingwei Tan,Qiyu Zhong,Zhengpeng Xie,Haopeng Li,Qinghao Huang,Dandan Shen,Tengjiao Ji,Wei Wang,Peicheng Wu,Yuxuan Zhao,Xiangyu Zhu,Welly Luo,Shurui Yang,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向社交交互场景的实时音视频自回归模型MaineCoon（22B参数），支持流式生成与亚秒级交互（单GPU达47.5 FPS），通过自重采样、跨模态对齐、领域偏好优化和强化在线策略蒸馏等技术实现高效训练，并设计了智能体流式推理框架支持千秒级生成长度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型成功模拟了物理环境或游戏世界，但忽略了以人类社交动态为核心的社交世界建模，而全球视频消费日益转向社交平台，因此亟需构建面向社交世界的视频生成模型。

Method: 提出MaineCoon模型，包括：(1) 自重采样（self-resampling）、(2) 跨模态表征对齐（cross-modal representation alignment）、(3) 领域感知偏好优化（domain-aware preference optimization）、(4) 强化在线策略蒸馏（ROPD）；并设计首个智能体流式推理框架，包含智能体缓存管理和提示规划以支持千秒级生成。

Result: MaineCoon在单GPU上达到47.5 FPS的实时流式生成帧率，支持亚秒级交互，是首个面向社交交互优化的实时音视频生成模型，在高质量、低延迟、长时序音视频自回归建模上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为音视频自回归模型设立了新的SOTA基准，还指出了下一代AI原生社交平台所需的范式转变。

Original Abstract: As an increasing majority of global video content is consumed on social platforms for interactive social purposes, video generation models built for social worlds are important but largely overlooked by previous studies. In this work, we define the position of social world models and build a prototype model as the first step towards this goal. While previous world models successfully simulate physical environments or gaming world exploration, they remain fundamentally detached from human-centric social dynamics. To bridge this gap as the first step to social world models, we present MaineCoon, the first real-time audio-visual autoregressive model that has 22B parameters and is capable of real-time streaming generation and sub-second interaction, with a record-breaking frame rate of up to 47.5 FPS, on a single GPU. To the best of our knowledge, MaineCoon is also the first real-time audio-visual generation model specifically optimized for social-interactive applications. To enable efficient and stable training, we introduce several novel techniques into MaineCoon, including self-resampling, cross-modal representation alignment, domain-aware preference optimization, and reinforced online-policy distillation (ROPD). We also design the first agentic streaming inference framework that supports thousand-second-scale or even longer generation while mitigating drift with agentic cache management and prompt planing. These innovations significantly accelerate training while optimizing real-time inference performance. We believe this work not only sets a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance benchmark for high-quality, low-latency, and long-horizon audio-visual autoregressive models, but also points out the paradigm shift desired for next-generation AI-native social platforms.

中文摘要翻译: 随着全球视频内容中越来越大的比例在社交平台上被消费用于互动社交目的，面向社交世界构建的视频生成模型至关重要，但以往研究大多忽略了这一点。在这项工作中，我们定义了社交世界模型的位置，并构建了一个原型模型作为迈向这一目标的第一步。虽然以往的世界模型成功模拟了物理环境或游戏世界探索，但它们从根本上脱离了以人类为中心的社交动态。为弥合这一差距，作为社交世界模型的第一步，我们提出了MaineCoon——首个实时音视频自回归模型，拥有22B参数，能够实现实时流式生成和亚秒级交互，在单GPU上创下了高达47.5 FPS的帧率记录。据我们所知，MaineCoon也是首个专门针对社交交互应用优化的实时音视频生成模型。为了实现高效稳定的训练，我们在MaineCoon中引入了多项创新技术，包括自重采样、跨模态表征对齐、领域感知偏好优化和强化在线策略蒸馏（ROPD）。我们还设计了首个智能体流式推理框架，支持千秒级甚至更长的生成，并通过智能体缓存管理和提示规划来缓解漂移。这些创新显著加速了训练，同时优化了实时推理性能。我们相信，这项工作不仅为高质量、低延迟、长时序的音视频自回归模型设立了新的最先进性能基准，还指出了下一代AI原生社交平台所需的范式转变。

</details>


### [63] [Million-scale multimodal pollen microscopy with expert-guided foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17809)
*András Biricz,Björn Gedda,Donát Magyar,Antonio Spanu,János Fillinger,Péter Pollner,István Csabai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pollen AI Atlas，一个百万级多模态花粉显微镜资源数据集，包含来自4个地理来源、4种扫描仪设置、31个科的46个分类标签的151万颗粒检测，并配有机器生成的结构化形态描述；实验表明该方法在花粉识别、跨区域迁移和图文检索上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前花粉自动识别系统难以在标本制备方式、扫描仪设置和地理来源之间泛化，且缺乏兼具规模与可解释性的多模态花粉显微镜资源，阻碍了空气生物学、古生态学和生物多样性监测领域的发展。

Method: 从纯种全切片明场图像出发，每张切片人工选取一个代表性花粉粒作为种子，通过token级挖掘与过滤生成151万颗粒检测；利用五种开源视觉-语言模型，结合专家验证的孢粉学锚点（纹孔系统、壁饰纹饰、形状、大小），为每颗粒生成机器化的结构化形态描述。

Result: 检测提案精度在专家标注测试区域达99.6%；Gemma4模型在文本可控性、无泄漏和检索性能上最优；基于冻结视觉特征的基线达88.16% top-1分类准确率；跨区域检索中，文本嵌入在图像相似度下降时仍保持稳健（mAP@20 0.811 vs 0.262）。

Conclusion: 该数据集与配套资源为花粉识别、跨区域域适应和领域特定多模态显微镜学习提供了标准基准。

Original Abstract: Automated pollen identification from microscopy remains a bottleneck in aerobiology, palaeoecology and biodiversity monitoring, because scalable systems must generalise across specimen preparation, scanner settings and geographic origins while retaining palynological interpretability. To address this gap, we present a million-scale multimodal pollen microscopy resource, Pollen AI Atlas, assembled from pure-species whole-slide bright-field images spanning four geographic origins, four scanner settings and 46 taxon labels across 31 botanical families. Seeded by one manually selected exemplar per source slide, token-level mining and filtering produced 1,511,390 released grain detections with 99.6\% proposal precision in expert-curated test regions. Each detection was paired with machine-generated grain-level morphological captions from five open-weight vision-language models, guided by expert-verified palynological anchors, yielding structured descriptions of aperture systems, wall ornamentation, shape and size. Among the evaluated models, Gemma4 provided the most controlled primary caption set, combining tight length control, no leakage and the strongest text-retrieval performance. Baseline benchmarks with frozen visual features reached 88.16\% top-1 accuracy, while cross-regional retrieval showed that caption-derived text embeddings remained robust when image similarity degraded (mAP@20 0.811 versus 0.262). Released data, annotations, captions, splits, code, and weights provide a benchmark for pollen recognition, cross-regional domain adaptation and domain-specific multimodal microscopy learning.

中文摘要翻译: 从显微镜图像中自动识别花粉仍然是空气生物学、古生态学和生物多样性监测领域的一个瓶颈，因为可扩展的系统必须在标本制备方式、扫描仪设置和地理来源之间实现泛化，同时保持孢粉学的可解释性。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Pollen AI Atlas——一个百万级多模态花粉显微镜资源数据集，该数据集由纯种全切片明场图像构建，涵盖四个地理来源、四种扫描仪设置、31个植物科的46个分类标签。以每张源切片中人工选取的一个代表性样本作为种子，通过token级挖掘与过滤，在专家标注的测试区域中以99.6%的提案精度生成了1,511,390个释放的颗粒检测结果。每次检测均与五种开源视觉-语言模型生成的机器化颗粒级形态描述配对，这些描述在专家验证的孢粉学锚点引导下生成，提供了关于纹孔系统、壁饰纹饰、形状和大小的结构化描述。在评估的模型中，Gemma4提供了最可控的主描述集，兼具严格长度控制、无泄漏和最强的文本检索性能。基于冻结视觉特征的基线基准达到了88.16%的top-1准确率，而跨区域检索实验表明，当图像相似度下降时，描述导出的文本嵌入仍然保持稳健（mAP@20为0.811对比0.262）。发布的数据、标注、描述、数据划分、代码和权重为花粉识别、跨区域域适应和领域特定多模态显微镜学习提供了基准。

</details>


### [64] [Human-in-the-Loop Atlas-Based 3D Asset Segmentation for Interactive Content Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17824)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Jakob Hansen,Robin Horst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协同的3D模型分割管线，通过贪婪视图选择、SAM~2交互式分割和UV回投影，生成统一的2D参数化分割图集，适用于交互媒体、游戏和XR内容制作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3D资产分割标准取决于具体应用场景且需要用户控制，现有方法难以在灵活性和自动化之间取得平衡。

Method: 首先利用贪婪集合覆盖策略在采样表面点上选择紧凑的渲染视图集；然后通过SAM~2和Label Studio对这些视图进行交互式分割；最后将分割掩码反投影到模型的UV参数化上，生成统一的分割图集。

Result: 在八件文化遗产物体上的演示性技术评估表明，该方法能在多样几何体上生成可用的分割图集，同时揭示了需要人工修正的常见问题来源：精细结构、空腔和弱外观边界。

Conclusion: 该人机协同管线能有效生成统一的分割图集，支持下游生产任务（如逐段材质分配、风格迁移和语义标注），但仍需针对精细结构和弱边界等场景进行改进。

Original Abstract: Segmenting 3D assets into meaningful regions remains challenging, especially when segmentation criteria are application-dependent and require user control. We present a human-in-the-loop pipeline for generating a segmented 2D parameterized atlas from a 3D model for interactive media, game, and XR content workflows. Our method first selects a compact set of rendered views using a greedy set cover strategy over sampled surface points, and then supports interactive segmentation of these views with SAM~2 and Label Studio. The resulting masks are back-projected onto the model's UV parameterization to produce a unified segmented atlas that supports downstream production tasks such as segment-wise material assignment, style transfer, and semantic labeling. We assess the pipeline through a demonstration-based technical evaluation on eight cultural heritage objects. The results show that the approach can generate usable segmented atlases across diverse geometries while revealing recurring sources of manual correction, particularly fine structures, cavities, and weak appearance boundaries.

中文摘要翻译: 将3D资产分割成有意义的区域仍然具有挑战性，尤其是当分割标准取决于具体应用且需要用户控制时。我们提出了一种人机协同管线，用于从3D模型生成分割后的2D参数化图集，适用于交互媒体、游戏和XR内容工作流。我们的方法首先通过基于采样表面点的贪婪集合覆盖策略选择紧凑的渲染视图集，然后使用SAM~2和Label Studio对这些视图进行交互式分割。所得掩码被反投影到模型的UV参数化上，生成统一的分割图集，支持逐段材质分配、风格迁移和语义标注等下游生产任务。我们通过对八件文化遗产物体的演示性技术评估来检验该管线。结果表明，该方法能在多样几何体上生成可用的分割图集，同时揭示了需要人工修正的常见问题来源，尤其是精细结构、空腔和弱外观边界。

</details>


### [65] [High-Fidelity 3D Geometric Reconstruction of Pelvic Organs from MRI: A Hybrid Deep Learning and Iterative Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17836)
*Hui Wang,Xiaowei Li,Chenxin Zhang,Yifan Feng,Jianwei Zuo,Yumeng Tang,Xiuli Sun,Jianliu Wang,Bing Xie,Jiajia Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合可变形形状建模框架，结合深度学习预测与迭代优化，从MRI数据中高保真、高质量地重建盆腔器官（膀胱、子宫、直肠）三维几何结构，在几何保真度、分割精度和网格质量上显著优于现有主流方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究主要关注图像分割或三维模型的下游应用，但高保真、高质量的几何重建仍然劳动密集且缺乏标准化，亟需一种自动化、高精度的重建方法。

Method: 提出混合可变形形状建模框架，包含三个核心组件：1）几何感知的多级深度学习架构，保持盆腔器官的拓扑一致性；2）两阶段摊销优化训练策略，平衡全局形状捕捉与局部表面细化；3）整体协同机制——训练阶段迭代优化为深度学习提供监督，推理阶段深度学习快速预测全局器官形态，再通过迭代优化细化局部表面和网格质量。

Result: 在几何保真度上显著优于主流深度学习器官重建模型；膀胱、直肠、子宫的Chamfer Distance值更低、Dice相似系数更高；在保持高计算效率的同时获得更优的整体体积网格质量；在患者级别上，minSICN和minSIGE最差10个元素的均值高于传统几何后处理算法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了盆腔器官的高保真三维几何重建，兼顾几何精度、拓扑一致性和计算效率，为盆腔底建模和患者特异性分析提供了标准化、自动化的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Patient-specific 3D reconstruction of pelvic organ geometry from MRI is important for pelvic floor modeling and downstream patient-specific analysis. However, while previous studies have focused primarily on either image segmentation or downstream use of 3D models, the reconstruction of high-fidelity, high-quality geometries remains labor-intensive and poorly standardized. The study introduced a hybrid deformable shape modeling framework that integrates deep learning prediction with iterative optimization for the reconstruction of the bladder, uterus, and rectum. The framework consists of three core components: a geometry-aware multi-level deep learning architecture that preserves topological consistency of pelvic organs; a two-stage amortized optimization training strategy that balances global shape capture and local surface refinement; and a holistic synergy mechanism--where iterative optimization provides supervision for deep learning during the training phase, and during inference, deep learning rapidly predicts the global organ morphology, followed by iterative optimization to refine local surfaces and mesh quality. This framework demonstrated marked superiority in geometric fidelity than current mainstream deep learning-based organ reconstruction models. For individual anatomical structures, the reconstructed 3D geometries for the bladder, rectum, and uterus achieved significantly lower Chamfer Distance values and higher Dice Similarity Coefficient scores. In addition, while maintaining high computational efficiency, the proposed architecture yielded superior overall volumetric mesh quality. At the patient level, the framework achieved higher mean values for the 10 worst elements for both minSICN and minSIGE compared to traditional geometric post-processing algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 基于MRI的患者特异性盆腔器官几何三维重建对于盆底建模和下游患者特异性分析具有重要意义。然而，以往研究主要关注图像分割或三维模型的下游应用，高保真、高质量的几何重建仍然劳动密集且缺乏标准化。本研究引入了一种混合可变形形状建模框架，将深度学习预测与迭代优化相结合，用于膀胱、子宫和直肠的重建。该框架包含三个核心组件：保持盆腔器官拓扑一致性的几何感知多级深度学习架构；平衡全局形状捕捉与局部表面细化的两阶段摊销优化训练策略；以及整体协同机制——训练阶段迭代优化为深度学习提供监督，推理阶段深度学习快速预测全局器官形态，随后通过迭代优化细化局部表面和网格质量。该框架在几何保真度上显著优于当前主流的基于深度学习的器官重建模型。针对各个解剖结构，重建的膀胱、直肠和子宫三维几何体实现了显著更低的Chamfer距离值和更高的Dice相似系数分数。此外，在保持高计算效率的同时，所提出的架构获得了更优的整体体积网格质量。在患者级别上，该框架在minSICN和minSIGE的最差10个元素平均值上均优于传统的几何后处理算法。

</details>


### [66] [A Quantitative Analysis of Multimodal Biomarkers in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17867)
*Antonio Scardace,Daniele Ravì*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对阿尔茨海默病研究中tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分和APOE4四种模态的生物标志物进行了系统性的定量分析，揭示了跨模态冗余与预测依赖关系，识别了tau蛋白病理与脑萎缩的关联区域，并分解了tau-认知关联中的萎缩相关与独立成分，最终确定了一条与认知衰退对齐的主要神经退行性轨迹。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管多模态方法在阿尔茨海默病研究中日益普及，但不同模态（分子、结构、临床、遗传）之间的关系尚不明确，缺乏系统性的定量分析来理解它们之间的动态交互，以改进疾病建模、识别冗余评估并减少患者负担和采集成本。

Method: 基于ADNI数据集的789名受试者数据，整合tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分（MMSE和CDR）和APOE4基因型四种模态，进行了四项分析：(A) 量化跨模态互信息和解释方差评估冗余与预测依赖；(B) 跨脑区分析tau拓扑与结构萎缩的关联以选择信息性ROI；(C) 对tau-认知关联进行统计分解，分离出萎缩相关与萎缩独立成分；(D) 识别与认知衰退对齐的主要神经退行性轨迹。

Result: 研究发现了不同模态生物标志物之间的冗余程度与预测依赖关系，明确了tau蛋白病理与特定脑区结构萎缩的关联模式，揭示了tau-认知关联中既有通过萎缩介导的路径也有独立于萎缩的直接路径，并识别出一条与认知衰退进程相一致的主导性神经退行性轨迹。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了跨模态关系的系统性表征，有助于提高阿尔茨海默病生物标志物的可解释性和选择合理性，为精简评估方案、降低患者负担提供了定量依据。

Original Abstract: Despite increasing adoption of multimodal approaches in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) research -- aimed at integrating molecular, structural, clinical, and genetic biomarkers to enhance disease characterization -- the relationships among these modalities remain poorly understood. A systematic analysis of their dynamic interaction is essential for improving disease modeling, identifying redundant assessments, and reducing patient burden and acquisition costs. In this paper, we present a quantitative analysis of multimodal AD biomarkers by integrating tau-PET, structural MRI, cognitive scores (MMSE and CDR), and APOE4 data from 789 subjects drawn from the ADNI dataset. In our analyses, we (A) quantify cross-modal mutual information and explained variance to assess redundancy and predictive dependencies; (B) examine associations between tau topologies and structural atrophy across brain regions to select informative ROIs; (C) perform a statistical decomposition of the tau-cognition association into atrophy-related and atrophy-independent components; (D) and identify a dominant neurodegenerative trajectory that aligns with cognitive decline. This study provides a systematic characterization of cross-modal relationships, improving the interpretability and selection of biomarkers in AD. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/antonioscardace/Multimodal-AD.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管在阿尔茨海默病(AD)研究中，整合分子、结构、临床和遗传生物标志物以增强疾病表征的多模态方法日益普及，但这些模态之间的关系仍不清楚。系统分析它们之间的动态交互对于改进疾病建模、识别冗余评估以及减少患者负担和采集成本至关重要。本文中，我们通过整合来自ADNI数据集的789名受试者的tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分（MMSE和CDR）及APOE4数据，对多模态AD生物标志物进行了定量分析。在我们的分析中，我们(A)量化了跨模态互信息和解释方差，以评估冗余性和预测依赖性；(B)检查了跨脑区tau拓扑结构与结构萎缩之间的关联，以选择信息性兴趣区域(ROI)；(C)对tau-认知关联进行了统计分解，将其分为萎缩相关成分和萎缩独立成分；(D)识别出一条与认知衰退对齐的主要神经退行性轨迹。本研究提供了跨模态关系的系统性表征，提高了AD生物标志物的可解释性和选择合理性。代码已公开在：https://github.com/antonioscardace/Multimodal-AD。

</details>


### [67] [Revisiting Structural Dependency in Autoregressive Multi-Task Table Recognition via Order-Independent Cell-Level Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17874)
*Takaya Kawakatsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构精炼模块，通过非因果注意力生成顺序无关的单元格表征，实现多任务表格识别中单元格的并行解码与全局上下文感知，在两大数据集上取得了更优的定位与识别效果，并将推理速度提升约三倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖自回归解码器生成表格结构并复用其隐状态进行单元格定位与内容识别，导致单元格表征顺序相关，削弱了跨单元格的全局一致性。

Method: 提出一个结构精炼模块，利用非因果注意力（non-causal attention）生成顺序无关的单元格特征，使每个单元格在条件化全局上下文的同时，能够并行推断其内容。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单元格定位和端到端识别任务上均取得一致提升，同时将推理时间降低约三倍。

Conclusion: 通过消除自回归解码带来的顺序依赖，结构精炼模块有效增强了全局一致性，实现了更高效的并行推理与更优的表格识别性能。

Original Abstract: Multi-task table recognition jointly addresses table structure prediction, cell localization, and cell content recognition within a unified framework. Existing approaches often rely on autoregressive decoders to generate table structures and reuse their hidden states for cell localization and content recognition. This autoregressive generation process can make cell representations order-dependent, degrading global consistency across cells. This paper proposes a structural refinement module that produces order-independent cell features through non-causal attention. This design enables parallel inference of cell contents while conditioning each cell on global context encoded in the refined features. Experiments on two large datasets demonstrate consistent gains in cell localization and end-to-end recognition, while reducing overall inference time by around threefold.

中文摘要翻译: 多任务表格识别在统一框架下联合处理表格结构预测、单元格定位和单元格内容识别。现有方法通常依赖自回归解码器生成表格结构，并复用其隐状态进行单元格定位与内容识别。这种自回归生成过程可能使单元格表征产生顺序依赖，从而削弱跨单元格的全局一致性。本文提出一种结构精炼模块，通过非因果注意力生成顺序无关的单元格特征。该设计使每个单元格在条件化精炼特征所编码的全局上下文的同时，能够并行推断其内容。在两个大规模数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单元格定位和端到端识别上均取得一致提升，同时将整体推理时间降低约三倍。

</details>


### [68] [MoonSplat: Monocular Online Gaussian Splatting with Sim(3) Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17935)
*Guo Pu,Yixuan Han,Haofeng Li,Yao Zhang,Hui Zhou,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoonSplat，一种结合全局Sim(3)优化的鲁棒高效在线体素化3DGS重建框架，解决了在线单目3D重建中相机姿态估计脆弱和优化效率低的问题，在多种数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线3DGS方法存在两个关键挑战：(1) 由于缺乏全局优化，相机姿态估计脆弱；(2) 在大规模或长序列场景中优化效率低下。

Method: (1) 提出集成全局Sim(3)优化的在线体素化3DGS框架，实现可靠的相机跟踪和高效的全局闭环（同时优化相机位姿和体素化3DGS）；(2) 引入颜色残差学习策略，加速体素化3DGS收敛并提升渲染质量。

Result: 在多种室内外数据集上，该方法在相机姿态估计精度和渲染质量上均达到SOTA，同时保持实时效率；在真实无人机主动重建系统中验证了鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种鲁棒高效的在线体素化3DGS重建框架，通过全局Sim(3)优化和颜色残差学习，有效解决了在线3DGS的核心挑战，在理论和实际应用中均表现出色。

Original Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction from monocular image sequences is a challenging and ongoing research topic. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), leveraging its high-quality real-time rendering capability, empowers online 3D reconstruction to represent dense scenes with enhanced expressiveness, and thus holds great promise for a wide range of applications such as robotics and AR/VR. However, existing online 3DGS methods still suffer from some key challenges: fragile camera pose estimation due to the lack of global optimization, and low optimization efficiency in large-scale or long-sequence scenarios. To address these issues, we propose a robust and efficient online voxelized 3DGS reconstruction framework integrated with global $\text{Sim}(3)$ optimization, which enables reliable camera tracking and efficient global loop closure for both camera poses and voxelized 3DGS. To accelerate the convergence of the voxelized 3DGS, we further introduce a color residual learning strategy, which not only boosts optimization speed but also enhances rendering quality. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera pose estimation accuracy and rendering quality, while retaining real-time efficiency. Additionally, we develop and deploy a real-world UAV-based active reconstruction system grounded on our proposed method, validating its robustness and generalizability for practical online 3D reconstruction tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/TrickyGo/MoonSplat.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目图像序列中进行在线三维重建是一个具有挑战性且持续活跃的研究课题。3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）凭借其高质量实时渲染能力，为在线三维重建提供了更强的表达能力来表征密集场景，因此在机器人和AR/VR等广泛应用中前景广阔。然而，现有的在线3DGS方法仍然面临一些关键挑战：由于缺乏全局优化，相机姿态估计较为脆弱；在大规模或长序列场景中优化效率低下。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种集成全局Sim(3)优化的鲁棒且高效的在线体素化3DGS重建框架，实现了对相机位姿和体素化3DGS的可靠相机跟踪与高效全局闭环。为加速体素化3DGS的收敛，我们进一步引入了一种颜色残差学习策略，该策略不仅提升了优化速度，还增强了渲染质量。在多种室内外数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法在相机姿态估计精度和渲染质量方面均达到了最先进水平，同时保持了实时效率。此外，我们基于所提出的方法开发并部署了一个真实的无人机主动重建系统，验证了该方法在实际在线三维重建任务中的鲁棒性和泛化性。我们的代码和数据可在 https://github.com/TrickyGo/MoonSplat 获取。

</details>


### [69] [Plug-and-Adapt: Multimodal Coreference Resolution at First Sight with a Pretrained Alignment Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17950)
*Jinghan Wu,Jing Li,Ivor W. Tsang,Xuetao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用的多模态指代消解方法，通过预训练细粒度对齐模型并利用证据理论融合视觉与类别线索，无需目标数据集训练或依赖大型视觉语言模型，在CIN基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MCR方法需要目标数据集标注训练才能应用，泛化能力差；而大型视觉语言模型参数量巨大、部署困难且多需付费API，难以直接使用。因此需要一种无需训练、不依赖大模型的零样本MCR方法。

Method: 首先利用视觉-语言对齐数据集预训练一个细粒度的文本与视觉上下文对齐模型；然后通过证据理论融合视觉线索和类别线索进行相似度聚合，将对齐模型重新用于MCR任务。

Result: 在CIN基准数据集上，CoNLL F1比专用SOTA方法提升5.31%，比流行VLLM提升2.12%；在掩码CIN数据集上验证了鲁棒性，在构建的VCR-MCR数据集上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的即插即用方法有效避免了目标数据集训练和大模型依赖，在零样本MCR任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能，同时具备良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Visual information helps resolve ambiguity in coreference resolution, leading to notable performance gains. However, existing Multi-modal Coreference Resolution (MCR) methods require training with (partially) annotated data from the target dataset before they can be applied, preventing their direct usability and raising concerns about generalization. While Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) with billions of parameters offer promising zero-shot capabilities, they remain largely inaccessible. Their massive size limits deployability, and many are only accessible through paid APIs. In this paper, we propose a plug-and-adapt method that strategically adapts a carefully pre-trained \emph{alignment model} for immediate use in MCR tasks, designed to eliminate the need for training on scarce benchmark datasets or relying on resource-intensive VLLMs. Specifically, we first pre-train a fine-grained alignment model between textual and visual contextual information using vision-language alignment datasets. We then repurpose the alignment model to MCR through similarity aggregation by fusing visual and categorical cues with evidence theory, thereby enhancing effectiveness. Experiments on the Coreference Image Narratives (CIN) benchmark dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, achieving a 5.31\% and 2.12\% improvement in CoNLL F1 over SOTA dedicated methods and popular VLLMs, respectively. We further evaluate our method on a masked CIN dataset for robustness testing and on a specially constructed VCR-MCR dataset for generalization assessment, with results confirming both capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉信息有助于消除指代消解中的歧义，从而带来显著的性能提升。然而，现有的多模态指代消解（MCR）方法在应用前需要使用目标数据集的（部分）标注数据进行训练，这阻碍了它们的直接可用性并引发了泛化方面的担忧。虽然拥有数十亿参数的视觉-语言大模型（VLLM）展现出有前景的零样本能力，但它们仍然在很大程度上难以获取。其庞大的规模限制了可部署性，且许多模型仅能通过付费API访问。本文提出了一种即插即用式适配方法，策略性地将精心预训练的对齐模型适配用于MCR任务，旨在消除对稀缺基准数据集训练或依赖资源密集型VLLM的需求。具体而言，我们首先利用视觉-语言对齐数据集预训练一个文本与视觉上下文信息之间的细粒度对齐模型；然后，通过证据理论融合视觉线索和类别线索进行相似度聚合，将对齐模型重新用于MCR任务，从而提升有效性。在Coreference Image Narratives（CIN）基准数据集上的实验证明了我们方法的有效性：在CoNLL F1指标上，相比专用SOTA方法和流行VLLM分别提升了5.31%和2.12%。我们进一步在掩码CIN数据集上进行了鲁棒性测试，并在专门构建的VCR-MCR数据集上进行了泛化评估，结果证实了在这两方面的能力。

</details>


### [70] [MLLMs Get It Right, Then Get It Wrong: Tracing and Correcting Late-Layer Textual Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17953)
*Xingming Li,Ao Cheng,Qiyao Sun,Xixiang He,Xuanyu Ji,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现多模态大模型在视觉与文本冲突时存在“后期层文本覆盖”现象——模型中间层原本基于视觉信息做出正确预测，但在最终输出层却被文本信息覆盖。作者提出CALRD方法，通过检测并恢复被抑制的正确视觉预测，在无需训练的情况下实现高达9.4%的性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉与文本冲突时倾向于盲从文本而忽略视觉证据，这一问题在需要视觉接地能力的应用中构成风险，但其根本原因尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示该偏差的内部机制并开发相应的矫正方法。

Method: 提出CALRD（Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding），一种无需训练的解码干预方法。通过检测模型推理过程中中间层预测的变化方向（视觉→文本或文本→视觉），识别出被后期层文本覆盖的正确视觉预测，并在检测到置信的视觉预测被抑制时将其恢复。

Result: 在五个不同架构的多模态大模型上，CALRD在冲突基准测试中实现最高9.4%的绝对性能提升，同时基本保持标准性能，无需训练或外部知识。

Conclusion: 模型已经编码了正确的视觉信息，但未能将其保留到最终输出。通过恢复被覆盖的视觉预测，可以有效缓解多模态大模型的文本偏见，且该方法具有训练无关、无需外部知识的优势。

Original Abstract: When vision contradicts text, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) consistently favor text, even when images provide clear evidence otherwise. This bias poses risks for applications requiring visual grounding, yet its cause remains unclear. In this paper, we uncover a surprising finding: models often get it right initially, forming correct vision-based predictions in their intermediate layers, before changing their minds and favoring text in the final output. We call this "late-layer textual override". The visual information is encoded, it simply does not survive to the output. More intriguingly, we find that how predictions change reveals whether they're correct: 85% of failures shift toward text, while 89% of successes shift toward vision. This directional signature enables a simple but powerful intervention: when we detect a confident visual prediction being suppressed, we restore it. We propose CALRD (Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding), a training-free method that recovers overridden predictions at inference time. Experiments across five MLLMs of varying architectures demonstrate up to 9.4% absolute improvements on conflict benchmarks while largely preserving standard performance, without training or external knowledge. It recovers what the model already knew but failed to preserve.

中文摘要翻译: 当视觉信息与文本相矛盾时，多模态大语言模型始终倾向于偏好文本，即使图像提供了清晰的反面证据。这种偏差对需要视觉接地能力的应用构成风险，但其原因尚不明确。本文发现了一个令人惊讶的现象：模型在中间层通常会基于视觉信息形成正确的预测，但在最终输出层却改变判断，转而偏好文本。我们将此称为"后期层文本覆盖"。视觉信息已经被编码，只是未能保留到输出层。更有趣的是，预测的变化方向揭示了其正确性：85%的错误预测向文本方向偏移，而89%的正确预测向视觉方向偏移。这一方向性特征催生了一种简单而有效的干预方法：当检测到置信的视觉预测被抑制时，我们将其恢复。我们提出CALRD（冲突感知层参考解码），一种在推理时恢复被覆盖预测的免训练方法。在五个不同架构的多模态大模型上的实验表明，该方法在冲突基准测试中实现了最高9.4%的绝对性能提升，同时在无需训练或外部知识的情况下基本保持了标准性能。CALRD恢复了模型已知但未能保留的正确信息。

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Visual Cues: CoT-Enhanced Reasoning for Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17958)
*Yuming Chen,Yuxin Xie,Tao Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CERS框架，将Chain-of-Thought推理引入半监督医学图像分割，通过大语言模型生成的推理描述构建知识库，并设计语义感知参考选择策略和多尺度坐标注意力模块，解决了视觉-语义不匹配问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法过度依赖像素级视觉模式匹配，在视觉相似但诊断结论不同的临床场景（视觉-语义不匹配）中表现不佳，无法捕捉专家背后的诊断逻辑。

Method: (1) 利用大语言模型构建包含语言推理描述的知识库；(2) 提出语义感知参考选择策略，先按形态过滤候选，再通过CoT一致性消除困难负样本；(3) 设计多尺度坐标注意力模块（MCAM）将推理上下文融入解码过程。

Result: 在多个实验上优于现有方法，特别是在解决边界模糊和语义不一致方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 超越视觉线索，结合CoT推理能够有效区分病理上不同的病例，提升半监督医学图像分割的语义准确性。

Original Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation has emerged as a dominant research problem in medical image analysis, mitigating annotation scarcity by leveraging consistency regularization on unlabeled data. However, existing approaches operate predominantly via visual pattern matching, relying heavily on pixel-level similarities. This visual-centric dependency often falters in clinical scenarios characterized by the visual-semantic mismatch, where visually similar lesions warrant distinct diagnostic conclusions, thus failing to capture the underlying diagnostic logic used by experts. To address this, we move beyond visual cues and propose CERS (CoT-Enhanced Reasoning Segmentation), a framework that integrates Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to distinguish pathologically distinct cases. Specifically, we construct a knowledge pool enriched with linguistic reasoning descriptions generated by large language models (LLMs). A semantic-aware reference selection strategy is introduced to identify historical evidence, filtering candidates first by morphology, and then refining them via CoT consistency to eliminate hard negatives. Furthermore, a multi-scale coordinate attention module (MCAM) is designed to effectively fuse this reasoning-derived context into the decoding process. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of CERS against state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in resolving boundary ambiguities and semantic inconsistencies. The code is available at https://github.com/cymasuna/CERS.

中文摘要翻译: 半监督医学图像分割已成为医学图像分析中的主流研究问题，它通过对无标注数据利用一致性正则化来缓解标注稀缺问题。然而，现有方法主要通过视觉模式匹配进行操作，严重依赖像素级相似性。这种以视觉为中心的依赖性在存在视觉-语义不匹配的临床场景中常常失效——视觉上相似的病变可能需要不同的诊断结论，从而无法捕捉专家使用的潜在诊断逻辑。为解决这一问题，我们超越视觉线索，提出CERS（CoT增强推理分割）框架，该框架集成链式思维（CoT）推理来区分病理上不同的病例。具体而言，我们构建了一个知识库，其中包含由大语言模型（LLM）生成的语言推理描述。引入了一种语义感知的参考选择策略来识别历史证据，首先按形态过滤候选，然后通过CoT一致性进行细化以消除困难负样本。此外，设计了多尺度坐标注意力模块（MCAM），以将推理得到的上下文有效融合到解码过程中。大量实验表明，CERS相比现有先进方法具有优越性，尤其在解决边界模糊和语义不一致方面。代码已在 https://github.com/cymasuna/CERS 开源。

</details>


### [72] [Robustness of Similarity-based Positional Encoding Under Rotations: Theoretical Analysis and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17961)
*Andrea Santomauro,Luigi Portinale,Giorgio Leonardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验验证，研究了基于相似度的位置编码（simPE）在旋转扰动下的鲁棒性。结果表明simPE并非旋转不变，但在轻微Lipschitz假设下对旋转扰动是稳定的，并给出了Frobenius范数下的显式扰动界。在四个数据集上的实验证实，simPE在旋转场景下优于标准可学习位置编码，尤其在中小角度范围内表现突出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: simPE最初为医学成像设计，在图像采集过程中由于成像仪器、患者体位或采集对齐误差，小角度旋转自然发生。尽管simPE在经验上表现出色，但其在几何扰动下的理论行为尚未被充分刻画，因此本文旨在理论层面系统分析simPE对旋转的鲁棒性。

Method: 首先从理论上证明simPE一般不具备旋转不变性；然后在组件满足Lipschitz连续的温和假设下，证明simPE在旋转扰动下是稳定的，并推导出Frobenius范数下的显式扰动界。最后在四个数据集（合成Arrow、合成Shapes、合成Digits和FashionMNIST）上进行实验验证，训练和验证图像保持固定标准方向，测试图像施加递增旋转角。

Result: 在所有四个数据集上，simPE在准确率、F1分数、精确率和召回率等指标上均一致优于标准可学习位置编码，尤其在中小旋转角度范围内优势显著，证实了理论稳定性保证。

Conclusion: simPE虽非旋转不变，但在旋转扰动下具有理论上的稳定性，实验证明其在实际应用中比标准位置编码更具鲁棒性，特别适用于存在小到中等角度旋转的场景（如医学成像）。

Original Abstract: Positional encoding is a fundamental component of Transformer architectures, as it injects information about the spatial or sequential arrangement of inputs. Among recent alternatives to standard absolute and sinusoidal encodings, similarity-based positional encoding (simPE) has emerged as a flexible framework for representing positional structure through pairwise relations. simPE was originally designed for medical imaging applications, where geometric robustness is especially relevant: small rotations naturally arise during image acquisition, induced by imaging instruments, patient positioning, or slight acquisition misalignments. Despite its empirical promise, the theoretical behavior of simPE under geometric perturbations has not been fully characterized. In this paper, we study the robustness of simPE with respect to rotations, combining formal theoretical analysis with experimental validation. We first show that simPE is generally not rotation-invariant. We then prove that, under mild Lipschitz assumptions on the elementary components, simPE is stable under rotational perturbations and derive explicit perturbation bounds in Frobenius norm. We validate these findings experimentally on four controlled datasets--a synthetic Arrow dataset, a synthetic Shapes dataset (four geometric shape categories), a synthetic Digits dataset, and a benchmark image classification dataset (FashionMNIST)--in which training and validation images are kept in a fixed canonical orientation while test images are subjected to increasing rotation angles. Across all datasets, simPE consistently outperforms standard learned positional encoding in terms of accuracy, F1 score, precision, and recall under rotation, particularly in the small-to-moderate angle regime, corroborating the theoretical stability guarantees.

中文摘要翻译: 位置编码是Transformer架构的基本组件，因为它注入了关于输入空间或序列排列的信息。在标准绝对位置编码和正弦位置编码的最新替代方案中，基于相似度的位置编码（simPE）已成为一种通过成对关系表示位置结构的灵活框架。simPE最初设计用于医学成像应用，在该场景中几何鲁棒性尤为重要：由成像仪器、患者体位或轻微采集对齐误差引起的微小旋转在图像采集过程中自然发生。尽管simPE在经验上展现出潜力，但其在几何扰动下的理论行为尚未被充分刻画。本文结合形式化理论分析与实验验证，研究了simPE对旋转的鲁棒性。我们首先证明simPE通常不具备旋转不变性。然后证明，在基本组件满足温和Lipschitz假设的条件下，simPE在旋转扰动下是稳定的，并推导了Frobenius范数下的显式扰动界。我们在四个受控数据集上实验验证了这些发现——合成Arrow数据集、合成Shapes数据集（四种几何形状类别）、合成Digits数据集以及基准图像分类数据集FashionMNIST——其中训练和验证图像保持固定的标准方向，而测试图像则经受递增的旋转角度。在所有数据集上，simPE在旋转条件下，特别是在小到中等角度范围内，在准确率、F1分数、精确率和召回率方面均一致优于标准的可学习位置编码，这证实了理论稳定性保证。

</details>


### [73] [Reload-Mamba: Hierarchical Anti-Dilution State-Space Modeling for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17966)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Hsin-Jui Pan,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reload-Mamba是一个基于Mamba的状态空间模型语义分割框架，通过边界监督局部细节先验、类别不确定性感知的Reload Gate和分层多级Reload机制，解决序列状态传播导致的分割边界和细节响应稀释问题，在ADE20K、Cityscapes和PASCAL VOC 2012上达到先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于Mamba的状态空间模型虽能实现线性时间的长程建模，但序列化的状态空间传播会削弱对边界和细节敏感的响应，而这对于多类别语义分割任务至关重要。现有反稀释架构是为二值化任务设计的，直接移植到多类别语义分割中效果有限。

Method: (i) 边界监督的局部细节先验：利用真实边界掩码显式训练，识别需要响应恢复的区域；(ii) 类别不确定性感知的Reload Gate：引入预重载辅助头的逐像素类别熵作为额外门控信号，该设计仅对多类别密集预测有效；(iii) 分层多级Reload机制：在三个解码器层级应用抗稀释精炼，并自上而下融合恢复后的表征。整体基于ConvNeXt-Tiny编码器、多尺度解码器和四方向Mamba扫描（含逐像素方向注意力）。

Result: ADE20K上达到47.9%单尺度(48.9%多尺度)mIoU，Cityscapes上达到83.2%单尺度mIoU，PASCAL VOC 2012 val上达到87.8% mIoU(ResNet-101+COCO预训练，标准DeepLab协议)。消融实验表明三个分割专用设计各自贡献显著，累积比直接移植基线提升+2.2 mIoU。

Conclusion: 面向语义分割任务定制的抗响应稀释设计（边界监督、类别不确定性门控、分层多级重载）能有效提升Mamba模型在多类别密集预测任务中的性能，且每个组件均不可或缺。

Original Abstract: Mamba-based state space models offer linear-time long-range modeling for high-resolution dense prediction, but sequential state-space propagation can attenuate boundary-sensitive and detail-sensitive responses that are critical in multi-class semantic segmentation. We propose Reload-Mamba, a semantic segmentation framework that addresses this propagation-induced response dilution through three segmentation-specific designs: (i) a boundary-supervised local detail prior that is explicitly trained with ground-truth boundary masks to identify regions requiring response restoration; (ii) a class-uncertainty-aware Reload Gate that incorporates per-pixel class entropy from a pre-reload auxiliary head as an additional gating signal, a formulation that is informative only under multi-class dense prediction; and (iii) a hierarchical multi-level Reload mechanism that applies anti-dilution refinement at three decoder levels and fuses the restored representations top-down. Built upon a ConvNeXt-Tiny encoder with a multi-scale decoder and four-directional Mamba scanning with pixel-wise directional attention, Reload-Mamba achieves 47.9% single-scale (48.9% multi-scale) mIoU on ADE20K and 83.2% single-scale mIoU on Cityscapes. With ResNet-101 + COCO pre-training under the standard DeepLab-style protocol, Reload-Mamba reaches 87.8% mIoU on PASCAL VOC 2012 val. Controlled ablations show that each of the three segmentation-specific designs contributes beyond a direct port of the prior anti-dilution architecture proposed for binarization, cumulatively improving over the direct-port baseline by +2.2 mIoU on ADE20K.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Mamba的状态空间模型为高分辨率密集预测提供了线性时间的长程建模能力，但序列化的状态空间传播可能会削弱在多类别语义分割中至关重要的边界敏感和细节敏感响应。我们提出了Reload-Mamba，一个语义分割框架，通过三种分割专用设计来解决这种传播引起的响应稀释问题：(i) 边界监督的局部细节先验，利用真实边界掩码显式训练，以识别需要响应恢复的区域；(ii) 类别不确定性感知的Reload Gate，将来自预重载辅助头的逐像素类别熵作为额外的门控信号引入，这一设计仅对多类别密集预测具有信息量；以及(iii) 分层多级Reload机制，在三个解码器层级应用抗稀释精炼并自上而下融合恢复后的表征。基于ConvNeXt-Tiny编码器、多尺度解码器和带逐像素方向注意力的四方向Mamba扫描，Reload-Mamba在ADE20K上达到47.9%单尺度（48.9%多尺度）mIoU，在Cityscapes上达到83.2%单尺度mIoU。采用ResNet-101 + COCO预训练，在标准DeepLab风格的协议下，Reload-Mamba在PASCAL VOC 2012 val上达到87.8% mIoU。控制消融实验表明，三种分割专用设计各自贡献显著，超越了直接移植先前为二值化提出的抗稀释架构，在ADE20K上相对于直接移植基线累积提升了+2.2 mIoU。

</details>


### [74] [SegDINO: Introducing Multi-Scale Structure into DINO for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17972)
*Sicheng Yang,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Sixiang Chen,Qiuxia Yang,Yize Mao,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegDINO 提出了一种高效分割框架，通过在 DINOv3 骨干中引入轻量级尺度建模（Token Pyramid Adaptation 和 Scale-Aware Decoding），在不依赖重型解码器的情况下实现 SOTA 分割性能，并在新构建的胰腺肿瘤 CT 数据集 PanCT 及三个公开基准上验证了其高效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的 DINO 模型应用于图像分割时，通常依赖带有复杂上采样的重型解码器，引入了大量参数和计算开销。作者观察到在 DINO 特征中引入尺度信息比增加解码器容量更为关键，因此希望设计一种轻量级的高效分割框架。

Method: SegDINO 包含两个核心模块：(1) Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA)，将 DINO 中间特征重组织为伪多尺度层次结构；(2) Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD)，实现高效的尺度内精炼和自上而下的多尺度传播。此外，作者还构建了一个包含 284 名患者、带有专家标注的胰腺肿瘤 CT 数据集 PanCT。

Result: 在 PanCT 和三个公开基准上的大量实验表明，SegDINO 以高计算效率取得了 state-of-the-art 的分割结果。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的尺度建模（而非增加解码器容量）即可显著提升 DINO 模型在图像分割任务上的性能，SegDINO 为高效分割提供了一种新的思路。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised DINO models provide strong transferable visual representations, yet applying them directly to image segmentation remains challenging. Existing approaches commonly rely on heavy decoders with complex upsampling, introducing substantial parameter and computational overhead. We observe that introducing scale into DINO features is far more critical than increasing decoder capacity. In this work, we present SegDINO, an efficient segmentation framework that integrates a DINOv3 backbone with lightweight scale modeling. SegDINO introduces Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) to reorganize intermediate DINO features into a pseudo multi-scale hierarchy, and Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) for efficient intra-scale refinement and top-down multi-scale propagation. We further curate PanCT, a new CT dataset containing 284 patients with expert-annotated pancreatic tumors, to assess SegDINO's ability to handle difficult small-lesion cases. Extensive experiments on PanCT and three public benchmarks demonstrate that SegDINO achieves state-of-the-art results with high efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/segdino_v2.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督 DINO 模型提供了强大的可迁移视觉表征，但直接将其应用于图像分割仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通常依赖带有复杂上采样机制的重型解码器，引入了大量参数和计算开销。我们观察到，在 DINO 特征中引入尺度信息远比增加解码器容量更为关键。本文提出了 SegDINO，一种将 DINOv3 骨干网络与轻量级尺度建模相结合的高效分割框架。SegDINO 引入 Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) 将中间 DINO 特征重组织为伪多尺度层次结构，并提出 Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) 实现高效的尺度内精炼及自上而下的多尺度传播。我们进一步整理了 PanCT，一个包含 284 名患者及专家标注胰腺肿瘤的新 CT 数据集，用于评估 SegDINO 处理困难小病灶病例的能力。在 PanCT 和三个公开基准上的大量实验表明，SegDINO 以高计算效率实现了最先进的分割结果。代码已开源：https://github.com/script-Yang/segdino_v2。

</details>


### [75] [Gaussian Light Field Splatting: A Physical Prior-Driven Vision Transformer for Unsupervised Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17985)
*Yuhan Chen,Wenxuan Yu,Guofa Li,Fuchen Li,Kunyang Huang,Yicui Shi,Ying Fang,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯光场抛雪花的视觉Transformer（GLFS），将连续物理光照建模引入Transformer，通过各向异性高斯基函数表示光照、自注意力中引入物理偏置生成空间增益场，并设计色矢量角损失和亮度边缘损失，实现非均匀光照下的低光图像增强，达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督低光增强方法在复杂非均匀光照下存在局部曝光不平衡和色彩失真；多数视觉Transformer缺乏对光照退化的物理先验的显式建模机制。

Method: 提出GLFS框架：1) 用各向异性高斯基函数的叠加表示场景光照；2) 在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置，自适应推断空间增益场；3) 设计色矢量角损失（保持色调一致性）和亮度边缘损失（提升局部细节结构保真度）。

Result: 大量消融研究和定量评估表明，GLFS在光照校正和细节保持上具有明显优势，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GLFS为低光图像增强提供了新的表示范式，在复杂照明条件下实现精确且均匀的恢复。

Original Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement methods often encounter local exposure imbalance and color distortion under complex non-uniform illumination. In addition, most Vision Transformers lack an explicit mechanism for modeling the physical priors of illumination degradation. To address these limitations, we propose GLFS, a Gaussian light field splatting-based Vision Transformer that integrates continuous physical illumination modeling from Gaussian splatting into the Transformer architecture. In GLFS, scene illumination is represented by a superposition of anisotropic Gaussian basis functions. Physics-guided biases are introduced into self-attention to adaptively infer a spatial gain field, enabling accurate and uniform restoration under complex illumination. To reduce color bias and structural degradation during enhancement, a color-vector angular loss and a luminance-edge loss are further developed. These losses enforce hue consistency and improve the structural fidelity of local details. Extensive ablation studies and quantitative evaluations show that GLFS provides clear advantages in illumination correction and detail preservation. It achieves state-of-the-art performance and offers a new representation paradigm for low-light image enhancement.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的无监督低光图像增强方法在复杂的非均匀光照下常常遇到局部曝光不平衡和色彩失真问题。此外，大多数视觉Transformer缺乏对光照退化的物理先验的显式建模机制。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了GLFS——一种基于高斯光场抛雪花的视觉Transformer，它将高斯抛雪花中的连续物理光照建模集成到Transformer架构中。在GLFS中，场景光照通过各向异性高斯基函数的叠加来表示。在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置，以自适应地推断空间增益场，从而在复杂光照下实现精确且均匀的恢复。为了减少增强过程中的色彩偏差和结构退化，进一步开发了色矢量角损失和亮度边缘损失。这些损失函数强制执行色调一致性并提升局部细节的结构保真度。大量的消融研究和定量评估表明，GLFS在光照校正和细节保持方面具有明显优势。它实现了最先进的性能，并为低光图像增强提供了一种新的表示范式。

</details>


### [76] [Recover Semantics First, Generate Better: Improved Latent Modeling for 3D MRI Reconstruction and Cross-Contrast Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17989)
*Yonghao Chen,Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义优先的隐空间建模框架，通过潜在协调编码器（LHE）、语义恢复模块（SRB）和解剖感知频率损失（AFL），解决了3D MRI跨对比度合成中长程解剖连贯性丢失、语义退化及过度平滑重建的问题，在两个公开数据集上取得了一致改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D MRI压缩架构存在三个关键缺陷：未能保持长程解剖连贯性、丢弃临床相关语义信息、使用导致重建过度平滑的优化目标，这些问题损害了下游生成模型的性能。

Method: 提出语义优先隐空间建模框架，包含三个组件：1）潜在协调编码器（LHE）捕获全局解剖依赖，确保体素表示连贯性；2）语义恢复模块（SRB）从自监督语义教师注入高层先验，增强隐空间中对比度感知的可分性；3）解剖感知频率损失（AFL）自适应保留诊断相关的高频结构。

Result: 在两个公开多对比度MRI数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 提出的语义优先隐空间建模框架有效解决了3D MRI压缩中的语义退化问题，提升了跨对比度合成效果。

Original Abstract: Multi-contrast magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provides complementary information for clinical diagnosis. However, acquiring all MRI sequences is often time-consuming and costly. Recent generative models perform cross-contrast synthesis to address this issue by inferring absent contrasts from the available ones. Nevertheless, synthesizing 3D MRI presents significant challenges. Due to the massive volume sizes, operating directly in the pixel space is computationally prohibitive; therefore, a common approach is to first compress the 3D volumes into a latent space and subsequently train generative models in that space. We observe that existing compression architectures face several critical issues: they under-preserve long-range anatomical coherence, discard clinically meaningful semantics, and rely on optimization objectives that lead to over-smoothed reconstructions. Ultimately, these shortcomings compromise the performance of subsequent generative models. In this work, we propose a semantics-first latent modeling framework for 3D MRI reconstruction and cross-contrast synthesis. Specifically, we introduce a Latent Harmonization Encoder (LHE) to capture global anatomical dependencies, ensuring coherent volumetric representations. To mitigate semantic degradation during latent compression, we further design a Semantic Recovery Block (SRB) that injects high-level priors from a self-supervised semantic teacher, enhancing contrast-aware separability in the latent space. Additionally, we propose an Anatomy-aware Frequency Loss (AFL) to adaptively preserve diagnostically relevant high-frequency structures. Extensive experiments on two public multi-contrast MRI datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in reconstruction fidelity and cross-contrast synthesis quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/RSF.

中文摘要翻译: 多对比度磁共振成像（MRI）为临床诊断提供互补信息。然而，采集所有MRI序列通常耗时且成本高昂。最近的生成模型通过从可用对比度推断缺失对比度来进行跨对比度合成以解决这一问题。尽管如此，合成3D MRI仍面临重大挑战。由于体数据规模庞大，直接在像素空间操作在计算上是不可行的；因此，常见方法是先将3D体数据压缩至隐空间，然后在该空间中训练生成模型。我们观察到现有压缩架构存在若干关键问题：它们未能充分保持长程解剖连贯性，丢弃了具有临床意义的语义信息，并且依赖导致重建过度平滑的优化目标。最终，这些缺陷损害了下游生成模型的性能。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种语义优先的隐空间建模框架，用于3D MRI重建和跨对比度合成。具体而言，我们引入了一个潜在协调编码器（LHE）来捕获全局解剖依赖关系，确保体素表示具有连贯性。为了减轻隐空间压缩过程中的语义退化，我们进一步设计了一个语义恢复模块（SRB），该模块从自监督语义教师中注入高层先验，增强隐空间中对比度感知的可分性。此外，我们提出了一种解剖感知频率损失（AFL），以自适应地保留诊断相关的高频结构。在两个公开多对比度MRI数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量上均取得一致改进。我们的代码可在https://github.com/script-Yang/RSF获取。

</details>


### [77] [AIGS-Net: Compact Illumination Field Modeling via 2D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17998)
*Yuhan Chen,Kunyang Huang,Fuchen Li,Zhuohan Qin,Guofa Li,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种超轻量级自适应照明高斯泼溅网络（AIGS-Net），通过输入自适应的2D高斯泼溅照明场和零参数多尺度上下文编码模块，以约40个可学习参数实现了低光照图像增强中质量与效率的有效平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有低光照图像增强方法在照明场建模的表征能力与计算复杂度之间存在瓶颈，亟需一种既轻量又高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出AIGS-Net，包含：(1) 输入自适应的2D高斯泼溅照明场，通过相对亮度统计动态调制高斯基函数的不透明度，并采用有序阿尔法混合渲染空间变化的照明补偿；(2) 零参数非线性多尺度上下文编码模块，无需额外卷积权重即可提取低频结构和局部对比度线索；(3) 噪声掩膜估计、锁定单通道Gamma映射、跨通道一致性正则化和目标颜色对齐约束，以抑制噪声放大和颜色偏差。

Result: 在LOL和LSRW基准上，AIGS-Net在仅需约40个可学习参数的情况下，提升了细节恢复和颜色保真度，在增强质量与极端推理效率之间实现了有效权衡。

Conclusion: AIGS-Net通过极简的参数设计和高效的照明场建模，成功解决了低光照增强中质量与效率的矛盾，验证了超轻量架构在该任务中的可行性。

Original Abstract: Existing low-light image enhancement methods often face a bottleneck between the representation capacity of illumination-field modeling and computational complexity. To address this issue, this paper proposes an Adaptive Illumination Gaussian Splatting Network (AIGS-Net), an ultra-lightweight architecture for fast low-light enhancement. Unlike conventional static priors, AIGS-Net constructs an input-adaptive 2D Gaussian Splatting illumination field. The opacity of Gaussian basis functions is dynamically modulated by relative luminance statistics of the input image, and spatially varying illumination compensation is rendered through ordered alpha compositing. To guide adaptive illumination compensation efficiently, a zero-parameter nonlinear multiscale contextual encoding module is introduced to extract low-frequency structures and local contrast cues without additional convolutional weights. To suppress noise amplification and sensor-induced color bias, AIGS-Net integrates noise-mask estimation, locked single-channel Gamma mapping, cross-channel consistency regularization, and target color-alignment constraints. Experiments on LOL and LSRW benchmarks show that AIGS-Net improves detail recovery and color fidelity while requiring only approximately 40 learnable parameters, achieving an effective trade-off between enhancement quality and extreme inference efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的低光照图像增强方法常常在照明场建模的表征能力与计算复杂度之间面临瓶颈。为解决这一问题，本文提出了一种自适应照明高斯泼溅网络（AIGS-Net），这是一种用于快速低光照增强的超轻量级架构。与传统的静态先验不同，AIGS-Net构建了一个输入自适应的2D高斯泼溅照明场。高斯基函数的不透明度由输入图像的相对亮度统计动态调制，并通过有序阿尔法混合渲染空间变化的照明补偿。为了高效地指导自适应照明补偿，引入了一个零参数非线性多尺度上下文编码模块，该模块无需额外卷积权重即可提取低频结构和局部对比度线索。为了抑制噪声放大和传感器引起的颜色偏差，AIGS-Net集成了噪声掩膜估计、锁定单通道Gamma映射、跨通道一致性正则化和目标颜色对齐约束。在LOL和LSRW基准上的实验表明，AIGS-Net在仅需约40个可学习参数的情况下，提升了细节恢复和颜色保真度，在增强质量与极端推理效率之间实现了有效权衡。

</details>


### [78] [PhaseWin: An Efficient Search Algorithm for Faithful Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18008)
*Zihan Gu,Ruoyu Chen,Junchi Zhang,Li Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseWin 提出了一种分阶段窗口搜索算法，将视觉归因中的贪心子集搜索从 O(n²) 复杂度降为 O(n)，同时保持接近贪心方法的忠实度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的忠实视觉归因方法将问题建模为有序子集搜索，但穷举搜索成本指数级，贪心搜索仍需二次数量的模型评估（每步重评所有剩余候选区域），计算效率低下。

Method: PhaseWin 将贪心区域选择重组为分阶段窗口搜索过程：交替进行全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口精炼，避免了每步重新评估全部候选集，同时保留了贪心搜索的核心区域排序行为。在单调证据累积条件下，利用特征级结构假设实现受控的线性评估复杂度。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述等任务上的大量实验表明，PhaseWin 在所有对比方法中以最少的前向传播次数达到最高的忠实度，经验上实现了从 O(n²) 到 O(n) 的复杂度降阶。

Conclusion: PhaseWin 通过结构性假设和分阶段搜索策略，在保持接近贪心方法忠实度的前提下，将视觉归因的计算复杂度从二次降至线性，为大规模模型的可解释性提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Visual attribution is a fundamental tool for interpreting modern vision and vision-language models, particularly when their decisions must be inspected, diagnosed, or audited. Its goal is to explain how a model's decision depends on local regions of the visual input, typically by assigning an importance ordering over candidate image regions. Given an image partitioned into $n$ regions, faithful attribution can be cast as an ordered subset-search problem, in which progressively inserting the selected regions should recover the target model response as early as possible. Exhaustive search over region subsets incurs exponential cost, while the widely used greedy search still requires a quadratic number of model evaluations, because every selection step rescores all remaining candidates. We propose PhaseWin, an efficient subset-search algorithm for faithful visual attribution. PhaseWin reorganizes greedy region selection into a phased window-search procedure: rather than re-evaluating the full candidate set at every step, it alternates between global candidate screening, adaptive pruning, and localized window refinement, while preserving the essential region-ranking behavior of greedy search. We analyze PhaseWin under monotone evidence-accumulation conditions and show that, under feature-level structural assumptions, it attains controllable linear evaluation complexity together with near-greedy faithfulness guarantees. Extensive experiments on image classification, object detection, visual grounding, and image captioning show that, among all compared attribution methods, PhaseWin reaches high faithfulness with the fewest forward passes, empirically realizing the predicted reduction from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The code is available at https://github.com/Qihuai27/phasewin-va.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉归因是解释现代视觉和视觉-语言模型的基本工具，尤其在需要检查、诊断或审计模型决策时。其目标是解释模型决策如何依赖于视觉输入的局部区域，通常通过为候选图像区域分配重要性排序来实现。给定一张被划分为 n 个区域的图像，忠实归因可被建模为一个有序子集搜索问题，其中逐步插入所选区域应尽可能早地恢复目标模型响应。对区域子集进行穷举搜索会产生指数级成本，而广泛使用的贪心搜索仍需要二次数量的模型评估，因为每次选择步骤都需要重新评分所有剩余候选区域。我们提出 PhaseWin，一种用于忠实视觉归因的高效子集搜索算法。PhaseWin 将贪心区域选择重组为分阶段窗口搜索过程：它并非在每一步重新评估完整候选集，而是在全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口精炼之间交替进行，同时保留贪心搜索的核心区域排序行为。我们在单调证据累积条件下分析 PhaseWin，并证明在特征级结构假设下，它能够实现可控的线性评估复杂度，同时具有接近贪心方法的忠实度保证。在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述上的大量实验表明，在所有对比的归因方法中，PhaseWin 以最少的前向传播次数达到最高的忠实度，经验上实现了从 O(n²) 到 O(n) 的复杂度降阶。代码已开源：https://github.com/Qihuai27/phasewin-va。

</details>


### [79] [When LLMs Analyze Scars: From Images to Clinically-Meaningful Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18063)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaFE框架，利用大语言模型作为知识驱动的特征工程师，通过生成特征提取代码将医学图像转化为低维可解释表示，在数据稀缺的病理性疤痕分类任务中优于端到端深度学习基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像分类面临数据稀缺的根本困境（标注成本高、隐私限制、疾病罕见），尤其在病理性疤痕分类中，区分瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕需要专家知识且标注图像极少，亟需一种数据高效且可解释的解决方案。

Method: 提出ScaFE框架：将LLM重新定位为知识驱动的特征工程师，通过向LLM输入既定的疤痕评估标准（如温哥华疤痕量表），使其生成确定性的Python特征提取代码，将高维图像转化为与临床评分系统对齐的低维可解释特征表示，再基于这些特征训练轻量级分类器。

Result: 在疤痕分类任务上，该方法在有限数据条件下持续优于端到端深度学习基线以及将LLM作为黑盒分类器的方案，同时实现了数据高效性、隐私保护和临床可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为将LLM集成到数据高效且临床透明的医学AI系统中提供了有前景的方向，通过将知识获取与统计学习解耦，突破了小样本场景下传统深度学习范式的局限。

Original Abstract: Medical image classification faces a fundamental dilemma: while deep learning models achieve remarkable performance at scale, real-world clinical scenarios often suffer from severe data scarcity due to annotation costs, privacy constraints, and disease rarity. This challenge is particularly pronounced in pathological scar classification, where differentiating keloids from hypertrophic scars requires subtle expert knowledge and labeled images are extremely limited. We propose a novel paradigm that repositions large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven feature engineers rather than end-to-end classifiers. We call this framework ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering). Our key insight is that LLMs encode rich medical knowledge that can be externalized as executable feature extraction code, enabling the transformation of high-dimensional images into low-dimensional, clinically interpretable representations. Specifically, we prompt an LLM with established scar assessment criteria to generate deterministic Python code that extracts features aligned with clinical scoring systems such as the Vancouver Scar Scale. Our approach offers three key advantages: (1) data efficiency, achieving robust performance with limited training samples by decoupling knowledge acquisition from statistical learning; (2) privacy preservation, as raw images are processed locally without exposure to external LLMs; and (3) interpretability, through explicit features grounded in clinical reasoning. Extensive experiments on scar classification demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms end-to-end deep learning baselines or using LLMs as black-box classifiers under limited data conditions, establishing a promising direction for integrating LLMs into data-efficient and clinically transparent medical AI systems.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像分类面临一个根本困境：虽然深度学习模型在大规模数据下表现卓越，但在真实临床场景中，由于标注成本、隐私约束和疾病罕见性，数据严重稀缺的问题普遍存在。这一挑战在病理性疤痕分类中尤为突出，因为区分瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕需要微妙的专家知识，且标注图像极为有限。我们提出了一种新范式，将大语言模型重新定位为知识驱动的特征工程师，而非端到端的分类器。我们将该框架命名为ScaFE（疤痕特征工程）。我们的核心洞察在于，LLM编码了丰富的医学知识，这些知识可以外化为可执行的特征提取代码，从而将高维图像转化为低维、临床可解释的表示。具体而言，我们利用既定的疤痕评估标准提示LLM，使其生成确定性的Python代码，用于提取与临床评分系统（如温哥华疤痕量表）对齐的特征。我们的方法具有三个关键优势：(1) 数据高效性——通过将知识获取与统计学习解耦，在有限的训练样本下仍能实现稳健性能；(2) 隐私保护——原始图像在本地处理，无需暴露给外部LLM；(3) 可解释性——通过植根于临床推理的显式特征实现。在疤痕分类上的大量实验表明，在有限数据条件下，我们的方法持续优于端到端深度学习基线以及将LLM作为黑盒分类器的方案，为将LLM集成到数据高效且临床透明的医学AI系统中开辟了有前景的方向。

</details>


### [80] [HLS-GPT: A Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) for Continental-Scale NASA Harmonized Landsat and Sentinel-2 (HLS) Reflectance Reconstruction Across All Bands on Arbitrary Dates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18115)
*Junjie Li,Hankui K. Zhang,David P. Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HLS-GPT，一种大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2（HLS）30米地表反射率数据，覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置。该模型采用层次化Transformer架构处理不同光谱波段配置，基于单像素12个月时间序列，在超过25万训练像素上训练9年数据，在多个评估指标上优于传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建方面存在局限性：光谱覆盖受限、地理可扩展性有限、或采用短时间上下文的基于块的设计。作者旨在解决这些问题，构建一个可覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置的大规模重建模型。

Method: HLS-GPT采用层次化Transformer架构处理Landsat和Sentinel-2的不同光谱波段配置，基于单像素12个月时间序列。模型使用美国本土超过25万像素的9年HLS时间序列进行训练，采用随机裁剪和掩码策略：提取不同起始日期的12个月周期，掩码50%的有效观测，训练模型从剩余观测中重建掩码的反射率值。

Result: 使用超过6.2万独立测试像素进行评估，所有HLS光谱波段的RMSE低于0.026，可见光波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%。红边波段误差与红波段和近红外波段相当。敏感性分析中，掩码10%-90%观测时，10%-50%掩码率下全波段RMSE低于0.028。在9个独立109×109 km的CONUS HLS图块上，HLS-GPT优于两种传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。

Conclusion: HLS-GPT在复杂地表条件（包括复杂的作物物候和稀疏不规则观测）下实现了稳健的重建性能，验证了大模型在遥感时间序列重建中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Recent deep learning methods for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance time series reconstruction remain limited by restricted spectral coverage, limited geographic scalability, or patch-based designs with short temporal contexts. We present HLS-GPT, a large-scale generative pretrained Transformer model for reconstructing NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30 m surface reflectance for all bands, any date, and any pixel location. HLS-GPT uses a hierarchical Transformer architecture to handle the different spectral band configurations of Landsat and Sentinel-2 and operates on single-pixel 12-month time series. To capture geographic and seasonal variability, the model was trained with nine years of HLS time series from more than 0.25 million training pixels across the conterminous United States. A random cropping and masking strategy extracts 12-month periods with varying start dates across epochs, masks 50% of valid observations, and trains the model to reconstruct the masked reflectance values from the remaining observations. Evaluation using more than 62,000 independent test pixels shows robust reconstruction under diverse land surface conditions, including complex crop phenology and sparse, irregular observations. Leave-one-observation-out evaluation achieved reconstruction RMSE below 0.026 for all HLS spectral bands, with relative RMSE below 35% for visible bands and below 13% for other bands. Red-edge band errors were comparable to red and near-infrared errors despite the absence of red-edge bands on Landsat. Sensitivity analyses that randomly masked 10% to 90% of test observations showed only modest degradation when 10% to 50% of observations were masked, with all-band RMSE below 0.028. Image reconstruction over nine independent 109 by 109 km CONUS HLS tiles further demonstrates that HLS-GPT outperforms two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的深度学习方法在Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建方面仍然存在局限性，包括光谱覆盖受限、地理可扩展性有限，或采用基于图像块的设计且时间上下文较短。我们提出HLS-GPT，一种大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2（HLS）30米地表反射率数据，覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置。HLS-GPT采用层次化Transformer架构处理Landsat和Sentinel-2的不同光谱波段配置，并基于单像素12个月时间序列运行。为捕捉地理和季节性变化，模型使用美国本土超过25万个训练像素的九年HLS时间序列进行训练。采用随机裁剪和掩码策略，提取具有不同起始日期的12个月周期，掩码50%的有效观测，训练模型从剩余观测中重建被掩码的反射率值。使用超过6.2万个独立测试像素进行的评估表明，模型在多种地表条件下（包括复杂的作物物候和稀疏、不规则的观测）实现了稳健的重建。留一观测评估中，所有HLS光谱波段的重建RMSE低于0.026，可见光波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%。尽管Landsat上缺乏红边波段，红边波段误差与红波段和近红外波段误差相当。对10%至90%的测试观测进行随机掩码的敏感性分析表明，当掩码10%至50%的观测时，性能仅适度下降，全波段RMSE低于0.028。在九个独立的109×109 km美国本土HLS图块上的图像重建进一步表明，HLS-GTR优于两种传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。

</details>


### [81] [Predicting Immune Biomarkers with MultiModal Mixture-of-Expert Pathology Foundation Models Empowers Precision Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18123)
*Tianyu Liu,Ziqing Wang,Zhaokang Liang,Tong Ding,Peter Humphrey,Lorraine Colón-Cartagena,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Kenneth Tou En Chang,Mohamed Kahila,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Tinglin Huang,Rex Ying,Kaize Ding,Faisal Mahmood,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTIME是一种多模态基础模型，采用混合专家（MoE）架构整合三种组织病理学基础模型（图像、图像-文本、图像-转录组），从HE全切片图像中预测多重免疫荧光蛋白表达，在17个蛋白标记物上达到SOTA性能，并提升了空间域识别、生存预测和病理报告生成等下游任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有预测肿瘤免疫微环境生物标志物的方法受限于单一图像模态、分辨率不足以及未能充分利用互补的临床与生物学信息，亟需一个多模态融合框架来提升预测性能。

Method: MixTIME采用混合专家（MoE）架构，整合三种不同模态训练的组织病理学基础模型：纯图像模型（UNIv2）、图像-文本模型（CONCHv1.5）和图像-转录组模型（STPath），通过可学习路由器动态加权各专家贡献，并使用分布感知和趋势感知损失函数进行训练，实现从HE全切片图像到像素级和切片级多重免疫荧光蛋白表达的预测。

Result: 在两个不同规模的数据集上，MixTIME在17个蛋白标记物上达到SOTA性能；预测的mIF谱显著提升了空间域识别、生存预测和AI辅助病理报告生成等下游任务；实现了跨临床时间点的蛋白质表达动态纵向追踪，揭示了与耐药性和免疫抑制相关的蛋白质-基因相互作用模式。

Conclusion: MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现和临床转化提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Original Abstract: Predicting immune biomarkers associated with the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) is critical for advancing precision oncology, yet existing approaches are largely limited to single image modalities and suffer from insufficient resolution and incomplete utilization of complementary clinical and biological information. Here we introduce MixTIME, a multimodal foundation model that leverages a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture to integrate pathology foundation models trained across distinct modalities: image only (UNIv2), image text (CONCHv1.5), and image transcriptomic (STPath) representations for pixel-level and slide-level prediction of multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) protein expression from hematoxylin and eosin (HE) whole-slide images. MixTIME employs a learnable router to dynamically weight expert contributions and is trained with a distribution- and tendency-aware loss function. Benchmarked on two datasets of different scales, MixTIME achieves state-of-the-art performance across 17 protein markers as measured by correlation metrics. The predicted mIF profiles substantially enhance downstream tasks, including spatial domain identification, survival prediction, and AI-assisted pathology report generation validated by expert pathologists from multiple institutes across the world. Furthermore, MixTIME enables longitudinal tracking of protein expression dynamics across clinical time points and reveals protein gene interaction patterns linked to drug resistance and immune suppression in tumor microenvironments. Collectively, MixTIME provides a scalable framework for multimodal biomarker discovery and clinical translation in computational pathology.

中文摘要翻译: 预测与肿瘤免疫微环境相关的免疫生物标志物对于推进精准肿瘤学至关重要，然而现有方法主要局限于单图像模态，存在分辨率不足以及未能充分利用互补的临床和生物学信息的问题。本文提出MixTIME，一个多模态基础模型，采用混合专家架构整合了在不同模态上训练的组织病理学基础模型：纯图像（UNIv2）、图像-文本（CONCHv1.5）和图像-转录组（STPath）表示，用于从苏木精-伊红全切片图像进行像素级和切片级的多重免疫荧光蛋白表达预测。MixTIME使用可学习路由器动态加权专家贡献，并通过分布感知和趋势感知损失函数进行训练。在两个不同规模的数据集上进行基准测试，MixTIME在17个蛋白标记物上达到了基于相关性指标评估的最优性能。预测的mIF谱显著增强了包括空间域识别、生存预测以及由全球多家机构的病理学专家验证的AI辅助病理报告生成等下游任务。此外，MixTIME实现了跨临床时间点的蛋白质表达动态纵向追踪，并揭示了与肿瘤微环境中耐药性和免疫抑制相关的蛋白质-基因相互作用模式。总之，MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现和临床转化提供了一个可扩展的框架。

</details>


### [82] [Neural Tree Reconstruction for the Open Forest Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18153)
*Marissa Ramirez de Chanlatte,Arjun Rewari,Trevor Darrell,Derek J. N. Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了开放森林观测站（OFO）项目，旨在通过低成本无人机和开源工具实现森林测绘，并探讨了将神经辐射场（NeRF）等先进3D重建技术引入现有数据集以提升质量，从而更好地支持气候应用（如再造林、野火减灾、碳封存监测）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前OFO森林地图数据库采用传统的运动恢复结构（SfM）技术生成3D树图，该方法易产生伪影、细节不足，尤其对林下区域重建困难（因冠层遮挡），且这些误差会传播到下游科学任务（如野火模拟）。因此需要引入更先进的3D重建方法。

Method: 探索将神经辐射场（NeRF）整合到OFO数据集中，利用NeRF对稀疏视角更鲁棒、支持数据驱动先验的优势来提升3D重建质量，并规划未来集成更先进的3D视觉模型。

Result: NeRF相比经典SfM方法能生成更高质量、更少伪影的3D重建结果，对稀疏视角更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 高质量的3D重建对于林业应用至关重要，将NeRF等先进技术纳入OFO数据库可以显著提升森林测绘数据的质量，从而更好地服务于再造林、野火减灾和碳封存监测等气候应用。

Original Abstract: The Open Forest Observatory (OFO) is a collaboration across universities and other partners to make low-cost forest mapping accessible to ecologists, land managers, and the general public. The OFO is building both a database of geospatial forest data as well as open-source methods and tools for forest mapping by uncrewed aerial vehicle. Such data are useful for a variety of climate applications including prioritizing reforestation efforts, informing wildfire hazard reduction, and monitoring carbon sequestration. In the current iteration of the OFO's forest map database, 3D tree maps are created using classical structure-from-motion techniques. This approach is prone to artifacts, lacks detail, and has particular difficulty on the forest floor where the input data (overhead imagery) has limited visibility. These reconstruction errors can potentially propagate to the downstream scientific tasks (e.g. a wildfire simulation.) Advances in 3D reconstruction, including methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), produce higher quality results that are more robust to sparse views and support data-driven priors. We explore ways to incorporate NeRFs into the OFO dataset, outline future work to support even more state-of-the-art 3D vision models, and describe the importance of high-quality 3D reconstructions for forestry applications.

中文摘要翻译: 开放森林观测站（OFO）是高校及其他合作伙伴之间的合作项目，旨在让生态学家、土地管理者及公众能够以低成本进行森林测绘。OFO正在构建一个地理空间森林数据库，同时开发基于无人机的开源森林测绘方法和工具。这些数据可用于多种气候应用，包括优化再造林工作、辅助野火减灾以及监测碳封存。在当前的OFO森林地图数据库迭代中，3D树图是使用经典的运动恢复结构（SfM）技术创建的。这种方法容易产生伪影、缺乏细节，并且在输入数据（冠层影像）可见性有限的林下区域尤为困难。这些重建误差可能传播到下游的科学任务（例如野火模拟）。3D重建领域的进展，包括神经辐射场（NeRF）等方法，能够生成更高质量的结果，对稀疏视角更鲁棒，并支持数据驱动的先验知识。我们探索了将NeRF整合到OFO数据集中的方式，概述了支持更先进3D视觉模型的未来工作，并阐述了高质量3D重建对于林业应用的重要性。

</details>


### [83] [ReAge3D: Re-Aging 3D Faces with View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18156)
*Libing Zeng,Li Ma,Mingming He,Ning Yu,Paul Debevec,Nima Khademi Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的3D人脸年龄变换框架，通过中心向外编辑传播策略和掩码扩散重龄化过程，实现多视图一致的高保真3D人脸重龄化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法适用于粗粒度语义变化，但难以处理重龄化任务——2D视图间的不一致性会导致与年龄相关的细微但感知重要的细节被过度平滑。

Method: 首先在合成图像对训练2D扩散重龄化模型DiffReaging；提出中心向外编辑传播策略：从重龄化的正面枢轴视图开始，通过扭曲和掩码扩散重龄化（Masked-DiffReaging）重建其余视图，确保重构区域与现有点保持连贯；最终用一致的多视图重龄化图像监督3D表示优化。

Result: 在视觉和定量指标上均优于现有3D编辑技术，实现了对3D人脸模型中年龄变换的平滑、细粒度控制。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够生成高保真、身份保留的3D人脸重龄化效果，有效解决了多视图一致性和细节保留的挑战。

Original Abstract: We present a novel framework for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging which produces highly detailed, identity-preserving results. Existing 3D editing methods, while effective for coarse semantic changes, are not well suited for re-aging, as even small inconsistencies across re-aged 2D views can lead to over-smoothing of subtle but perceptually important age-related details. To address this challenge, we first introduce a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model, DiffReaging, trained on synthetically generated image pairs. We further propose a center-out editing propagation strategy that leverages this re-aging model to reconstruct multi-view-consistent re-aged images. Specifically, starting from a re-aged frontal pivot view, we reconstruct the remaining views through warping and our proposed Masked-DiffReaging process. By injecting existing content at every step of the diffusion process, Masked-DiffReaging ensures that the reconstructed regions remain coherent with existing pixels. The resulting consistent set of re-aged views supervises the optimization of the re-aged 3D representation. Our method outperforms existing 3D editing techniques both visually and quantitatively, enabling smooth, fine-grained control over age transformations in 3D face models.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的框架，用于实现逼真且可控的3D人脸重龄化，能够生成高度细致且保留身份特征的结果。现有的3D编辑方法虽然对粗粒度语义变化有效，但并不适用于重龄化任务，因为重龄化后的2D视图中即使存在微小不一致，也会导致对细微但感知上重要的年龄相关细节产生过度平滑。为应对这一挑战，我们首先引入了一个基于2D扩散的重龄化模型DiffReaging，该模型在合成生成的图像对上训练。我们进一步提出了一种中心向外编辑传播策略，利用该重龄化模型重建多视图一致的重龄化图像。具体而言，从一个重龄化的正面枢轴视图开始，我们通过扭曲和提出的掩码扩散重龄化（Masked-DiffReaging）过程重建其余视图。通过在扩散过程的每一步注入现有内容，Masked-DiffReaging确保重建区域与现有像素保持连贯。由此得到的一致重龄化视图集用于监督重龄化后的3D表示优化。我们的方法在视觉和定量指标上均优于现有的3D编辑技术，能够对3D人脸模型中的年龄变换实现平滑、细粒度的控制。

</details>


### [84] [EgoCS-400K: An Egocentric Gameplay Dataset for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18180)
*Rongjin Guo,Dong Liang,Yuhao Liu,Fang Liu,Tianyu Huang,Gerhard P. Hancke,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoCS-400K，一个基于CS/CS2职业比赛回放构建的大规模第一人称数据集，包含40万+视频、1万小时游戏时长，提供动作、状态、事件等多模态对齐数据，用于支撑世界模型的交互式视觉建模任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从视频生成到交互式世界建模的转变需要动作-视频-语言轨迹对齐的大规模数据，但现有网络视频缺乏可执行动作和可靠状态，机器人数据成本高且场景多样性有限，模拟器缺乏大规模人类交互轨迹，因此亟需一个兼具视觉覆盖、动作标注和状态信息的数据集。

Method: 利用公开的职业CS/CS2比赛回放文件（demo），解析并提取玩家状态、视角方向、移动、键盘输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件和回合上下文等信息，同时渲染出干净的第一人称视频，构建了超过40万条第一人称视频轨迹的数据集。

Result: EgoCS-400K包含超过400,000个第一人称视频、10,000小时游戏时长、1,000+场比赛、40,000+回合、13张地图、每回合10个玩家视角，支持动作条件未来预测、状态/事件感知场景展开、回放字幕生成和智能体自我中心动作理解等任务。

Conclusion: EgoCS-400K通过大规模连接视觉观察与人类动作、相机运动、游戏状态和事件，在被动网络视频、可控游戏模拟和昂贵的真实世界具身数据之间建立了实用桥梁。

Original Abstract: The shift from video generation to interactive world modeling places new demands on data: beyond captioned videos, world models require temporally aligned video-action-language trajectories grounded in the actions, camera motion, states, and events that drive future scene changes. However, such data is difficult to obtain at scale. Web video datasets offer broad visual coverage but lack executable actions and reliable states; robotic datasets provide action and state supervision but are costly and limited in scene diversity; and existing simulators often lack large-scale human-driven interaction trajectories.
