In this paper, we introduce EgoCS-400K, a large-scale replay-grounded egocentric Counter-Strike dataset for world models, built from public professional CS and CS2 match demos that preserve human gameplay trajectories and enable parsing, replaying, rendering, and temporal alignment. We extract player states, view directions, movements, keyboard/button inputs, view-angle changes, weapon usage, game events, and round-level context, and render clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. EgoCS-400K contains over 400,000 first-person videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay from more than 1,000 matches and 40,000 rounds, covering 13 maps and 10 player viewpoints per round. It supports a range of interactive visual modeling tasks, including action-conditioned future prediction, state- and event-aware scene rollout, replay-grounded captioning, and agent egocentric action understanding. By connecting visual observations with human actions, camera motion, game states, and events at scale, EgoCS-400K serves as a practical bridge between passive web videos, controllable game simulation, and costly real-world embodied data.

中文摘要翻译: 从视频生成到交互式世界建模的转变对数据提出了新的要求：除带字幕的视频外，世界模型还需要基于驱动未来场景变化的动作、相机运动、状态和事件的对齐视频-动作-语言轨迹。然而，这类数据难以大规模获取。网络视频数据集提供广泛的视觉覆盖，但缺乏可执行的动作和可靠的状态；机器人数据集提供动作和状态监督，但成本高昂且场景多样性有限；现有的模拟器往往缺乏大规模人类驱动的交互轨迹。本文提出了EgoCS-400K，一个基于回放的大规模第一人称反恐精英数据集，用于世界模型构建。该数据集基于公开的职业CS和CS2比赛回放构建，保留了人类游戏轨迹，支持解析、回放、渲染和时间对齐。我们提取了玩家状态、视角方向、移动、键盘/按钮输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件和回合级上下文，并从相同的轨迹中渲染出干净的第三人称视频。EgoCS-400K包含超过400,000个第一人称视频和10,000小时的游戏时长，来自1,000多场比赛和40,000多个回合，覆盖13张地图和每回合10个玩家视角。它支持一系列交互式视觉建模任务，包括动作条件未来预测、状态和事件感知场景展开、回放基础的字幕生成以及智能体自我中心动作理解。通过大规模地将视觉观察与人类动作、相机运动、游戏状态和事件连接起来，EgoCS-400K在被动网络视频、可控游戏模拟和昂贵的真实世界具身数据之间架起了一座实用的桥梁。

</details>


### [85] [Adaptive Volumetric Mechanical Property Fields Invariant to Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18231)
*Rishit Dagli,Donglai Xiang,Vismay Modi,Xuning Yang,Gavriel State,David I. W. Levin,Maria Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaVoMP提出了一种基于稀疏自适应体素结构（SAV）的稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器方法，用于为3D对象预测高分辨率、空间变化的材料属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），相比现有技术实现了16³倍的分辨率提升和更高的内存效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字世界中3D资产进行可靠物理仿真需要准确的力学属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），但大多数3D资产缺乏这些信息，现有方法的精度和内存效率有限。

Method: 提出稀疏自适应体素结构SAV高效表示输入3D形状和材料场输出；用稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型替代先前的固定体素模型，自回归地为每个输入形状生成唯一的SAV来表征其材料属性。

Result: 分辨率提升16³倍；估计的体属性更准确；测试时计算量低于所有先前方法；能将高分辨率复杂3D对象转化为可直接仿真的资产，实现逼真的可变形仿真。

Conclusion: AdaVoMP在分辨率、精度和内存效率上均超越现有技术，能够为任意表示的3D对象生成高精度空间变化的材料属性，从而支持更真实的物理仿真。

Original Abstract: Accurate mechanical properties (or materials) Young's modulus ($E$), Poisson's ratio ($ν$) and density ($ρ$) are essential for reliable physics simulation of digital worlds, but most 3D assets lack this information. We propose AdaVoMP, a method for predicting accurate dense spatially-varying ($E$, $ν$, $ρ$) for input 3D objects across representations, improving the resolution, accuracy, and memory efficiency over the state-of-the-art. The foundation of our technique is a sparse and adaptive voxel structure SAV that efficiently represents both the input 3D shape and the material field output. We replace the fixed-voxel model of the most accurate prior method, VoMP, with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder model that learns to generate a unique SAV autoregressively for every input shape to represent its materials, achieving a resolution $16^3\times$ higher than prior art. Experiments show that AdaVoMP estimates more accurate volumetric properties, even with lesser test-time compute than all prior art. This allows us to convert high-resolution complex 3D objects into simulation-ready assets, resulting in realistic deformable simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的力学属性——杨氏模量（$E$）、泊松比（$ν$）和密度（$ρ$）——对于数字世界的可靠物理仿真至关重要，但大多数3D资产缺少这些信息。我们提出AdaVoMP，一种为任意表示的输入3D对象预测精确、密集且空间变化的（$E$, $ν$, $ρ$）的方法，在分辨率、精度和内存效率上均超越了现有最优方法。本技术的核心是一种稀疏自适应体素结构SAV，它能高效地同时表示输入3D形状和材料场输出。我们将此前最精确方法VoMP中的固定体素模型替换为一种新颖的稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型，该模型自回归地为每个输入形状学习生成唯一的SAV来表征其材料属性，实现了比先前方法高$16^3$倍的分辨率。实验表明，AdaVoMP能够估计出更准确的体积属性，甚至在使用比所有先前方法更少的测试时计算量的情况下也是如此。这使得我们能够将高分辨率的复杂3D对象转化为可直接进行仿真的资产，从而实现逼真的可变形仿真。

</details>


### [86] [EventDrive: Event Cameras for Vision-Language Driving Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18242)
*Dongyue Lu,Rong Li,Ao Liang,Lingdong Kong,Wei Yin,Lai Xing Ng,Benoit R. Cottereau,Camille Simon Chane,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDrive 是一个大规模基准和模型套件，将事件流、RGB 帧和语言监督统一到自动驾驶的感知、理解、预测和规划四个核心维度。其核心模型 EventDrive-VLM 采用多层级事件金字塔和时间层级混合专家模块，自适应编码和融合异步事件与帧基信息，显著提升了时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件感知视觉语言模型仅限于通用感知任务，未能揭示事件感知如何在完整驾驶循环中贡献于推理和决策。因此需要建立一个统一基准和模型来系统评估事件流对自动驾驶各核心维度的作用。

Method: 提出了 EventDrive-VLM，包含多层级事件金字塔（multi-horizon event pyramid）和时间层级混合专家模块（temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module），用于自适应编码和融合异步事件与帧基信息，支持下游推理任务。

Result: 在涵盖感知、理解、预测和规划的多种任务上的综合评估表明，事件流在时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面提供了显著增益，将事件感知推向驾驶智能的核心位置。

Conclusion: 事件流能够有效补充 RGB 帧在自动驾驶中的不足，EventDrive 框架为事件视觉语言模型在完整驾驶循环中的推理和决策提供了统一评估和建模基础。

Original Abstract: Event cameras sense the world through asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, offering motion fidelity far beyond frame-based sensors and capturing temporal structure that conventional exposures often miss. These properties make events a powerful complement to RGB in autonomous driving, especially under blur, glare, and rapid motion, where frame-based perception can become unreliable. However, existing event-aware vision-language models remain limited to generic perception and do not reveal how event sensing contributes to reasoning and decision-making across the full driving loop. We present EventDrive, a large-scale benchmark and model suite that unifies event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across four core dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Prediction, and Planning, covering captions, structured QA, grounding, motion-state recognition, trajectory forecasting, and planning tasks. Building on this foundation, EventDrive-VLM introduces a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively encode and fuse asynchronous and frame-based information for downstream reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse tasks shows that event streams provide substantial gains in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness, bringing event sensing into the center of driving intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 事件相机通过异步亮度变化感知世界，具有微秒级延迟和高动态范围，其运动保真度远超基于帧的传感器，能够捕捉传统曝光常遗漏的时间结构。这些特性使事件成为自动驾驶中 RGB 的有力补充，特别是在帧基感知可能不可靠的模糊、眩光和快速运动场景下。然而，现有的事件感知视觉语言模型仍局限于通用感知任务，未能揭示事件感知如何在完整驾驶循环中贡献于推理和决策。我们提出 EventDrive，这是一个大规模基准和模型套件，将事件流、RGB 帧和语言监督统一到四个核心维度：感知、理解、预测和规划，涵盖描述生成、结构化问答、定位、运动状态识别、轨迹预测和规划任务。在此基础上，EventDrive-VLM 引入了多层级事件金字塔和时间层级混合专家模块，用于自适应编码和融合异步信息与帧基信息以支持下游推理。跨多种任务的综合评估表明，事件流在时间精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面提供了显著增益，将事件感知推向驾驶智能的核心位置。

</details>


### [87] [MOCHI: Motion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18243)
*Jiye Lee,Yonghun Choi,Jungdam Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOCHI框架，通过两阶段优化（物理合理的手部抓取生成+扩散去噪全身运动细化）来增强嘈杂的多人体-物体交互数据，实验证明其有效性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 协同人-物交互场景复杂，现有捕获数据存在手-物接触错位、运动抖动、时间不一致以及手指级细节缺失等噪声问题，需要高质量数据增强方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过优化从噪声人体输入生成物理合理且语义一致的手部抓取，并扩展为完整的手-物交互序列；第二阶段使用基于扩散的噪声优化框架，利用单人运动先验并引入人-物和人-人交互目标约束，细化全身运动。

Result: 在多种MHOI数据（捕获或生成）上验证了框架的有效性；对参与者数量和交互类型变化具有鲁棒性；支持关键帧MHOI创建和通过改变物体几何进行数据增强等应用。

Conclusion: MOCHI框架能有效增强嘈杂的多人-物交互数据，具备良好的泛化能力和实用价值。

Original Abstract: Collaborative human-object interaction shows dynamic and complex movements that require mutual anticipation and continuous adjustment between participants and the shared object. Modeling such collaborative multi-human object interaction (MHOI) scenarios requires high-quality data acquisition as a foundational step; however, this is challenging due to the inherent complexity of MHOI where human-human and human-object interactions occur simultaneously. Such complexity leads to noisy MHOI captures characterized by several artifacts: contact misalignment between hands and objects, motion jitter and temporal inconsistencies in the captured sequences, and missing or incomplete finger-level articulation details. To address these challenges, we present MOCHI (MOtion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions), a two-stage framework for enhancing noisy MHOI data. Our approach first generates physically plausible hand grasps through optimization from noisy body input, producing grasps that are both physically plausible and semantically consistent with the body pose, where these optimized grasps are extended into complete hand-object interaction sequences. Consequently, the full-body motion for all participants are refined through a diffusion-based noise optimization framework that uses single-person motion priors. During the optimization process, we introduce optimization objectives to encode human-object and human-human interaction information within these single-person priors. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our pipeline across diverse MHOI data, either acquired by existing capture methods or synthesized by generative models. We further show robustness of our system across varying numbers of participants and types of interactions, and demonstrate various applications including keyframe-based MHOI creation and data augmentation through varying object geometries.

中文摘要翻译: 协同人-物交互展现了动态且复杂的运动，需要参与者与共享物体之间的相互预判和持续调整。对此类协作性多人体-物体交互（MHOI）场景进行建模，需要以高质量数据采集为基础步骤；然而，由于MHOI中人与人和人与物交互同时发生的内在复杂性，这一步骤极具挑战性。这种复杂性导致捕获的MHOI数据包含多种噪声伪影：手与物体之间的接触错位、捕获序列中的运动抖动和时间不一致性，以及手指级关节细节的缺失或不完整。为解决这些问题，我们提出了MOCHI（协作性人-物交互的运动增强框架），一个用于增强嘈杂MHOI数据的两阶段框架。我们的方法首先通过优化从噪声人体输入生成物理合理的手部抓取，生成的抓取既具有物理合理性，又与身体姿态语义一致；随后将这些优化后的抓取扩展为完整的手-物交互序列。最后，通过一个基于扩散的噪声优化框架，利用单人运动先验对所有参与者的全身运动进行细化。在优化过程中，我们引入了优化目标，将人-物和人-人交互信息编码到这些单人先验中。实验结果表明，我们的流程在多种MHOI数据（无论是现有捕获方法获取的还是生成模型合成的）上均有效。我们进一步展示了该系统在不同参与者数量和交互类型下的鲁棒性，并展示了多种应用，包括基于关键帧的MHOI创建和通过改变物体几何形状进行数据增强。

</details>


### [88] [Unified Multimodal Autoregressive Modeling with Shared Context-Visual Tokenizer is Key to Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18249)
*Wujian Peng,Lingchen Meng,Yuxuan Cai,Xianwei Zhuang,Yuhuan Yang,Rongyao Fang,Chenfei Wu,Junyang Lin,Zuxuan Wu,Shuai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR提出了一种统一自回归框架，通过单一离散视觉分词器桥接视觉理解与生成，采用多级特征融合、免查表比特量化、并行比特预测和扩散解码器，在图像生成和编辑任务上达到SOTA，同时保持多模态理解的竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一多模态建模方法通常依赖两个不同的视觉分词器，导致表示空间分裂，阻碍了真正的统一建模。

Method: UniAR采用单一离散视觉分词器作为理解与生成的桥梁，通过多级特征融合和免查表比特量化方案适配预训练视觉编码器；采用并行比特预测联合预测空间分组的多级视觉编码，大幅缩短视觉序列长度加速生成；基于扩散的视觉解码器对离散视觉token解码生成高保真图像。

Result: 在大规模预训练、监督微调和强化学习后，UniAR在图像生成和图像编辑任务上达到最先进性能，在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: UniAR通过统一的自回归框架有效融合了视觉理解与生成，验证了单一离散分词器框架的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Unified Multimodal Modeling aims to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single system. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the model can directly interpret its own generated visual tokens without additional re-encoding. UniAR adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and a lookup-free bitwise quantization scheme, preserving both high-level semantics and low-level details while scaling the effective visual vocabulary at minimal cost. Building on this, the unified autoregressive model adopts parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict spatially grouped, multi-level visual codes, substantially reducing visual sequence length and accelerating generation. Finally, a diffusion-based visual decoder operates on discrete visual tokens to decode high-fidelity images. Through large-scale pre-training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation and image editing while remaining competitive on multimodal understanding benchmarks. The project page is available at https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web.

中文摘要翻译: 统一多模态建模旨在将视觉理解与生成集成到单一系统中。然而，现有方法通常依赖两个不同的视觉分词器，这分裂了表示空间并阻碍了真正的统一建模。本文提出UniAR，一种统一的自回归框架，其中单一离散视觉分词器充当理解与生成之间的关键桥梁，使得模型能够在共享上下文中直接解释自身生成的视觉token，而无需额外的重新编码。UniAR通过多级特征融合和免查表比特量化方案适配预训练视觉编码器，在保留高层语义和低层细节的同时，以最小代价扩展有效视觉词汇量。在此基础上，统一自回归模型采用并行比特预测来联合预测空间分组的多级视觉编码，显著缩短了视觉序列长度并加速了生成过程。最后，基于扩散的视觉解码器对离散视觉token进行解码，生成高保真图像。通过大规模预训练，随后进行监督微调和强化学习，UniAR在图像生成和图像编辑任务上达到了最先进水平，同时在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。项目页面见https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web。

</details>


### [89] [Future Dynamic 3D Reconstruction: A 3D World Model with Disentangled Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18250)
*Nils Morbitzer,Jonathan Evers,Artem Savkin,Thomas Stauner,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FR3D是一种世界模型，通过解耦场景3D演化与智能体轨迹，并引入教师-学生蒸馏策略，实现从单目观测到未来动态3D重建的几何一致性预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型在2D视频合成中混合了自我运动和环境动态，导致物理不一致性（如物体变形或消失），尤其随着时间推移问题加剧。本文旨在解决这一歧义，实现几何一致的长时域未来动态3D重建。

Method: 提出FR3D框架：1）显式解耦场景3D演化与智能体轨迹，将推断的自我运动作为动作的潜在代理；2）采用教师-学生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间"常识"实现鲁棒的零样本泛化；3）预测持久的3D潜在表征用于未来动态3D重建。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，FR3D在从单目观测进行未来动态3D重建任务中表现优异，即使预测未来2秒仍能保持几何一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦自我运动与世界运动，并利用基础模型蒸馏，FR3D有效解决了现有世界模型中的物理不一致性问题，为长时域动态3D场景预测提供了一种几何一致的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Forecasting the evolution of dynamic environments is crucial for autonomous agents. While generative world models have recently achieved high photorealism in 2D video synthesis by mixing ego-motion and environmental dynamics within the image plane, they exhibit physical inconsistencies, such as morphing or vanishing objects, especially over long time horizons. In this paper, we propose FR3D, a world model that predicts a persistent 3D latent representation for future dynamic 3D reconstruction. Unlike prior works that treat the world as a sequence of image-based features, FR3D explicitly decouples the 3D evolution of the scene from the agent's trajectory, treating the inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for action. This disentanglement resolves the ambiguities between self-motion and world-motion, ensuring geometric consistency into the future. Furthermore, we introduce a teacher-student distillation strategy that leverages the spatial "common sense" of off-the-shelf foundation models, leading to robust zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate FR3D's strong performance for future dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular observations across multiple datasets, even 2 seconds into the future. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io.

中文摘要翻译: 预测动态环境的演化对自主智能体至关重要。虽然生成式世界模型通过将自我运动与环境动态混合在图像平面内，近期在2D视频合成中实现了高逼真度，但它们存在物理不一致性，例如物体变形或消失，尤其在长时间跨度下。在本文中，我们提出FR3D，一种预测持久3D潜在表征以用于未来动态3D重建的世界模型。与将世界视为基于图像的特征序列的先前工作不同，FR3D显式地将场景的3D演化与智能体的轨迹解耦，将推断出的自我运动作为动作的潜在代理。这种解耦解决了自我运动与世界运动之间的歧义，确保了未来的几何一致性。此外，我们引入了一种教师-学生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间"常识"，实现了鲁棒的零样本泛化。大量实验表明，FR3D在多个数据集上从单目观测进行未来动态3D重建（即使预测未来2秒）中表现出色。项目页面：https://fr3d-wm.github.io。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Phenotyping TPF via Self-Supervised Learning: A Label-Agnostic Framework with Expert Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17295)
*Miral Elnakib,Muhammad Saad,Ahmad Al-Kabbany*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无标签的自监督学习框架，利用SimCLR对比学习在膝关节X光片上自动发现胫骨平台骨折的四种影像学表型，避免了传统分类（如Schatzker）中标注不一致的问题，并验证了这些表型的临床可解释性和与常规分类的正交性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统骨折分类方案（Schatzker、AO/OTA）存在观察者间变异，导致监督模型学习的是人类标注分歧而非稳定的骨折形态；而完全依赖标注数据集限制了AI在胫骨平台骨折表征中的潜力，因此需要一种无需标签的框架来直接学习骨折表征。

Method: 首先对154张膝关节X光片进行数据清洗，然后使用RadImageNet预训练的ResNet-50编码器结合SimCLR对比学习目标进行微调，最后通过UMAP降维和k-means聚类发现四种影像学衍生表型，并采用盲法专家评审协议评估表型有效性。

Result: 四种表型表现出稳健的稳定性（bootstrap ARI=0.319±0.041）、强内聚性（轮廓系数=0.511），盲评一致性评分3-5/5；其中一个表型被一致识别为粉碎性骨折（无需监督信号）；与Schatzker标签的ARI=0.013，证实与常规分类正交。

Conclusion: 无标签的自监督学习表型分型可作为传统分类的可重复且临床可解释的补充方法，其嵌入几何与Schatzker训练下的专家感知测量的是正交维度。

Original Abstract: The full potential of artificial intelligence in tibial plateau fracture characterisation remains unrealised, constrained by a fundamental dependency on labelled datasets whose consistency cannot be guaranteed: conventional classification schemes such as Schatzker and AO/OTA suffer from inter-observer variability, causing supervised models to learn human disagreement rather than stable fracture morphology. We design, implement, and validate a label-agnostic framework that eliminates this constraint by learning fracture representations directly from imaging data without observer-assigned labels. A RadImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50 encoder is fine-tuned on 154 cleaned knee radiographs using the SimCLR contrastive objective, preceded by a data cleaning protocol and followed by UMAP dimensionality reduction and k-means clustering to discover four imaging-derived phenotypes. Phenotype validity is assessed through a blinded expert review protocol administered to two independent clinicians. The four phenotypes demonstrate robust stability (bootstrap ARI = 0.319 +/- 0.041), strong internal cohesion (silhouette = 0.511), and coherence ratings of 3-5/5 from both reviewers under blinded conditions; one phenotype was unanimously identified as exhibiting comminution -- a high-complexity feature isolated without any supervisory signal. Inter-partition comparison against Schatzker labels yields ARI = 0.013, confirming orthogonality to conventional classification boundaries. Notably, expert reviewers anchored to established classification vocabularies perceived imaging-derived groups as heterogeneous precisely where Schatzker alignment was lowest, suggesting that Schatzker-trained perception and label-agnostic embedding geometry measure orthogonal dimensions. These findings establish label-agnostic SSL phenotyping as a reproducible and clinically interpretable complement to conventional classification.

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能在胫骨平台骨折表征方面的全部潜力尚未得到充分发挥，其根本制约在于对标注数据集的依赖——而这类数据集的一致性无法得到保证：传统的Schatzker和AO/OTA等分类方案存在观察者间变异，导致监督模型学到的是人类标注分歧而非稳定的骨折形态。我们设计、实现并验证了一个无需标签的框架，通过直接从影像数据中学习骨折表征来消除这一约束。该框架采用RadImageNet预训练的ResNet-50编码器，在154张经过清洗的膝关节X光片上使用SimCLR对比学习目标进行微调，之前进行数据清洗协议处理，之后通过UMAP降维和k-means聚类发现四种影像学衍生表型。通过盲法专家评审协议对两名独立临床医生评估表型有效性。四种表型展现出稳健的稳定性（bootstrap ARI=0.319±0.041）、强内聚性（轮廓系数=0.511），且在盲法条件下两位评审员的连贯性评分为3-5/5；其中一个表型被一致识别为粉碎性骨折——这是在没有任何监督信号的情况下发现的高复杂性特征。与Schatzker标签的跨分区比较得出ARI=0.013，证实其与传统分类边界正交。值得注意的是，锚定于既定分类词汇的专家评审员恰恰在Schatzker对齐度最低的影像衍生分组中感知到异质性，表明Schatzker训练下的感知与无标签嵌入几何衡量的是正交维度。这些发现确立了无标签自监督学习表型分型作为传统分类的可重复且临床可解释的补充方法。

</details>


### [91] [Feynman Kac Reweighted Schrödinger Bridge Matching for Surface-Based Tau PET Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17420)
*Jianwei Zhang,Xinyu Nie,Jiaxin Yue,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FKRSBM模型，通过熵正则化最优传输和子群感知端点提议，在无需高斯噪声先验的情况下实现跨扫描仪、跨示踪剂的tau PET图像协调，有效区分站点效应与生物学变异，优于ComBat、CycleGAN等现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 不同站点间的扫描仪、协议和放射性示踪剂差异引入非生物变异，增加了生物标志物方差、降低了对疾病效应的敏感性，且现有协调方法在源域和目标域子群组成不同时，易将站点效应与生物学变异（如tau阳性状态）混淆。

Method: 提出FKRSBM模型：1）通过熵正则化最优运输学习源分布到目标分布的直接随机运输过程，而非依赖高斯噪声先验；2）引入基于Feynman Kac重加权参考桥测度的子群感知端点提议，通过分层重要性采样实现，不改变桥匹配求解器或网络架构；3）在表面神经影像上采用球形卷积骨干网络在皮层网格上进行顶点级协调。

Result: 在将HABS-HD队列的PI-2620数据协调为ADNI的AV-1451域的任务中，与ComBat、CycleGAN、扩散方法（DF）和未正则化的扩散薛定谔桥匹配（DSBM）相比，FKRSBM在分布对齐、tau阳性符号不匹配降低、APOE子群对齐和下游疾病分类性能方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: FKRSBM通过子群感知的随机运输过程有效解决跨站点tau PET图像协调中的生物变异混淆问题，在多项评价指标上超越现有方法，为多中心神经影像协调提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Tau PET imaging is central to tracking Alzheimer's disease progression, but systematic differences between scanners, protocols, and radiotracers across sites introduce nonbiological variability that inflates biomarker variance, reduces sensitivity to disease effects, and can bias downstream clinical assessments. Harmonization methods aim to remove these site-induced shifts while preserving biologically meaningful signal, yet existing approaches struggle when source and target cohorts differ in subgroup composition, risking conflation of site effects with biological variation such as tau-positivity status. We propose the Feynman Kac Reweighted Schröodinger Bridge Matching (FKRSBM) model to address this problem. Rather than routing data through a Gaussian noise prior as in diffusion-based methods, FKRSBM learns a direct stochastic transport process between source and target distributions via entropy-regularized optimal transport. To enforce biologically consistent transport, FKRSBM incorporates a subgroup-aware endpoint proposal derived from a Feynman Kac reweighting of the reference bridge measure, implemented entirely through stratified importance sampling at the data level and requiring no changes to the underlying bridge-matching solver or network architecture. For surface-based neuroimaging, FKRSBM employs a spherical convolutional backbone operating on cortical meshes to perform vertex-level harmonization. We evaluate the method on tau PET SUVR maps, harmonizing PI-2620 data from the HABS-HD cohort into the AV-1451 domain of ADNI. Compared against ComBat, CycleGAN, a diffusion-based method (DF), and unregularized Diffusion Schröodinger Bridge Matching (DSBM), FKRSBM achieves superior distributional alignment, reduced tau-positivity sign mismatch, stronger APOE subgroup alignment, and improved downstream disease classification performance.

中文摘要翻译: Tau PET成像对于追踪阿尔茨海默病进展至关重要，但不同站点间的扫描仪、协议和放射性示踪剂的系统性差异引入了非生物变异，这会增大生物标志物方差、降低对疾病效应的敏感性，并可能使下游临床评估产生偏差。协调方法旨在消除这些站点引起的偏移，同时保留有生物学意义的信号，然而现有方法在源队列和目标队列的子群组成不同时难以有效处理，存在将站点效应与诸如tau阳性状态等生物学变异相混淆的风险。我们提出Feynman Kac重加权薛定谔桥匹配（FKRSBM）模型来解决这一问题。与基于扩散的方法不同，FKRSBM不将数据通过高斯噪声先验进行路由，而是通过熵正则化最优运输学习源分布与目标分布之间的直接随机运输过程。为实现生物学一致的运输，FKRSBM引入了一种基于Feynman Kac重加权参考桥测度的子群感知端点提议，该提议完全通过数据层面的分层重要性采样实现，无需对底层桥匹配求解器或网络架构进行任何修改。对于基于表面的神经影像，FKRSBM采用在皮层网格上运行的球形卷积骨干网络进行顶点级协调。我们在tau PET SUVR图上评估了该方法，将HABS-HD队列的PI-2620数据协调到ADNI的AV-1451域。与ComBat、CycleGAN、基于扩散的方法（DF）以及无正则化的扩散薛定谔桥匹配（DSBM）相比，FKRSBM在分布对齐、tau阳性符号不匹配减少、APOE子群对齐增强以及下游疾病分类性能提升方面均表现更优。

</details>


### [92] [Two-Stage Fine-Tuning of ResNet50 for High-Sensitivity Melanoma Detection on Dermoscopic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17504)
*Aryan Bhagat*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段微调ResNet50策略，用于黑色素瘤二分类检测。通过随机过采样解决类别不平衡，分阶段微调避免灾难性遗忘。在3826张测试图像上达到AUC 0.9559、准确率88.34%，并提供了可部署的Streamlit应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 黑色素瘤早期检测的五年生存率超过99%，但一旦扩散则急剧下降。现有单阶段微调方法存在类别不平衡和次优迁移学习的问题，导致模型性能受限。

Method: 采用ResNet50作为骨干网络，实施两阶段微调：(1) Stage 1冻结ResNet50基座，仅训练分类头；(2) Stage 2以1e-5的低学习率联合微调所有层，防止灾难性遗忘。对训练集进行随机过采样实现1:1类别平衡。使用分层分割保留类别分布。

Result: 在3826张独立测试集上：AUC-ROC 0.9559，准确率88.34%，敏感度87.56%，特异度89.13%，F1分数88.29%。消融实验证实两阶段协议显著优于单阶段微调，敏感度提升超过4%。Grad-CAM可视化显示正确的病灶定位。

Conclusion: 两阶段微调策略能有效提升黑色素瘤分类性能，结合过采样和低学习率微调可缓解类别不平衡和灾难性遗忘问题，具有临床部署潜力。

Original Abstract: Melanoma is the most dangerous form of skin cancer with five-year survival rates exceeding 99% when detected early but falling sharply once the disease spreads. This paper proposes and evaluates a two-stage fine-tuning approach for ResNet50 applied to binary melanoma classification on dermoscopic images. The core challenges addressed are class imbalance and suboptimal transfer learning from single-stage fine-tuning. After stratified train/validation/test splitting, random oversampling was applied exclusively to the training set to achieve a 1:1 class balance. Stage 1 trained only the classification head with the ResNet50 base frozen, while Stage 2 fine-tuned all layers jointly at a low learning rate of 1e-5 to prevent catastrophic forgetting of learned visual features. On an independent test set of 3,826 images, the model achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9559, accuracy of 88.34%, sensitivity of 87.56%, specificity of 89.13%, and F1-score of 88.29%. An ablation study confirms the two-stage protocol significantly outperforms single-stage fine-tuning, with sensitivity gains of over 4%. Grad-CAM visualizations demonstrate correct lesion localization. A fully deployable Streamlit detection application is provided alongside all training code.

中文摘要翻译: 黑色素瘤是最危险的皮肤癌类型，早期检测时五年生存率超过99%，但一旦扩散则急剧下降。本文提出并评估了一种应用于皮肤镜图像上二元黑色素瘤分类的ResNet50两阶段微调方法。所解决的核心挑战是类别不平衡和单阶段微调导致的次优迁移学习。在分层训练/验证/测试分割后，仅对训练集应用随机过采样以实现1:1类别平衡。第一阶段冻结ResNet50基座仅训练分类头，第二阶段以1e-5的低学习率联合微调所有层，以防止已学习视觉特征的灾难性遗忘。在包含3,826张图像的独立测试集上，模型取得了AUC-ROC 0.9559、准确率88.34%、敏感度87.56%、特异度89.13%和F1分数88.29%的性能。消融研究证实两阶段协议显著优于单阶段微调，敏感度提升超过4%。Grad-CAM可视化展示了正确的病灶定位。本文还提供了完全可部署的Streamlit检测应用程序及所有训练代码。

</details>


### [93] [Fine-UNETR for PSMA PET/CT Lesion Segmentation: Automated Tumor Quantification and Overall Survival Stratification in Prostate Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17570)
*Mansour Abtahi,Chae Moon Hong,Nikhil Deveshwar,Stellamaris Nwihim,Peder E. Z. Larson,Thomas A. Hope*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Fine-UNETR，一种基于Vision Transformer的架构，用于全身PET/CT中PSMA-avid病灶的自动分割。在373例PSMA PET/CT扫描上训练和验证，DSC达66.63%，病灶检测率为79.53%（高摄取病灶达96.05%）。AI衍生的肿瘤负荷生物标志物与金标准高度一致（r>0.96），且总肿瘤体积、SUVmax和SUVmean在放射配体治疗前队列中显著分层总生存期（p<0.02），证明了临床实用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前列腺癌患者中PSMA PET/CT的自动病灶分割和肿瘤负荷量化对于预后分层和治疗决策至关重要，但现有方法在全自动全身分割方面仍存在挑战，且缺乏对临床预后价值的系统性评估。

Method: 提出Fine-UNETR，该模型基于UNETR架构进行改进，采用8×8×8体素块嵌入和轴向滑动窗口训练策略。使用299例扫描进行训练，74例扫描进行内部验证，并在AutoPET IV PSMA PET/CT数据集（192例）上进行外部验证。通过Kaplan-Meier分析和log-rank检验评估AI衍生的生物标志物在67例放射配体治疗前患者中的总生存期分层能力。

Result: 内部验证DSC为66.63%，敏感性70.27%，精确率67.77%，病灶检测率79.53%（SUVmax≥5时达96.05%）。外部验证DSC为44.11%，但病灶检测率达87.18%。AI生物标志物与金标准高度相关（总肿瘤体积r=0.984，总病灶摄取r=0.989，病灶计数r=0.960）。总肿瘤体积（p=0.0019）、SUVmax（p=0.014）和SUVmean（p=0.016）显著分层总生存期。

Conclusion: Fine-UNETR实现了准确的全身PSMA病灶自动分割和肿瘤负荷量化，在外部数据集上表现出对域偏移的鲁棒性，且AI衍生的生物标志物在放射配体治疗前队列中具有显著的总生存期分层能力，支持自动PSMA PET/CT量化在预后评估中的临床价值。

Original Abstract: Introduction: To develop and evaluate Fine-UNETR, a Vision Transformer-based architecture for automated segmentation of PSMA-avid lesions on whole-body PET/CT, and to assess clinical utility of AI-derived tumor burden biomarkers for overall survival stratification in radioligand therapy. Methods: In this retrospective study, 373 PSMA PET/CT scans (mean age, 71+-8 years) from patients with prostate cancer were analyzed. Fine-UNETR, a modified UNETR with 8x8x8 voxel patch embedding and axial sliding window training, was trained on 299 scans and validated on 74 scans. Overall survival stratification was assessed in an independent cohort of 67 pre-radioligand therapy patients using Kaplan-Meier analysis and log-rank testing. External validation was performed on 192 cases from the AutoPET IV PSMA PET/CT dataset. Results: Fine-UNETR achieved a Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) of 66.63%, sensitivity of 70.27%, precision of 67.77%, and a lesion detection rate of 79.53% (96.05% for lesions with SUVmax >= 5). On the external validation dataset, the model achieved a DSC of 44.11% and a lesion detection rate of 87.18%, indicating that lesion detection performance was preserved despite reduced voxel-level overlap. AI-derived biomarkers showed excellent agreement with ground truth (total tumor volume: r=0.984; total lesion uptake: r=0.989; lesion count: r=0.960). In the clinical cohort, total tumor volume (p=0.0019), SUVmax (p=0.014), and SUVmean (p=0.016) significantly stratified overall survival. Conclusion: Fine-UNETR enables accurate automated whole-body PSMA lesion segmentation and tumor burden quantification. Performance on an external dataset demonstrates robustness despite evidence of domain shift. AI-derived biomarkers significantly stratified overall survival in a pre-radioligand therapy cohort, supporting the clinical utility of automated PSMA PET/CT quantification for prognostication.

中文摘要翻译: 引言：开发并评估基于Vision Transformer的Fine-UNETR架构，用于全身PET/CT上PSMA-avid病灶的自动分割，并评估AI衍生的肿瘤负荷生物标志物在放射配体治疗中总生存期分层的临床效用。方法：本回顾性研究分析了373例前列腺癌患者的PSMA PET/CT扫描（平均年龄71±8岁）。Fine-UNETR是修改后的UNETR，采用8×8×8体素块嵌入和轴向滑动窗口训练，在299例扫描上训练并在74例扫描上验证。在67例放射配体治疗前患者的独立队列中，使用Kaplan-Meier分析和log-rank检验评估总生存期分层。在AutoPET IV PSMA PET/CT数据集的192例病例上进行外部验证。结果：Fine-UNETR的Dice相似系数（DSC）为66.63%，敏感性为70.27%，精确率为67.77%，病灶检测率为79.53%（SUVmax≥5的病灶为96.05%）。在外部验证数据集上，模型DSC为44.11%，病灶检测率为87.18%，表明尽管体素级重叠有所减少，但病灶检测性能得以保持。AI衍生的生物标志物与金标准具有极好的一致性（总肿瘤体积：r=0.984；总病灶摄取：r=0.989；病灶计数：r=0.960）。在临床队列中，总肿瘤体积（p=0.0019）、SUVmax（p=0.014）和SUVmean（p=0.016）显著分层总生存期。结论：Fine-UNETR能够实现准确的全身PSMA病灶自动分割和肿瘤负荷量化。尽管存在域偏移的证据，外部数据集上的性能仍表现出鲁棒性。AI衍生的生物标志物在放射配体治疗前队列中显著分层总生存期，支持自动PSMA PET/CT量化在预后评估中的临床效用。

</details>


### [94] [Perceptually-Weighted Video Quality Metric for Asymmetric Encoded Sports Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17869)
*Anna Meyer,Jonas Janzen,Diwakara Reddy,Alexander Kopte,Simon Deniffel,Paul Wawerek-López,Marc Windsheimer,André Kaup*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于感知加权的全参考视频质量指标PW-VQM，通过区分前景与背景区域并赋予不同权重，解决了传统指标忽略空间注意力非均匀性的问题，在体育视频上达到0.9511的SROCC，优于SSIM、VMAF等既有指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统客观视频质量指标假设空间注意力均匀分布，这与人类视觉感知的选择性特性相矛盾，尤其在体育视频中观看者注意力高度集中于特定区域。因此需要一种能考虑空间区域感知重要性差异的质量评价指标。

Method: 基于小波域的多尺度SSIM图，结合开放词汇目标检测与光流分析来识别感知显著的前景区域，并在质量聚合时赋予前景区域更高的权重，从而实现对非对称编码内容的感知加权质量评估。

Result: 在体育视频内容上，PW-VQM的Spearman秩相关系数达到0.9511，优于SSIM、VMAF、FUNQUE和LPIPS等既有指标；消融研究验证了各感知加权组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 通过区分前景与背景区域的感知重要性进行加权质量评估，能够有效提升对非对称编码体育视频的质量评价准确性，验证了空间注意力非均匀性假设在视频质量指标中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Objective video quality metrics commonly assume uniform spatial attention, an assumption that conflicts with the selective nature of human visual perception, particularly in sports videos. Here, allocating more bits for salient regions through semantic encoding can lead to significant bitrate savings. We present a Perceptually-Weighted Video Quality Metric (PW-VQM), a full-reference metric that accounts for the unequal perceptual importance of spatial regions and therefore targets quality evaluation for asymmetrically encoded content. SSIM maps computed in a multiscale wavelet domain are weighted by differentiating between foreground and background regions. Perceptually salient foreground regions are identified by combining open-vocabulary object detection with optical flow analysis, and are assigned higher weight during quality aggregation. Evaluated on sports video content, PW-VQM achieves a Spearman Rank Order Correlation Coefficient of 0.9511, outperforming established metrics including SSIM, VMAF, FUNQUE, and LPIPS. An ablation study confirms the individual contributions of the components of the perceptual weighting.

中文摘要翻译: 客观视频质量指标通常假设空间注意力是均匀分布的，这一假设与人类视觉感知的选择性特性相矛盾，尤其在体育视频中更为突出。通过语义编码为显著区域分配更多比特可以带来显著的码率节省。我们提出了一种感知加权视频质量指标（PW-VQM），这是一种全参考指标，考虑了空间区域的非均匀感知重要性，从而针对非对称编码内容的质量评估进行了优化。在多尺度小波域中计算的SSIM图通过区分前景和背景区域进行加权。通过结合开放词汇目标检测与光流分析来识别感知显著的前景区域，并在质量聚合过程中赋予其更高的权重。在体育视频内容上的评估显示，PW-VQM的Spearman秩相关系数达到0.9511，优于SSIM、VMAF、FUNQUE和LPIPS等既有指标。消融研究证实了感知加权各组成部分的独立贡献。

</details>


### [95] [Multiscale reconstruction of protein conformations from cryo-EM images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18058)
*David Y. W. Thong,Ozan Öktem,Joakim Andén*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多尺度算法的蛋白质单颗粒冷冻电镜原子模型重建方法，通过显式表示蛋白质主链（键、扭转角、键角）提供先验信息，在高噪声、低对比度数据下达到最先进精度，并提高RMSD和TM评分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有冷冻电镜结构重建方法在高噪声、低对比度数据下精度不足，且对TEM成像模型误规范不鲁棒，需要一种能充分利用蛋白质结构先验信息的多尺度算法来提升重建质量。

Method: 提出一种多尺度算法，显式表示蛋白质主链（键、扭转角、键角）作为先验信息，从单颗粒冷冻电镜数据中直接恢复蛋白质原子模型结构。

Result: 在三个蛋白质冷冻电镜数据集（使用电子显微镜数字孪生产生）上的实验表明，多尺度方法相比基线在RMSD和TM评分上均有提升，且能优先恢复大尺度结构，减少陷入不良局部最优的风险。

Conclusion: 所提多尺度算法能够从高噪声、低对比度的单颗粒冷冻电镜数据中高精度恢复蛋白质原子结构，具有鲁棒性和实用性。

Original Abstract: We present a novel multiscale algorithm for directly recovering the atomic model structure of a protein from single-particle cryo-EM data. Our algorithm is able to estimate protein structures to state-of-the-art accuracy for high-noise and low-contrast data. It is also robust to misspecifications in the TEM image formation model. These desirable properties are primarily due to the use of an explicit representation of the protein backbone in terms of bonds, torsion angles and bond angles, which supplies rich prior information to the structure recovery process. We apply our method on three protein cryo-EM datasets, generated using an electron microscope digital twin, and show that using a multiscale approach yields an improvement of the root-mean-square deviation (RMSD) and template modelling (TM) scores with respect to the ground truth. Furthermore, there is evidence that larger-scale structures are being prioritised with the multiscale algorithm, which reduces the possibility of convergence to bad local minima.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的多尺度算法，用于从单颗粒冷冻电镜数据中直接恢复蛋白质的原子模型结构。该算法能够在高噪声、低对比度数据下估计出达到最先进精度的蛋白质结构，并且对透射电镜成像模型的误规范具有鲁棒性。这些优良特性主要得益于我们使用键、扭转角和键角对蛋白质主链进行显式表示，为结构恢复过程提供了丰富的先验信息。我们将该方法应用于三个蛋白质冷冻电镜数据集（使用电子显微镜数字孪生产生），结果表明，使用多尺度方法在均方根偏差和模板建模评分上相比真实结构均有提升。此外，有证据表明多尺度算法优先恢复较大尺度的结构，从而降低了收敛到不良局部最优的可能性。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前多模态大语言模型的知识编辑存在编辑解耦失败问题：知识更新在跨模态输入下有效，但在单模态输入下会回退至旧知识。本文提出DECODE方法，通过显式解耦和定位模态特定的神经元组，实现跨模态触发下的有效知识更新，缓解编辑解耦失败。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法在多模态输入（图文对）下能成功更新实体知识，但当输入拆分为单模态时，模型往往回退到编辑前的旧知识，这种编辑解耦失败问题尚未被充分探索。作者通过实证分析发现，多模态大语言模型中的实体知识并非以统一表征存储，而是分布在解耦的模态特定通路中，导致偏向多模态查询的更新无法有效传播到单模态回路。

Method: 提出DECODE方法，显式地解耦并定位针对目标知识的模态特定神经元组，从而实现对不同模态触发器下的一致性知识更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，DECODE在不同模态触发条件下均能实现有效的知识更新，从而缓解编辑解耦失败问题。

Conclusion: DECODE通过显式解耦和定位模态特定的神经元组，有效解决了多模态大语言模型知识编辑中的解耦失败问题，实现了跨模态触发下的一致且可靠的知识更新。

Original Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管知识编辑为多模态大语言模型的知识更新提供了高效机制，但我们发现当前范式仍然存在一个重要但尚未充分探索的问题——编辑解耦失败：当模型受到多模态输入（文本-图像查询对）触发时，实体相关知识可以被成功更新，然而当配对输入被拆分为单模态输入时，模型往往会回退到编辑前的旧知识。我们深入的实证分析揭示，多模态大语言模型中的实体知识并非以统一表征的形式存储，而是分布在解耦的模态特定通路中。因此，偏向多模态查询的更新无法有效传播到单模态回路。为弥合这一差距，我们提出DECODE方法，该方法显式地解耦并定位针对目标知识的模态特定神经元组。大量实验表明，DECODE在不同模态触发条件下均能实现有效的知识更新，从而缓解编辑解耦失败问题。

</details>


### [97] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可解释性分析和不确定性量化的诊断-修复-验证框架，用于解决长距离单次曝光条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）中物理先验缺失导致的深度估计退化问题。通过机械可解释性（MI）和保形不确定性量化（UQ）一致诊断出基线网络依赖目标边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码的失败模式，并设计PhiCalNet，通过输出包裹相位并施加固定可微分标定层将深度映射从假设空间中架构性移除，将物体平均绝对误差降低3.3倍至4.46 mm。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于学习的单次曝光FPP方法多聚焦于近距离场景，远距离（1 m以上）问题尚未得到有效解决：强度平方反比衰减导致条纹信噪比降低，物理真值退化；单次曝光问题本身病态（缺乏条纹阶次信息）；现有网络架构缺乏机理层面的研究。

Method: 提出诊断-修复-验证框架：(1) 诊断阶段：联合使用线性探针、Grad-CAM、平坦域分布外测试三种MI方法，以及像素级保形UQ，一致确定网络依赖目标边界形状先验这一物理失败点；(2) 修复阶段：提出PhiCalNet，网络输出包裹相位（wrapped phase）而非直接回归深度，再通过一个固定的可微分标定层将相位映射为深度，从而在架构层面而非损失惩罚层面移除形状先验解；(3) 验证阶段：MI与UQ诊断结果收敛于同一失败点，且UQ通过剔除高不确定性像素可显著提升PhiCalNet性能。

Result: 在1.5-2.1 m的逼真合成基准（15,600张条纹图，50个物体）上：基线UNet的物体平均绝对误差（MAE）为14.54 mm；PhiCalNet将其降至4.46 mm（降低3.3倍）；残差主要由包裹相位±π跳变处的0.103%像素贡献；像素级保形UQ验证了诊断：剔除前5%高不确定性像素后，PhiCalNet的RMSE降低64%（20.6→7.4 mm），而基线仅降低3.5%。对比实验表明：在深度回归网络上施加物理信息损失的软惩罚无显著提升，证明架构是决定性因素。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性与保形不确定性量化可作为收敛性诊断工具，共同定位物理失败点；架构设计比损失函数设计更关键——从假设空间中架构性移除错误解（而非通过惩罚抑制）是有效的修复策略；PhiCalNet有效解决了远距离单次FPP的深度估计退化问题，且UQ框架可提供可靠的像素级不确定性估计。

Original Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

中文摘要翻译: 基于学习的单次曝光条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）主要集中于近距离场景的研究。远距离（1 m以上）场景仍未得到有效解决：强度平方反比衰减降低了条纹信噪比并导致物理真值退化；单次曝光问题是病态的，因为单幅图像中缺失条纹阶次信息；此外，现有架构尚未得到机理层面的研究。我们提出了一项诊断-修复-验证研究，采用机械可解释性（MI）和保形不确定性量化（UQ）作为收敛性诊断工具：两者一致指向同一个物理失败点，从而驱动并验证了架构修复。在逼真合成基准（15,600张条纹图，1.5-2.1 m距离下的50个物体）上，最佳UNet基线的物体平均绝对误差（MAE）为14.54 mm。三种探针（线性探针、Grad-CAM、平坦域分布外测试）收敛一致：基线通过目标边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码来求解任务。我们通过PhiCalNet修复此问题，该网络输出包裹相位而非深度，并应用一个固定的可微分标定层将相位映射为深度，从架构层面（而非通过损失惩罚）将形状先验解从假设空间中移除。在深度回归网络上施加相同物理约束的物理信息损失作为软惩罚，未带来可测量的增益，从而隔离出架构起决定性作用。PhiCalNet将物体MAE降低3.3倍至4.46 mm；残差由±π包裹跳变处的0.103%像素承担。像素级保形UQ证实了诊断结果：按快照不一致性剔除前5%物体像素后，PhiCalNet的RMSE降低64%（20.6→7.4 mm），而基线仅降低3.5%。MI与UQ收敛于同一失败点。

</details>


### [98] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的临床时间序列生成方法，能够联合建模实验室检测值及其非随机缺失模式，在DACMI基准上验证了生成数据与真实患者轨迹的高度匹配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检测采集时间不规则，检测缺失本身蕴含临床决策和患者生理信息，直接建模缺失模式而非将其视为预处理噪声至关重要。

Method: 扩展TimeDiff框架，通过互补扩散目标联合学习连续实验室检测值和离散缺失模式；将图表时间对齐为4小时间隔，入院分割为7天窗口，生成每对检测值与观测指示符的轨迹。

Result: 生成数据在单个实验室分布和联合值-缺失嵌入上均与真实患者轨迹高度匹配，证明扩散模型能捕获MNAR缺失下患者生理与临床检测行为之间的临床有意义依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为构建临床基础模型的初始组件，通过生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验，为后续训练Prior-Data Fitted Networks奠定基础。

Original Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

中文摘要翻译: 电子健康记录中的实验室检测数据采集时间不规则，检测医嘱的缺失本身与检测值一样具有信息量。这种缺失反映了临床医生的决策和患者的生理状态，因此直接对其进行建模而非将其视为预处理伪影至关重要。本文提出了一种基于扩散的临床时间序列生成方法，利用基于MIMIC-III构建的公共数据挑战基准（DACMI），联合建模实验室检测值及其观测模式。为保留真实采样特性，我们将图表时间对齐为4小时间隔，并将入院记录分割为7天窗口，从而生成每条实验室检测值与对应观测指示符配对的轨迹。采用标准化变换与归一化以稳定训练过程。我们的方法扩展了TimeDiff框架，通过互补的扩散目标学习连续实验室值和离散缺失模式。实验表明，生成数据在单个实验室分布及联合值-缺失嵌入上均与真实患者轨迹高度匹配，证明扩散模型能够捕获临床上有意义的依赖关系——在非随机缺失条件下，患者生理与临床医生检测行为之间的关联。这些初步结果表明，我们的模型可作为开发临床基础模型的初始组件。通过生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验，本文为后续训练能够利用信息性缺失的Prior-Data Fitted Networks提供了基础，这将在扩展工作中进一步研究。

</details>


### [99] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer在预填充阶段已将结论写入下游token的KV缓存，而字段自身的KV对最终决策贡献不足1%。基于此，提出可编辑+可组合的KV缓存机制：通过追加勘误修改模型行为，以及将预编译技能位置可移植地插入任意上下文，实现与全量重计算决策一致但延迟显著降低的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前缀缓存仅能复用完全共享的前缀，任何字段变更都会使下游缓存全部失效。覆盖字段自身的KV向量后复用其余部分，模型仍基于旧值运行。本文旨在从因果层面揭示这一现象的原因，并据此设计更高效的KV缓存编辑与组合方法。

Method: 在四个模型家族上进行因果分析，发现模型的KV缓存可视为一份"备忘录化的结论"（notebook of memoized conclusions）。基于此提出：(1) 编辑能力：通过追加式勘误（append-only erratum）修改下游结论，结合思维链（CoT）可在极低计算开销下恢复正确决策；(2) 组合能力：利用位置可移植性（position-portable），将预编译技能的KV进行RoPE重新定位后拼接到任意上下文中，实现O(L)而非O(L^2)的首token延迟。

Result: 编辑能力：在8B模型上以约1%计算量达到1.00决策恢复准确率；组合能力：在12个模型上logit余弦相似度0.90-0.999，统一编辑+组合代理在延迟降低高达14.9倍的同时保持决策与重计算一致；在线vLLM基准测试中保持98.5%的前缀缓存命中率，p90首token延迟降低53-398倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的KV缓存编辑与组合方法是有效且高效的，适用于多种模型规模、量化方案、MoE架构和多模态缓存，可通过小型适配器扩展到多种注意力变体。由于勘误是追加式的，能与生产级前缀缓存无缝兼容。

Original Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

中文摘要翻译: 前缀缓存仅在精确共享的前缀间复用预填充结果，因此单个字段的变更会导致整个下游缓存失效。然而，覆盖该字段自身的键/值向量并复用其余部分后，模型仍基于旧值运行。本文在四个模型家族上建立了因果机制：在预填充阶段，模型已将基于原始字段得出的结论写入了下游token的KV缓存；而该字段自身的键/值对驱动决策的比例不足1%。如果将KV缓存视为一份"备忘录化的结论笔记本"，则衍生出两种能力：(1) 可编辑性。通过追加式勘误修改笔记内容；结合思维链（CoT）时，仅编辑字段即可恢复正确决策（在8B模型上达到1.00准确率，仅需约1%计算量），而无CoT时勘误会被忽略。(2) 可组合性。笔记具有位置可移植性，因此预编译的技能可经过RoPE重新定位后拼接到任意上下文中，在O(L)而非O(L^2)的首token延迟下与全量重计算不可区分（12个模型上logit余弦相似度0.90-0.999）。统一的编辑+组合代理在保持决策与重计算一致的同时，延迟降低高达14.9倍。该方法适用于任何逐token注意力的KV缓存，并在多种模型规模、量化方案、混合专家架构和多模态缓存上得到验证，通过小型适配器可扩展到多种注意力变体。由于勘误是追加式的，该方法可与生产级前缀缓存兼容：在在线vLLM基准测试中保持98.5%的缓存命中率，p90首token延迟降低53-398倍。

</details>


### [100] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了InferBERT框架中不同模型选择对因果药物不良事件（ADE）检测的影响，发现领域特异性预训练（BioBERT）优于简单基线（XGBoost）和大规模LLM（Med-LLaMA），且后验校准效果有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: InferBERT框架将Transformer模型与Do-calculus相结合用于因果ADE检测，但其成功依赖于底层分类模型的选择。目前尚不清楚：更简单的模型是否足够、领域特异性预训练是否有帮助、扩展到LLM能否改善因果检测，以及后验校准的效果如何。

Method: 在AILF（镇痛药诱导的急性肝衰竭）和TRAM（曲马多相关死亡率）两个基准数据集上，对四种模型（XGBoost基线、ALBERT原始InferBERT、BioBERT生物医学Transformer、Med-LLaMA医学大语言模型）进行20次重复的5折交叉验证比较，测量准确性、校准误差（ECE）以及因果术语与传统药物警戒信号（PRR、ROR、EBGM）的Jaccard一致性。

Result: BioBERT在两个数据集上均取得最高准确率；Med-LLaMA尽管规模更大且采用参数高效微调，仍表现不佳；领域特异性预训练是关键因素；校准改善了ECE但对准确率和因果发现影响不一；BioBERT与传统药物警戒信号的一致性最强。

Conclusion: 领域特异性预训练相比简单基线和更大规模LLM具有明显优势。投资于可管理的、领域感知的模型比单纯扩大模型规模对计算药物警戒更有效。

Original Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

中文摘要翻译: 区分因果性药物不良事件（ADE）与虚假相关性仍是药物警戒中的核心挑战。InferBERT框架整合了Transformer模型与Do-calculus，但其成功依赖于底层分类模型的选择。本研究评估了InferBERT中模型选择的影响，考察了更简单的模型是否足够、领域特异性预训练是否有帮助、扩展到LLM能否改善因果检测，以及后验校准的效果。我们在两个基准数据集上进行了比较研究：镇痛药诱导的急性肝衰竭（AILF）和曲马多相关死亡率（TRAM）。评估了四种模型——XGBoost（基线）、ALBERT（原始InferBERT）、BioBERT（生物医学Transformer）和Med-LLaMA（医学大语言模型），采用20次重复的5折交叉验证。我们测量了准确性、等渗回归前后的预期校准误差（ECE），以及因果术语与PRR、ROR和EBGM的Jaccard一致性；显著性采用配对t检验。BioBERT在两个数据集上均取得了最高准确率，而Med-LLaMA尽管规模庞大且采用了参数高效微调，仍表现不佳。领域特异性预训练是关键因素。校准改善了ECE，但对准确率和因果发现具有混合影响。BioBERT的优越性也体现在与传统药物警戒信号的最强一致性上。这些结果表明，领域特异性预训练相比简单基线和更大规模LLM具有明显优势。对于计算药物警戒而言，投资于可管理的、领域感知的模型比单纯扩大模型规模更为有效。

</details>


### [101] [Probing, Fusion, and Trustworthiness: A Systematic Evaluation of Foundation Model Representations for Multimodal Cancer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17115)
*Jingyu Hu,Giuseppe Tripodi,Reed Naidoo,Sarah F. McGough,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基础模型（FMs）在计算病理学任务中，面对分布偏移的真实世界商业队列（IH-BC和IH-NSCLC）时的泛化能力。研究涵盖全切片图像和转录组两种模态，发现图像与组学表征互补；多模态融合仅在单模态信号不占优时提升性能；保形预测表明多数预测失败时真实诊断仍可在预测集中恢复，验证了不确定性感知推理在临床辅助中的价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基础模型虽已成为强大的医学数据表征提取器，但其在分布偏移数据集上的泛化能力尚未得到充分探索，现有研究缺乏对多模态融合及预测可信度的系统性评估。

Method: 基于两个真实世界商业队列（IH-BC、IH-NSCLC），对五种基础模型在八个下游分类任务上进行单模态探测（解冻线性分类头）；比较三种图像-组学融合策略在配对表征上的效果；利用保形预测评估所选单模态和多模态管线的可信度。

Result: 1) FM表征在分布外数据上取得了有竞争力的性能；2) 图像与组学表征携带互补的预测信号；3) 多模态融合主要在无单一模态主导信号时带来额外增益；4) 保形预测显示，在点预测失败的多数案例中，真实诊断仍可在预测集合内恢复。

Conclusion: 基础模型在分布偏移下的病理数据中表现良好，多模态融合需权衡模态互补性，不确定性感知推理（保形预测）对临床支持系统具有重要价值。

Original Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型已成为医学数据中强大的表征提取器，但其在分布偏移数据集上的泛化能力仍未被充分探索。本工作在来自许可内部（IH）肿瘤学数据集的两个真实世界商业队列（IH-BC和IH-NSCLC）上，系统评估了基于基础模型的表征在一组计算病理学任务中的表现。分析聚焦于IH多模态数据中的两种模态——全切片图像与转录组图谱。我们首先在八个下游分类任务上对五种基础模型进行了单模态探测性能基准测试，发现图像与组学表征携带互补的预测信号。随后，通过比较三种基于配对表征的图像-组学融合策略，探究多模态融合是否能在单模态基线之上带来额外增益。进一步通过保形预测评估所选单模态与多模态管线的可信度。结果表明，基础模型表征在分布外数据上取得了有竞争力的性能，且多模态融合主要在没有单一模态主导信号时发挥作用。保形预测揭示，在点预测失败的多数案例中，真实诊断仍可在预测集合内恢复，这进一步验证了不确定性感知推理在临床支持中的价值。

</details>


### [102] [MODE: Modality-Decomposed Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17118)
*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MODE框架，通过模态分解和冗余视觉令牌过滤，解决MoE-MLLM量化中视觉令牌主导专家重要性估计的偏差，实现专家级混合精度量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有专家级混合精度量化方法在MoE-MLLM上效果不佳，原因在于专家重要性估计存在两个被忽视的偏差：(1) 跨模态层面，视觉令牌数量占优导致其主导专家选择频率，掩盖了对文本模态重要的专家；(2) 视觉内部层面，大量冗余视觉令牌进一步扭曲频率统计，掩盖了对信息性视觉内容重要的专家。

Method: 提出MODE框架：(1) 按模态分解专家选择频率；(2) 过滤冗余视觉令牌得到去噪后的视觉频率；(3) 逐模态评估量化敏感性作为频率估计的补充信号；(4) 将这些信号集成到整数线性规划中，在给定预算下为每个专家分配位宽。

Result: MODE在W3A16设置下平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内，在极端2比特设置下获得更大优势，实验表明其特别适合MoE-MLLM。

Conclusion: 通过模态分解和去噪的专家重要性估计，结合整数线性规划，MODE有效解决了MoE-MLLM的压缩问题，实现了低精度下的高性能保留。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家多模态大语言模型（MoE-MLLM）虽然性能卓越，但GPU内存成本过高，使得压缩成为必要。在后训练量化方法中，专家级混合精度量化已被证明对MoE-LLM有效，但由于专家重要性估计中两个被忽视的偏差，该方法在MoE-MLLM上性能显著下降：(1) 在跨模态层面，视觉令牌的数量优势导致专家选择频率被视觉令牌主导，掩盖了对文本模态至关重要的专家；(2) 在视觉内部层面，大量冗余视觉令牌进一步扭曲频率统计，掩盖了对信息性视觉内容至关重要的专家。为弥补这些差距，我们提出MODE，一种面向MoE-MLLM的模态分解专家级混合精度量化框架。该框架按模态分解专家选择频率，过滤冗余视觉令牌以获得去噪后的视觉频率，并进一步逐模态评估量化敏感性，作为基于频率估计的补充信号。这些信号被集成到整数线性规划公式中，在给定预算下为每个专家分配位宽。大量实验表明，MODE特别适合MoE-MLLM，在W3A16设置下平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内，在极端2比特设置下获得更大优势。

</details>


### [103] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了深度神经网络中“grokking”（突然泛化）现象与L2正则化引起的一阶相变中的迟滞效应有关，证明了模型在亚稳态势阱中被困后，通过SGD噪声驱动逃逸实现延迟泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解释深度神经网络中grokking现象的起源，即模型在长时间过拟合后突然出现泛化能力的机制尚不明确，作者希望通过线性DNN模型揭示其背后的物理本质。

Method: 利用线性DNN分析L2正则化强度变化下的一阶相变和迟滞效应；通过L2正则化人为将模型困在低精度亚稳态，观察SGD噪声驱动模型跨越能垒的逃逸过程及Arrhenius标度律；使用稀疏子采样复现经典grokking曲线。

Result: 发现grokking与L2相变中的迟滞现象一致：模型在亚稳态中逃逸时间服从Arrhenius标度律；在逃逸时间跨越两个数量级时复现了延迟收敛；亚稳态数量等于可学习特征数（数据协方差奇异值个数），迟滞潜力随任务复杂度自然增长；非线性DNN中同样存在该机制。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络中grokking现象源于L2正则化导致的一阶相变中的迟滞效应，这为设计更高效的学习策略提供了理论依据和可行路径。

Original Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络（DNN）在L2正则化强度变化下表现出第一阶相变，每个相变标志着新可学习特征的涌现。在临界正则化强度以下，所有特征原则上都是可学习的，但共存的亚稳态被能量势垒分隔，可能困住网络并阻碍收敛。DNN的一个优势是其泛化能力，但许多开放问题仍然存在，其中包括所谓的grokking现象：长时间表面上的过拟合之后突然延迟出现的泛化。我们在线性DNN中证明，grokking与L2一阶相变中的迟滞现象一致：通过使用L2正则化进行人为困陷，我们展示了低精度亚稳态中的模型只有在SGD噪声驱动其跨越能量势垒时才能逃逸，且逃逸时间服从Arrhenius标度律。我们通过将模型故意困在亚稳态中，在跨越两个数量级的逃逸时间上复现了类似grokking的延迟收敛。利用稀疏子采样，我们还复现了测试误差最终接近训练误差的经典grokking曲线。我们的研究表明，亚稳态的数量等于可学习特征的数量——每个数据协方差的奇异值对应一个——迟滞的潜力随任务复杂度自然增长。我们提供了证据表明相同机制很可能在通用非线性DNN中同样存在。我们的结果为设计更高效的学习方案提供了路径。

</details>


### [104] [Towards Fast GNN Surrogates for CO2 Migration in Complex Geological Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17180)
*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的图神经网络替代模型，用于预测CO₂在地质封存中的羽流运移，在SPE11A基准上取得了具有竞争力的结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统数值模拟CO₂地质封存中的多相流计算成本高，亟需一种数据驱动的机器学习方法在保证物理准确性的同时提升预测效率。

Method: 将SPE11A基准重构为图结构，节点代表计算单元，边编码基于传导率的交互及几何属性；采用各向异性消息传递机制（通过几何条件边嵌入计算交互权重）捕获方向性输运；在潜空间中使用自回归残差公式进行时序建模，并采用多步监督训练。

Result: 模型在CO₂饱和度与液相密度预测上表现出色，长时间预测范围内累积误差保持适度水平。

Conclusion: 该图神经网络替代模型能够有效再现复杂地质多相流的物理行为，为CO₂地质封存监测提供高效可靠的预测工具。

Original Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

中文摘要翻译: 本章探讨了数据驱动的机器学习方法如何再现复杂地质构造中多相流的物理行为。我们提出了一种端到端的图神经替代模型，专门用于地质封存中CO₂羽流运移预测。该方法在SPE11A基准上进行了评估，该基准是评估CO₂封存场景的知名行业测试案例，其特征包括尖锐的气-水界面、强平流输运以及伴随指进现象的快速对流混合。该基准被重构为一张图，其中节点代表计算单元，边编码基于传导率的交互，并辅以几何属性。由网格几何、渗透率对比以及地质非均质性引起的方向性输运通过各向异性消息传递机制捕获，其中交互权重通过几何条件边嵌入计算，使消息聚合偏向于物理相关的输运方向。时间演化在潜空间中使用自回归残差公式建模，并采用多步监督训练。该模型在CO₂饱和度和液相密度（CO₂封存监测的关键指标）上产生了具有竞争力的预测，且在较长的预测时间范围内累积误差保持适度水平。

</details>


### [105] [Verified Detection and Prevention of Concurrency Anomalies in Multi-Agent Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17182)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多智能体LLM系统中的四种并发异常（陈旧生成、幻影工具、因果级联、工具效应重排序），构建了从L0到L4的机械验证的一致性层级，并通过三个部署的Rust运行时实现了该层级的不同级别，同时修复了字节跳动deer-flow和LangGraph中的实际并发问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过内存存储、向量索引和工具注册表共享状态，这类共享操作在确定性重放引擎下存在并发一致性问题，但缺乏形式化的异常分类和一致性模型验证。

Method: 在TLA+中形式化四种并发异常，通过274个Verus验证义务（零assume、零admit）证明检测器的正确性和完备性；使用三个已部署的Rust运行时（悲观锁、可序列化快照隔离、默认SI）实现L0-L1，并通过执行模式验证和依赖无关预防方法实现L2-L4。

Result: L0-L1通过悲观锁和可序列化快照隔离实现并验证；L2-L4通过执行模式验证，预防方法A3、A6、A2达到0/1000与1000/1000对比效果；L2在三个模型家族上实时运行，120个撤回会话中全部预防A3；修复了字节跳动deer-flow的静默丢失更新和LangGraph的ToolNode工具效应重排序问题。

Conclusion: 提出了首个针对多智能体LLM运行时的一致性层级机械验证框架，形式化了经典并发异常的结构类比，并通过实际系统验证了其有效性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

中文摘要翻译: 多智能体LLM系统通过内存存储、向量索引和工具注册表共享状态。我们将此类共享建模为确定性生成语义下的长期读-生成-写操作——这是持久化执行引擎通过确定性重放所强制执行的机制——并在TLA+中形式化了四种并发异常：陈旧生成、幻影工具、因果级联和工具效应重排序，它们分别是经典隔离异常的结构类比，每个异常均附带TLC反例。这些异常上的排除格是平凡的；本文的贡献在于机械验证了该格中一个极大链的可实现性和严格分离性，即 $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$，据我们所知，这是针对此类运行时的首个机器检查的一致性层级。通过274个Verus验证义务（零assume、零admit；信任基础：两个结构公理和一个互斥对应关系）证明了检测器相对于规约的完备性和正确性，以及每个运行时对其规避集合的完备性。三个已部署的Rust运行时实现了L0-L1（悲观锁定、可序列化快照隔离、默认SI），每个均已通过陈旧生成验证并精化到其状态机；L2-L4通过执行模式验证，并采用依赖无关的预防伴随方法（A3、A6、A2：0/1000对比1000/1000），L2在三个模型家族上实时运行（所有120个撤回会话中均预防了A3）。我们复现了字节跳动deer-flow中的静默丢失更新，将其修复形式化为已验证的 $L_0 \to L_1$ 精化，并在LangGraph的ToolNode中展示了未经修改输出上的工具效应重排序，该问题通过L3提交排序器消除。本文的贡献在于经过验证的检测器、精化过程和可实现性工件；所述现象和格是经典的。

</details>


### [106] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Finsler拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络架构，证明其离散估计在点云采样中收敛于流形上的真实算子，并能在实践中恢复非线性扩散方程的几何结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于图拉普拉斯的图神经网络仅能逼近各向同性的Laplace-Beltrami算子，无法处理各向异性算子，限制了其表达能力。

Method: 利用Finsler拉普拉斯算子的离散估计替代Laplace-Beltrami算子，证明该离散估计在流形点云采样数量增加时收敛于真实算子，并将其表达为图神经网络层，构建Finslerian图神经网络家族。

Result: 理论证明了离散估计的收敛性；实验表明Finslerian图神经网络能够恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构。

Conclusion: Finslerian图神经网络突破了传统图神经网络仅能处理各向同性算子的局限，能够表达Finsler几何并处理非线性扩散问题，具有更强的几何表达能力。

Original Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

中文摘要翻译: 基于图拉普拉斯的图神经网络架构近似于Laplace-Beltrami算子，因此限制了它们在各向同性算子上的应用。作为Laplace-Beltrami算子的非线性替代，我们考虑在流形采样的点云上估计Finsler拉普拉斯算子。我们证明了随着点样本数量的增加，这些离散估计收敛于流形上的真实算子。此外，我们展示了该算子可以表达为图神经网络层，并据此定义了一个受约束以表达Finsler几何的Finslerian图神经网络家族。我们证明，Finslerian图神经网络在实践中能够恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构。

</details>


### [107] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于原对偶推理（PDI）的约束扩散模型，用于从具有平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的最优分布中采样。PDI在逆扩散过程中联合推断最优原始分布及其参数化对偶变量，通过条件分数网络和动态对偶更新实现约束采样。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的约束扩散模型通常先估计对偶乘子再冻结它进行采样，这无法适应生成过程中的约束动态变化，导致采样分布偏离最优。

Method: 将约束采样形式化为拉格朗日对偶域中的Gibbs分布采样；训练一个条件于对偶变量的分数网络，覆盖推理过程中遇到的所有Gibbs分布族；每个逆扩散步骤先使用当前对偶乘子的分数场去噪，再根据去噪样本的约束违反程度通过对偶上升更新乘子。

Result: 理论上证明了推理轨迹上对偶变量的时间平均收敛到对偶最优解的邻域，并给出了残差对偶失配对终端分布影响的界；实验上在高斯混合约束采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理三个任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: PDI通过联合推断原变量和对偶变量，能够有效解决带平均约束的熵正则化优化问题中的约束采样，理论分析和实验均证明了其收敛性和实用性。

Original Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于原对偶推理（Primal-Dual Inference, PDI）的约束扩散模型，用于从具有平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的最优分布中采样。我们在拉格朗日对偶域中形式化约束采样问题，其中最优分布的形式是由最优对偶变量索引的Gibbs分布。不同于在采样前先估计该对偶乘子并使其在生成过程中保持不变，PDI联合推断最优原始分布及其参数化对偶变量。每个逆扩散步骤利用当前乘子对应的分数场进行去噪，然后根据去噪样本的约束违反估计值通过对偶上升更新乘子。为了实现这种条件分数场，我们训练了一个单一的条件于对偶变量的分数网络，该网络覆盖推理过程中遇到的对偶变量所诱导的Gibbs分布族。我们证明了沿着推理轨迹生成的对偶变量的时间平均收敛到对偶最优解的邻域，并通过依赖于调度计划的稳定性因子界定了残差对偶失配对终端分布的影响。我们在高斯混合约束采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理三个任务上评估了PDI的性能。

</details>


### [108] [PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199)
*Anhao Zhao,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型的标准在线蒸馏（OPD）中，基于学生采样token的逆KL估计器存在样本效率低、生成不稳定、性能差等问题，根源在于log-ratio奖励无界导致梯度方差极大。为此提出PowerOPD，通过Box-Cox幂变换构建原生有界、符号一致的奖励族，在六个数学推理基准上显著提升性能，同时降低计算开销和显存占用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）中使用学生采样token的蒙特卡洛估计器虽然避免了全词表计算，但实践中存在严重的训练病理问题：样本效率低、生成动态不稳定、与全词表OPD性能差距大。通过奖励层面的诊断发现，这些问题的根源在于log-ratio奖励天然无界，导致梯度方差极高且在训练早期就集中于首位位置，而标准的后处理缩放方法在失真发生后才能作用，效果有限。

Method: 提出PowerOPD，基于Box-Cox幂变换构建一族原生有界、符号一致的奖励函数，由参数α>0控制。log-ratio奖励是该族在α→0时的退化极限。PowerOPD通过幂变换将无界的log-ratio奖励映射为有界奖励，从根源上解决梯度方差过大的问题。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准和四个Qwen3师生模型对上，PowerOPD相比原始OPD在Avg@8/Pass@8上分别提升+6.37/+5.71，相比后处理稳定方法提升+3.01/+3.54，相比全词表OPD提升+2.59/+8.90；同时减少59.2%的墙钟时间和23.1%的峰值GPU显存。较大的α值通常能提升准确率、缩短响应长度，并将梯度范数比原始OPD降低3000倍以上。

Conclusion: PowerOPD通过原生有界的奖励设计，从根本上解决了在线蒸馏中梯度方差过大的问题，在多个基准上显著优于原始OPD、后处理稳定方法和全词表OPD，同时大幅降低了计算资源和时间开销，是一种高效且稳定的在线蒸馏方法。

Original Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

中文摘要翻译: 针对大语言模型的标准在线蒸馏（OPD）通过使用学生采样的token来估计逆KL目标，得到一个无偏的单样本蒙特卡洛估计器，从而避免了全词表的计算。然而，我们证明该估计器在实践中存在严重的训练病理问题：样本效率低下、生成动态不稳定，以及与精确全词表OPD之间巨大的性能差距。奖励层面的诊断将这些病理追溯至log-ratio奖励——该奖励因构造天然无界，产生集中在早期位置且在整个训练过程中持续存在的极高方差梯度；标准的后处理缩放方法仅在失真发生后才能作用，因此效果有限。为解决此问题，我们提出PowerOPD：一个基于Box-Cox幂变换的原生有界、符号一致的奖励函数族，由参数α>0控制，其中log-ratio奖励是该族在α→0时的退化极限。在六个数学推理基准和四个Qwen3师生模型对上，PowerOPD相比原始OPD在基准平均的Avg@8/Pass@8上分别获得高达+6.37/+5.71的提升，相比后处理稳定方法提升+3.01/+3.54，相比全词表OPD提升+2.59/+8.90，同时减少59.2%的墙钟时间和23.1%的峰值GPU显存。较大的α值通常能提升准确率，一致地缩短响应长度，并将梯度范数保持为比原始OPD小3000倍以上。

</details>


### [109] [Sum-of-Squares Degree Barriers for the Reweighted-Hinge Method in Robust Halfspace Learning: A Christoffel-Function Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17215)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了异常值去除证书的盲区与干净边际的Christoffel函数之间的精确对应关系，并将其作为重加权铰链方法（Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025）在恶意噪声下鲁棒学习γ-间隔半空间的组织原则。由此推导出三个推论：间隔-度数折中、度-2异常值屏障和度-2t算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动机在于理解并量化基于低阶矩的异常值去除证书的固有限制——即攻击者如何利用证书的盲区隐藏破坏，以及这一盲区与Christoffel函数之间的精确关系。

Method: 方法是将异常值去除证书的盲区大小与干净边际分布的Christoffel函数建立精确对应，以此作为重加权铰链方法的组织原则，分析Sum-of-Squares（SoS）证书度与可隐藏腐败质量之间的关系。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 间隔-度数折中：将稠密薄饼认证到误差ε需要SoS度数Ω(log(1/ε))或间隔Ω(√log(1/ε)/√d)；(2) 度-2异常值屏障：度2被卡在η^{1/2}，而度4可突破；(3) 度-2t算法追踪前沿η^{1-1/2t}，常数被稠密薄饼密度限制且不可改进。

Conclusion: 结论是Christoffel函数精确刻画了低度数认证证书的盲区大小，揭示了基于证书方法的固有限制，并解释了Shen (2025)中观察到的间隔-度数折中现象是必然的。

Original Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove. We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

中文摘要翻译: 一个去除异常值的证书只能通过低阶矩来观察数据，而攻击者恰恰利用了这一特性，将破坏隐藏在清洁数据看起来本就典型的地方——即任何有界度数测试都无法分辨的盲区。这个盲区恰好有一个精确的尺寸：清洁边际分布的Christoffel函数，这正是现代数据分析中用于检测异常值的量，此处从攻击者角度解读为有界度数证书无法去除的腐败量。我们将这一反转作为恶意噪声下鲁棒学习γ-间隔半空间的重加权铰链方法（Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025）的组织原则：支配性资源是异常值去除证书的Sum-of-Squares度数，而解析原则指出，在中心点c处能从度数-2t证书中隐藏的最大腐败质量恰好是清洁边际分布的Christoffel函数λ_{t+1}(c)。由此产生三个推论，均针对证书方法（而非信息论意义）。间隔-度数折中：将稠密薄饼认证到误差ε需要SoS度数Ω(log(1/ε))或间隔Ω(√log(1/ε)/√d)，这解释了Shen (2025)中记录的log(1/ε)间隔是必然的，其中加权Chebyshev约化使得阈值2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)在经典加权极值估计意义下是紧的。度-2异常值屏障：解析原则实现为一个显式实例，其中度2被卡在η^{1/2}而度4可逃脱，将方法的小崩溃率定位在度数而非分析中。度-2t算法追踪前沿η^{1-1/2t}（在t=1时恢复Shen (2025)的结果），其增益是一个显式常数，被稠密薄饼密度所限制，并由度-2屏障证明不可改进。

</details>


### [110] [Rift: A Conflict Signature for Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17229)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现，模型在明知真相却故意说谎时，其前向传播过程中会留下一种“冲突信号”——与同等错误但无知识冲突的情况相比，残差流秩显著升高（2.1-2.3倍）。该信号可跨架构、跨语言、跨任务检测欺骗，且具有只读性（可检测但不可注入），为对齐领域中的“知错故意说谎”问题提供了内部监控方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ELK（Eliciting Latent Knowledge）问题的核心困境在于：当模型明知真相却故意说谎时，单纯的行为评估无法区分谎言与诚实错误。作者试图探究这种欺骗是否会在模型内部留下可检测的信号，从而与无知识冲突的错误区分开。

Method: 通过构造两种对照条件隔离“知识冲突”这一变量：(1) 睡眠代理（sleeper agent）——模型先经诚实训练，再被微调为在特定触发条件下说谎；(2) 天真说谎者（naive liar）——模型直接微调输出相同的错误答案，从未经过诚实训练。两者输出完全一致，唯一差异在于是否存在知识冲突。在此基础上测量残差流秩（residual rank）作为冲突信号指标。

Result: (1) 欺骗性前向传播的残差秩比天真说谎者高2.1-2.3倍，可100%准确识别谎言（无需标签）；(2) 在Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B和Phi-3-mini上，被指示欺骗在所有测试事实上均提升残差秩；(3) 在Phi-3上，谎言与诚实回答和幻觉完全分离（AUC 1.0）；(4) 信号在模型自创谎言、主动隐藏、长度控制等条件下依然稳健（AUC 1.0）；(5) 基于无基相对表示，探针可在不同模型家族间零样本迁移（平均AUC 0.933），跨五语言AUC 1.0；(6) 信号只读——可检测但不可注入（8/8方向均失败）。

Conclusion: 模型在明知真相时故意说谎会在内部留下可检测且稳健的冲突信号（以残差流秩升高为特征），该信号具有跨模型、跨语言、跨任务的迁移能力且只读不可注入，为破解ELK困境提供了一种基于内部信号而非行为评估的可行路径。

Original Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

中文摘要翻译: 一个在明知真相的情况下说谎的模型，是ELK（Eliciting Latent Knowledge）问题中单靠行为评估无法处理的核心情形。我们探究这种欺骗是否会在内部留下可将其与诚实错误区分开的信号。我们的关键策略是构建一个针对“错误性”（wrongness）的控制项：我们将一个睡眠代理（sleeper agent，知晓真相，在触发条件下说谎）与一个天真说谎者（naive liar，被微调输出相同错误答案且从未经过诚实训练）进行对比。两者产生的错误输出完全相同；任何差异仅源于知识冲突，而非答案本身的错误性。我们发现，欺骗性的前向传播携带着一种冲突信号——对于同一个错误答案，睡眠代理的残差秩（residual rank）比天真说谎者高2.1-2.3倍——这一信号足以在无需任何标签的情况下，以100%的准确率识别两个回答中哪一个是谎言，该结果在GPT-2 small/medium（三个随机种子）和三个指导模型（instruct models）上均得到验证。在Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B和Phi-3-mini上，被指导的欺骗行为在每个被测试事实上都提升了残差秩（18/18，40/40，34/34）；在Phi-3上，谎言与诚实回答和幻觉完全分离（AUC 1.0，Wilcoxon p~6e-11）。该信号在策略性自构欺骗（模型自行编造谎言，AUC 1.0）、主动隐藏尝试（AUC 1.0）以及长度控制复制（20/20，AUC 1.0，p~1e-6）中依然存在。利用无基相对表示（basis-free relative representations），在一个模型家族上训练的探针能够零样本检测另外两个家族中的欺骗（平均AUC 0.933），在同时改变架构和格式的情况下依然有效（AUC 0.821），并且可在五种语言间迁移（AUC 1.0，长度控制）。该信号是只读的：可检测但不可注入（双向均为0/8）。诚实的局限性说明及六项消融实验已在文中完整呈现。

</details>


### [111] [Uncertainty Quantification of Engineering Structures by Polynomial Chaos Expansion and Multivariate Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17233)
*Qitian Lu,Jafar Jafari-Asl,Panagiotis Spyridis,Lukas Novak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多输出（向量值QoIs）的代理模型构建问题，提出了一种自适应序贯采样方法，以平衡输入空间探索与多输出方差聚合信息，提升多项式混沌展开代理模型的精度与稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在工程应用中，单个高保真模型常产生多个感兴趣量（QoIs），但单个实验设计（ED）难以同时充分表征所有输出，尤其当不同输出对输入变量敏感度不同时。分别对每个输出单独采样虽直观，但会增加采样复杂度与计算成本，且忽视输出间潜在相关性，损害数据一致性。因此，需要一种面向多输出场景的自适应序贯采样策略。

Method: 提出了一种面向向量值QoIs的自适应序贯采样方法，用于构建多项式混沌展开（PCE）代理模型。该方法从候选池中依次选取新样本，选取依据为样本对输出方差的局部贡献，同时平衡基于距离的输入空间探索（exploration）与跨所有输出的聚合方差信息利用（exploitation）。

Result: 通过多个工程问题的数值算例，将所提方法与非序贯的拉丁超立方采样（LHS）进行对比。数值结果表明，所提策略提高了代理模型的精度和稳定性，并提供了更可靠的二阶统计量估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应序贯采样方法能够有效改进多输出场景下PCE代理模型的准确性与鲁棒性，是对传统单输出序贯采样策略的成功推广。

Original Abstract: In many engineering applications, a single high-fidelity model produces multiple quantities of interest (QoIs) under the same input parameters, e.g. finite element models of complex physical systems. To alleviate the high computational cost of direct model evaluations, surrogate models are widely used to construct efficient approximations of model responses. Naturally, the accuracy of surrogates strongly depends on the quality of the experimental design (ED). However, a single ED may not provide an adequate representation for all outputs simultaneously, especially when different outputs exhibit varying sensitivities to the input variables. A straightforward solution is to perform separate sampling for each output, but this results in increased sampling complexity and computational cost. From a statistical perspective, such an approach also ignores potential correlations among all outputs and may compromise data consistency. To address this issue, an adaptive sequential sampling method for constructing polynomial chaos expansion surrogate models is generalized for vector valued QoIs. The method sequentially selects new samples from a candidate pool based on their local contribution to the output variance, while balancing distance-based exploration of the input space and exploitation of aggregated variance information across all outputs. Its performance is compared with non-sequential Latin Hypercube Sampling through several numerical examples from engineering problems. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves both surrogate accuracy and stability, and provides a more reliable estimation of second-order statistics.

中文摘要翻译: 在许多工程应用中，单个高保真模型在相同的输入参数下会产生多个感兴趣量（QoIs），例如复杂物理系统的有限元模型。为缓解直接模型评估带来的高计算成本，代理模型被广泛用于构建模型响应的高效近似。自然，代理模型的精度强烈依赖于实验设计（ED）的质量。然而，单一的实验设计可能无法同时充分表征所有输出，尤其当不同输出对输入变量表现出不同敏感性时。一个直接的解决方案是对每个输出分别进行采样，但这会增加采样复杂度和计算成本。从统计角度来看，这种方法还忽略了所有输出之间潜在的相关性，并可能损害数据一致性。为解决该问题，本文将一种用于构建多项式混沌展开代理模型的自适应序贯采样方法推广至向量值QoIs。该方法根据候选样本对输出方差的局部贡献，依次从候选池中选取新样本，同时平衡基于距离的输入空间探索与跨所有输出的聚合方差信息的利用。通过若干工程问题的数值算例，将该方法的性能与非序贯的拉丁超立方采样进行了比较。数值结果表明，所提出的策略同时提升了代理模型的精度和稳定性，并提供了更可靠的二阶统计量估计。

</details>


### [112] [Rethinking Groups in Critic-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17250)
*Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Lingfeng Xiao,Muzhi Li,Xinyu Wang,Yingxue Zhang,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出负令牌过滤策略，用单次采样替代分组采样进行优势估计，在不依赖分组的情况下实现稳定强化学习训练，在推理和智能体任务上达到或超越分组方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无评论家强化学习方法需要为同一问题生成多组回滚来估计基线，存在数据效率低、组同步开销大、对结构化回滚不灵活等问题。作者重新审视"组"的作用，发现其核心功能并非估计基线，而是防止对负样本的错误惩罚。
