Method: 提出负令牌过滤（negative token filtering）策略，在单次回滚训练中，通过过滤掉负样本中的错误惩罚信号，实现稳定的优势计算，无需分组采样估计基线。

Result: 将该方法应用于两种批级优势方法，在推理任务上取得与分组RL方法相当的性能，在智能体任务上取得更强的性能。

Conclusion: 组的核心作用是防止负样本被错误惩罚而非估计基线；基于此洞察提出的负令牌过滤可以在单次回滚下稳定训练，提升数据效率和灵活性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for post-training large language models. Existing critic-free RL methods typically generate a group of rollouts for the same question to estimate value baselines for advantage computation. However, this design suffers from data inefficiency, group synchronization barriers, and inflexibility with structured rollouts. In this work, we revisit the role of the ``group'' and show that its underlying function is not merely to estimate baselines but to prevent false penalties on negative samples. Building on this insight, we propose negative token filtering, a simple and effective strategy that enables stable single-rollout training. We apply it to two batch-level advantage methods, achieving comparable performance on reasoning tasks and stronger performance on agentic tasks relative to group-based RL techniques.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习已成为大型语言模型后训练的核心范式。现有的无评论家强化学习方法通常为同一个问题生成多组回滚来估计价值基线，用于优势计算。然而，这种设计存在数据效率低下、组同步障碍以及对结构化回滚缺乏灵活性的问题。在本文中，我们重新审视了"组"的作用，并表明其底层功能并非仅仅是估计基线，而是防止对负样本的错误惩罚。基于这一见解，我们提出了负令牌过滤——一种简单有效的策略，能够实现稳定的单回滚训练。我们将其应用于两种批级优势方法，在推理任务上取得了与基于组的强化学习技术相当的性能，在智能体任务上取得了更强的性能。

</details>


### [113] [ProCUA-SFT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17321)
*Jaehun Jung,Ximing Lu,Brandon Cui,Muhammad Khalifa,Shaokun Zhang,Hao Zhang,Jin Xu,Amala Sanjay Deshmukh,Karan Sapra,Andrew Tao,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz,Mingjie Liu,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCUA-SFT数据集，包含310万个步骤级SFT样本，通过全自动流水线从934个合成轨迹中蒸馏得到，涵盖2484个应用组合。该数据集解决了现有AgentNet数据集在训练计算机使用智能体时导致的负迁移问题，使UI-TARS 7B模型在OSWorld任务上的成功率从训练前的26.3%（训练后降至8-10%）提升至45.0%，提升18.7个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有最大的公共计算机使用轨迹数据集AgentNet（22.5K人类轨迹）用于监督微调时会导致负迁移——在AgentNet上继续训练UI-TARS 7B使其在OSWorld上的成功率从26.3%骤降至8-10%。因此需要构建更高质量、更大规模、且能避免负迁移的训练数据集。

Method: 提出全自动流水线：(i) 在真实桌面环境中合成基于真实世界内容（912个SpreadsheetBench电子表格、约10K个Zenodo10K演示文稿、多应用OSWorld配置）的可执行任务；(ii) 通过二元前置条件检查验证任务可行性后再执行回放；使用单一VLM（Kimi-K2.5）同时作为目标生成器、前置条件判断器和轨迹执行器，消除规划器-执行器能力差距。每个轨迹被扩展为步骤前缀样本，精确复现推理时的上下文布局。

Result: UI-TARS 7B在ProCUA-SFT上微调一个epoch后，OSWorld任务成功率达45.0%，比基线模型提升18.7个百分点，比AgentNet训练版本高出35%以上。ProCUA子集已被纳入Nemotron 3 Nano Omni模型的训练数据，为其计算机使用能力做出贡献。

Conclusion: ProCUA-SFT数据集通过大规模合成轨迹和全自动质量控制流水线，有效解决了计算机使用智能体训练中的数据稀缺和负迁移问题，显著提升了模型在桌面环境中的任务执行能力。

Original Abstract: Training computer-use agents (CUAs) -- models that interact with graphical desktops through screenshots and keyboard/mouse actions -- requires large-scale, diverse trajectory data collected in full desktop environments. The largest public resource, AgentNet (22.5K human trajectories), leads to negative transfer when used for supervised fine-tuning (SFT): continuing training UI-TARS 7B on AgentNet causes OSWorld success rate to fall from 26.3% to 8-10%. We present ProCUA-SFT, a dataset of 3.1M step-level SFT samples distilled from 93K synthetic trajectories across 2,484 application combinations. The dataset is produced by a fully automated pipeline that (i) synthesizes grounded tasks on live desktops seeded with real-world content -- 912 spreadsheets from SpreadsheetBench, approximately 10K permissively-licensed presentations from Zenodo10K, and multi-application OSWorld configs -- and (ii) verifies each task's feasibility through binary precondition checking before rollout. A single VLM (Kimi-K2.5) serves as goal generator, precondition judge, and trajectory executor, eliminating planner-actor capability gaps. Each trajectory is expanded into step-prefix samples that exactly reproduce the context layout seen at inference time. Fine-tuning UI-TARS 7B on ProCUA-SFT for one epoch yields 45.0% on OSWorld -- an 18.7 percentage-point improvement over the base model and over 35% above AgentNet-trained counterparts. A subset of ProCUA was incorporated into the training data for the Nemotron 3 Nano Omni model, contributing to its computer-use capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 训练计算机使用智能体（CUA）——通过截图和键盘/鼠标操作与图形桌面交互的模型——需要在全桌面环境中收集大规模、多样化的轨迹数据。最大的公共资源AgentNet（22.5K条人类轨迹）在用于监督微调（SFT）时会导致负迁移：在AgentNet上继续训练UI-TARS 7B使其在OSWorld上的成功率从26.3%降至8-10%。我们提出ProCUA-SFT，一个包含310万个步骤级SFT样本的数据集，这些样本是从涵盖2484个应用组合的93K条合成轨迹中蒸馏得到的。该数据集由全自动流水线生成，该流水线(i)在实时桌面环境中合成基于真实世界内容的可执行任务——912个来自SpreadsheetBench的电子表格、约10K个来自Zenodo10K的宽松许可演示文稿，以及多应用OSWorld配置——并且(ii)在回放前通过二元前置条件检查验证每个任务的可行性。单一视觉语言模型（Kimi-K2.5）同时作为目标生成器、前置条件判断器和轨迹执行器，消除了规划器与执行器之间的能力差距。每条轨迹被扩展为步骤前缀样本，精确复现推理时看到的上下文布局。在ProCUA-SFT上微调UI-TARS 7B一个epoch后，OSWorld成功率达45.0%——比基线模型提升18.7个百分点，比AgentNet训练版本高出35%以上。ProCUA的一个子集已被纳入Nemotron 3 Nano Omni模型的训练数据，为其计算机使用能力做出了贡献。

</details>


### [114] [Decision-Driven Geosteering Under Uncertainty: A Unified Framework for Sequential Decision Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17331)
*Hibat Errahmen Djecta,Sergey Alyaev,Kristian Fossum,Reidar B. Bratvold,Ressi Bonti Muhammad,Apoorv Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将粒子滤波与强化学习相结合的、面向地质导向的不确定性感知决策框架，并在工业级仿真器中验证了三种决策策略（ADP、Deep Q-learning、Dual DRL）的性能与稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统地质导向方法多采用确定性轨迹修正，未能充分利用钻头前方地质不确定性信息进行序贯决策，且缺乏对决策策略在钻井过程中动态行为的系统性评估。

Method: 提出一个不确定性感知的地质导向框架，核心将粒子滤波（PF）用于概率性地下解释和信念更新，并耦合三种信念-informed决策策略：可解释的近似动态规划（ADP）、深度Q学习（Deep Q-learning）基线、以及基于目标Q网络和决斗架构的双深度强化学习（Dual DRL）。框架集成到工业级地质导向仿真器中，在真实测量噪声和钻井约束下进行验证。

Result: 在相同地质实现、操作限制和奖励定义下，实验对比了三种策略在整个钻井过程中的行为差异，不仅评估最终井轨迹性能，还使用稳定性指标量化了转向平滑度随不确定性演变的动态响应。

Conclusion: 信念-informed决策策略相比确定性方法能更好地应对地质不确定性；引入稳定性指标有助于评估策略的操作用性；Dual DRL在平衡性能和稳定性方面表现出潜力。

Original Abstract: Geosteering requires navigating a well trajectory through an unknown geological configuration, while sequentially updating decisions based on indirect measurements acquired during drilling. This work presents an uncertainty-aware geosteering framework that tightly integrates particle filtering for probabilistic subsurface interpretation with value-based reinforcement learning for sequential decision-making. Geological uncertainty ahead of the drill bit is represented explicitly through a particle filter (PF), enabling belief-informed control rather than deterministic trajectory correction.
  The framework couples PF belief updates with belief-informed decision policies and evaluates three decision-making options that operate under identical uncertainty representations: an interpretable Approximate Dynamic Programming (ADP) scheme, a Deep Q-learning baseline, and a Dual Deep Reinforcement Learning (Dual DRL) architecture trained with a target Q-network scheme for stability, using a dueling (value/advantage) decomposition for Q-value parameterization. Beyond final placement performance, we assess policy behavior using stability-oriented metrics that quantify steering smoothness over time, providing additional operational insight into how decision policies respond as uncertainty evolves.
  The framework is integrated with an API for validation within an industrial geosteering simulator under realistic measurement noise and drilling constraints. Using identical geological realizations, operational limits, and reward definitions across methods, the experiments provide a controlled and high-fidelity evaluation of how alternative decision policies behave throughout the drilling process, rather than evaluating performance solely from the final well trajectory.

中文摘要翻译: 地质导向需要在未知地质构造中导航井轨迹，同时根据钻井过程中获取的间接测量结果顺序更新决策。本文提出了一种不确定性感知的地质导向框架，该框架将用于概率性地下解释的粒子滤波与用于序贯决策的基于值的强化学习紧密结合。钻头前方的地质不确定性通过粒子滤波显式表示，从而实现信念驱动的控制，而非确定性的轨迹修正。该框架将粒子滤波的信念更新与信念驱动的决策策略耦合，评估了在相同不确定性表示下运行的三种决策方案：一种可解释的近似动态规划方案、一种深度Q学习基线，以及一种双深度强化学习架构——该架构采用目标Q网络方案以保持训练稳定性，并使用决斗（价值/优势）分解进行Q值参数化。除了最终的井位性能外，我们还使用稳定性导向的指标评估策略行为，这些指标量化了转向随时间的平滑程度，从而提供关于决策策略如何随不确定性演变响应的额外操作洞察。该框架与一个API集成，可在工业级地质导向仿真器中进行验证，仿真中考虑了真实的测量噪声和钻井约束。通过在所有方法中使用相同的地质实现、操作限制和奖励定义，实验提供了可控且高保真的评估，考察了不同决策策略在整个钻井过程中的行为表现，而非仅根据最终的井轨迹进行性能评估。

</details>


### [115] [Counterfactual Optimization of Baseball Pitch Sequences and Estimation of Its Impact on Season-Level Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17345)
*Ryota Takamido,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用Transformer模型和反事实分析方法，基于MLB Statcast数据研究了棒球投球序列（包括最终球和设置球）对赛季级投手表现的影响，发现优化投球序列可显著提升K/9等关键指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以往研究主要关注单次打席中最后一球的优化，忽视了前面设置球的作用及其对长期赛季级表现的影响。

Method: 使用Transformer模型预测目标投球是否会导致击球进场或挥空；通过反事实分析生成替代投球序列（替换最终球或设置球），选择最小化预测进场概率的最优序列；利用回归模型将模型输出与赛季统计数据关联，估计对投手赛季统计的预期影响。

Result: 最终球和设置球的优化均能显著影响赛季级表现，包括K/9提升超过1.0；研究还提供了速度区间特异性有效位置、投球命令的重要性以及通过中速球扩大投球选择范围等实用见解。

Conclusion: 定量支持了棒球中投球序列策略的重要性，表明优化投球序列（而非仅关注最后一球）可实质性提升投手赛季表现。

Original Abstract: Although pitch sequencing is a central topic in baseball analytics, previous studies have primarily focused on optimizing the final pitch within a single plate appearance, leaving the role of preceding setup pitches and their impact on long-term season-level performance insufficiently examined. To address these issues, this study conducted counterfactual analyses using MLB Statcast data. A Transformer-based machine-learning model was trained to predict whether a target pitch would result in an in-play outcome or swing-out. Counterfactual pitch sequences were then generated by replacing either the final pitch or the preceding setup pitch with alternative pitch types and locations while keeping the surrounding contextual information fixed. Optimal counterfactual selections were defined as those that minimized the predicted in-play probability, and their expected effects on pitchers' seasonal statistics were estimated using regression models linking model outputs to season statistics. The results suggest that the optimization of both final and setup pitches may substantially influence season-level performance, including improvements of more than 1.0 in K/9. The analyses also provided several practical insights, including velocity-band-specific effective locations, the importance of pitch commands, and the expansion of pitch-selection options through middle-velocity pitches. These findings quantitatively support the strategic importance of pitch sequencing in baseball.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管投球序列是棒球分析学的核心议题，但以往研究主要聚焦于优化单次打席中的最后一球，而对前面设置球的作用及其对长期赛季级表现的影响关注不足。为解决这些问题，本研究利用MLB Statcast数据进行了反事实分析。训练了一个基于Transformer的机器学习模型，用于预测目标投球是否会导致击球进场或挥空。随后，通过用替代投球类型和位置替换最终球或前面的设置球，同时固定周围情境信息，生成了反事实投球序列。最优反事实选择被定义为那些能最小化预测击球进场概率的序列，并通过将模型输出与赛季统计数据关联的回归模型，估计其对投手赛季统计数据的预期影响。结果表明，最终球和设置球的优化都可能显著影响赛季级表现，包括K/9提升超过1.0。分析还提供了若干实用见解，包括速度区间特异性有效位置、投球命令的重要性以及通过中速球扩大投球选择范围。这些发现定量支持了棒球中投球序列策略的重要性。

</details>


### [116] [MM++: Unsupervised Scale-Invariant Multilayer OOD Detection via Top-K Gated Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17352)
*Rahim Hossain,Md Tawheedul Islam Bhuian,Md Farhan Shadiq,Kyoung-Don Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MM++是一个完全无监督、严格事后且尺度不变的OOD检测框架，通过构建联合特征空间并融合中间层与终端表示，结合Ledoit-Wolf正则化协方差矩阵，实现了鲁棒的近端和远端OOD检测，无需辅助数据或模型微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法在尺度不变性和层次表达性之间存在权衡，且大多依赖辅助数据、微调或架构修改，缺乏一个无需监督、无需修改模型且尺度鲁棒的统一框架。

Method: 1）通过测量熵密度下降来识别具有判别性的中间层，标记语义压缩边界；2）将选中的中间层与终端表示融合，捕获跨层相关性并抑制早期层噪声；3）使用Ledoit-Wolf正则化绑定的协方差矩阵稳定统一特征空间，实现可靠的距离估计。

Result: 在不同架构上，MM++在近端和远端OOD检测任务中均表现出稳健性能，且无需辅助OOD数据、分类器微调或架构修改。

Conclusion: MM++为OOD检测提供了一种完全无监督、事后且尺度不变的解决方案，在不依赖额外数据或模型修改的前提下，有效平衡了尺度不变性与层次表达性。

Original Abstract: We introduce MM++ (Multilayer Mahalanobis++), a fully unsupervised, strictly post-hoc, and scale-invariant framework for Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection. To address the trade-off between scale invariance and hierarchical expressivity, MM++ constructs a principled joint feature space. It first identifies discriminative intermediate layers by measuring entropy density drops, which mark the boundaries of sharp semantic compression. By fusing these selected layers with the terminal representation, the framework captures latent cross-layer correlations while mitigating early-layer noise. Crucially, a Ledoit-Wolf regularized tied covariance matrix stabilizes this unified space, enabling reliable distance estimation. Requiring no auxiliary OOD data, classifier fine-tuning, or architectural modifications, MM++ delivers robust performance across distinct architectures for both near- and far-OOD detection.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了MM++（多层马氏距离++），一个完全无监督、严格事后处理且尺度不变的分布外（OOD）检测框架。为了解决尺度不变性与层次表达性之间的权衡问题，MM++构建了一个原则性的联合特征空间。它首先通过测量熵密度下降来识别具有判别性的中间层，这些下降标志着清晰语义压缩的边界。通过将这些选定的层与终端表示相融合，该框架捕获了潜在的跨层相关性，同时减轻了早期层的噪声。关键在于，一个Ledoit-Wolf正则化的绑定协方差矩阵稳定了这个统一空间，使得距离估计更加可靠。无需辅助OOD数据、分类器微调或架构修改，MM++在不同的架构上为近端和远端OOD检测均提供了稳健的性能。

</details>


### [117] [Performance-Driven Environment Abstraction with Multi-Timescale Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17377)
*Yue Guan,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向性能驱动的环境抽象方法，通过状态聚合和动作共享来优化大规模MDP中的决策质量，并设计了一种多时间尺度强化学习框架，联合优化策略与树状环境抽象，实现了显著的状态压缩和样本效率提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统环境抽象方法通常保留几何或拓扑结构，而非直接优化决策质量。本文旨在建立一种直接以决策质量为导向的抽象框架，并给出理论性能保证。

Method: 将抽象建模为受控近似，通过聚合状态空间并在每个聚合状态内强制执行共享动作分布。基于固定分区的性能保证分析，设计多时间尺度强化学习框架，联合调整策略和树状结构的环境抽象，根据Q值差异细化或粗化状态空间区域。

Result: 与actor-critic基线相比，该方法实现了显著的状态压缩、更高的样本效率和更快的重规划速度。

Conclusion: 所提出的性能驱动抽象方法能够在保证决策质量的前提下大幅压缩状态空间，提升学习与规划效率，为大规模MDP中的抽象学习提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Original Abstract: We study performance-driven environment abstraction for decision-making in large Markov decision processes. Rather than preserving geometric or topological structure, we seek abstractions that directly optimize decision quality. We model abstraction as a controlled approximation obtained by aggregating the state space and enforcing a shared action distribution within each aggregated state. For a fixed partition, we establish a performance guarantee that separates value-function approximation error from the loss introduced by action sharing. Guided by this analysis, we develop a multi-timescale reinforcement learning framework that jointly adapts the policy and a tree-structured environment abstraction. The resulting algorithm refines and coarsens regions of the state space based on Q-value discrepancies, balancing performance against abstraction size and complexity. Empirical results demonstrate substantial state compression, improved sample efficiency, and faster replanning compared to actor-critic baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究面向性能驱动的环境抽象方法，用于大规模马尔可夫决策过程中的决策优化。不同于保留几何或拓扑结构，我们寻求直接优化决策质量的抽象。我们将抽象建模为一种受控近似，通过聚合状态空间并在每个聚合状态内强制执行共享动作分布来实现。对于固定分区，我们建立了一个性能保证，将值函数近似误差与动作共享引入的损失分离开来。在该分析的指导下，我们开发了一个多时间尺度强化学习框架，联合调整策略与树状结构的环境抽象。所得算法基于Q值差异对状态空间区域进行细化和粗化，在抽象规模与复杂度之间取得平衡。实验结果表明，与actor-critic基线相比，该方法实现了显著的状态压缩、更高的样本效率和更快的重规划速度。

</details>


### [118] [The Discrete-Log Clock: How a Transformer Learns Modular Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17399)
*Huu Danh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，小变压器在"grok"模乘法时，其学习到的嵌入在标准加性DFT下看似稠密，但在乘法特征变换下变得高度稀疏（基尼系数从0.07升至0.58），仅需4个关键频率。进一步发现96.9%的MLP神经元调谐于单一乘法频率，且神经元激活热图按离散对数重排后呈现二维周期结构，表明变压器在离散对数空间中实现了乘法到加法的约化，即执行"离散对数时钟"算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前人工作发现变压器在模乘法任务上学习到的嵌入具有"稠密"傅里叶谱（需要所有频率），这与模加法任务仅需少量关键频率形成鲜明对比。作者认为这种稠密性是由于在错误的基（标准加性DFT）下分析造成的假象，因此希望找到正确的分析基来揭示模乘法中变压器的内在工作机制。

Method: 采用乘法特征变换（即乘法群$(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^*$上的不可约表示分解）替代标准加性DFT，对在$a \cdot b \bmod 113$上训练过的变压器进行分析。具体包括：计算嵌入在乘法特征基下的频谱稀疏性（基尼系数）、分析MLP神经元的频率调谐特性、按离散对数重排神经元激活热图以观察周期性结构。

Result: (1) 在乘法特征基下，嵌入频谱高度稀疏：基尼系数0.58（加性基下仅0.07），仅4个关键频率携带显著能量；(2) 96.9%的MLP神经元干净地调谐于单一乘法频率；(3) 按离散对数重排后，神经元激活热图呈现二维周期结构，表明模型在离散对数空间中实现了乘法到加法的约化。

Conclusion: 变压器在模乘法任务上执行的是"离散对数时钟"算法（类似于Nanda等人在加法中发现的时钟算法），即通过离散对数将乘法转化为加法来运算。更重要的是，方法论上有普适意义：当分析基与任务的代数结构相匹配时，原本看似噪声的结构会展现出可解释的规律。

Original Abstract: When small transformers grok modular multiplication, prior work reports that the learned embedding has a "dense" Fourier spectrum requiring all frequencies. This contrasts with modular addition, where only a sparse set of key frequencies suffices. We show this density is an artifact of analyzing in the wrong basis. The natural Fourier transform for multiplication is not the standard additive DFT but the multiplicative character transform, which decomposes functions on the multiplicative group $(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^*$ into its irreducible representations. Applying this transform to a grokked transformer trained on $a \cdot b \bmod 113$, we find the embedding spectrum becomes highly sparse (Gini coefficient 0.58 vs. 0.07 in the additive basis) with only 4 key frequencies carrying significant energy. Furthermore, 96.9% of MLP neurons are cleanly tuned to a single multiplicative frequency, and neuron activation heatmaps reveal 2D-periodic structure when reordered by the discrete logarithm. These results demonstrate the transformer reduces multiplication to addition in discrete-log space, implementing a "Discrete-Log Clock" algorithm analogous to Nanda et al.'s Clock algorithm for addition. The methodology generalizes: matching the analysis basis to the algebraic structure of the task reveals interpretable structure where standard tools see noise.

中文摘要翻译: 当小型变压器"grok"模乘法时，先前工作报告其学习到的嵌入具有需要所有频率的"稠密"傅里叶谱。这与模加法形成对比——后者仅需一稀疏的关键频率集即可。我们证明这种稠密性是在错误基下分析的假象。乘法的自然傅里叶变换并非标准加性DFT，而是乘法特征变换——它将乘法群$(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^*$上的函数分解为其不可约表示。将这一变换应用于在$a \cdot b \bmod 113$上训练过的grokked变压器，我们发现嵌入频谱变得高度稀疏（加性基下基尼系数0.07 vs. 乘法基下0.58），仅4个关键频率携带显著能量。此外，96.9%的MLP神经元被干净地调谐至单一乘法频率，且按离散对数重排后，神经元激活热图呈现二维周期结构。这些结果表明，变压器将乘法约化为离散对数空间中的加法，实现了与Nanda等人的加法时钟算法类似的"离散对数时钟"算法。该方法具有普适性：当分析基与任务的代数结构匹配时，标准工具视为噪声的结构将展现出可解释的规律。

</details>


### [119] [Discrete Autoregressive Transformer for Generative Mechanism Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17409)
*Anar Nurizada,Anurag Purwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将平面路径综合问题转化为条件自回归序列建模任务，利用解码器-only Transformer和VAE潜在表示，在超过一百万个机构的数据集上实现了从目标曲线到机构关节坐标的生成，取得了低Chamfer距离和动态时间规整的优异结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 平面路径综合需要机构耦合曲线匹配预定轨迹，而曲线到连杆机构的映射本质上是一对多的，涉及四杆、六杆和八杆拓扑结构。现有方法难以高效生成多样且精确的候选机构。

Method: 将综合问题形式化为条件自回归序列建模：将关节坐标均匀量化到token，由解码器-only Transformer生成，并引入目标曲线的VAE潜在表示和显式的机构类型token。训练采用token交叉熵损失和高斯平滑的bin辅助损失；推理时通过有界潜在噪声调度解码所有机构类型，保留几何误差最小的前五名候选。

Result: 在保留测试集上，聚合平均Chamfer距离为0.0132，平均动态时间规整为0.153；基于VAE空间中训练集邻居潜在表示的k近邻基线达到匹配拓扑平均Chamfer距离0.0071和平均动态时间规整0.117。

Conclusion: 该方法无需数据集查找即可生成多样且精确的机构候选，在路径综合问题上表现优异，且潜在k近邻基线进一步提升了匹配拓扑下的性能。

Original Abstract: Planar path synthesis requires mechanisms whose coupler curves match a prescribed trajectory; the mapping from curve to linkage is inherently one-to-many across four-, six-, and eight-bar topologies. We address this design problem with simulation-grounded evaluation on a curated corpus of over one million mechanisms, reporting Chamfer distance and dynamic time warping after forward kinematics and geometric alignment. We formulate synthesis as conditional autoregressive sequence modeling: joint coordinates are uniformly quantized to tokens and generated by a decoder-only transformer with a variational-autoencoder (VAE) latent of the target curve and an explicit mechanism-type token. Training combines token cross-entropy with a Gaussian-smoothed bin auxiliary loss that respects ordinal structure among bins. At inference, a bounded latent-noise schedule decodes all mechanism types at each noise level; we retain the top five candidates by geometric error, yielding diverse accurate families without dataset lookup. On held-out tests, aggregate mean Chamfer distance is $0.0132$ and mean dynamic time warping is $0.153$; a latent $k$-nearest-neighbor baseline that conditions on training-set neighbor latents in VAE space achieves matched-topology mean Chamfer distance $0.0071$ and mean dynamic time warping $0.117$ using the same decoder.

中文摘要翻译: 平面路径综合需要机构的耦合曲线匹配预定轨迹；从曲线到连杆机构的映射在四杆、六杆和八杆拓扑中本质上是一对多的。我们通过在超过一百万个机构的精选语料库上进行模拟评估来解决这一设计问题，报告了经过正向运动学和几何对齐后的Chamfer距离和动态时间规整。我们将综合问题形式化为条件自回归序列建模：关节坐标被均匀量化到token，并由一个解码器-only Transformer生成，该Transformer包含目标曲线的变分自编码器（VAE）潜在表示和一个显式的机构类型token。训练结合了token交叉熵损失和一个高斯平滑的bin辅助损失，该损失尊重bin之间的序数结构。在推理时，一个有界潜在噪声调度在每个噪声水平解码所有机构类型；我们保留几何误差最小的前五名候选，从而无需数据集查找即可获得多样且精确的机构系列。在保留测试集上，聚合平均Chamfer距离为0.0132，平均动态时间规整为0.153；一个潜在k近邻基线——该基线以VAE空间中训练集邻居潜在表示为条件——使用相同的解码器实现了匹配拓扑平均Chamfer距离0.0071和平均动态时间规整0.117。

</details>


### [120] [Amortized Probabilistic Retrieval of Atmospheric CO2 from OCO-2 Spectra Using Deep Learning with Laplace Approximations and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17413)
*Alejandro Calle-Saldarriaga,Felix Jimenez,Jack Grosskreuz,Jiazheng Wang,Jonathan Hobbs,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的框架，用于从OCO-2卫星高光谱数据中快速估算大气CO2柱浓度（XCO2），并实现高效的不确定性量化，相比传统全物理方法在速度、精度和不确定性校准方面具有显著优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前OCO-2卫星的 operational 反演算法计算成本高昂，且无法恰当地量化不确定性，需要更高效、更可靠的替代方案。

Method: 采用多分支神经网络编码光谱波段，结合拉普拉斯近似和归一化流两种可扩展的不确定性量化方法，在高保真仿真数据集上训练，该数据集显式包含了前向模型误差以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 相比传统全物理求解器，该方法在五个方面具有优势：(1)推理速度提升数个数量级，支持实时处理；(2)对模型误差具有鲁棒性；(3)点估计精度更优；(4)不确定性校准更好；(5)使用归一化流时可建模非高斯后验分布。

Conclusion: 基于仿真的深度学习方法是发展下一代业务化卫星反演处理系统的可行路径。

Original Abstract: Space-based monitoring of atmospheric carbon dioxide (CO2) is essential for constraining the global carbon budget. NASA's Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 (OCO-2) estimates column-averaged dry-air mole fractions of CO2 (XCO2) using high-resolution spectra. However, current operational retrieval algorithms are computationally expensive and do not properly quantify uncertainties. We present a novel deep learning framework that addresses these challenges. Due to the difficulties of ground-truth data for real satellite observations, we develop and validate our approach using a high-fidelity simulation dataset. This dataset, created to support OCO-2 uncertainty quantification (UQ), incorporates realistic forward model errors. Our architecture encodes spectral bands using a multi-branch neural network and estimates posteriors of the full CO2 column or desired summaries thereof using two scalable UQ methods: Laplace approximations and normalizing flows. Our approach has five key advantages relative to operational "full-physics" solvers: (1) Amortization: Inference is orders of magnitude faster, enabling real-time processing of massive data streams; (2) Model error robustness: By training on simulations that explicitly include model discrepancies, our method accounts for systematic errors often neglected by standard inversions; (3) Point estimate accuracy: We achieve superior predictive accuracy compared to baseline methods; (4) Improved UQ: The probabilistic outputs yield better-calibrated uncertainty estimates; and (5) Non-Gaussian posteriors: When utilizing normalizing flows, our framework successfully models complex, asymmetric posterior distributions, overcoming the limitations of the Gaussian assumption. These results suggest that simulation-based deep learning is a viable path toward next-generation operational processing systems.

中文摘要翻译: 基于空间的大气二氧化碳（CO2）监测对于约束全球碳收支至关重要。NASA的轨道碳观测者2号（OCO-2）利用高分辨率光谱估算CO2的柱平均干空气摩尔分数（XCO2）。然而，当前的业务化反演算法计算成本高昂，且无法恰当地量化不确定性。我们提出了一种新型深度学习框架来解决这些挑战。由于真实卫星观测缺乏地面真值数据，我们使用高保真仿真数据集来开发和验证我们的方法。该数据集为支持OCO-2不确定性量化（UQ）而创建，并融入了真实的前向模型误差。我们的架构使用多分支神经网络编码光谱波段，并通过两种可扩展的不确定性量化方法——拉普拉斯近似和归一化流——来估计完整CO2柱的后验分布或其所需汇总统计量。我们的方法相对于传统的"全物理"求解器具有五个关键优势：(1) 摊销：推理速度提升数个数量级，支持海量数据流的实时处理；(2) 模型误差鲁棒性：通过在显式包含模型差异的仿真数据上训练，我们的方法能够考虑标准反演中常被忽略的系统误差；(3) 点估计精度：与基线方法相比，我们实现了更优的预测精度；(4) 改进的不确定性量化：概率输出提供了校准更好的不确定性估计；(5) 非高斯后验：当使用归一化流时，我们的框架成功建模了复杂的非对称后验分布，克服了高斯假设的局限性。这些结果表明，基于仿真的深度学习是发展下一代业务化处理系统的可行路径。

</details>


### [121] [Memory-Efficient Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Safety-Critical Control in Adversarial Spacecraft Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17414)
*Alejandro Posadas-Nava,Richard Linares,Minduli Wijayatunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过元强化学习（meta-RL）调优输入约束控制障碍函数（ICCBF）中class-$\mathcal{K}$函数的最佳架构与训练算法组合，针对自主航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）中的安全关键控制问题。实验表明，Mamba状态空间模型结合PPO算法在所有合作与非合作场景中均优于LSTM、GRU及SAC等组合，在任务完成率、安全性和燃油经济性方面表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在自主航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）中，控制器需在推力约束下保证安全并最小化燃料消耗。已有工作通过元强化学习（meta-RL）学习定义ICCBF递归的class-$\mathcal{K}$函数，提供了一种鲁棒的非贪婪安全控制方法。本文旨在进一步探索不同循环网络架构与训练算法的最优组合，以提升该方法在合作与非合作场景下的性能。

Method: 本文研究了三种循环网络架构（LSTM、GRU、Mamba状态空间模型）和两种训练算法（PPO、SAC）的组合，通过元强化学习（meta-RL）调优输入约束控制障碍函数（ICCBF）中的class-$\mathcal{K}$函数。实验在合作场景及目标航天器呈现对抗行为的非合作场景下进行性能评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，Mamba状态空间模型与PPO算法结合在所有合作与非合作场景中，在任务完成率、安全性和燃油经济性方面均优于LSTM、GRU与其他训练算法组合。

Conclusion: Mamba状态空间模型配合PPO训练算法是实现ICCBF中class-$\mathcal{K}$函数元强化学习调优的最佳架构选择，能够有效提升自主航天器RPO任务的安全性与燃料效率。

Original Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) require controllers that guarantee safety under thrust constraints while minimizing fuel expenditure. Input-constrained control barrier functions (ICCBFs) provide a control method for nonlinear systems with actuation constraints that construct a forward-invariant safe set. Previous work has shown that learning class-$\mathcal{K}$ functions defining the ICCBF recursion via meta reinforcement learning (meta-RL) yields a robust, non-greedy approach to safety-critical control in RPO. This paper extends that framework further by investigating the performance of three recurrent network architectures (Long Short Term Memory (LSTM), Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), Selective State Space Model (Mamba)) and two training algorithms (Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor Critic (SAC)) to identify the best setup for tuning ICCBF class-K functions via meta-RL. In addition to cooperative test cases, performance is evaluated in the presence of adversarial behavior where the target spacecraft behaves in a way that worsens the safety of the chaser spacecraft. Results indicate that state space models such as Mamba when used with PPO achieve superior task completion, safety, and fuel-savings compared to other architectures, across all cooperative and uncooperative scenarios tested.

中文摘要翻译: 自主航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）需要控制器在推力约束下保证安全并最小化燃料消耗。输入约束控制障碍函数（ICCBF）为具有驱动约束的非线性系统提供了一种控制方法，能够构建前向不变的安全集。已有工作表明，通过元强化学习（meta-RL）学习定义ICCBF递归的class-$\mathcal{K}$函数，可为RPO中的安全关键控制提供鲁棒、非贪婪的方法。本文进一步扩展该框架，研究了三种循环网络架构（长短期记忆网络LSTM、门控循环单元GRU、选择性状态空间模型Mamba）和两种训练算法（近端策略优化PPO和软演员-评论家SAC）的性能，以确定通过元强化学习调优ICCBF class-$\mathcal{K}$函数的最佳设置。除合作测试案例外，本文还在目标航天器表现出对抗行为（恶化追踪航天器安全性）的场景下进行评估。结果表明，在所有合作与非合作测试场景中，Mamba等状态空间模型配合PPO算法在任务完成率、安全性和燃料节省方面均优于其他架构。

</details>


### [122] [Generalization Guarantees for Multi-Input Neural Operator Learning in Sobolev Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17419)
*Yahong Yang,Zecheng Zhang,Wei Zhu,Wenjing Liao,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多输入神经算子建立了Sobolev范数下的逼近与泛化误差界，量化了各输入空间对最终误差的贡献，为多输入算子学习提供了统一的理论框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有算子学习理论主要关注单输入函数设定，但实际科学计算和PDE问题常涉及多个定义在不同域上的输入函数，缺乏系统的理论分析框架。

Method: 通过理论推导，建立了多输入神经算子在Sobolev范数下的逼近误差和泛化误差上界，显式刻画了各输入空间的维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数对误差的贡献。

Result: 在平衡机制下，逼近和泛化速率由输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数之间的交互决定；模型复杂度依赖项保留\(\log\log/\log\)型结构。

Conclusion: 该分析为多输入算子学习（包括Sobolev训练）提供了通用理论框架，适用于偏微分方程和科学计算中的算子学习问题。

Original Abstract: We develop approximation and generalization error estimates for multi-input neural operators, with the output error measured in Sobolev norms. In contrast to standard operator-learning settings with a single input function, our framework allows multiple input functions defined on possibly different domains, with different dimensions and Sobolev regularities. The derived rates explicitly quantify the contribution of each input space to the final error bound. In particular, in the balanced regime, the approximation and generalization rates are governed by the interaction between the input dimensions, regularities, and Sobolev orders, while the dependence on the model complexity retains a \(\log\log/\log\)-type structure. Our analysis provides a general theoretical framework for multi-input operator learning, including Sobolev training, and is applicable to operator learning problems arising from partial differential equations and scientific computing.

中文摘要翻译: 我们针对多输入神经算子发展了逼近误差和泛化误差的估计，其中输出误差在Sobolev范数下度量。与仅含单个输入函数的标准算子学习设定不同，我们的框架允许使用多个可能定义在不同域上的输入函数，这些函数具有不同的维度和Sobolev正则性。推导出的速率显式量化了每个输入空间对最终误差界的贡献。特别地，在平衡机制下，逼近和泛化速率由输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数之间的交互作用所主导，而对模型复杂度的依赖则保留了\(\log\log/\log\)型结构。我们的分析为包括Sobolev训练在内的多输入算子学习提供了一个通用理论框架，并适用于源自偏微分方程和科学计算的算子学习问题。

</details>


### [123] [MorphStrata: Layer-Specific Perturbations for Generating Morphence Students in Time-Series Moving Target Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17435)
*Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Thanh Quynh Nhu Ta,Mohammad Masum,Robert Chun,Jaydip Sen,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MorphStrata，一种通过选择性逐层随机噪声注入生成学生模型的移动目标防御策略，在几乎不增加训练成本的前提下显著提升时间序列预测模型对梯度攻击的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型易受梯度对抗攻击，而传统防御机制（如Morphence）在鲁棒性与计算成本之间存在权衡，且移动目标防御（MTD）中维护多个随机模型实例会大幅增加训练开销。

Method: 以Transformer为教师模型，通过选择性扰动随机架构块并注入逐层随机噪声，生成具有结构化异质性的学生模型集成，以此拓展Morphence防御框架。

Result: 在Jena Climate、Electricity Load Diagrams、Appliances Energy Prediction三个基准上，面对FGSM、BIM、PGD攻击，MorphStrata在保持可比对抗RMSE的同时，在AEP高频周期数据集上实现最佳性能：FGSM下提升24.11%，BIM下提升97.97%（epsilon=0.5，30次随机试验）。训练时间增幅不足1%，但实现了两位数的对抗RMSE降幅。学生模型间的L2距离与防御效果呈正相关。

Conclusion: MorphStrata作为一种移动目标防御策略，能够以极小的计算开销增量维持强大的对抗鲁棒性，优于现有基线方法。

Original Abstract: Time-series forecasting models remain vulnerable to gradient-based adversarial attacks while existing defense mechanisms typically incur a trade-off in robustness for bounded response and compute cost. The problem is pronounced in Moving Target Defense where maintaining multiple randomized model instances substantially exacerbates the training overhead. In this work, we introduce MorphStrata, a student generation strategy with selective, layer-specific stochastic noise injection that extends the traditional Morphence defense. MorphStrata uses a Transformer backbone as the teacher and perturbs randomly selected architectural blocks to create structured heterogeneity across student models in response to varied data distributions and threat models. We evaluate against vanilla Transformer and Morphence backbones on a suite of benchmarks including the Jena Climate, Electricity Load Diagrams, and Appliances Energy Prediction using FGSM, BIM and PGD attacks across multiple attack strengths. Across datasets and attack regimes, the proposed ensemble maintains comparable adversarial RMSE. Specifically, for high entropy, periodic datasets as in the case of the AEP data, MorphStrata achieves the lowest RMSE across all attacks and perturbation budgets, improving over the static baseline by up to 24.11% and 97.97% under FGSM and BIM respectively at an epsilon value of 0.5 over 30 randomized trials. Targeting the layers to generate MorphStrata students accounts for less than 1% increase in train-times over the Morphence MTD baseline for most of the experiments, while accounting for double digit gains in adversarial RMSE reduction. We also observe a positive correlation between higher pairwise L2 distance (among generated students) and overall defense effectiveness. In summary, MorphStrata maintains adversarial robustness as an MTD defense at marginal cost deltas when compared to existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测模型仍然容易受到基于梯度的对抗攻击，而现有的防御机制通常需要在鲁棒性与有界响应和计算成本之间进行权衡。这一问题在移动目标防御（Moving Target Defense）中尤为突出，因为维护多个随机化模型实例会显著加剧训练开销。在本工作中，我们提出了MorphStrata，一种采用选择性、逐层随机噪声注入的学生生成策略，扩展了传统的Morphence防御方法。MorphStrata以Transformer骨干网络作为教师模型，扰动随机选择的架构块，从而在学生模型之间创建结构化的异质性，以应对不同的数据分布和威胁模型。我们在多个基准数据集（包括Jena Climate、Electricity Load Diagrams和Appliances Energy Prediction）上，使用FGSM、BIM和PGD攻击在多种攻击强度下，与vanilla Transformer和Morphence骨干网络进行了评估。跨数据集和攻击机制下，所提出的集成模型保持了可比的对抗RMSE。具体而言，对于像AEP数据这样的高熵、周期性数据集，MorphStrata在所有攻击和扰动预算下均取得了最低的RMSE；在epsilon值为0.5的30次随机试验中，相较于静态基线，在FGSM和BIM攻击下分别提升了24.11%和97.97%。在大多数实验中，生成MorphStrata学生模型所需的针对特定层的操作，相较于Morphence MTD基线，训练时间增幅不足1%，同时实现了两位数的对抗RMSE降幅。我们还观察到，学生模型之间更高的成对L2距离与整体防御效果之间存在正相关。综上所述，与现有基线相比，MorphStrata以极小的成本增量维持了作为MTD防御的对抗鲁棒性。

</details>


### [124] [Toward Controllable Catalyst Inverse Design via Large-Scale Autoregressive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17445)
*Dong Hyeon Mok,Jonggeol Na,Seoin Back*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于GPT架构的条件催化剂生成模型，通过数值嵌入层在自回归框架中实现类别和连续属性条件控制。模型在1.33亿结构上预训练，约46万优化结构上微调，实现了98%的结构有效性和高达93%的条件匹配率，将筛选效率提升1.5-4倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统高通量筛选效率随搜索空间扩大而下降，而催化剂表面存在复杂结构多样性和耦合的吸附质-表面相互作用，亟需开发能直接生成具有目标属性催化剂的生成模型。

Method: 基于GPT架构的条件催化剂生成模型，引入数值嵌入层实现类别和连续属性的联合条件控制，采用自回归框架进行序列化结构生成。模型先在1.33亿催化剂结构上进行预训练，再在约46万优化结构上微调以学习类别属性和结合能条件。

Result: 模型达到98%结构有效性、95%优化有效性、93%吸附质类型与组成联合匹配率；结合能条件匹配率约20%（较基线提升4倍），生成分布系统性向目标值偏移，无需额外微调即可将筛选效率提升1.5-4倍。

Conclusion: 大规模自回归预训练结合显式属性条件化为可控催化剂生成和加速催化剂发现提供了实用途径。

Original Abstract: Inverse design of heterogeneous catalysts remains challenging because catalyst surfaces exhibit substantial structural complexity with coupled surface-adsorbate interactions across a vast chemical space that is difficult to explore efficiently through conventional screening alone. Although machine learning-based high-throughput screening has accelerated catalyst discovery, its efficiency inevitably declines as the search space grows, motivating the development of generative models that can directly construct catalysts with target properties. Here, we present a conditional catalyst generative model based on the Generative Pretrained Transformer architecture with a numerical embedding layer that enables the generation of catalyst structures conditioned on both categorical and continuous properties within a single autoregressive framework. The model was pretrained on 133 million catalyst structures and subsequently fine-tuned on approximately 460,000 optimized structures with associated categorical properties and binding energies for conditional generation. The resulting model achieved 98% structural validity, 95% optimization validity, and high categorical condition fidelity, with a 93 % joint match rate for adsorbate type and composition. For binding energy conditioning, the match rate of approximately 20% represents a four-fold improvement over the baseline training distribution, and the generated distributions shift systematically toward the target values, enabling a 1.5 to 4-fold improvement in screening efficiency for reaction-targeted catalyst discovery without additional fine-tuning. These results show that large-scale autoregressive pre-training, combined with explicit property conditioning, provides a practical route toward controllable catalyst generation and accelerated catalysts discovery.

中文摘要翻译: 异质催化剂的反向设计仍然具有挑战性，因为催化剂表面表现出显著的结构复杂性，耦合了表面-吸附质相互作用，且跨越广阔的化学空间，传统筛选方法难以高效探索。尽管基于机器学习的高通量筛选加速了催化剂发现，但其效率不可避免地随搜索空间扩大而下降，这推动了能够直接构建具有目标属性催化剂的生成模型的发展。本文提出了一种基于生成式预训练Transformer架构的条件催化剂生成模型，该模型引入数值嵌入层，能够在单一自回归框架内实现以类别和连续属性为条件的催化剂结构生成。模型在1.33亿催化剂结构上进行了预训练，随后在约46万优化结构上微调，这些结构带有相关的类别属性和结合能，用于条件生成。最终模型实现了98%的结构有效性、95%的优化有效性以及较高的类别条件 fidelity，其中吸附质类型和组成的联合匹配率达到93%。在结合能条件设定下，约20%的匹配率是基线训练分布的四倍提升，生成分布系统性向目标值偏移，使得面向反应目标的催化剂发现筛选效率提升了1.5至4倍，且无需额外微调。这些结果表明，大规模自回归预训练结合显式属性条件化为可控催化剂生成和加速催化剂发现提供了实用途径。

</details>


### [125] [Credibility-Weighted Pricing of Autonomous Vehicle Liability Under Operational Design Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17451)
*Doyeon Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种层次化贝叶斯可信度框架，通过ODD相似性核函数跨城市、软件版本和区域汇聚稀疏事故数据，在Waymo真实数据上验证了该方法相对于无汇聚的显著优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动驾驶系统部署面临数据稀疏、运营设计域（ODD）变化以及软件版本更新带来的非平稳风险，传统的精算定价方法难以有效处理这些挑战。

Method: 提出层次化贝叶斯可信度框架，利用学习的ODD相似性核函数跨城市、软件版本和区域进行信息汇聚，将Buhlmann-Straub模型作为极限特例嵌套其中。

Result: 基于NHTSA Standing General Order数据库中Waymo在美国四个大都市区648起已确认介入事故及1.16亿匹配里程数据，城市层面可信度权重为0.12-0.46；部分汇聚显著优于无汇聚；功效分析表明学习到的核函数优势在约12个部署城市时可被检测到。

Conclusion: 层次化贝叶斯可信度框架能够有效处理自动驾驶系统的稀疏事故数据和非平稳风险，当部署城市达到一定数量时，基于ODD相似性核的学习方法具有明显的统计优势。

Original Abstract: Automated Driving System deployments create a foundational ratemaking challenge: sparse experience, shifting operational design domains, and non-stationary risk across software releases. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian credibility framework pooling across cities, software versions, and territories via a learned ODD-similarity kernel, nesting Buhlmann-Straub as a limiting case. Demonstrated on 648 verified-engaged Waymo crashes across four U.S. metros from the NHTSA Standing General Order database against 116 million matched miles, city-aggregate credibility weights are moderate (0.12-0.46), partial pooling decisively outperforms no pooling, and a power analysis shows the learned kernel's advantage becomes detectable at approximately twelve deployed cities.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶系统的部署带来了一个基础性的费率制定挑战：稀疏的经验数据、不断变化的运营设计域（ODD）以及软件版本更新带来的非平稳风险。我们提出了一种层次化贝叶斯可信度框架，通过从数据中学习的ODD相似性核函数，跨城市、软件版本和地区进行信息汇聚，并将Buhlmann-Straub模型作为其极限特例。基于NHTSA Standing General Order数据库中Waymo在美国四个大都市区的648起已确认介入事故及1.16亿匹配里程数据的实验表明：城市层面的可信度权重为中等水平（0.12-0.46），部分汇聚方法显著优于无汇聚方法，且功效分析显示，学习到的核函数的优势在约十二个部署城市时即可被检测到。

</details>


### [126] [Operator Boosting Produces Pareto-Efficient PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17460)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Operator Boosting框架，通过逐阶段残差学习构建紧凑的神经算子代理模型，在保持或提升精度的同时将参数量减少72%-95%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全尺寸神经算子模型在存储、部署和评估时成本高昂，需要一种直接构建紧凑代理模型的方法，而非先训练大模型再压缩。

Method: 从归一化输出坐标的经验均值预测器开始，依次在残差场上训练一系列极小的同族神经算子，并通过验证集选择的收缩系数整合每次修正。在FNO、DeepONet和CNO三种架构上实例化该框架。

Result: 在30个数据集-架构对中，21个显示正向平均精度提升，17个置信区间为正；所有堆叠模型参数量减少72%-95%；在10个完成的PDE基准中有7个实现经验帕累托改进。

Conclusion: Operator Boosting通常能改善神经PDE代理模型的精度-参数帕累托前沿，但也揭示了残差提升无法抵消压缩损失的PDE和架构依赖场景。

Original Abstract: Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子被广泛用作偏微分方程(PDEs)的代理解映射，但在多查询科学工作流中，全尺寸模型的存储、部署和评估成本高昂。本文提出Operator Boosting，一种逐阶段残差学习框架，用于直接构建紧凑的神经算子代理模型，而非先训练大模型再压缩。该方法从归一化输出坐标中的经验均值预测器开始，依次在残差场上训练一系列极小的同族神经算子，并通过验证集选择的收缩系数整合每次修正。我们以傅立叶神经算子(FNOs)、DeepONets和卷积神经算子(CNOs)实例化该框架，并将增强的微小堆叠模型与全尺寸单一基线在来自PDEBench、APEBench和The Well的一维、二维和三维PDE基准上进行对比。在30个数据集-架构对中，21个显示出正向平均精度提升，17个具有正向置信区间，而所有增强堆叠模型将可训练参数量减少约72%-95%。最佳模型比较显示，在10个完成的PDE基准中有7个实现了经验帕累托改进，包括二维Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、Darcy流、一维输运和反应系统，以及三维可压缩Navier-Stokes。这些结果表明，Operator Boosting通常能够改进神经PDE代理模型的经验精度-参数帕累托前沿，同时也揭示了残差提升无法抵消压缩损失的PDE和架构依赖场景。

</details>


### [127] [ResAware: Cross-Environment Website Fingerprinting via Resource-Privileged Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17462)
*Chongru Fan,Wei Wang,Wentao Huang,Zhenquan Ding,Jinqiao Shi,Lei Cui,Zhiyu Hao,Xiaochun Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ResAware，一种跨环境资源感知的知识蒸馏框架，通过在资源层面训练教师模型并蒸馏到仅使用加密流量的学生模型，显著提升网站指纹攻击在真实环境中的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有WF攻击在受控实验室环境下表现良好，但由于时空漂移、浏览器异构性、代理混淆等因素，在真实环境中性能大幅下降，根本原因在于它们仅依赖低级流量特征，这些特征噪声大且对环境扰动高度敏感。

Method: 提出ResAware框架，采用训练丰富/推理贫乏的非对称设定。首先在资源级特征上训练教师模型，然后通过异构知识蒸馏将特权知识转移到仅使用加密流量进行推理的学生模型中，部署时无需额外开销。

Result: 在跨越5个月、6个全球分布观测点的16万+配对样本的大规模数据集上，ResAware显著提升了多种WF基线的跨环境鲁棒性。在150天时间漂移下，Var-CNN的F1-score从72.77%提升至81.49%，开放世界TPR@1%FPR从22.40%提升至27.20%。

Conclusion: 资源级监督能够在不扩展在线观测能力的前提下有效提升WF攻击的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: While Website Fingerprinting (WF) attacks achieve high accuracy in controlled laboratory settings, they often degrade substantially in real-world environments due to spatio-temporal drift, browser heterogeneity, proxy obfuscation and etc. This limitation stems from their sole reliance on low-level traffic features that are noisy and highly sensitive to environmental perturbations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{ResAware}, a cross-environment resource-aware distillation framework under a \textit{training-rich/inference-poor} asymmetric setting. Specifically, ResAware trains a teacher model on resource-level features, and then distills the resulting privileged knowledge into a student model through heterogeneous knowledge distillation. At deployment time, the student model performs inference using only encrypted traffic, incurring zero additional cost. We evaluate ResAware on a large-scale dataset collected over five months from six globally distributed vantage points, comprising more than $160{,}000$ paired samples. The results show that ResAware significantly enhances the cross-environment robustness of diverse WF baselines. Under a 150-day temporal drift, for example, ResAware improves the F1-score of Var-CNN from $72.77\%$ to $81.49\%$ and the open-world $TPR@1\%FPR$ from $22.40\%$ to $27.20\%$. Our results demonstrate that resource-level supervision improves WF robustness without expanding online observation capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管网站指纹（WF）攻击在受控实验室环境中取得了高精度，但在现实环境中，由于时空漂移、浏览器异构性、代理混淆等因素，其性能往往会大幅下降。这一局限性源于它们仅依赖低层流量特征，而这些特征噪声大且对环境扰动高度敏感。为解决这一问题，我们提出了\textbf{ResAware}——一种在\textit{训练丰富/推理贫乏}非对称设定下的跨环境资源感知蒸馏框架。具体而言，ResAware在资源级特征上训练教师模型，然后通过异构知识蒸馏将所得的特权知识转移到学生模型中。在部署时，学生模型仅使用加密流量进行推理，产生零额外成本。我们在跨越五个月、从六个全球分布观测点收集的大规模数据集上评估了ResAware，该数据集包含超过$160{,}000$个配对样本。结果表明，ResAware显著增强了多种WF基线的跨环境鲁棒性。例如，在150天的时间漂移下，ResAware将Var-CNN的F1-score从$72.77\%$提升至$81.49\%$，开放世界$TPR@1\%FPR$从$22.40\%$提升至$27.20\%$。我们的结果表明，资源级监督能够在不扩展在线观测能力的前提下提升WF的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [128] [CheckMIABench: Firm Foundations For Membership Inference Attacks on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17464)
*Jeffrey G. Wang,Jason Wang,Marvin Li,Seth Neel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于训练过程中间检查点的原则性基准测试框架，用于评估针对大语言模型的成员推断攻击，解决了因成员与非成员集分布偏移导致的评估失效问题，并在Pythia和OLMo系列模型上验证了多种攻击方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对语言模型的成员推断攻击评估存在严重缺陷：成员集与非成员集之间存在微妙的分布偏移，导致统计效度受损；甚至"盲目"方法（不访问模型）在相同基准上的表现远优于已发表方法。因此需要构建一个原则性的评估基准。

Method: 利用训练数据在训练过程中某一固定时间点前后均来自同一分布这一关键洞见，将所有具有中间检查点和公开训练数据的开源模型转化为MIA测试平台。具体地，将检查点之前的训练数据视为成员集，之后的视为非成员集，从而消除分布偏移。

Result: 将所提出的框架应用于Pythia和OLMo系列模型（70M到7B参数）上的半打已发表攻击方法，验证了该基准的有效性，并开源了模块化攻击库。

Conclusion: 通过利用训练过程中的中间检查点，可以构建无分布偏移的MIA评估基准，为LLM隐私属性的原则性评估提供了可靠方案。

Original Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are a canonical way to assess a machine learning model's privacy properties. Although several attempts have been made to evaluate MIAs on language models, the extant literature has suffered numerous difficulties in constructing clean evaluations to test new techniques. In particular, subtle distribution shifts between member and non-member sets can undermine the statistical validity of MIAs; recent work has underscored this by showing that "blind" methods with no access to the underlying model can perform far better than published methods on the same benchmarks. This paper constructs a benchmark for principled evaluation of MIAs against LLMs, by leveraging the insight that training data before and after a fixed point during training are drawn from the same distribution. Therefore, all open-source models with intermediate checkpoints and public training data can be converted into MIA testbeds. We apply our framework to a half-dozen published attacks on the Pythia and OLMo family of models, from 70M to 7B parameters. To facilitate further privacy research, we open-source a modular library for designing and implementing attacks in this setting: https://github.com/safr-ai-lab/pandora_llm.

中文摘要翻译: 成员推断攻击（MIA）是评估机器学习模型隐私属性的经典方法。尽管已有多次尝试在语言模型上评估MIA，但现有文献在构建干净评估以测试新技术方面遇到了诸多困难。特别是，成员集与非成员集之间细微的分布偏移会破坏MIA的统计效度；最近的研究通过展示"盲目"方法（不访问底层模型）在同一基准上的表现远优于已发表方法，进一步凸显了这一问题。本文基于一个关键洞见构建了针对大语言模型的原则性MIA评估基准：训练数据在训练过程中某一固定时间点前后均来自同一分布。因此，所有具有中间检查点和公开训练数据的开源模型都可以转化为MIA测试平台。我们将该框架应用于Pythia和OLMo系列模型（70M到7B参数）上的半打已发表攻击方法。为促进进一步的隐私研究，我们开源了一个用于在此设置下设计和实现攻击的模块化库：https://github.com/safr-ai-lab/pandora_llm。

</details>


### [129] [Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17465)
*Shiqi Zhang,Wuwei Wu,Jaemin Oh,Jie Chen,Xiaoning Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Perron-Frobenius算子匹配（PFOM），一个通过积分PF算子匹配密度演化的生成框架，统一了流模型、扩散模型和跳跃模型。证明了在Bregman散度中仅KL散度能保持密度级与样本条件目标的一致性，并开发了Nesterov加速的训练与采样方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型（流、扩散、跳跃）建立在不同的理论基础上，缺乏统一的算子理论框架，且训练与采样的收敛速度和稳定性仍有改进空间。

Method: 1) 提出Perron-Frobenius算子匹配（PFOM）框架，通过积分PF算子统一描述密度演化；2) 证明在Bregman散度中仅KL散度能保持密度级与样本条件目标等价，导出与Koopman路径匹配等价的实用损失；3) 开发Nesterov加速的训练与采样算法以稳定离散化并加速收敛。

Result: 在高斯混合和二 moons 数据集上，PFOM在KL散度、Wasserstein-2距离和最大均值差异（MMD）上实现更快下降，并取得更好的实际时间效率。

Conclusion: PFOM统一了算子理论辨识与现代生成建模，为自适应字典和高维应用开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: We introduce Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching (PFOM), a generative framework that matches density evolution via the integral PF operator, subsuming flow, diffusion, and jump models. We prove that among Bregman divergences, only Kullback--Leibler divergence preserves equality between density-level and sample-conditioned objectives, yielding a practical loss equivalent to Koopman path matching. We further develop Nesterov-accelerated training and sampling that stabilize discretization and accelerate convergence. %On Gaussian mixtures and two-moons, PFOM achieves faster KL/$W_2$/MMD decrease and improved wall-clock efficiency with empirical validation. PFOM unifies operator-theoretic identification with modern generative modeling and opens paths to adaptive dictionaries and high-dimensional applications.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Perron-Frobenius算子匹配（PFOM），一种通过积分PF算子匹配密度演化的生成框架，统一了流模型、扩散模型和跳跃模型。我们证明了在Bregman散度中，仅有Kullback-Leibler散度能保持密度级目标与样本条件目标之间的等价性，从而导出一个与Koopman路径匹配等价的实用损失函数。我们进一步开发了Nesterov加速的训练与采样方法，能够稳定离散化过程并加速收敛。PFOM将算子理论辨识与现代生成建模统一起来，为自适应字典和高维应用开辟了新路径。

</details>


### [130] [Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18122)
*Mostafa Darvishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地总结了面向微控制器平台的嵌入式机器学习工作流，重点关注从数据采集、特征提取到模型部署的全流程工程决策，并通过惯性运动识别和关键词 spotting 两个典型案例加以说明。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 嵌入式机器学习将推理从云端迁移到资源受限设备，但通用机器学习教程往往忽视采样缓冲、特征降维、类别不平衡验证、模型/运行时协同设计和流式部署等关键工程决策，因此需要一篇系统性的工程视角综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统综合的方法，以惯性运动识别和关键词识别两个代表性信号家族为例，详细阐述了嵌入式机器学习全流程：包括采样与缓冲策略、基于降维思想的特征提取（均方根和频谱特征、梅尔频率倒谱系数）、类别不平衡下的验证方法、模型与运行时的协同设计以及流式部署方案。

Result: 通过两个案例（两秒三轴加速度计窗口的RMS和频谱特征分类，以及音频采样、抗混叠、MFCC变换后经紧凑型一维卷积网络处理）展示了嵌入式机器学习的完整工作流。

Conclusion: 总结出面向鲁棒设备端推理的实用设计规则，包括数据整理、量化、阈值设定、调度和现场监控。

Original Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 嵌入式机器学习将推理从云服务转移到资源受限的设备上，这些设备必须在内存、能耗和延迟的严格限制下获取数据、预处理信号、运行模型并执行操作。本文针对微控制器平台，对嵌入式机器学习工作流进行了系统性的工程视角综合。重点放在通用机器学习教程中常被忽视的工程决策上：采样与缓冲、作为降维手段的特征提取、类别不平衡下的验证、模型与运行时的协同设计，以及流式部署。本文选用两个具代表性的信号族进行阐述。其一是惯性运动识别，将一段两秒的三轴加速度计窗口从原始样本转换为均方根和频谱特征后进行分类。其二是关键词识别，对音频进行采样、抗混叠滤波、转换为梅尔频率倒谱系数，再由紧凑型一维卷积网络处理。本文最后总结了面向鲁棒设备端推理的实用设计规则，包括数据整理、量化、阈值设定、调度和现场监控。

</details>


### [131] [ReRAM-aware Model Finetuning addressing I-V Non-linearity and Retention Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17471)
*Ching-Yi Lin,Shamik Kundu,Arnab Raha,Sahil Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，通过在微调过程中引入范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差纳入正则化损失，从而在ReRAM上实现鲁棒的DNN部署，且仅需极少的额外训练开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统CPU、GPU和NPU架构受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈，而基于ReRAM交叉阵列的内存计算虽具有高密度、高能效的优势，但其非理想特性严重制约了实际部署；现有硬件感知训练框架通常需要从头训练，对于现代大规模模型而言计算成本过高。

Method: 提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，通过范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差以正则化损失的形式融入微调过程。

Result: 在ResNet18和DeiT-Tiny等大规模模型上达到与基础模型相近的准确率；在ImageNet上MobileNetV3系列精度下降不超过2%；在SQuAD v2数据集上F-1分数仅下降1点。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于微调的硬件感知训练方法能够以极小的训练开销实现ReRAM上鲁棒的DNN部署，有效缓解了硬件非理想特性带来的性能退化。

Original Abstract: Traditional CPU, GPU, and NPU architectures are increasingly limited by the von Neumann bottleneck. While In-Memory Computing (IMC) using ReRAM crossbar arrays offers a high-density, energy-efficient alternative, its practical deployment is constrained through their non-idealities. Existing hardware-aware training frameworks often require training from scratch, which is computationally prohibitive for modern large-scale models. In this work, we propose a finetuning-based hardware-aware training algorithm that enables robust DNN deployment on ReRAM with minimal training overhead. Our approach mitigates I-V non-linearity by applying a range-shrunk sinh transformation and incorporates retention errors directly into a regularization loss during the finetuning process. We evaluate our framework across models and tasks such as image classification and question-answering (QA). Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves similar accuracy on large-scale models like ResNet18 and DeiT-Tiny as the base model. In-case of ImageNet for MobileNetV3 families the technique has only less than 2% accuracy degradation. Further, applying the technique on the SQuAD v2 dataset results in only 1 point degradation of F-1 score.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的CPU、GPU和NPU架构正日益受到冯·诺依曼瓶颈的限制。虽然基于ReRAM交叉阵列的内存计算提供了一种高密度、高能效的替代方案，但其实际部署受到非理想特性的制约。现有的硬件感知训练框架通常需要从头训练，这对于现代大规模模型而言计算成本过高。本文提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，能够以极小的训练开销在ReRAM上实现鲁棒的深度神经网络部署。我们的方法通过应用范围收缩的sinh变换来缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差直接以正则化损失的形式融入微调过程。我们在图像分类和问答等多个模型与任务上评估了所提框架。实验结果表明，该方法在ResNet18和DeiT-Tiny等大规模模型上达到了与基础模型相近的准确率。在ImageNet上，对于MobileNetV3系列，该技术的精度退化不超过2%。此外，将该技术应用于SQuAD v2数据集，F-1分数仅下降1点。

</details>


### [132] [Multi-Adapter PPO: A Cross-Attention Enhanced Wavelength Selection Framework for LIBS Quantitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17476)
*Hao Li,Man Fung Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多适配器PPO框架，将激光诱导击穿光谱（LIBS）定量分析中的波长选择问题转化为强化学习问题，利用交叉注意力机制和多专用适配器捕捉复杂光谱关系，在钢铁和煤炭数据集上综合评分平均提升28.4%，预测精度提升45.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LIBS定量分析中，高维光谱数据导致波长选择面临关键挑战，传统方法难以在预测精度和特征效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出了一种多适配器PPO（Multi-Adapter PPO）框架，将波长选择建模为强化学习问题，采用交叉注意力机制和多个专用适配器来捕获复杂的光谱关系。

Result: 相比传统粒子群优化（PSO），综合评分平均提升28.4%，预测精度平均提升45.2%，在钢铁和煤炭数据集上均取得最优结果。

Conclusion: 该方法在预测精度与特征效率之间实现了更好的平衡，在LIBS定量分析中取得了最先进的性能，同时保持了可解释性和计算效率。

Original Abstract: Laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) quantitative analysis faces critical challenges in wavelength selection due to high-dimensional spectral data and the fundamental trade-off between prediction accuracy and feature efficiency. This paper presents a novel Multi-Adapter PPO framework that transforms wavelength selection into a reinforcement learning problem, leveraging cross-attention mechanisms and multiple specialized adapters to capture complex spectral relationships. Our approach outperforms traditional Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) by an average of 28.4\% in comprehensive score and 45.2\% in prediction accuracy across steel and coal datasets. The proposed method demonstrates superior performance in balancing prediction accuracy with feature efficiency, achieving state-of-the-art results in LIBS quantitative analysis while maintaining interpretability and computational efficiency. We released our code and dataset here: https://github.com/Hflying/MAPPO

中文摘要翻译: 激光诱导击穿光谱（LIBS）定量分析面临由于高维光谱数据以及预测精度与特征效率之间的根本权衡所导致的波长选择关键挑战。本文提出了一种新颖的多适配器PPO框架，将波长选择转化为强化学习问题，利用交叉注意力机制和多个专用适配器来捕获复杂的光谱关系。我们的方法在钢铁和煤炭数据集上相比传统粒子群优化（PSO）综合评分平均提升28.4%，预测精度平均提升45.2%。所提出的方法在平衡预测精度与特征效率方面展现出优越性能，在LIBS定量分析中取得了最先进的结果，同时保持了可解释性和计算效率。我们在以下网址发布了代码和数据集：https://github.com/Hflying/MAPPO

</details>


### [133] [Online LLM Selection via Constrained Bandits with Time-Varying Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17489)
*Yin Huang,Qingsong Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究边缘-云推理系统中，在硬性资源预算（如费用限制）和软性服务要求（如延迟保证）约束下，在线为大语言模型选择适配任务的算法。问题被建模为带约束的随机赌博机学习，提出一种结合置信区间估计与需求预测的在线学习算法，理论证明其亚线性遗憾与亚线性约束违反，实验验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在边缘-云推理系统中部署时面临任务需求多样、模型性能异构且未知、任务需求随时间变化等挑战，静态选择策略难以满足服务质量与资源效率要求，且实际部署中存在硬性预算约束和软性服务水平约束，亟需一种能在线自适应决策的模型选择方法。

Method: 将问题形式化为带约束的随机赌博机学习问题，包含打包型（硬）约束和覆盖型（软）约束，且任务需求时变。提出一种在线学习算法，利用置信区间估计来应对未知的奖励/成本/延迟分布，并结合需求预测来实现长期约束满足与奖励最大化之间的平衡。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法相对于拥有完全信息的离线基准具有亚线性遗憾和亚线性覆盖约束违反。在合成工作负载上的实验结果表明该方法在动态、资源受限环境下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习算法能够有效应对边缘-云推理系统中LLM选择面临的模型异构、性能未知、需求时变等多重挑战，在硬软约束下实现奖励最大化与约束满足的平衡，具有良好的理论保障和实际表现。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in edge-cloud inference systems to handle diverse user tasks with heterogeneous accuracy, latency, and cost profiles. Selecting the appropriate LLM for each incoming task is critical for ensuring service quality and efficient resource utilization. However, model heterogeneity, stochastic and unknown performance characteristics, and time-varying task demands make static selection strategies inadequate. Real-world deployments often impose hard resource budgets such as monetary expenditure limits, along with soft service-level requirements such as latency guarantees. These constraints introduce additional challenges for online decision-making. We formulate this problem as a constrained stochastic bandit learning task, where the learner sequentially selects models under both packing-type (hard) and covering-type (soft) constraints, while adapting to time-varying task demand. The learner operates without access to the underlying reward, cost, or latency distributions and must rely on partial feedback. We develop a novel online learning algorithm that leverages confidence-bound estimates and demand predictions to balance reward maximization with long-term constraint satisfaction. We provide theoretical guarantees showing sublinear regret and sublinear covering constraint violations compared to an offline benchmark with full information. Experimental results on synthetic workloads demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach in dynamic, resource-constrained environments.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地部署在边缘-云推理系统中，以处理具有不同准确性、延迟和成本特征的多样化用户任务。为每个传入任务选择合适的LLM对于确保服务质量和高效资源利用至关重要。然而，模型异构性、随机且未知的性能特征以及时变的任务需求使得静态选择策略难以胜任。实际部署中往往施加硬性资源预算（如货币支出限制）以及软性服务水平要求（如延迟保证）。这些约束为在线决策带来了额外挑战。本文将该问题形式化为一个带约束的随机赌博机学习任务，学习者在打包型（硬）约束和覆盖型（软）约束下顺序选择模型，同时适应时变的任务需求。学习器无法访问底层的奖励、成本或延迟分布，必须依赖部分反馈。我们提出了一种新颖的在线学习算法，利用置信区间估计和需求预测来平衡奖励最大化与长期约束满足。我们提供了理论保证，表明与拥有完全信息的离线基准相比，该算法具有亚线性遗憾和亚线性覆盖约束违反。在合成工作负载上的实验结果证明了我们的方法在动态、资源受限环境中的有效性和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [134] [Reconfigurable Computing Challenge: Transformer for Jet Tagging on Versal AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17500)
*Gram Koski,Sean Lipps,Zhenghua Ma,G. Abarajithan,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AMD Versal AI Engine的量化整数Transformer框架，用于CERN LHC喷注标记，通过将稠密层和多头注意力层映射到AIE瓦片上，并开发了从高层Python模型自动生成Vitis图代码的可重用软件框架，解决了低延迟、资源受限触发系统中的部署难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于Transformer的模型在CERN LHC喷注标记中表现出色，但将其部署到低延迟、资源受限的触发系统中面临挑战，需要一种高效的硬件实现方案。

Method: 提出了一种量化的整数Transformer实现方法，将稠密层和多头注意力层映射到AMD Versal AI Engine的AIE瓦片上，并开发了可重用的软件框架，将Transformer层表示为可组合的AIE构建模块，从高层Python模型描述自动生成对应的Vitis图代码。

Result: 实现了量化整数Transformer在AMD Versal AIE上的初始部署，开发了可重用框架并开源发布。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来在资源受限的触发系统中部署Transformer模型的研究提供了基础，并且已作为开源软件发布。

Original Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance for jet tagging at the CERN LHC, but deploying them in low-latency, resource-constrained trigger systems is challenging. We present an initial implementation of a quantized, integer-only transformer for jet tagging on the AMD Versal AI Engine (AIE), mapping dense and multi-head attention (MHA) layers to AIE tiles. The main contribution is a reusable software framework that represents transformer layers as composable AIE building blocks and automatically generates the corresponding Vitis graph code from a high-level Python model description. This framework provides a foundation for future research and is released as open-source software at https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Transformer的模型在CERN大型强子对撞机的喷注标记任务中展现出强大性能，但将它们部署到低延迟、资源受限的触发系统中仍然面临挑战。本文提出了一种量化的整数Transformer的初始实现方案，用于在AMD Versal AI引擎（AIE）上进行喷注标记，将稠密层和多头注意力层映射到AIE瓦片上。主要贡献在于开发了一个可重用的软件框架，该框架将Transformer层表示为可组合的AIE构建模块，并能从高层Python模型描述自动生成相应的Vitis图代码。该框架为未来研究奠定了基础，并已作为开源软件发布于https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie。

</details>


### [135] [When the Next Step Is Not One Step: Distribution-Aware Execution Modeling for Concurrent Go Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17508)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对并发程序下一步预测中调度器非确定性导致单一标签训练失效的问题，提出利用非确定性作为训练信号：多次运行程序聚合经验分布，用KL散度微调7B模型。在生产级Go bug数据集上达到36.2%准确率，优于Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和未微调基线（28.6%），且降低了期望校准误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法训练模型预测并发程序下一步时，由于调度器的非确定性，同一程序从相同前缀出发可能产生多个合法后续事件，而模型仅用单一标签训练相当于在猜测随机过程的一个结果，泛化能力差且校准不佳。

Method: 对每个程序多次运行，将观察到的下一步事件聚合为经验分布；使用KL散度目标对7B模型进行微调，使模型输出的概率分布匹配经验分布，而非拟合单一标签。

Result: 在来自CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd等生产级Go bug的798个保留预测上，微调不到一千条轨迹即达36.2%准确率，优于Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和未微调基线（28.6%）；分布训练与交叉熵准确率相当（35.8% vs 36.2%），但将期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。

Conclusion: 利用非确定性作为训练信号是有效且校准良好的并发程序预测方法；分布训练在保持准确率的同时显著改善概率校准；此外还推导了一类select阻塞goroutine的形式化泄漏签名。

Original Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

中文摘要翻译: 训练模型预测并发程序下一步比看起来更困难：同一程序的两次运行从相同轨迹前缀出发可能产生不同的后续事件，且两者均合法，这是因为调度器具有非确定性。使用单一标签训练的模型实际是在学习猜测一个随机过程的结果。我们反其道而行之，将非确定性用作训练信号。我们对每个程序多次运行，将观察到的后续事件聚合成经验分布，并使用KL散度目标对7B模型进行微调以匹配该分布。在从真实生产级Go bug（CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd）中抽取的798个保留预测上，使用不到一千条轨迹进行微调即可达到36.2%的准确率，优于零样本的Gemini 3.5 Flash（34.8%）和未经微调的同一模型（28.6%）。分布训练在准确率上与交叉熵相当（35.8% vs 36.2%），同时将期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。我们还针对一类select阻塞的goroutine推导出形式化的goroutine泄漏签名，其中P(GoUnblock)=0由调度器语义保证而非学习得到。我们发布了数据集、训练好的适配器以及所有工具。

</details>


### [136] [Geometry-Aware Post-Hoc Uncertainty Quantification in Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17513)
*Oriol Vendrell-Gallart,Nima Negarandeh,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REEF-GP框架，利用冻结神经算子的内部嵌入特征的残差拟合高斯过程，在保持预测精度的同时实现几何感知的不确定性量化，计算成本远低于深度集成方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经算子在需要不确定性量化（UQ）的任务中受限，尤其是面对几何变化时；已有方法主要建模网络参数的不确定性，忽略了算子本身学习的几何感知表示。

Method: 提出REEF-GP（Residual on Embedded Features Gaussian Process），是一种事后UQ框架：在冻结的神经算子的残差上拟合高斯过程，利用算子的内部嵌入定义核特征空间；通过谱归一化投影、异方差几何感知噪声和高效子集训练实现稳定性和可扩展性。

Result: 在五个不同几何的PDE基准上，REEF-GP保持预测精度的同时实现了与深度集成竞争力的校准不确定性估计，但成本极低；在几何分布偏移下仍保持鲁棒，不确定性集中在物理有意义的区域（如激波前沿）。

Conclusion: 准确且可扩展的事后UQ可以直接在神经算子的学习特征空间中实现，为参数中心的方法提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for PDEs but their deterministic predictions limit their use in tasks requiring uncertainty quantification (UQ), especially under geometric variability. Existing approaches primarily model uncertainty in network parameters, largely overlooking the geometry-aware representations learned by the operator itself. We propose REEF-GP (Residual on Embedded Features Gaussian Process), a post-hoc UQ framework that fits a GP to the residuals of a frozen neural operator whose internal embeddings define the kernel feature space. Rather than learning a separate feature map, REEF-GP adapts the operator's intrinsic coordinate-feature representations to construct geometry-aware uncertainties. To ensure stability and scalability on unstructured domains, REEF-GP incorporates spectral-normalized projections, heteroscedastic geometry-aware noise, and efficient subset-based training that avoids restrictive low-rank approximations. Across five PDE benchmarks with varying geometries, REEF-GP preserves predictive accuracy while achieving calibrated uncertainty estimates competitive with deep ensembles but at a fraction of their cost. Our approach remains robust under geometric distribution shift, with uncertainty concentrating in physically meaningful regions (e.g., shock fronts). Our results demonstrate that accurate and scalable post-hoc UQ for neural operators can be achieved directly in their learned feature space, offering a practical alternative to parameter-centric approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子为偏微分方程提供了快速替代模型，但其确定性预测限制了它们在需要不确定性量化（UQ）任务中的使用，尤其是在几何变化情况下。现有方法主要建模网络参数的不确定性，很大程度上忽略了算子本身学习的几何感知表示。我们提出REEF-GP（基于嵌入特征残差的高斯过程），一种事后UQ框架，在冻结神经算子的残差上拟合高斯过程，该算子的内部嵌入定义了核特征空间。REEF-GP无需学习单独的特征映射，而是利用算子固有的坐标-特征表示来构建几何感知的不确定性。为确保在非结构化域上的稳定性和可扩展性，REEF-GP引入了谱归一化投影、异方差几何感知噪声以及高效子集训练，避免了限制性的低秩近似。在五个具有不同几何结构的PDE基准测试中，REEF-GP在保持预测精度的同时，实现了与深度集成相媲美的校准不确定性估计，但成本仅为后者的一小部分。我们的方法在几何分布偏移下仍保持鲁棒，不确定性集中在物理有意义的区域（如激波前沿）。结果表明，准确且可扩展的事后UQ可以直接在神经算子的学习特征空间中实现，为参数中心的方法提供了一种实用的替代方案。

</details>


### [137] [FoundCause: Causal Discovery with Latent Confounders from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17516)
*Patrick Blöbaum,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoundCause 是一种基于合成数据训练的摊销因果发现模型，通过单次前向传播将数据集直接映射为因果图，首次显式建模潜在混杂变量，在15个真实数据集上优于11种非摊销方法和4种摊销方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统因果发现方法需要干预实验，且难以同时恢复有向结构和处理潜在混杂变量。现有摊销方法也未能显式建模混杂因素，因此需要一种能端到端学习、处理混杂且高效推理的方法。

Method: 提出FoundCause模型：1）使用置换不变的Transformer编码器，在样本和变量维度交替进行注意力计算；2）通过统计条件注意力注入经典非对称统计特征；3）分解式解码器分离边存在性与方向性；4）三角细化模块推理高阶因果模式（链、对撞）；5）基于可学习隐变量的混杂模块显式建模潜在共同原因；6）通过掩码输入表示处理缺失数据。模型完全在合成数据上训练。

Result: 在15个真实数据集上，FoundCause相比最强的非摊销方法：F1提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构汉明距离降低18.9%，同时仅需单次前向传播完成推理。

Conclusion: FoundCause证明了基于合成数据训练的摊销模型可以超越经典非摊销方法，且首次实现了对潜在混杂变量的显式建模，为高效、可扩展的因果发现提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the need to recover directed structure and latent confounding without interventions. We propose FoundCause, an amortized causal discovery model trained entirely on synthetic data that maps datasets directly to causal graphs in a single forward pass. By learning from large collections of simulated structural causal models, FoundCause captures transferable statistical patterns that generalize beyond individual datasets. The architecture incorporates several key inductive biases for causal discovery. It uses a permutation-invariant transformer encoder with alternating attention over samples and variables to jointly model cross-variable dependence and per-variable distributions. Pairwise statistical features derived from classical asymmetry measures are injected through statistics-conditioned attention, guiding the model toward known causal signals. A factorized decoder separates edge existence from direction, while a triangular refinement module enables reasoning over higher-order causal motifs such as chains and colliders. In addition, a dedicated confounder module based on learnable latent tokens explicitly models hidden common causes, and the model explicitly handles missing data via its masked input representation. To our knowledge, FoundCause is the first amortized causal discovery approach to explicitly model latent confounding. FoundCause outperforms 11 classical non-amortized methods (e.g., PC, GES, NOTEARS-style optimization) and 4 amortized causal discovery methods on 15 real-world datasets, achieving +9.6% improvement in $F_1$, +1.2% in AUROC, and an 18.9% reduction in structural Hamming distance relative to the strongest non-amortized methods, while performing inference in a single forward pass.

中文摘要翻译: 从观测数据进行因果发现仍然具有挑战性，原因在于需要在没有干预的情况下恢复有向结构和潜在混杂。我们提出FoundCause，一种完全在合成数据上训练的摊销因果发现模型，能够在单次前向传播中将数据集直接映射为因果图。通过学习大量模拟的结构因果模型，FoundCause捕获了可迁移的统计模式，这些模式能够泛化到单个数据集之外。该架构融合了若干关键的因果发现归纳偏置。它使用一个置换不变的Transformer编码器，在样本和变量维度上交替进行注意力计算，以联合建模跨变量依赖关系和每个变量的分布。从经典非对称性度量中导出的成对统计特征通过统计条件注意力注入，引导模型关注已知的因果信号。一个分解式解码器将边的存在性与方向性分离，而三角细化模块则支持对链式和对撞器等高阶因果模式进行推理。此外，一个基于可学习隐变量的专用混杂模块显式地建模隐藏的共同原因，并且该模型通过其掩码输入表示显式处理缺失数据。据我们所知，FoundCause是第一个显式建模潜在混杂的摊销因果发现方法。FoundCause在15个真实数据集上优于11种经典非摊销方法（例如PC、GES、NOTEARS风格优化）和4种摊销因果发现方法，相比最强的非摊销方法，F1分数提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构汉明距离降低18.9%，同时仅需单次前向传播即可完成推理。

</details>


### [138] [Learning to Refine Hidden States for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17524)
*Chia-Hsuan Hsu,Jui-Ming Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReLAR 提出了一种基于强化学习的潜在表示精炼框架，通过迭代更新隐藏状态来提升大模型在多步推理中的稳定性和准确性，同时比显式思维链方法更高效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂多步推理中，早期隐藏状态的错误会传播导致最终预测错误，内部推理过程不稳定。现有显式思维链方法推理开销大，缺乏自适应调整机制。

Method: 提出 ReLAR 框架，维护紧凑的潜在推理状态，使用学习的深度控制器和动作控制器自适应决定精炼步数和方向，基于策略梯度（以步级似然提升为目标）训练控制器，在解码前迭代更新隐藏表示。

Result: 在医疗、数学、多跳推理和开放生成基准测试上，ReLAR 提升了准确性、生成质量和推理稳定性，推理开销显著低于显式推理基线。

Conclusion: ReLAR 通过强化学习引导的潜在精炼，实现了高效且稳定的输入自适应推理，无需显式思维链生成，验证了潜在层次推理的可行性。

Original Abstract: Large language models show strong reasoning ability, but their internal reasoning process can remain unstable in complex multi-step settings, where early hidden-state errors may propagate to incorrect predictions. We propose ReLAR, a reinforcement-guided latent refinement framework that iteratively updates hidden representations before decoding. ReLAR maintains a compact latent reasoning state and uses learned depth and action controllers to adaptively determine both the number and direction of refinement steps. The controllers are trained with a policy gradient objective based on step-wise likelihood improvement, enabling efficient input-dependent reasoning without explicit chain-of-thought generation. Experiments on medical, mathematical, multi-hop reasoning, and open-ended generation benchmarks show that ReLAR improves accuracy, generation quality, and reasoning stability with substantially lower inference overhead than explicit reasoning baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型展现出强大的推理能力，但其内部推理过程在复杂的多步设定中仍可能不稳定，早期隐藏状态的错误可能传播并导致错误的预测。我们提出了 ReLAR，一种强化引导的潜在精炼框架，该框架在解码前迭代地更新隐藏表示。ReLAR维护一个紧凑的潜在推理状态，并使用学习得到的深度控制器和动作控制器自适应地决定精炼步数和方向。控制器基于步级似然提升的策略梯度目标进行训练，从而无需显式的思维链生成即可实现高效的输入依赖推理。在医疗、数学、多跳推理以及开放生成基准测试上的实验表明，与显式推理基线相比，ReLAR在显著降低推理开销的同时，提升了准确性、生成质量和推理稳定性。

</details>


### [139] [MGUP: A Momentum-Gradient Alignment Update Policy for Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17526)
*Da Chang,Ganzhao Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MGUP机制，通过每轮对固定比例参数使用大步长、其余使用小步长，增强动量优化器。与AdamW、Lion、Muon等优化器即插即用，提供收敛保证，在MAE预训练、LLM预训练和下游微调中取得更优或更稳定的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有层内选择性更新缺乏通用机制，无法在保证收敛的同时实现细粒度控制。

Method: 提出MGUP机制，为动量优化器增加选择性更新：每轮迭代对固定比例的选定参数使用大步长，其余参数使用较小的非零步长。作为即插即用模块，可无缝集成到AdamW、Lion、Muon等优化器中。

Result: 在MAE预训练、LLM预训练和下游微调等多样化任务上，MGUP增强的优化器相比原始优化器取得了更优或更稳定的性能。

Conclusion: MGUP提供了一种有原则、通用且理论扎实的层内选择性更新策略，能加速并稳定大规模模型的训练。

Original Abstract: Efficient optimization is essential for training large language models. Although intra-layer selective updates have been explored, a general mechanism that enables fine-grained control while ensuring convergence guarantees is still lacking. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{MGUP}, a novel mechanism for selective updates. \textbf{MGUP} augments standard momentum-based optimizers by applying larger step-sizes to a selected fixed proportion of parameters in each iteration, while applying smaller, non-zero step-sizes to the rest. As a nearly {plug-and-play} module, \textbf{MGUP} seamlessly integrates with optimizers such as AdamW, Lion, and Muon. This yields powerful variants such as \textbf{MGUP-AdamW}, \textbf{MGUP-Lion}, and \textbf{MGUP-Muon}. Under standard assumptions, we provide theoretical convergence guarantees for \textbf{MGUP-AdamW} (without weight decay) in stochastic optimization. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks, including MAE pretraining, LLM pretraining, and downstream fine-tuning, demonstrate that our \textbf{MGUP}-enhanced optimizers achieve superior or more stable performance compared to their original base optimizers. We offer a principled, versatile, and theoretically grounded strategy for efficient intra-layer selective updates, accelerating and stabilizing the training of large-scale models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/MaeChd/MGUP.

中文摘要翻译: 高效优化对训练大型语言模型至关重要。尽管层内选择性更新已被探索，但仍缺乏一种既能实现细粒度控制又能保证收敛的通用机制。为填补这一空白，我们提出了\textbf{MGUP}，一种新颖的选择性更新机制。\textbf{MGUP}对基于动量的标准优化器进行增强，在每轮迭代中对选定固定比例的参数施加较大的步长，而对其余参数施加较小的非零步长。作为一个近乎即插即用的模块，\textbf{MGUP}可以无缝集成到AdamW、Lion和Muon等优化器中，产生如\textbf{MGUP-AdamW}、\textbf{MGUP-Lion}和\textbf{MGUP-Muon}等强大的变体。在标准假设下，我们为随机优化中的\textbf{MGUP-AdamW}（不含权重衰减）提供了理论收敛保证。涵盖MAE预训练、LLM预训练和下游微调等多种任务的广泛实验表明，我们经过\textbf{MGUP}增强的优化器相比原始基础优化器取得了更优或更稳定的性能。我们提供了一种有原则、通用且理论扎实的高效层内选择性更新策略，可加速并稳定大规模模型的训练。代码已公开在https://github.com/MaeChd/MGUP。

</details>


### [140] [Non-negative Matrix Factorisation with Topological Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17531)
*Matias de Jong van Lier,Shizuo Kaji,Keunsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在非负矩阵分解（NMF）中引入持续同调作为拓扑正则化项，学习具有可解释性的基函数，使基函数在空间、时间、图信号等结构化数据上保持拓扑一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多数据模态可视为定义在结构化域上的非负函数，基函数的质量与其支撑集的拓扑结构密切相关，但现有方法因离散性和阈值依赖而难以融入连续优化。

Method: 利用持续同调作为稳定的、无阈值的拓扑量化工具，设计拓扑得分作为正则化项，将其集成到NMF目标函数中进行联合优化。

Result: 所提出的框架能够统一建模空间上的图像组件、周期性的时间序列结构以及团状的图信号，在多个数据模态上实现拓扑可解释的基函数学习。

Conclusion: 持续同调正则化能有效将拓扑先验融入NMF，提升基函数的可解释性，框架具有跨模态的通用性。

Original Abstract: We investigate the learning of interpretable bases in non-negative matrix factorisation (NMF) by regularising the topology of the learned basis functions. Our approach is motivated by the observation that many data modalities can be viewed as non-negative functions on a structured domain, where the quality of a basis is intrinsically linked to its topology. However, naive methods for incorporating the topology of the support are often hindered by discreteness and threshold dependence, rendering them unsuitable for continuous optimisation. We address these challenges by employing persistent homology as a stable, threshold-free topological quantifier and by designing topological scores that integrate into the NMF objective as regularisers. The resulting framework encompasses spatially coherent image components, periodic time-series structures, and clique-like graph signals within a unified modelling language.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在非负矩阵分解（NMF）中通过学习基函数的拓扑正则化来获取可解释基函数的方法。我们的动机源于观察到许多数据模态可以看作是定义在结构化域上的非负函数，其中基函数的质量与其支撑集的拓扑结构内在相关。然而，朴素的拓扑融入方法常受离散性和阈值依赖问题所困扰，无法适用于连续优化。为应对这些挑战，我们采用持续同调作为一种稳定的、无阈值的拓扑量化工具，并设计拓扑得分作为正则化项集成到NMF目标函数中。由此形成的框架能够在统一的建模语言下涵盖空间上连贯的图像组件、周期性的时间序列结构以及团状图信号。

</details>


### [141] [Offline Preference-Based Trajectory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17541)
*Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于偏好的轨迹评估方法，通过比较时间维度的进度和返回时间曲线来替代传统的任务级成功指标，显著降低评估中的平局率（从75%降至35%），提升区分能力、排名稳定性和数据效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的离线智能体系统评估仅关注最终任务是否成功，忽略了轨迹过程中的部分进展信息，导致大量平局（约75%），降低了有效样本量和统计效率，难以区分不同系统的优劣。

Method: 提出偏好式轨迹评估（preference-based trajectory evaluation），直接比较两条轨迹在时间维度上的进度（progress）和返回时间（time-to-return）曲线，通过时间偏好来评估系统性能。

Result: 在多个智能体与交互式基准测试中，传统成功指标在约75%的实例上产生平局，而轨迹感知偏好将平局率降至约35%，显著提升了区分能力、排名稳定性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 基准测试饱和现象可能不仅源于数据收集质量差或问题难度，评估指标的选择本身也是一个重要因素。

Original Abstract: Offline evaluation of agentic systems often collapses trajectories to terminal success, discarding information about partial progress and inducing widespread ties, creating substantial statistical inefficiency by reducing effective sample size and weakening the ability to distinguish systems. We propose preference-based trajectory evaluation, which compares trajectories directly through temporal preferences over progress and time-to-return profiles. We find that, across diverse agentic and interactive benchmarks, standard success-based metrics produce tied comparisons on roughly 75% of instances, whereas trajectory-aware preferences reduce ties to roughly 35%, improving discriminative power, ranking stability, and data efficiency. Our results suggest that benchmark saturation, often attributed to poor data collection or problem difficulty, may also be explained by the choice of evaluation measure.

中文摘要翻译: 对智能体系统进行离线评估时，通常会将其轨迹坍缩为最终是否成功，这丢弃了关于部分进展的信息并导致广泛的平局，从而降低了有效样本量并削弱了区分不同系统的能力，造成显著的统计效率低下。我们提出基于偏好的轨迹评估方法，该方法通过比较轨迹在时间维度上的进度和返回时间曲线来直接进行对比。我们发现，在多种智能体和交互式基准测试中，基于是否成功的标准指标在大约75%的实例上产生平局比较，而轨迹感知偏好将平局率降低至约35%，从而提升了区分能力、排名稳定性和数据效率。我们的结果表明，基准测试饱和现象——通常归因于数据收集质量差或问题难度——也可能由评估指标的选择所解释。

</details>


### [142] [Continuous-time Optimal Stopping through Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17545)
*Cosmin Borsa,Michael Ludkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARLOS算法，一种基于强化学习的最优停时问题求解方法，通过聚合深度神经网络学习联合时空决策边界，并采用自适应采样策略，能够在任意精细时间分辨率下学习停时规则，克服了传统动态规划方法在粗网格下估值不足和细网格下误差累积的局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于动态规划的最优停时问题求解器需要对停时决策进行离散化。粗网格下仅有少量停时机会会导致期望奖励被低估，而细网格下近似误差会通过反向递推累积。为了消除这一局限性，需要一种能够在任意精细时间分辨率下学习停时规则的新方法。

Method: 提出了CARLOS（连续时间自适应强化学习最优停时）算法。该方法利用聚合深度神经网络（ADNN）学习联合时空决策边界；从粗时间网格开始，逐步增加停时机会的频率，同时并行训练ADNN以精化其时机价值估计；并设计了自适应采样策略，逐步将训练集中到停时边界附近。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，CARLOS能提供比现有百慕大求解器更高的价格，接近美式期权上界，并且与非强化学习对照方法相比具有较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: CARLOS算法有效克服了传统动态规划方法在网格离散化上的固有限制，通过强化学习框架实现了任意精细时间分辨率下的最优停时求解，在定价精度和计算效率方面均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Simulation based solvers for optimal stopping problems must discretize the stopping decision. Under classical dynamic programming, a coarse exercise grid with only a few stopping opportunities can materially undervalue the optimal expected reward, whereas on a very fine grid, approximation errors accumulate through the backward recursion. To remove this limitation, we develop a new reinforcement-learning inspired algorithm that enables us to learn the exercise rule at arbitrarily fine time resolution. Our CARLOS (Continuous-time Adaptive Reinforcement Learning for Optimal Stopping) algorithm utilizes an aggregate deep neural network (ADNN) to learn a joint space-time decision boundary. Starting from a coarse time grid, we progressively increase the frequency of stopping opportunities, while in parallel training the ADNN to refine its timing-value estimates. We moreover design an adaptive sampling strategy that gradually concentrates training effort near the stopping boundary. Benchmarked results show that CARLOS delivers higher prices than existing Bermudan solvers, approaching the American upper bound, and achieves high computational efficiency relative to non-RL comparators.

中文摘要翻译: 基于模拟的最优停时问题求解器必须对停时决策进行离散化。在经典动态规划框架下，仅有少量停时机会的粗网格会严重低估最优期望回报，而在非常细的网格上，近似误差会通过反向递推不断累积。为消除这一局限性，我们提出了一种受强化学习启发的新算法，使我们能够在任意精细的时间分辨率下学习停时规则。我们的CARLOS（连续时间自适应强化学习最优停时）算法利用聚合深度神经网络（ADNN）学习一个联合时空决策边界。从粗时间网格出发，我们逐步增加停时机会的频率，同时并行训练ADNN以精化其时点价值估计。此外，我们还设计了一种自适应采样策略，逐步将训练集中在停时边界附近。基准测试结果表明，与现有百慕大求解器相比，CARLOS能提供更高的价格，接近美式期权上界，并且相对于非强化学习对照方法实现了较高的计算效率。

</details>


### [143] [Reversal Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17551)
*Aditya Oberai,Seohong Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RQL算法，将流匹配的迭代生成过程建模为扩展MDP，通过反向流生成虚拟on-policy轨迹并结合偏差-方差缩减技术，实现高效离策略强化学习，在50个模拟机器人任务上达到最优离线性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于流的离线RL方法存在通过时间反向传播（BPTT）的计算负担、对学习到的价值函数利用不充分、以及无法直接训练完整的表达性流策略等问题，亟需一种更高效且表达能力更强的流策略训练算法。

Method: 将流匹配的每一步细化视为扩展MDP中的独立动作，通过"反转"流生成虚拟的on-policy轨迹以兼容历史数据，并应用偏差-方差缩减技术来缓解离策略RL中的维度灾难问题，最终提出Reversal Q-learning（RQL）算法。

Result: 在50个具有挑战性的模拟机器人任务上，RQL相比当前最先进的基于流的离线RL算法取得了最好的平均离线RL性能。

Conclusion: RQL避免了通过时间反向传播，能更好地利用学习到的价值函数，并直接训练完整的表达性流策略，在离线RL任务上表现优异。

Original Abstract: Iterative generative modeling techniques, such as flow matching, provide powerful tools to model complex behaviors for effective offline reinforcement learning (RL). In this work, we propose a new off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy based on prior data. Our idea starts from the "expanded" Markov decision process (MDP) framework, which treats individual flow refinement steps as separate actions in an MDP. To enable off-policy RL within this framework, we apply two techniques: we generate virtual on-policy trajectories (by "reversing" flows) to make this framework compatible with prior data, and we apply a bias-and-variance reduction technique to mitigate the curse of horizon in off-policy RL. We call the resulting algorithm Reversal Q-learning (RQL). RQL has several advantages over previous flow-based RL methods: it does not suffer from backpropagation through time, makes better use of the learned value function, and directly trains the full, expressive flow policy. Through our experiments on 50 challenging simulated robotic tasks, we show that RQL leads to the best average offline RL performance compared to state-of-the-art flow-based offline RL algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如流匹配等迭代式生成建模技术为建模复杂行为以进行有效的离线强化学习（RL）提供了强大工具。本文提出了一种新的离策略RL算法，该算法基于先验数据训练一个流策略。我们的思路源于"扩展"马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架，该框架将单个流的细化步骤视为MDP中的独立动作。为了在此框架内实现离策略RL，我们应用了两种技术：通过"反转"流生成虚拟的on-policy轨迹，使该框架与先验数据兼容；以及应用偏差-方差缩减技术来缓解离策略RL中的维度灾难问题。我们将所得到的算法称为Reversal Q-learning（RQL）。RQL相比先前基于流的RL方法具有若干优势：它无需通过时间反向传播、能更好地利用学习到的价值函数、并且直接训练完整的、具有表达力的流策略。通过在50个具有挑战性的模拟机器人任务上的实验，我们证明与最先进的基于流的离线RL算法相比，RQL取得了最好的平均离线RL性能。

</details>


### [144] [SpatioTemporal Causal Network Diagnostics for Geographic Tipping Point Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17553)
*Zhaoyuan Yu,Zhangyong Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时空因果网络诊断（ST-CND）框架，通过将地理场建模为时变有向因果网络，解决传统空间指标在局部早期预警中面临的空间稀释、欧几里得假设和噪声相关三大问题，在合成分岔和SST基准上实现更优的局部可解释预警。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统空间指标（如Moran's I）在生态系统、气候子系统或冰盖的地理临界点预警中效果不佳，主要存在三个问题：空间稀释（全局指标弱化局部信号）、欧几里得假设（固定邻域忽略非欧信息流）和相关噪声（空间相关噪声导致虚警）。因此需要一种能够实现局部化、数据驱动且抗噪声的早期预警方法。

Method: ST-CND框架包含三步核心流程：(1) 利用转移熵推断空间节点间的信息流拓扑，取代固定欧几里得邻域；(2) 通过动态模态分解在每个候选子网络内估计局部恢复率；(3) 综合高内部波动、高内部同步和低外部耦合三个信号识别最脆弱子网络，从而抑制空间相关噪声引起的虚警。
