These dynamics arise across domains - vehicular sensing, drone-based monitoring, smartphone crowdsensing - yet the interplay of privacy, spatial heterogeneity, and temporal drift severely undermines conventional learning strategies. Therefore, we propose C2FL, a fully distributed Federated Learning (FL) approach where nodes self-organize into learning groups through spatial clustering, reflecting the geographic structure of the environment. To counteract temporal drift, each node combines experience replay with a dwell-time-aware adaptive averaging step, progressively incorporating the regional consensus as it remains longer within the same area, while preserving previously acquired knowledge under evolving distributions. We evaluate our approach on synthetic experiments that systematically reproduce spatial and temporal shifts, showing that standard federated strategies degrade significantly under these conditions and that our method restores robust collective adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 集体自适应系统（CAS）日益依赖机器学习，使每个节点能够从本地感知数据中学习，从而使其行为与周围环境对齐。然而，扩展这种智能带来了根本性挑战：感知数据通常涉及隐私，无法集中收集；节点具有移动性，穿越不同区域时，附近节点感知到相似现象，而远处节点观察到截然不同的条件，从而形成自然的空间聚类；这些分布因移动性随时间演化，产生时间漂移，使本地模型逐渐过时。这些动态在多个领域普遍存在——车辆感知、无人机监测、智能手机众感知——然而隐私、空间异质性和时间漂移之间的相互作用严重削弱了传统学习策略。因此，我们提出C2FL，一种全分布式联邦学习方法，其中节点通过空间聚类自组织成学习组，反映环境的地理结构。为应对时间漂移，每个节点将经验回放与驻留时间感知的自适应平均步骤相结合，在同一区域停留时间越长，逐步融入区域共识，同时在不断变化的分布下保留先前获得的知识。我们在系统性地再现空间和时间偏移的合成实验上评估了该方法，结果表明标准联邦策略在这些条件下性能显著下降，而我们的方法恢复了鲁棒的集体自适应。

</details>


### [175] [Recursive Scaling in Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18022)
*Alba Carballo-Castro,Julianna Piskorz,Paulius Rauba,Mihaela van der Schaar,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归掩码扩散模型（R-MDMs），通过在扩散步骤内重复使用相同的去噪变换器来增加递归深度作为第三缩放轴，从而在不增加参数量的情况下提升参数效率和推理时计算分配效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的掩码扩散模型（MDMs）缩放主要通过增加参数量或去噪步数实现，缺乏更高效的缩放维度，作者希望引入递归深度作为新的缩放轴，以在不显著增加参数的前提下提升模型性能。

Method: 在掩码扩散模型的每个扩散步骤中，重复应用同一个去噪变换器多次（即递归迭代），通过参数重用来实现输出迭代精炼，从而增加有效模型深度而不增加参数量。

Result: 在数独和Countdown等结构化生成任务上，R-MDMs表现出显著的参数效率提升：具有L次递归迭代的模型性能往往与参数量约L倍的非递归基线模型相当；递归精炼可部分替代额外的去噪步数，以更少的前向传播达到相同生成质量。

Conclusion: 递归深度是掩码扩散模型的一种实用的缩放机制，能同时提升参数效率和测试时计算资源的分配效率。

Original Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for sequence generation. Scaling MDMs is conventionally achieved by increasing the parameter count or the number of denoising steps. We introduce Recursive Masked Diffusion Models (R-MDMs), which add recursive depth as a third scaling axis by repeatedly applying the same denoising transformer within each diffusion step. Recursion enables iterative refinement of the output through parameter reuse, increasing effective model depth without increasing parameter count. Across structured generation tasks, including Sudoku and Countdown, we show that R-MDMs achieve substantially improved parameter efficiency: a model with $L$ recursive iterations often matches the performance of non-recursive baselines with roughly $L\times$ more parameters. Moreover, recursive refinement can partially substitute for additional denoising steps, allowing recursive models to reach the same generation quality with fewer forward passes at inference time. These results suggest that recursive depth is a practically useful scaling mechanism for MDMs, improving both parameter efficiency and the allocation of test-time compute.

中文摘要翻译: 掩码扩散模型（MDMs）最近已成为序列生成的一种有前景的范式。传统上，缩放MDMs通过增加参数量或去噪步数来实现。我们提出了递归掩码扩散模型（R-MDMs），通过在每个扩散步骤内重复应用相同的去噪变换器，将递归深度作为第三缩放轴。递归通过参数重用来实现输出的迭代精炼，在增加有效模型深度的同时不增加参数量。在包括数独和Countdown在内的结构化生成任务上，我们证明R-MDMs实现了显著的参数效率提升：具有L次递归迭代的模型性能往往与非递归基线中参数量约L倍的模型相当。此外，递归精炼可以部分替代额外的去噪步骤，使递归模型在推理时以更少的前向传播达到相同的生成质量。这些结果表明，递归深度是MDMs的一种实用的缩放机制，能同时提升参数效率和测试时计算资源的分配效率。

</details>


### [176] [LoopCoder-v2: Only Loop Once for Efficient Test-Time Computation Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18023)
*Jian Yang,Shawn Guo,Wei Zhang,Tianyu Zheng,Yaxin Du,Haau-Sing Li,Jiajun Wu,Yue Song,Yan Xing,Qingsong Cai,Zelong Huang,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Xianglong Liu,Wayne Xin Zhao,Mingjie Tang,Weifeng Lv,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并行循环Transformer（PLT）并研究了循环次数选择问题，发现两轮循环效果最佳，三轮及以上会导致性能退化，原因是增益-成本权衡：循环提供的细化收益递减，而跨循环位置偏移造成的成本固定不变。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 循环Transformer虽能扩展潜在计算，但顺序循环增加了延迟和KV缓存内存消耗。并行循环Transformer（PLT）通过跨循环位置偏移和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力缓解了这一问题，使循环次数成为可实际设计的选择，因此需要研究如何选择最优循环次数。

Method: 提出并行循环Transformer（PLT），采用跨循环位置偏移和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力机制。训练了7B参数的LoopCoder-v2模型家族（不同循环次数），在18T token上从零训练，再进行指令微调，并通过增益-成本视角分析循环次数选择问题。

Result: 两循环变体相比无循环基线在代码生成、代码推理、智能体软件工程和工具使用等多个基准上取得显著提升（SWE-bench Verified从43.0提升至64.4，Multi-SWE从14.0提升至31.0）。三循环及以上变体性能退化。诊断表明循环2提供主要细化收益，后续循环收益递减且产生振荡更新，而CLP导致的偏移成本基本固定，导致增益-成本失衡。

Conclusion: PLT模型存在循环次数的饱和点（两轮），循环次数选择受增益-成本权衡驱动：早期循环提供实质性细化，后期循环收益递减而位置偏移成本固定，由此解释了PLT在两轮处饱和的现象，并为循环次数选择提供了诊断方法。

Original Abstract: Looped Transformers scale latent computation by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but sequential looping increases latency and KV-cache memory with the loop count. Parallel loop Transformers (PLT) alleviate this cost through cross-loop position offsets (CLP) and shared-KV gated sliding-window attention, making loop count a practical design choice. We therefore study PLT loop-count selection through a gain--cost view: an extra loop may refine representations, but CLP also introduces a positional mismatch at each loop boundary. We instantiate this study by training LoopCoder-v2, a family of 7B PLT coders with different loop counts, from scratch on 18T tokens, followed by matched instruction tuning and evaluation. Empirically, the two-loop variant delivers broad gains over the non-looped baseline across code generation, code reasoning, agentic software engineering, and tool-use benchmarks, improving SWE-bench Verified from 43.0 to 64.4 points and Multi-SWE from 14.0 to 31.0 points. In contrast, variants with three or more loops regress, revealing a strongly non-monotonic loop-count effect. Our diagnostics show that loop 2 provides the main productive refinement, while later loops yield diminishing, oscillatory updates and reduced representational diversity. Because the CLP-induced mismatch remains roughly fixed as refinement gains shrink, the offset cost increasingly dominates. This gain--cost trade-off explains PLT's saturation at two loops and provides diagnostics for loop-count selection.

中文摘要翻译: 循环Transformer通过重复应用共享模块来扩展潜在计算，但顺序循环的延迟和KV缓存内存会随循环次数增加而增长。并行循环Transformer（PLT）通过跨循环位置偏移（CLP）和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力缓解了这一代价，使得循环次数成为实际可设计的选择。因此，我们从增益-成本视角研究PLT的循环次数选择：额外的循环可能细化表示，但CLP在每个循环边界引入了位置不匹配。我们通过训练LoopCoder-v2系列模型实例化这一研究，这是一个从零开始在18T token上训练、具有不同循环次数的7B PLT编码器家族，随后进行匹配的指令微调和评估。实验表明，两循环变体在代码生成、代码推理、智能体软件工程和工具使用基准上相比无循环基线取得了广泛提升，将SWE-bench Verified从43.0提高到64.4分，Multi-SWE从14.0提高到31.0分。相比之下，三轮及以上循环的变体出现性能退化，揭示了强烈的非单调循环次数效应。我们的诊断表明，循环2提供了主要的有效细化，而后续循环产生递减且振荡的更新以及降低的表示多样性。由于CLP引起的位置不匹配在细化收益缩小时基本保持不变，偏移成本日益占据主导。这种增益-成本权衡解释了PLT在两轮处的饱和现象，并为循环次数选择提供了诊断方法。

</details>


### [177] [Catastrophic Forgetting is Low-Rank: A Function-Space Theory for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18024)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角（NTK框架）分析持续适应中的灾难性遗忘，发现新任务训练导致的旧任务预测漂移可通过跨任务核精确预测，并揭示遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模态中，据此提出针对性谱正则化方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有持续适应研究多从参数漂移、重放或蒸馏角度理解灾难性遗忘，但无法识别哪些输出空间方向容易受到遗忘影响。本文旨在从函数空间视角揭示遗忘的分布规律，并利用NTK理论给出精确的闭式预测。

Method: 在NTK机制下，通过跨任务核推导出新任务训练导致的旧任务预测漂移的闭式表达式（遗忘向量预测器）；在冻结主干+线性头的PEFT-CL场景中预测精确成立，在非线性适配器/全微调场景下作为局部NTK近似；据此提出针对性的谱正则化方法。

Result: 遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模态中；冻结线性头下给出易遗忘秩的Kronecker缩放规则；阐明了与先前NTK重叠理论的关系；解释了参数空间正则化器无法捕捉输出空间干扰的原因。

Conclusion: 函数空间NTK视角能精确刻画持续适应中的遗忘模式，并指导设计更有效的输出空间正则化策略。

Original Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual adaptation is usually studied through parameter drift, replay, or distillation, but these views do not identify which output-space directions are vulnerable. We give a function-space account in the NTK regime: new-task training induces old-task prediction drift through the cross-task kernel, yielding a closed-form predictor for the forgetting vector before any new-task gradient step. In frozen-backbone linear-head PEFT-CL, where the model is linear in the trainable parameters, the predictor is exact up to numerical precision; for nonlinear adapters/full fine-tuning, it is a local NTK approximation. The same expression reveals that forgetting concentrates in a small number of old-task NTK eigenmodes and under frozen linear heads gives a Kronecker scaling rule for the vulnerable rank. These results clarify the relation to prior NTK-overlap theory, explain why parameter-space regularizers can miss output-space interference, and motivate a targeted spectral regularizer.

中文摘要翻译: 持续适应中的灾难性遗忘通常通过参数漂移、重放或蒸馏来研究，但这些视角无法识别哪些输出空间方向是脆弱的。我们在NTK机制下给出一个函数空间解释：新任务训练通过跨任务核诱导旧任务预测漂移，从而在新任务梯度步骤之前就能得到遗忘向量的闭式预测器。在冻结主干线性头的PEFT-CL中（模型对可训练参数是线性的），该预测器精确到数值精度；对于非线性适配器/全微调，它则是局部NTK近似。同一表达式揭示遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模态中，且在冻结线性头下给出易遗忘秩的Kronecker缩放规则。这些结果阐明了与先前NTK重叠理论的关系，解释了参数空间正则化器为何会遗漏输出空间干扰，并启发了一种针对性的谱正则化方法。

</details>


### [178] [ConTex: Reformulating Counterfactual Generation For Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18049)
*Jan Voets,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen,Sebastian Baum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTex框架，将时间序列预测中的反事实生成重构为全局一致的干预策略学习问题，通过分解架构实现单次前向传播生成可解释、稀疏的反事实解释，支持实时应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的时间序列预测模型缺乏可操作洞察，无法指导如何修改当前条件以将预测结果转变为期望的未来情景。现有反事实解释方法基于实例级优化，存在跨实例不一致、计算成本高、实时应用受限等问题。

Method: 提出ConTex（Counterfactual Time Series Explanations），一种模型无关的分解架构，包含时间上下文编码器和条件编码器，后接两个头分别捕获时间相关性和修改强度，通过单个共享函数学习全局一致的干预策略，单次前向传播即可生成反事实解释。

Result: 在多个预测架构和基准数据集上，ConTex实现了最先进的有效性，生成的稀疏反事实最小化必要干预次数；计算成本降低至少12-36倍，支持约0.007秒的实时推理。

Conclusion: ConTex通过全局一致的干预策略学习，克服了实例级反事实生成的不稳定性和不一致性，在保持高有效性的同时大幅降低计算成本，适用于实时场景。

Original Abstract: Decision-making with deep learning-based time series forecasting requires not only accurate predictions but also actionable insights. However, current architectures do not inherently provide such information. Specifically, guidance is needed on how current conditions must be modified to shift from a predicted outcome to a desired future scenario. Counterfactual explanations provide a natural framework for this task, as they represent minimal input changes that alter the model's prediction, indicating when and how intervention is required. Existing approaches rely on instance-wise optimization, leading to inconsistency across instances, high computational costs, and limited applicability in real-time settings.
  To address these limitations, we reformulate counterfactual generation for time series forecasting as the problem of learning a globally consistent intervention strategy, allowing counterfactuals to be generated through a single shared function. We propose Counterfactual Time Series Explanations (ConTex), a model-agnostic, decomposed architecture comprising a temporal context encoder and a conditional encoder, followed by two heads that capture interventions in terms of temporal relevance and modification strength. This structure overcomes the instability and inconsistency of instance-based approaches by producing targeted, interpretable interventions across time and feature dimensions in a single forward pass, making it suitable for real-time applications.
  Across multiple forecasting architectures and benchmark datasets, ConTex achieves state-of-the-art validity while generating sparse counterfactuals that minimize the number of necessary interventions. Additionally, our approach reduces computational cost by at least 12-36x compared to instance-wise generation and supports real-time inference at approximately 0.007 seconds.

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度学习时间序列预测的决策制定不仅需要准确的预测，还需要可操作的洞察。然而，当前架构本身并不提供此类信息。具体而言，需要指导如何修改当前条件，以便从预测结果转变为期望的未来情景。反事实解释为这一任务提供了天然框架，因为它们表示能够改变模型预测的最小输入变化，指示何时以及如何进行干预是必要的。现有方法依赖于实例级优化，导致跨实例不一致、计算成本高，且在实时环境中适用性有限。
为应对这些局限性，我们将时间序列预测中的反事实生成重新表述为学习全局一致的干预策略问题，使得可以通过单个共享函数生成反事实解释。我们提出ConTex（Counterfactual Time Series Explanations，反事实时间序列解释），一种模型无关的分解架构，包含时间上下文编码器和条件编码器，后接两个头分别以时间相关性和修改强度的形式捕获干预信息。该结构通过在单次前向传播中跨时间和特征维度生成针对性、可解释的干预，克服了基于实例方法的不稳定性和不一致性，使其适用于实时应用。
在多种预测架构和基准数据集上，ConTex实现了最先进的有效性，同时生成最小化必要干预次数的稀疏反事实。此外，与实例级生成相比，我们的方法计算成本降低至少12-36倍，并支持约0.007秒的实时推理。

</details>


### [179] [NoiseTilt: Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernels for Diffusion Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18066)
*Jisung Hwang,Yunhong Min,Jaihoon Kim,I-Chao Shen,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出噪声倾斜反向核（NTRK），一种奖励引导的扩散采样器，通过将奖励梯度注入噪声项而非改变反向均值，在保持生成质量的同时实现高效奖励对齐，仅需单步单样本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有奖励引导扩散采样方法存在两难：基于梯度的方法通过偏移反向均值引导生成，但会将中间状态推离训练分布，降低质量；基于搜索的方法保留质量但无法利用梯度信号。尚无方法能同时兼顾两者。

Method: NTRK保持预训练扩散模型的反向均值不变，通过一个白化算子（whitening operator）对奖励梯度进行变换，使其可以安全地作为噪声注入到噪声项中，从而引导生成朝向高奖励区域，且每步仅需单样本。

Result: 在多个奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在不损失样本质量的前提下超越当前最优基线。在美学生成任务中，NTRK仅用25次NFE即达到最佳基线在500次NFE下的奖励分数，计算量降低20倍。

Conclusion: NTRK通过将奖励梯度注入噪声项而非偏移均值，成功解决了奖励引导扩散采样中质量保持与梯度信号利用之间的矛盾，实现了高效且高质量的奖励对齐生成。

Original Abstract: We introduce the Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel (NTRK), a reward-guided diffusion sampler that injects reward gradients through the noise term, leaving the pretrained reverse kernel unchanged and requiring only a single sample per step. Reward-guided sampling at inference time has greatly expanded the versatility of pretrained diffusion models. Yet existing methods face a trade-off. Gradient-based guidance shifts the reverse mean, steering generation but pushing intermediate states outside the region that the model was trained on and degrading quality. Search-based methods preserve quality but gain no gradient signal. No prior method achieves both. NTRK resolves this by keeping the reverse mean fixed and biasing the noise term toward high reward. We introduce a whitening operator, the central mechanism behind NTRK, that makes the reward gradient safe to inject as noise without losing its guiding signal. Across various reward alignment tasks, NTRK outperforms recent state-of-the-art baselines without losing sample quality. Remarkably, on aesthetic generation, NTRK surpasses the reward of the best baseline at 500 NFEs using only 25 NFEs, a 20$\times$ reduction in compute.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了噪声倾斜反向核（Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel, NTRK），一种奖励引导的扩散采样器。该方法将奖励梯度注入噪声项，同时保持预训练的反向核不变，且每步仅需单样本。推理阶段的奖励引导采样极大地扩展了预训练扩散模型的通用性，然而现有方法存在权衡：基于梯度的引导会偏移反向均值，虽能引导生成，但会将中间状态推离模型训练区域，降低生成质量；基于搜索的方法虽能保留质量，但无法获取梯度信号。此前没有方法能同时兼顾二者。NTRK通过保持反向均值固定，并将噪声项偏向高奖励区域来解决这一矛盾。我们引入了白化算子（whitening operator），这是NTRK背后的核心机制，该算子使得奖励梯度能够安全地作为噪声注入，而不会丢失其引导信号。在多种奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在不损失样本质量的情况下超越了最新的基线方法。值得注意的是，在美学生成任务上，NTRK仅使用25次NFE即超越了最佳基线在500次NFE下达到的奖励分数，计算量减少了20倍。

</details>


### [180] [Volterra Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18071)
*Yusen Jia,Bingyan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Volterra生成模型，一种基于分数核路径依赖噪声的连续时间得分匹配框架，通过有限维马尔可夫提升处理非马尔可夫与非半鞅动力学，实验表明在MNIST和CIFAR-10上能够改进得分生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准得分扩散模型使用布朗运动扰动，具有无记忆性的加噪过程，限制了噪声路径的依赖性。本文旨在引入具有记忆性的分数阶噪声，以增强生成模型的表达能力。

Method: 提出Volterra生成模型，前向过程通过分数核注入路径依赖噪声；利用高斯求积和混合有限差分指数近似构造有限维马尔可夫提升；推导增广线性高斯前向过程；通过残差状态和解析辅助高斯得分保持数据维度学习；针对退化问题提出稳定化条件处理和高斯桥重建采样器。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，具有小马尔可夫提升的持久分数扰动能够改进得分生成质量；桥采样器为大提升提供了稳定性机制。

Conclusion: 分数阶路径依赖噪声是传统布朗扰动的一种有前景的扩展，Volterra生成模型能够提升生成质量，并为自然图像生成提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Score-based diffusion models typically use Brownian perturbations, which provide tractable reverse-time dynamics but impose memoryless noising. We introduce Volterra generative models, a continuous-time score-based framework whose forward process injects path-dependent noise through fractional kernels. To handle the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale dynamics, we construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature in both regimes and a hybrid finite-difference exponential approximation in the smooth regime. We prove squared error bounds, derive an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, and show that the learning can remain data-dimensional by considering residual states and analytic auxiliary Gaussian scores. We also identify covariance and reverse-time degeneracies caused by shared Brownian factors and signed smooth-regime weights. The degeneracy motivates stabilized conditioning and, for stiff larger lifts, a Gaussian-bridge reconstruction sampler. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that persistent fractional perturbations with small Markovian lifts can improve score-based generation on MNIST and provide a promising extension to natural images, while the bridge sampler provides a stability mechanism for larger lifts.

中文摘要翻译: 基于分数的扩散模型通常使用布朗扰动，这类扰动提供了可逆的反向时间动力学，但施加的是无记忆性的加噪过程。本文提出Volterra生成模型，一种连续时间基于分数的框架，其前向过程通过分数核注入路径依赖噪声。为处理非马尔可夫和非半鞅动力学，我们利用高斯求积在不同模式下构造有限维马尔可夫提升，并在平滑模式下使用混合有限差分指数近似。我们证明了平方误差上界，推导了增广线性高斯前向过程，并通过考虑残差状态和解析辅助高斯得分表明学习可以保持数据维度。我们还发现了由共享布朗因子和有符号平滑模式权重引起的协方差与反向时间退化问题，该退化问题激发了稳定化条件处理以及针对刚性更大提升的高斯桥重建采样器。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，具有小马尔可夫提升的持久分数扰动能够改进MNIST上的得分生成，并为自然图像提供了有前景的扩展，同时桥采样器为更大提升提供了稳定性机制。

</details>


### [181] [Edge Flow: A Tractable and Predictive Continuous-Time Model for Gradient Descent at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18080)
*Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Edge Flow，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的连续时间模型，用于忠实预测梯度下降在稳定性边缘（EoS）的动力学行为，包含自稳定反馈回路。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习中的梯度下降在稳定性边缘运行时，经典分析工具（如梯度流和下降引理）不再适用，因此需要一种有效的连续时间模型来捕捉该区域的动力学行为。

Method: 提出Edge Flow模型，将动力学分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度三个耦合分量，分别用改进的梯度流、瑞利商动力学和指数增长/衰减来描述。离散化仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量乘积。

Result: 实验表明Edge Flow至少与先前提出的连续时间EoS模型一样忠实地跟踪梯度下降动力学，并且能额外解析EoS初始阶段的锐度振荡现象。

Conclusion: Edge Flow为理解和缓解稳定性边缘区域的不稳定性提供了一个原则性框架。

Original Abstract: Gradient descent in deep learning may operate at the edge of stability (EoS), a regime in which the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$, where $η$ is the learning rate. Classical analysis tools such as gradient flow and the descent lemma do not apply here, motivating the search for a continuous-time model valid at EoS. We propose Edge Flow, a system of three coupled ordinary differential equations that provides a tractable, faithful, and predictive model of gradient descent dynamics at EoS. Edge Flow decomposes the dynamics into a center, an oscillation direction, and an oscillation magnitude. The center follows a modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss; the direction tracks a top eigenvector of the Hessian via Rayleigh quotient dynamics; and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially depending on whether the sharpness exceeds or falls below the threshold $2/η$. Crucially, sharpness stabilization emerges from the coupled dynamics via a self-stabilization feedback loop. Discretizing Edge Flow only requires two gradient evaluations and one Hessian--vector product at each iteration. We demonstrate empirically that Edge Flow tracks the dynamics of gradient descent at least as faithfully as previously proposed continuous-time EoS models, while in addition resolving the oscillation of the sharpness at the onset of EoS, and that it provides a principled framework for understanding and mitigating instabilities in this regime.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习中的梯度下降可能在稳定性边缘（EoS）运行，此时损失Hessian矩阵的最大特征值徘徊在稳定性阈值$2/η$附近，其中$η$是学习率。梯度流和下降引理等经典分析工具在此不再适用，这促使我们寻找一个在EoS下有效的连续时间模型。我们提出Edge Flow，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的系统，为EoS下梯度下降动力学提供了易于处理、忠实且具有预测性的模型。Edge Flow将动力学分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度三个分量。中心服从对称化损失上的改进梯度流；方向通过瑞利商动力学跟踪Hessian矩阵的顶特征向量；而幅度根据锐度是否超过或低于阈值$2/η$呈指数增长或衰减。关键在于，锐度稳定化是通过耦合动力学的自稳定反馈回路实现的。对Edge Flow进行离散化仅需每次迭代两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量乘积。我们通过实验证明，Edge Flow至少与之前提出的连续时间EoS模型一样忠实地跟踪梯度下降动力学，此外还能解析EoS初始阶段的锐度振荡，并且为理解和缓解该区域的不稳定性提供了一个原则性框架。

</details>


### [182] [From Reasoning Traces to Reusable Modules: Understanding Compositional Generalization in Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18089)
*Lingjing Kong,Xin Liu,Guangyi Chen,Martin Q. Ma,Xiangchen Song,Yuekai Sun,Mikhail Yurochkin,Taylor W. Killian,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Kun Zhang,Eric P. Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SFT和RL在LLM后训练中扮演非对称互补角色：SFT提供组合轨迹中的原始模块材料，RL分解这些轨迹以识别潜在原子模块并实现组合泛化。理论框架基于层次化潜在选择模型，实验验证了RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重新组合解决新配置。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）在后训练流程中协同作用的内在机制，证明其成功源于组合泛化，并为两者如何分工配合提供理论基础。

Method: 通过层次化潜在选择模型形式化组合泛化，将推理轨迹建模为离散潜在选择变量的级联；理论上证明SFT与RL的非对称互补角色；设计控制实验验证理论预测。

Result: ①RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重新组合解决新配置；②在复合轨迹上训练比在孤立原子模块上训练产生更强的泛化能力；③识别出有效协议：SFT通过组合轨迹确保所有原子模块的覆盖，RL聚焦于SFT覆盖范围之外的新组合以驱动探索。

Conclusion: SFT和RL在LLM后训练中发挥非对称互补作用，共同实现组合泛化；最优策略是SFT提供覆盖所有原子模块的复合轨迹，RL则专注于探索新的组合方式。

Original Abstract: Post-training pipelines that combine supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) have emerged as the key recipe for transforming large language models (LLMs) into robust reasoners. We argue that this combined success is driven by compositional generalization, which we formalize through a hierarchical latent selection model. In this framework, reasoning traces are generated by a cascade of discrete latent selection variables corresponding to reusable atomic modules, including both skills (local operations) and routing mechanisms (how intermediate information is selected, reused, and composed). Within this model, we theoretically show that SFT and RL play asymmetric, complementary roles: SFT supplies the raw module materials in compositional traces, and RL decomposes those traces to identify the latent atomic modules and enable compositional generalization. We design controlled experiments to validate this theory. Our results demonstrate that RL can extract atomic modules from compound traces supplied by SFT and recombine them to solve new configurations. Moreover, we find that training on compound traces yields stronger generalization than training on isolated atomic modules. Finally, we investigate the relationship between SFT and RL data and identify an effective protocol in which SFT ensures coverage of all atomic modules through compositional traces, while RL focuses on novel compositions outside the SFT support to drive exploration.

中文摘要翻译: 结合监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）的后训练流程，已成为将大型语言模型（LLMs）转化为强推理能力模型的关键方法。我们认为，这种协同成功源于组合泛化，并通过层次化潜在选择模型对其进行了形式化。在该框架中，推理轨迹由一系列离散的潜在选择变量级联生成，这些变量对应于可重用的原子模块，既包括技能（局部操作），也包括路由机制（中间信息的选择、重用与组合方式）。在该模型下，我们从理论上证明SFT和RL发挥着非对称且互补的作用：SFT提供组合轨迹中的原始模块材料，而RL则分解这些轨迹以识别潜在的原子模块，从而实现组合泛化。我们设计了控制实验来验证这一理论。结果表明，RL能够从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块，并将它们重新组合以解决新配置问题。此外，我们发现，在复合轨迹上进行训练比在孤立的原子模块上训练能产生更强的泛化能力。最后，我们探究了SFT与RL数据之间的关系，并确定了一种有效的策略：SFT通过组合轨迹确保对所有原子模块的覆盖，而RL则专注于SFT覆盖范围之外的新组合，以驱动探索。

</details>


### [183] [S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18096)
*Marco Deano,Filippo Ziche,Nicola Bombieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对S4/S4D状态空间模型的增量式、算子级结构化剪枝方法，可在剪枝高达70%算子时保持模型性能，显著降低推理延迟，推动SSM在资源受限场景下的实际部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管S4和S4D等结构化状态空间模型在捕捉序列长程依赖方面表现出色，但在时间和资源受限的环境中，其计算和内存开销仍然过高，亟需有效的模型压缩方法以降低推理成本。

Method: 提出一种增量式、算子级结构化剪枝方法，通过将结构化掩码与微调交替进行，逐步剪枝模型算子，同时联合监控准确率和推理延迟，实现在统一训练评估框架下对效率-精度权衡的系统探索。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，剪枝高达70%的模型算子在大多数情况下仍能保持原始模型的性能，同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 结构化算子剪枝是提升SSM效率的有效且此前未被探索的策略，有助于推动SSM在资源受限的实际场景中的部署。

Original Abstract: Structured State Space Models (SSMs), including the S4 and S4D architectures, have recently emerged as powerful alternatives to attention-based models for capturing long-range dependencies in sequential data. Despite their strong empirical performance, deploying these models in time- and resource-constrained settings remains challenging due to their computational and memory demands. In this paper, we propose a novel incremental, operator-level pruning approach for S4- and S4D-based models that significantly reduces inference cost while preserving predictive performance. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically investigate structured operator pruning for SSMs. Our method progressively prunes model operators by interleaving structured masking with fine-tuning, while jointly monitoring accuracy and inference latency. We implement this approach within a unified training and evaluation framework that enables systematic exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that pruning up to 70% of the model operators preserves the performance of the original models in most cases, while substantially reducing inference latency. These results demonstrate that structured operator pruning is an effective and previously unexplored strategy for improving the efficiency of SSMs and facilitate their deployment in practical, resource-constrained scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 结构化状态空间模型（SSMs），包括S4和S4D架构，最近已成为基于注意力的模型在捕捉序列数据长程依赖方面的强大替代方案。尽管这些模型具有强大的实证性能，但由于其计算和内存需求，在时间和资源受限的环境中部署仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的增量式、算子级剪枝方法，用于基于S4和S4D的模型，该方法在保留预测性能的同时显著降低推理成本。据我们所知，这是首次系统性地研究SSM的结构化算子剪枝工作。我们的方法通过将结构化掩码与微调交替进行，同时联合监控准确率和推理延迟，逐步剪枝模型算子。我们在一个统一的训练和评估框架中实现这一方法，从而能够系统性地探索效率与精度之间的权衡。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，剪枝高达70%的模型算子在大多数情况下仍能保持原始模型的性能，同时大幅降低推理延迟。这些结果表明，结构化算子剪枝是一种有效的且此前未被探索的提升SSM效率的策略，有助于推动SSM在资源受限的实际场景中的部署。

</details>


### [184] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Minimum Zero-Forcing Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18106)
*Steve Halley,Maurício Gruppi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架SD-ZFS（改编自S2V-DQN架构），用于解决无向图上的最小零强制集问题（NP-hard）。实验表明，该框架在不同图结构数据集上相比最优解和贪婪启发式算法表现有效，并分析了模型在泛化、扩展和迁移方面的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 最小零强制集问题在网络安全、网络控制和逻辑电路设计等领域有重要应用，但该问题已被证明是NP-hard的，传统方法难以高效求解，因此需要借助机器学习方法寻找近似最优解。

Method: 提出SD-ZFS框架，将强化学习中的S2V-DQN架构适配到零强制集问题中。在多种不同结构的图数据集上训练多个模型，并评估其泛化能力、扩展性和迁移性。

Result: 与最优解和贪婪启发式算法相比，SD-ZFS框架表现出有效性；模型的泛化、扩展和迁移性能在不同网络类型上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法可以有效地解决最小零强制集问题，网络结构对问题求解有显著影响，该框架为进一步研究提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: This paper explores the problem of finding the minimum zero-forcing set on undirected graphs and proposes an adapted machine-learning framework to solve the problem. The minimum zero-forcing set problem is a graph coloring problem where the color of an initial set of nodes propagates throughout a network. The set of nodes is zero-forcing if it forces all uncolored nodes to change color under the constraint of the color-change rule. There are several applications to this problem across different domains such as network science, network control, and designing logical circuits. Finding the minimum zero-forcing set is shown to be NP-hard. We propose a reinforcement learning framework, SD-ZFS, that adapts the S2V-DQN architecture to the ZFS problem. We train several models on this adapted framework and analyze the performance across graph datasets that have varying structures. We evaluate how the models trained on the framework generalize, scale, and transfer to different network types. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework when compared against the optimal solution and greedy heuristic. We provide further insight into how the ZFS problem can be solved through machine-learning and the influence of network structure on the problem.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在无向图中寻找最小零强制集的问题，并提出了一种适配的机器学习框架来求解该问题。最小零强制集问题是一种图着色问题，其中初始节点集合的颜色会通过网络传播。如果某个节点集能在颜色变化规则的约束下迫使所有未着色节点改变颜色，则该节点集是零强制集。该问题在网络科学、网络控制和逻辑电路设计等多个领域有着广泛的应用。已证明寻找最小零强制集是NP-hard问题。我们提出了一种强化学习框架SD-ZFS，该框架将S2V-DQN架构适配到零强制集问题。我们在该适配框架上训练了多个模型，并分析了它们在具有不同结构的图数据集上的性能。我们评估了在该框架上训练的模型在不同网络类型上的泛化、扩展和迁移能力。结果表明，与最优解和贪婪启发式算法相比，该框架具有有效性。我们进一步深入探讨了如何通过机器学习求解零强制集问题，以及网络结构对该问题的影响。

</details>


### [185] [Learning Fair Pareto-Optimal Policies in Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18111)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多策略多目标强化学习（MORL）中的公平性问题，提出将广义基尼福利函数（GGF）与多策略MOQL结合，通过学习非平稳策略和随机策略，生成一组覆盖所有用户偏好的帕累托最优公平策略集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单策略MORL方法虽能用福利函数（如GGF）为固定偏好学习公平策略，但在用户偏好动态变化或未知时无法提供多样化策略集。因此需要形式化多策略MORL中的公平优化问题。

Method: (1) 证明对凹分段线性福利函数（如GGF），公平策略仍属于凸覆盖集(CCS)；(2) 论证带累积奖励历史的非平稳策略和随机策略能通过动态适应历史不公平性来提升公平性；(3) 提出三种算法：GGF+多策略MOQL、状态增强多策略MOQL学习非平稳策略、以及学习随机策略的扩展方法。

Result: 在多个领域的实验结果表明，所提方法能学习到一组公平策略，适应不同用户偏好，且优于现有的MORL基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功地形式化了多策略MORL中的公平优化问题，并验证了利用凸覆盖集性质以及非平稳/随机策略能够有效学习面向所有用户偏好的公平帕累托最优策略集。

Original Abstract: Fairness is an important aspect of decision-making in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), where policies must ensure both optimality and equity across multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. While single-policy MORL methods can learn fair policies for fixed user preferences using welfare functions such as the generalized Gini welfare function (GGF), they fail to provide the diverse set of policies necessary for dynamic or unknown user preferences. To address this limitation, we formalize the fair optimization problem in multi-policy MORL, where the goal is to learn a set of Pareto-optimal policies that ensure fairness across all possible user preferences. Our key technical contributions are threefold: (1) We show that for concave, piecewise-linear welfare functions (e.g., GGF), fair policies remain in the convex coverage set (CCS), which is an approximated Pareto front for linear scalarization. (2) We demonstrate that non-stationary policies, augmented with accrued reward histories, and stochastic policies improve fairness by dynamically adapting to historical inequities. (3) We propose three novel algorithms, which include integrating GGF with multi-policy multi-objective Q-Learning (MOQL), state-augmented multi-policy MOQL for learning non-statoinary policies, and its novel extension for learning stochastic policies. We evaluate our algorithms across various domains and compare our methods against the state-of-the-art MORL baselines. The empirical results show that our methods learn a set of fair policies that accommodate different user preferences.

中文摘要翻译: 公平性是多目标强化学习（MORL）决策中的一个重要方面，策略必须在多个潜在冲突的目标之间同时确保最优性和公平性。虽然单策略MORL方法能够使用福利函数（如广义基尼福利函数GGF）为固定的用户偏好学习公平策略，但无法提供应对动态或未知用户偏好所需的多样化策略集。为解决这一局限，我们在多策略MORL中形式化了公平优化问题，其目标是学习一组帕累托最优策略，确保在所有可能的用户偏好下都能实现公平。我们的关键技术贡献有三点：(1) 证明了对于凹分段线性福利函数（如GGF），公平策略仍属于凸覆盖集（CCS），即线性标量化下的近似帕累托前沿；(2) 论证了带有累积奖励历史增强的非平稳策略以及随机策略，能够通过动态适应历史不公平性来提升公平性；(3) 提出了三种新算法，包括将GGF与多策略多目标Q学习（MOQL）相结合、用于学习非平稳策略的状态增强多策略MOQL，以及用于学习随机策略的新型扩展方法。我们在多个领域评估了所提算法，并将我们的方法与最先进的MORL基线进行了比较。实验结果表明，我们的方法能够学习一组适应不同用户偏好的公平策略。

</details>


### [186] [Ternary Mamba: Grouped Quantization-Aware Training of W1.58A16 State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18114)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Sahil Dilip Panse,Swathika N*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从预训练检查点出发、结合分组量化感知训练（QAT）和知识蒸馏（KD）的路径，仅需1亿个token即可将Mamba-2 1.3B模型三值量化至3.61倍压缩比（744 MB），零样本准确率接近Bi-Mamba基线，相比从头训练节省1000倍边际token预算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 状态空间模型（SSM）如Mamba-2虽然推理复杂度为线性，但其内存占用仍然限制了边缘端部署。此前的工作（Slender-Mamba）需要从头训练150B tokens才能实现三值SSM，训练成本极高。本文旨在探索能否利用预训练检查点以极低的数据量实现高效三值量化，大幅降低边际训练成本。

Method: 采用分组量化感知训练（Grouped QAT），结合冻结的FP16教师模型进行知识蒸馏（KD），从预训练的Mamba-2 1.3B检查点出发进行三值量化压缩，无需从头训练。

Result: 将Mamba-2 1.3B从2,687 MB压缩至744 MB（3.61倍压缩），仅用1.02亿token（4 GPU小时，单张H100）即达到48.1%零样本准确率（7任务平均），接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%（置信区间±0.9pp内）。发现了一种新的不稳定性——零比例坍缩（zero-ratio collapse），由可学习的量化尺度导致，在从头训练中不会出现。还发现Transformer中有效的后处理校正策略因SSM的递归特性会导致误差累积而失效。

Conclusion: 三值SSM无需昂贵的从头训练，从预训练检查点出发结合QAT和KD是一种数据高效的替代方案。

Original Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba-2 offer linear-time inference but their memory footprint limits edge deployment. Prior ternary SSM work (Slender-Mamba) trains from scratch on 150B tokens; we show a pretrained checkpoint suffices, reducing the marginal token budget by 1,000x. Using grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) with knowledge distillation from a frozen FP16 teacher, we compress Mamba-2 1.3B to 3.61x (2,687 to 744 MB) and achieve 48.1% zero-shot accuracy (7-task average) in just 102M tokens (4 GPU-hours, single H100) -- approaching Bi-Mamba's 48.4% (within +/-0.9pp CI). This QAT-from-pretrained setting reveals zero-ratio collapse, a novel instability caused by learnable quantization scales that does not arise in from-scratch training. We further show that post-hoc correction strategies effective for Transformers fail for SSMs due to error accumulation through the recurrence. These results demonstrate that ternary SSMs do not require expensive from-scratch training: QAT from pretrained checkpoints with KD is a data-efficient alternative.

中文摘要翻译: 状态空间模型（SSMs）如Mamba-2虽然提供了线性时间的推理能力，但其内存占用限制了边缘端部署。先前的三值SSM工作（Slender-Mamba）在150B token上从头训练；我们证明预训练检查点足以胜任，将边际token预算降低了1000倍。通过使用分组量化感知训练（QAT）结合冻结的FP16教师模型的知识蒸馏，我们将Mamba-2 1.3B压缩了3.61倍（从2,687 MB降至744 MB），并在仅1.02亿token（4 GPU小时，单张H100）上实现了48.1%的零样本准确率（7任务平均）——接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%（在±0.9pp置信区间内）。这种基于预训练模型的QAT设置揭示了零比例坍缩（zero-ratio collapse），这是一种由可学习量化尺度引起的新颖不稳定性，在从头训练中不会出现。我们进一步证明，对于Transformer有效的后处理校正策略在SSM上会因递归导致的误差累积而失效。这些结果表明，三值SSM不需要昂贵的从头训练：从预训练检查点出发结合知识蒸馏的量化感知训练是一种数据高效的替代方案。

</details>


### [187] [Kolmogorov Regression for Robust Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18186)
*Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于后向Kolmogorov方程的有限维扩散策略改进方法，通过将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间并引入精度加权损失，解决了时间漂移问题，在操作任务和制造系统上显著提升了性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 有限维扩散策略由于离散化伪影存在时间漂移，导致在物理系统上长期部署时性能下降。

Method: 引入后向Kolmogorov方程将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间（Hilbert空间的子集），用确定性边界值PDE问题替代随机分数匹配；从色噪声分布中实现扩散噪声协方差算子，推导出精度加权的Cameron-Martin损失函数，并引入Kolmogorov残差作为推理时的PDE诊断工具。

Result: PushT操作任务：最大回合奖励提升17%（0.95 vs 0.78），步间漂移减少67.6%；6站CONWIP产线：RMSE比LSTM降低28.4%，饥饿事件召回率1.0，瓶颈识别Precision@1=1.0，信噪比13倍；结合Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论将死锁事件减少96%。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了收敛性保证（常数取决于核有效秩而非动作维数），通过谱加权改善了轨迹正则性，并实现了无需奖励信号的确定性故障检测器。

Original Abstract: Finite-dimensional (FD) diffusion policies exhibit temporal drift owing to discretization artifacts that degrade long-horizon performance (when deployed on physical systems). We introduce a backward Kolmogorov equation that lifts diffusion policies to a Cameron-Martin space -- a subset of the Hilbert space. Essentially, replacing stochastic score matching with a deterministic boundary-value PDE problem. Our core innovation thrives on Gaussian measure theory whereupon the diffusion noise covariance operator is realized from a colored noise distribution which prescribes a notion of regularity on samples from the model at inference time. We train the diffusion model with a derived precision-weighted Cameron- Martin loss and a Kolmogorov residual is introduced as a PDE diagnostic during inference. These substitutions yield (i) convergence guarantees where the bound's constants depend on the effective rank of the kernel rather than action dimension, (ii) improved trajectory regularity via spectral weighting, and (iii) a deterministic failure detector without reward signals. Validation across two application domains demonstrates substantial improvements: on the PushT manipulation benchmark, the Cameron-Martin loss achieves a 17% improvement in maximum episode reward (0.95 vs. 0.78 for MSE) and 67.6% reduction in inter-step drifts during inference via the introduced residual magnitude. Similarly, on a 6-station manufacturing line with constant work-in-process (CONWIP) flow control, we achieve 28.4% lower RMSE than classical LSTM baselines; a high starvation-event recall (1.0 in test cycles), and effective bottleneck identification (Precision@1 = 1.0 in test set, 13x signal-to-noise ratio). We then certify the dispatch policies with Hamilton-Jacobi reachability theory which reduces deadlock events by 96% compared to uncontrolled dispatch over 100 simulated runs (351 events prevented).

中文摘要翻译: 有限维扩散策略因离散化伪影而产生时间漂移，这损害了在物理系统上部署时的长期性能。我们引入了一个后向Kolmogorov方程，将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间——希尔伯特空间的一个子集。本质上，这是用确定性边界值PDE问题替代了随机分数匹配。我们的核心创新基于高斯测度理论，其中扩散噪声协方差算子由色噪声分布实现，该分布规定了模型在推理时样本的正则性概念。我们使用推导出的精度加权Cameron-Martin损失来训练扩散模型，并引入Kolmogorov残差作为推理时的PDE诊断工具。这些替换带来了：(i) 收敛性保证，其边界常数取决于核的有效秩而非动作维数；(ii) 通过谱加权改进的轨迹正则性；(iii) 无需奖励信号的确定性故障检测器。在两个应用领域的验证展示了显著改进：在PushT操作基准测试中，Cameron-Martin损失实现了最大回合奖励17%的提升（0.95对比MSE的0.78），并且通过引入的残差幅度在推理期间减少了67.6%的步间漂移。类似地，在一个具有恒定在制品（CONWIP）流量控制的6站制造线上，我们实现了比经典LSTM基线低28.4%的RMSE；高饥饿事件召回率（测试周期中为1.0），以及有效的瓶颈识别（测试集中Precision@1=1.0，信噪比13倍）。然后，我们使用Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论验证调度策略，与100次模拟运行中的无控制调度相比，死锁事件减少了96%（防止了351个事件）。

</details>


### [188] [Looped World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18208)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L. Victor Wei,Qun Zhang,Jinrui Zeng,Bowen Cao,Lingwei Meng,Mocheng Li,Zezhong Wang,Haonan Yin,Naifu Xue,Minyu Chen,Cenyuan Zhang,Zefan Zhang,Hao Wei,Jiawei Zhou,Haoran Xu,Hao Yang,Ronglai Zuo,Tongda Xu,Yonghao Li,Jian Chen,Hebin Wang,Zeyu Gao,Yang Li,Wei Zhao,Qimin Zhong,Siqi Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Leyan Cui,Zhangyu Wang,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环世界模型(LoopWM)，通过参数共享的Transformer块迭代细化隐状态，以极低的参数量实现高质量长程世界模拟，并建立“迭代潜在深度”作为新的扩展维度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型在长程模拟时面临矛盾：忠实模拟需要深层计算，但深层模型部署成本高且易累积误差。

Method: 提出首个循环架构的世界模型LoopWM，使用参数共享的Transformer块对潜在环境状态进行迭代细化，实现自适应计算深度。

Result: 相比传统方法，在保持模拟质量的同时实现了高达100倍的参数效率，且计算深度能根据每个预测步骤的复杂度自动调整。

Conclusion: LoopWM将迭代潜在深度确立为世界模拟的新扩展轴，与模型规模和数据规模正交，有望推动该领域发展。

Original Abstract: Current world models face a fundamental tension: faithful long-horizon simulation demands deep computation, but deeper models are expensive to deploy and prone to compounding errors. We resolve this by introducing Looped World Models (LoopWM), which are the first looped architectures for world modelling. Our method iteratively refines latent environment states through a parameter-shared transformer block. This yield up to 100x parameter efficiency over conventional approaches with adaptive computation that automatically scales depth to match the complexity of each prediction step. Orthogonal to scaling model size and training data, LoopWM establishes iterative latent depth as a new scaling axis for world simulation, which might significantly push the community forward.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的世界模型面临一个根本性矛盾：忠实的长程模拟需要深层计算，但越深的模型部署成本越高且容易累积复合误差。我们通过引入循环世界模型（LoopWM）解决了这一问题，这是首个用于世界建模的循环架构。我们的方法通过一个参数共享的Transformer块对潜在环境状态进行迭代细化。与传统方法相比，这不仅实现了高达100倍的参数效率，还具备自适应计算能力，能够自动调整深度以匹配每个预测步骤的复杂度。与扩展模型规模和训练数据正交，LoopWM将迭代潜在深度确立为世界模拟的一个新扩展轴，这可能极大地推动该领域向前发展。

</details>


### [189] [Rethinking Dataset Distillation for Classification: Do Distilled Sets Outperform Coresets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18209)
*Trisha Mittal,Akshay Mehra,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模标准化实验，系统评估了数据集蒸馏（DD）方法的实际效果，发现最先进的DD方法在大规模数据集上的性能与coreset选择（CS）方法相当甚至更差，且构建成本更高，质疑了DD方法相对于数据剪枝方法的优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法在评估协议上不一致，从标准ERM到单/多教师监督不等，难以分离蒸馏数据本身的有效性与评估方式的影响；同时许多方法声称DD优于数据剪枝方法（如coreset选择），但这一结论缺乏严格的标准化对比验证。

Method: 在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100和ImageNette三个数据集上，使用三种广泛采用的训练协议，对七种最先进的数据集蒸馏方法与三种coreset选择策略进行标准化基准对比实验；并从准确率之外还评估了紧凑数据集的代表性、多样性和质量。

Result: 部分DD方法甚至不如简单的随机子集；SOTA DD方法在大规模数据集上与coreset方法相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高；coreset在覆盖原始数据分布方面始终优于DD方法。

Conclusion: 当前数据集蒸馏方法的实际优势有限，coreset选择方法仍然具有竞争力，且通常是数据中心学习更具计算效率的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) has emerged as a prominent approach in data centric machine learning, aiming to synthesize compact training sets for efficient training by compressing the information in large datasets into a small number of synthetic samples. However, DD methods are often evaluated under inconsistent evaluation protocols, ranging from standard ERM to single/multi-teacher supervision, making it difficult to isolate the effectiveness of distilled data from evaluation. Moreover, many prior methods claim that DD outperforms data pruning approaches such as coreset selection (CS), based on the assumption that restricting condensed datasets to subsets of real samples fundamentally limits their expressiveness. In this work, we critically evaluate DD methods through large-scale experiments using standardized datasets and evaluation protocols to assess their intrinsic effectiveness. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art (SOTA) DD methods on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette, using three widely adopted training protocols against three CS strategies. Our results show that while some DD methods fail to outperform even simple random subsets, the SOTA DD approaches are comparable to or worse than coresets on large-scale datasets and incur a substantially higher cost for construction. Beyond accuracy, we also evaluate the representativeness, diversity, and quality of condensed sets, and find that coresets consistently achieve better coverage of the original data distribution. These findings highlight the limited practical advantages of current DD methods and show that coresets remain competitive and are often a more computationally efficient alternative for data-centric learning.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集蒸馏（Dataset Distillation, DD）已成为数据驱动机器学习中的一种重要方法，旨在通过将大数据集中的信息压缩到少量合成样本中，合成紧凑的训练集以实现高效训练。然而，DD方法常常在不一致的评估协议下进行评估——从标准的经验风险最小化（ERM）到单/多教师监督——这使得难以从评估中分离出蒸馏数据本身的有效性。此外，许多先前方法声称DD优于数据剪枝方法（如coreset选择, CS），其假设是将精炼数据集限制为真实样本的子集会从根本上限制其表达能力。在本工作中，我们通过使用标准化数据集和评估协议进行大规模实验，批判性地评估了DD方法的内在有效性。我们在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100和ImageNette上，使用三种广泛采用的训练协议，对七种最先进（SOTA）的DD方法以及三种CS策略进行了基准测试。我们的结果表明，虽然部分DD方法甚至无法胜过简单的随机子集，但SOTA的DD方法在大规模数据集上与coreset方法相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高。除了准确率之外，我们还评估了精炼数据集的代表性、多样性和质量，发现coreset在覆盖原始数据分布方面始终表现更好。这些发现揭示了当前DD方法的实际优势有限，并表明coreset仍然具有竞争力，且通常是数据中心学习中更具计算效率的替代方案。

</details>


### [190] [Sign-Rank, Index, and List Replicability: Connections and Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18236)
*Ari Blondal,Hamed Hatami,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元概念类的符号秩（sign rank）下界问题，通过比较两种下界度量——Z₂-指数（Z₂-index）和列表可复现数（list replicability number），证明了Z₂-指数受列表可复现数的线性函数约束，从而解决了Frick等人的一个开放问题，并进一步建立了列表可复现数与组合度量之间的上界关系及组合性质。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 符号秩的下界难以直接证明，近期出现两种更易分析的度量（Z₂-指数和列表可复现数），但两者之间的关系尚不明确。本文旨在厘清这两种度量之间的序关系，并解答Frick、Hosseini和Vasileuski提出的关于符号秩与Z₂-指数之间分离性的开放问题。

Method: (1) 证明Z₂-指数受列表可复现数的线性函数上界约束，从而建立两者之间的序关系；(2) 通过高度（height）和最小星数（minimum star number）两种组合度量来建立列表可复现数的上界；(3) 证明概念类乘积的列表可复现数受两个类各自列表可复现数之和约束的组合结果。

Result: (1) 证明了Z₂-指数 ≤ 列表可复现数的线性函数，实现了符号秩与Z₂-指数的强分离，解决了Frick等人的开放问题；(2) 建立了列表可复现数由高度和最小星数度量的上界；(3) 证明了乘积概念类的列表可复现数满足可加性（即≤ 两个类列表可复现数之和）。

Conclusion: 列表可复现数是比Z₂-指数更强的下界度量，值得深入研究；通过组合度量可以有效地约束列表可复现数，且该度量具有良好的组合性质。

Original Abstract: In learning theory, the sign rank of a binary concept class captures the smallest dimension in which it can be represented by points and halfspaces. Despite tremendous interest, lower bounds on sign rank are notoriously difficult to come by. Two recent approaches to the problem establish lower bounds on sign rank by measures that are easier to analyze: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and the list replicability number.
  We order these measures, showing that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is upper-bounded by a linear function of the list replicability number. As a main consequence, we obtain a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, thereby resolving a question of Frick, Hosseini, and Vasileuski.
  This motivates a thorough study of list replicability, the stronger of the two lower-bounding measures. We establish upper bounds on the list replicability number by two combinatorial measures: height and minimum star number. We also prove a fundamental composition result, showing that the product of two concept classes has list replicability number bounded by the sum of the list replicability numbers of the two classes.

中文摘要翻译: 在学习理论中，二元概念类的符号秩（sign rank）刻画了能够通过点和半空间表示该类的最小维度。尽管备受关注，符号秩的下界却难以证明。近期有两种方法通过更易分析的度量来建立符号秩的下界：Z₂-指数（Z₂-index）和列表可复现数（list replicability number）。本文对这些度量进行排序，证明Z₂-指数受列表可复现数的线性函数上界约束。由此主要结论，我们实现了符号秩与Z₂-指数之间的强分离，从而解决了Frick、Hosseini和Vasileuski提出的一个问题。这促使我们对列表可复现数（两种下界度量中更强的那个）进行深入研究。我们通过两种组合度量——高度（height）和最小星数（minimum star number）——建立了列表可复现数的上界。我们还证明了一个基本的组合结果：两个概念类的乘积，其列表可复现数受这两个类各自列表可复现数之和的约束。

</details>

### [191] [Ten Years of the Stochastic Resonance Model of Tinnitus: From Phantom Perception to Adaptive Sensory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17736)
*Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文综述了随机共振（SR）模型从现象学假说发展为神经计算理论的过程，该模型将耳鸣重新定义为听觉系统在听力损失后为优化信息传递而自适应上调内部神经噪声的副产物，而非传统认为的病理功能障碍。研究得到计算建模、临床分析和动物实验的支持，并催生了基于近阈值噪声刺激的治疗方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 回顾随机共振模型从2016年提出至今的发展历程，总结其理论演进和实验验证，并展望未来在机制验证和临床转化方面的方向，以重新定义耳鸣的神经机制。

Method: 采用综述方法，按时间顺序梳理随机共振模型的发展脉络，整合计算建模、大规模临床分析和动物实验等多源证据，并集成随机共振、中枢增益、稳态可塑性和预测编码等理论框架。

Result: 主要理论和实验进展包括：特定噪声条件下听觉检测能力提升、频率特异性的幻听感知、基于频谱匹配近阈值噪声刺激的治疗方法，以及将随机共振与中枢增益、稳态可塑性、预测编码整合为统一的听觉幻听感知理论。

Conclusion: 随机共振模型将耳鸣从病理功能障碍重新定义为适应性感觉计算的副产物，将研究焦点从病理机制转向神经系统的信息优化原理，为耳鸣的机制理解和临床治疗提供了新的理论视角。

Original Abstract: Subjective tinnitus - the perception of sound in the absence of an external acoustic stimulus - remains one of the most debated phenomena in auditory neuroscience. In 2016, the stochastic resonance (SR) model was introduced as an alternative account of tinnitus-related neuronal hyperactivity, proposing that internally generated neural noise is adaptively upregulated to restore information transmission after hearing loss. Rather than interpreting increased spontaneous activity as maladaptive, the model reframed it as a functional mechanism that enhances signal detection near sensory thresholds, with tinnitus emerging as a side effect of adaptive sensory optimization. Over the past decade, this framework has evolved from a phenomenological hypothesis into a broader neurocomputational theory linking information theory, adaptive signal detection, multichannel auditory processing, and cross-modal plasticity. Computational modeling, large-scale clinical analyses, and animal experiments have provided converging support for key predictions, including improved detectability under specific noise conditions and frequency-specific phantom percepts. The framework has also inspired a therapeutic approach based on spectrally matched near-threshold noise stimulation and has recently been integrated into a unified account of auditory phantom perception that combines stochastic resonance, central gain, homeostatic plasticity, and predictive coding. This review provides a chronological overview of the development of the stochastic resonance model, summarizes major theoretical and empirical advances, and outlines future directions for mechanistic validation and clinical translation. By redefining tinnitus as a consequence of adaptive sensory computation, the model shifts the conceptual focus from pathological dysfunction toward principles of information optimization in neural systems.

中文摘要翻译: 主观性耳鸣——在缺乏外部声刺激时感知到声音——仍然是听觉神经科学中最具争议的现象之一。2016年，随机共振（SR）模型被提出作为耳鸣相关神经元过度活跃的替代解释，该模型认为内部产生的神经噪声被自适应上调以在听力损失后恢复信息传递。该模型没有将增加的自发放电活动解释为适应不良，而是将其重新定义为一种增强感觉阈值附近信号检测的功能性机制，耳鸣则作为适应性感觉优化的副产物出现。在过去的十年中，这一框架已从现象学假说发展为更广泛的神经计算理论，将信息论、自适应信号检测、多通道听觉处理和跨模式可塑性联系起来。计算建模、大规模临床分析和动物实验为关键预测提供了汇聚性支持，包括特定噪声条件下检测能力的提升以及频率特异性的幻听感知。该框架还启发了一种基于频谱匹配近阈值噪声刺激的治疗方法，并最近被整合进一个统一的听觉幻听感知理论中，该理论结合了随机共振、中枢增益、稳态可塑性和预测编码。本综述按时间顺序概述了随机共振模型的发展，总结了主要理论和实验进展，并概述了机制验证和临床转化的未来方向。通过将耳鸣重新定义为适应性感觉计算的结果，该模型将概念焦点从病理功能障碍转向神经系统中信息优化的原理。

</details>


### [192] [Separating wiring-specific from statistical control of dynamics in a complete connectome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17745)
*Stavros Therianos*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过将完整果蝇幼虫连接组作为固定速率动态模型运行，证明神经回路的整体动态特性（响应强度与丰富度）主要由连接组的粗粒度统计特征决定，而精确的连接模式则决定活动传播路径和哪些回路塑造动态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究仅凭神经回路的结构连接图（连接组）能在多大程度上固定电路的活动模式，而不依赖未被记录的精细生理细节。

Method: 将完整的果蝇幼虫连接组作为固定、基于速率的动态算子运行，不拟合任何单神经元参数；并将其与一系列保持不同粗粒度连接描述的随机化网络进行比较。

Result: 1) 模型的整体动态特性是统计性的，保持粗粒度连接统计的随机网络可复现该特性；2) 精确连接决定活动传播位置与塑造动态的回路；3) 稀疏输入被限制在紧凑的嗅觉通路中，随机网络会使该通路泛滥；4) 蘑菇体在伴随侧主导模式中发挥超常作用。

Conclusion: 粗粒度统计设定动态机制，精确连接模式设定几何结构，这种分离澄清了哪些基于连接组的结论仅依赖于布线本身。

Original Abstract: Electron-microscopy reconstruction now yields complete synaptic wiring diagrams, or connectomes, of entire small brains, including the larval Drosophila, the first insect brain reconstructed in full. How far a wiring diagram alone fixes a circuit's activity, as opposed to the finer physiological detail it does not record, is debated. We run a complete connectome as a fixed, rate-based dynamical operator in which no single-neuron parameter is fitted, so that, at one fixed dynamical regime, the model's behavior reflects the wiring and its connection strengths rather than tuned single-neuron physiology, and compare it against a hierarchy of randomized networks that each preserve a coarser description of the wiring. The model's overall dynamical regime, how strongly and how richly it responds, is mostly statistical: networks keeping only the connectome's coarse wiring statistics reproduce it. The wiring beyond these statistics instead sets where activity travels and which circuits shape it. Sparse input is confined to a compact olfactory pathway that randomized networks flood, and the mushroom body, the insect learning center, takes an outsized role in the leading adjoint-side modes, the directions that weigh which neurons shape the recurrent dynamics. Coarse statistics set the regime; the precise pattern of connections sets the geometry, a separation that clarifies which connectome-based claims rest on wiring alone.

中文摘要翻译: 电子显微镜重建现已能产生完整的小型脑的突触布线图（即连接组），包括首个被完整重建的昆虫脑——果蝇幼虫脑。关于单独的布线图（而非其未记录的更精细的生理细节）在多大程度上固定了电路的活动，仍存在争议。本文将完整连接组作为一个固定的、基于速率的动态算子运行，其中没有任何单神经元参数经过拟合，因此在固定动态机制下，模型的行为反映的是布线及其连接强度而非调谐的单神经元生理特性，并将其与一系列随机化网络进行比较，每个随机化网络保留连接组的某种更粗粒度的描述。模型的整体动态机制（其响应的强度与丰富度）主要是统计性的：仅保留连接组粗粒度连接统计特性的网络即可复现该机制。超出这些统计特性的布线则决定了活动传向何处以及哪些回路塑造活动。稀疏输入被限制在一个紧凑的嗅觉通路中，而随机化网络会使该通路泛滥；蘑菇体（昆虫的学习中枢）在领先的伴随侧模式（即决定哪些神经元塑造循环动态的方向）中发挥了超常的作用。粗粒度统计设定机制，精确连接模式设定几何结构——这种分离澄清了哪些基于连接组的结论仅依赖于布线本身。

</details>
